# 运维跨国会议｜100场景｜长对话版

长对话修订版：100个场景，每组12轮，共1,200条中英对照发言，英文正文共28,420词。每组保留背景、角色、完整讨论、可复用表达与落实动作。围绕常见企业运维环境编写；全部案例、系统数据和时间安排为模拟，可替换为你的实际业务。

## 语音与练习方式

- 语音采用美式英语合成声音，仅朗读英文对话。A、B、C分别使用不同声音，便于识别角色；声音分配只用于练习，不代表岗位或性别。
- 每个音频含简短英文场景编号和分类引导，随后朗读12轮对话。技术缩写可能按字母或全称朗读，意思与文字稿保持一致。
- 音频文件为audio/001.mp3至audio/100.mp3；同名SRT文件提供英文字幕。角色表对应文字稿中的A/B/C标记。
- 建议先完整听一遍，再对照译文理解；第二遍逐句暂停跟读；第三遍只听对方角色，暂停后自行回答。
- 每天练习3—5组。优先练001—010、071—080、091—100，再根据日常工作补齐技术专题。
- 发言标记示例：01 [A] 表示第1次发言由角色A说；02 [B] 表示第2次发言由角色B说。序号和方括号标记无需朗读。

## 001 介绍职责与支持范围
**分类：** 跨国会议沟通  /  **英文词数：** 257
**音频：** [播放 MP3](audio/001.mp3)  /  [英文字幕](audio/001.srt)

**背景：** 你第一次参加全球运维例会，需要说明亚太区的支持范围，并明确与应用团队和全球值班团队的交接方式。

**角色：** A — Ops Engineer（运维工程师）；B — Global Lead（全球团队负责人）

**01 [A] / Ops Engineer：** I support production operations for the APAC region. My responsibilities include infrastructure checks, application monitoring, deployment support, and incident coordination.
> 我负责亚太区的生产运维，包括基础设施检查、应用监控、发布支持和故障协调。

**02 [B] / Global Lead：** Thanks for the introduction. When an application problem requires a code change, does your team make that change or involve another owner?
> 谢谢你的介绍。如果应用问题需要修改代码，是你们团队处理，还是联系其他负责人？

**03 [A] / Ops Engineer：** The application team owns code changes. We gather operational evidence, coordinate the response, and help validate the service after their fix is deployed.
> 代码修改由应用团队负责。我们收集运维证据、协调响应，并在修复部署后协助验证服务。

**04 [B] / Global Lead：** That helps. How should the global team contact you during your coverage hours, and who takes over after your regional shift ends?
> 这样就清楚了。全球团队在你们的支持时段如何联系你们？区域班次结束后由谁接手？

**05 [A] / Ops Engineer：** Please use the shared operations channel during our shift. For urgent incidents, page the current on-call engineer through the incident system.
> 我们值班期间，请使用共享运维频道。遇到紧急故障，请通过故障系统呼叫当前值班工程师。

**06 [B] / Global Lead：** Could you clarify whether your team also approves production changes? Some regional requests have been sent directly to individual engineers.
> 能说明一下你们团队是否也批准生产变更吗？有些区域请求直接发给了个别工程师。

**07 [A] / Ops Engineer：** We review operational readiness, but the designated change approver authorizes production changes. Sending a request to us does not replace that approval.
> 我们评审运维准备情况，但生产变更由指定审批人授权。向我们提交请求不能代替该审批。

**08 [B] / Global Lead：** Understood. Please join our weekly service review and highlight regional risks, especially capacity concerns or repeated incidents that need broader support.
> 明白。请参加每周服务评审，重点提出区域风险，尤其是容量问题或需要更广泛支持的重复故障。

**09 [A] / Ops Engineer：** I can do that. Would you prefer a short written update beforehand so the meeting can focus on decisions and blocked items?
> 可以。你是否希望我提前提供简短书面更新，让会议重点讨论决策和受阻事项？

**10 [B] / Global Lead：** Yes. Post the update one business day before the review, with the impact, current owner, and any decision needed from this group.
> 是的。请在评审前一个工作日发布更新，写明影响、当前负责人以及需要本团队作出的决定。

**11 [A] / Ops Engineer：** I will share our coverage schedule and responsibility map by 16:00 UTC tomorrow, including the handoff process and escalation contacts.
> 我会在明天16:00 UTC前分享支持时间表和职责分工，包含交接流程和升级联系人。

**12 [B] / Global Lead：** Great. I will review those details and add you to the recurring meeting. We can resolve any remaining ownership gaps there.
> 好的。我会核对这些信息，并把你加入周期会议。剩余的职责空缺可以在会上解决。

**可复用表达：**

- The application team owns code changes. — 代码修改由应用团队负责。
- Sending a request to us does not replace that approval. — 向我们提交请求不能代替该审批。

**落实动作：** 运维工程师在明天16:00 UTC前分享支持时间和职责分工；全球负责人核对并添加例会邀请。
**练习重点：** 介绍职责、划清审批边界，并确认支持渠道与交接安排。

<a id="s002"></a>
## 002 开场与确认议程
**分类：** 跨国会议沟通  /  **英文词数：** 260
**音频：** [播放 MP3](audio/002.mp3)  /  [英文字幕](audio/002.srt)

**背景：** 你主持一次三十分钟的跨团队运维会议，应用负责人临时提出发布阻塞事项，需要重新安排议程并保留决策时间。

**角色：** A — Meeting Facilitator（会议主持人）；B — Application Lead（应用负责人）；C — Service Owner（服务负责人）

**01 [A] / Meeting Facilitator：** We have thirty minutes today. I suggest ten minutes for service health, ten for open incidents, and five for the maintenance window.
> 今天有三十分钟。我建议服务健康状况用十分钟，未关闭故障用十分钟，维护窗口用五分钟。

**02 [B] / Application Lead：** Could we add a deployment blocker? The staging test failed yesterday, and we need a decision before the release team starts preparation.
> 能加一个发布阻塞事项吗？昨天预生产测试失败，我们需要在发布团队开始准备前作出决定。

**03 [A] / Meeting Facilitator：** Yes. What decision do you need from this group? Can we cover the supporting evidence within five minutes?
> 可以。你需要本团队作出什么决定？相关证据能在五分钟内介绍完吗？

**04 [B] / Application Lead：** We need to decide whether to keep the maintenance booking while we investigate. I can summarize the failed test and remaining checks.
> 我们需要决定排查期间是否保留维护预约。我可以概述失败测试和剩余检查。

**05 [C] / Service Owner：** That affects our customer notice. Could we review the blocker before the maintenance discussion so the notice reflects the actual plan?
> 这会影响客户通知。能在讨论维护安排前先看阻塞问题吗？这样通知才能反映实际计划。

**06 [A] / Meeting Facilitator：** Agreed. We will shorten the health overview to five minutes, cover incidents next, then discuss the blocker and maintenance together.
> 同意。我们把健康概览缩短到五分钟，接着讨论故障，再一起讨论发布阻塞和维护安排。

**07 [B] / Application Lead：** I will share the test summary now. The detailed logs are linked, so we can avoid reading through every result during the meeting.
> 我现在分享测试摘要。详细日志已经附上链接，会上就不用逐条查看所有结果了。

**08 [C] / Service Owner：** Please also clarify whether the blocker affects all services or only the reporting component. That changes which business teams need an update.
> 也请说明阻塞问题影响所有服务，还是仅影响报表组件。这决定需要通知哪些业务团队。

**09 [B] / Application Lead：** Only the reporting component is affected by the failed test. The other services have completed validation, but the shared release still needs review.
> 失败测试只涉及报表组件。其他服务已完成验证，但整个联合发布仍需评审。

**10 [A] / Meeting Facilitator：** Thanks. I will reserve the final five minutes for decisions, owners, and deadlines. Any detailed troubleshooting can continue in a separate working session.
> 谢谢。我会保留最后五分钟确认决定、负责人和截止时间。详细排障可以在单独的工作会议中继续。

**11 [C] / Service Owner：** That works. I will own the customer notice once we confirm the maintenance decision, and I will flag any communication deadline.
> 可以。维护决定确认后，我负责客户通知，并指出相关沟通截止时间。

**12 [A] / Meeting Facilitator：** The revised agenda is in the meeting chat. Let us start with service health and return to the release decision at minute twenty.
> 调整后的议程已发到会议聊天。我们先看服务健康状况，在会议进行到第二十分钟时讨论发布决定。

**可复用表达：**

- What decision do you need from this group? — 你需要本团队作出什么决定？
- I will reserve the final five minutes for decisions, owners, and deadlines. — 我会保留最后五分钟确认决定、负责人和截止时间。

**落实动作：** 主持人按新议程控时，第二十分钟开始讨论发布决定，最后五分钟确认责任和截止时间；服务负责人负责后续客户通知。
**练习重点：** 说明会议目标、合理调整优先级，并把讨论转化为明确决策。

<a id="s003"></a>
## 003 时区与夏令时确认
**分类：** 跨国会议沟通  /  **英文词数：** 276
**音频：** [播放 MP3](audio/003.mp3)  /  [英文字幕](audio/003.srt)

**背景：** 你安排亚太、欧洲和美国团队的变更会议。美国团队刚经历夏令时切换，大家需要核对日期、当地时间和轮换机制。

**角色：** A — Ops Coordinator（运维协调员）；B — US Lead（美国团队负责人）；C — EU Lead（欧洲团队负责人）

**01 [A] / Ops Coordinator：** The proposed meeting is November 12, 2026, at 14:00 UTC. Please check whether your change reviewers can attend for forty minutes.
> 建议会议安排在2026年11月12日14:00 UTC。请确认你们的变更评审人能否参加四十分钟。

**02 [B] / US Lead：** Our clocks changed recently, so the usual calendar shortcut may be misleading. Could you include the local date and time for each location?
> 我们最近切换了夏令时，常用的日历快捷换算可能不准确。能注明各地的当地日期和时间吗？

**03 [A] / Ops Coordinator：** Yes. Please confirm the cities or named time zones your reviewers use. I will verify conversions for that specific date before sending the invitation.
> 可以。请确认评审人所在城市或使用的具体时区。我会先核对当天的时间换算，再发送邀请。

**04 [C] / EU Lead：** Our reviewers are in Berlin. That slot is workable, but we should avoid assuming every European office follows exactly the same working hours.
> 我们的评审人在柏林。这个时段可以，但不要假定所有欧洲办公室的工作时间都完全一致。

**05 [B] / US Lead：** The US reviewers are in New York and Phoenix. They do not necessarily follow the same seasonal clock rules, so please check both.
> 美国评审人分别在纽约和菲尼克斯。他们未必遵循相同的季节性调时规则，所以请分别核对。

**06 [A] / Ops Coordinator：** Understood. I will use the calendar time-zone database and display UTC in the description, with each location listed separately for confirmation.
> 明白。我会使用日历的时区数据库，在说明中显示UTC时间，并逐一列出地点供确认。

**07 [C] / EU Lead：** What about the APAC team? We should check their availability before treating this as the final slot, especially if it falls outside normal hours.
> 亚太团队呢？把这个时段定下来之前，也要确认他们是否方便，尤其可能超出正常工作时间。

**08 [A] / Ops Coordinator：** Their regional coordinator has accepted the proposed slot, but I am still checking whether the database reviewer can attend or needs a delegate.
> 亚太协调员已接受建议时段，但我还在确认数据库评审人能否参加，或者是否需要委派代表。

**09 [B] / US Lead：** If a required reviewer cannot join, can we collect written feedback first? I would prefer that to repeatedly moving the meeting at short notice.
> 如果必要评审人无法参加，能先收集书面意见吗？这样比临时反复改会更合适。

**10 [A] / Ops Coordinator：** Yes, provided the change process allows that reviewer to delegate or comment asynchronously. I will confirm the requirement with the change coordinator.
> 可以，前提是变更流程允许该评审人授权代表或异步提交意见。我会向变更协调员确认要求。

**11 [C] / EU Lead：** Please rotate the inconvenient slot for the next review. We can keep a simple record so the same region does not always absorb it.
> 下次评审请轮换承担不方便时段的团队。可以做个简单记录，避免总是由同一区域承担。

**12 [A] / Ops Coordinator：** I will send the verified invitation by 17:00 UTC today and record the rotation. Please report any calendar mismatch before accepting it.
> 我会在今天17:00 UTC前发送核实后的邀请，并记录轮换安排。接受邀请前，如发现日历时间不一致，请告知。

**可复用表达：**

- I will verify conversions for that specific date before sending the invitation. — 我会先核对当天的时间换算，再发送邀请。
- Please rotate the inconvenient slot for the next review. — 下次评审请轮换承担不方便时段的团队。

**落实动作：** 运维协调员核对纽约、菲尼克斯、柏林和亚太参会者的时间与资格，今天17:00 UTC前发送邀请，并记录下次时段轮换。
**练习重点：** 明确日期与UTC时间，核实地点差异，礼貌协商跨区域公平安排。

<a id="s004"></a>
## 004 没听清时请求重述
**分类：** 跨国会议沟通  /  **英文词数：** 279
**音频：** [播放 MP3](audio/004.mp3)  /  [英文字幕](audio/004.srt)

**背景：** 跨国故障会议中，工程师语速较快。你需要请对方重述，再逐步确认区域网关请求失败的真实范围。

**角色：** A — Ops Engineer（运维工程师）；B — Network Engineer（网络工程师）

**01 [B] / Network Engineer：** The failures seem to affect only requests passing through the regional gateway, although direct tests against the application endpoint have remained successful.
> 失败似乎只影响经过区域网关的请求，不过直接访问应用端点的测试一直成功。

**02 [A] / Ops Engineer：** I missed the last part. Could you repeat it a little more slowly? I especially need the difference between the two request paths.
> 我没听清最后一部分。能稍微慢一点再说一遍吗？特别是两种请求路径的区别。

**03 [B] / Network Engineer：** Certainly. Direct requests to the application succeed. Requests routed through the regional gateway fail intermittently, and the failures are returning gateway timeout errors.
> 当然。直接发送到应用的请求成功。经过区域网关的请求间歇性失败，返回网关超时错误。

**04 [A] / Ops Engineer：** Let me confirm: the issue is intermittent and appears limited to the gateway path. We have not established that every regional user is affected.
> 我确认一下：问题是间歇性的，而且看起来仅限网关路径。我们尚未确定该区域的每位用户都受影响。

**05 [B] / Network Engineer：** Correct. Our sample covers two endpoints over ten minutes. About eight percent of gateway requests failed, but that is not a user count.
> 对。样本包括十分钟内的两个端点。约8%的网关请求失败，但这不代表受影响用户数量。

**06 [A] / Ops Engineer：** Thank you. When you say direct tests, are those internal health checks, or do they also exercise the same authentication and application functions?
> 谢谢。你说的直接测试是内部健康检查，还是也覆盖相同的认证和应用功能？

**07 [B] / Network Engineer：** They use the same read-only application request and test account. However, they bypass the gateway policy layer, so the paths are not identical.
> 它们使用相同的只读应用请求和测试账号，但绕过了网关策略层，因此路径并不完全相同。

**08 [A] / Ops Engineer：** That distinction matters. Could you put the endpoint names, test interval, and sample sizes in the incident ticket so I can summarize them accurately?
> 这个区别很重要。能把端点名称、测试区间和样本数量写进故障工单吗？这样我可以准确概括。

**09 [B] / Network Engineer：** Yes. I will include request identifiers as well. We still need to compare gateway logs with application arrival times before identifying the failing component.
> 可以。我也会附上请求标识。还需要对比网关日志和应用收到请求的时间，才能确定故障组件。

**10 [A] / Ops Engineer：** One more clarification: has anyone changed gateway configuration today, or is that still being checked? I do not want to report an assumption.
> 再确认一点：今天是否有人修改过网关配置，还是仍在核查？我不希望把推测当作结论汇报。

**11 [B] / Network Engineer：** That is still being checked. I will review the change history and update the ticket by 15:20 UTC with confirmed findings and remaining questions.
> 这仍在核查。我会检查变更历史，并在15:20 UTC前更新工单，列明确认的发现和待解决的问题。

**12 [A] / Ops Engineer：** Understood. I will report the observed request impact and the next update time, while keeping the gateway explanation as a working hypothesis.
> 明白。我会汇报已观察到的请求影响和下次更新时间，同时把网关方面的解释保留为待验证假设。

**可复用表达：**

- Could you repeat it a little more slowly? — 能稍微慢一点再说一遍吗？
- I do not want to report an assumption. — 我不希望把推测当作结论汇报。

**落实动作：** 网络工程师在15:20 UTC前补充测试证据和变更核查；运维工程师准确传达请求影响，不把请求比例等同于用户比例。
**练习重点：** 请求重述、复述确认，并区分观察结果、样本限制和技术假设。

<a id="s005"></a>
## 005 礼貌打断并纠正信息
**分类：** 跨国会议沟通  /  **英文词数：** 274
**音频：** [播放 MP3](audio/005.mp3)  /  [英文字幕](audio/005.srt)

**背景：** 项目负责人根据旧消息认为服务完全中断。你需要礼貌打断，纠正影响范围，并帮助团队同步一致的对外表述。

**角色：** A — Ops Engineer（运维工程师）；B — Project Lead（项目负责人）

**01 [B] / Project Lead：** The service has been fully unavailable since 09:00 UTC, so I think we should tell all business teams that work has stopped.
> 服务从09:00 UTC起一直完全不可用，所以我认为应该通知所有业务团队，工作已经停止。

**02 [A] / Ops Engineer：** May I jump in with a clarification? The latest evidence shows failures on new login requests, while existing authenticated sessions remain active.
> 我能插一句澄清一下吗？最新证据显示新登录请求存在失败，而现有已认证会话仍然有效。

**03 [B] / Project Lead：** Thanks for correcting that. The earlier message said the service was down. Do we know when the login failures actually started?
> 谢谢纠正。之前的消息说服务中断了。我们知道登录失败实际从什么时候开始吗？

**04 [A] / Ops Engineer：** The first confirmed failure was at 09:12 UTC. We are reviewing earlier logs, so I would describe that as the confirmed start, pending further evidence.
> 首次确认的失败发生在09:12 UTC。我们正在检查更早日志，因此暂时把它作为已确认的开始时间，后续再看证据。

**05 [B] / Project Lead：** Are existing sessions completely unaffected, or could users encounter problems when their session expires and they need to authenticate again?
> 现有会话完全不受影响，还是用户会在会话到期、需要重新认证时遇到问题？

**06 [A] / Ops Engineer：** Existing sessions can still perform the tested business actions. When a session expires, the next login could fail, so the impact may expand over time.
> 现有会话仍可执行已测试的业务操作。会话到期后，下次登录可能失败，因此影响可能随时间扩大。

**07 [B] / Project Lead：** What figures should we use in the update? Leadership will ask whether the percentage refers to requests, accounts, or active users.
> 更新里应该用哪些数字？管理层会问这个比例指请求、账号，还是活跃用户。

**08 [A] / Ops Engineer：** During the last ten minutes, eighteen percent of login requests failed. We have not calculated unique affected accounts, and retries can inflate request counts.
> 过去十分钟内，18%的登录请求失败。我们尚未统计受影响的独立账号，重试也可能增加请求数量。

**09 [B] / Project Lead：** That is clearer. Please share the current dashboard and a timestamped summary so people do not keep forwarding the earlier description.
> 这样更清楚。请分享当前看板和带时间戳的摘要，避免大家继续转发之前的描述。

**10 [A] / Ops Engineer：** I will post both in the incident channel now. I suggest saying that login is degraded and existing sessions continue to work in our checks.
> 我现在把两者发到故障频道。建议表述为登录服务降级，且现有会话在我们的检查中仍然可用。

**11 [B] / Project Lead：** Agreed. I will correct the business update and ask regional leads to reference that summary. When is the next impact review?
> 同意。我会更正业务更新，并请区域负责人引用这份摘要。下次影响评估是什么时候？

**12 [A] / Ops Engineer：** We will review the impact again at 09:45 UTC and update sooner if conditions change. I will own the technical figures and their wording.
> 我们会在09:45 UTC再次评估影响，情况变化时提前更新。我负责技术数据及其表述。

**可复用表达：**

- May I jump in with a clarification? — 我能插一句澄清一下吗？
- We have not calculated unique affected accounts, and retries can inflate request counts. — 我们尚未统计受影响的独立账号，重试也可能增加请求数量。

**落实动作：** 运维工程师立即发布带时间戳的技术摘要并在09:45 UTC复核；项目负责人据此更正业务通知。
**练习重点：** 礼貌纠正旧信息，准确界定影响范围和统计口径。

---

<a id="s006"></a>
## 006 表达不同意见
**分类：** 跨国会议沟通  /  **英文词数：** 279
**音频：** [播放 MP3](audio/006.mp3)  /  [英文字幕](audio/006.srt)

**背景：** 发布负责人希望在流量高峰期实施基础设施变更。你需要解释风险、回应业务压力，并提出可执行的替代安排。

**角色：** A — Ops Engineer（运维工程师）；B — Release Lead（发布负责人）

**01 [B] / Release Lead：** Can we deploy the infrastructure change during today’s peak traffic? The project is already late, and the business team wants progress this afternoon.
> 我们能在今天的流量高峰期发布基础设施变更吗？项目已经延期，业务团队希望今天下午能有进展。

**02 [A] / Ops Engineer：** I understand the urgency, but I am concerned about recovery if it fails. This change affects shared routing for three customer-facing services.
> 我理解很紧急，但担心失败后的恢复。这项变更会影响三个面向客户服务的共享路由。

**03 [B] / Release Lead：** The configuration passed staging tests. What additional risk do you see in production that would justify waiting for the approved maintenance window?
> 配置已经通过预生产测试。你认为生产环境还有什么额外风险，足以支持等待已批准的维护窗口？

**04 [A] / Ops Engineer：** Production has much higher connection volume. We have not validated the recovery procedure under that load, and peak traffic leaves less spare capacity.
> 生产环境连接量高得多。我们尚未在这种负载下验证恢复流程，而且高峰流量期间剩余容量更少。

**05 [B] / Release Lead：** Could we reduce the scope to one gateway and move forward? A smaller change might allow us to demonstrate progress without a full rollout.
> 能把范围缩小到一个网关再推进吗？较小的变更也许可以体现进展，不必全面发布。

**06 [A] / Ops Engineer：** A limited rollout could reduce exposure, but it still needs an approved plan, clear stop conditions, and evidence that traffic can return safely.
> 有限发布可以降低影响范围，但仍需要获批方案、明确停止条件，以及流量能安全切回的证据。

**07 [B] / Release Lead：** What would you recommend we complete today, and what should remain scheduled for the low-traffic window? I need a concrete plan to share.
> 你建议今天完成什么，哪些工作留到低流量窗口？我需要一个可以分享的具体方案。

**08 [A] / Ops Engineer：** Today we can verify configuration differences, rehearse rollback in staging, and confirm monitoring coverage. The production change should stay in the approved window.
> 今天可以核对配置差异、在预生产环境演练回退，并确认监控覆盖。生产变更应保留在已批准的窗口。

**09 [B] / Release Lead：** If the business insists that waiting has a material cost, is there a formal route to review the timing rather than arguing informally?
> 如果业务坚持认为等待会造成实质成本，有正式途径重新评审时间安排吗？这样就不用私下争论了。

**10 [A] / Ops Engineer：** Yes. The change owner can request an expedited risk review with the designated approver, including business impact, technical evidence, and available recovery options.
> 有。变更负责人可以向指定审批人申请加急风险评审，提交业务影响、技术证据和可用恢复方案。

**11 [B] / Release Lead：** I will explain the options and keep the current window unless a revised plan is approved. Can you finish the readiness checks by 17:00 UTC?
> 我会说明这些选项；除非新方案获批，否则保留当前窗口。你能在17:00 UTC前完成准备检查吗？

**12 [A] / Ops Engineer：** Yes. I will publish the checklist results by then and highlight any unresolved risks. We can use those results for the final readiness decision.
> 可以。我会在那之前发布检查结果，并标明未解决的风险。最终准备情况决策可以依据这些结果。

**可复用表达：**

- I understand the urgency, but I am concerned about recovery if it fails. — 我理解很紧急，但担心失败后的恢复。
- The production change should stay in the approved window. — 生产变更应保留在已批准的窗口。

**落实动作：** 生产实施保留在已批准的低流量窗口；运维工程师17:00 UTC前提交准备证据，若需改期则走正式加急风险评审。
**练习重点：** 用具体风险支持不同意见，提供替代动作并尊重审批流程。

---

<a id="s007"></a>
## 007 请求具体进展
**分类：** 跨国会议沟通  /  **英文词数：** 286
**音频：** [播放 MP3](audio/007.mp3)  /  [英文字幕](audio/007.srt)

**背景：** 网络团队尚未复现跨区域连接故障。你在例会上追问已完成工作、证据缺口、下一步任务和明确更新时间。

**角色：** A — Ops Coordinator（运维协调员）；B — Network Engineer（网络工程师）

**01 [A] / Ops Coordinator：** Could you give us an update on the network investigation? The incident ticket still says you are checking connectivity, without a recent timestamp.
> 能更新一下网络排查进展吗？故障工单仍写着正在检查连通性，没有近期时间戳。

**02 [B] / Network Engineer：** We reviewed the firewall rules and found no unexpected changes. We also tested the affected route, but we have not reproduced the failure yet.
> 我们检查了防火墙规则，未发现意外变更，也测试了受影响路由，但还没有复现故障。

**03 [A] / Ops Coordinator：** Which source and destination did you test, and did the test run during the same period when the application team observed connection failures?
> 测试使用了哪个源地址和目的地址？测试期间是否与应用团队观察到连接失败的时段重合？

**04 [B] / Network Engineer：** We tested from the regional operations host to the application endpoint. The checks were successful, but they started fifteen minutes after the reported failures.
> 我们从区域运维主机测试到应用端点，检查成功，但开始时间比报告的失败晚了十五分钟。

**05 [A] / Ops Coordinator：** That means the successful tests do not rule out an intermittent problem. What evidence would help you compare the failed and successful connections?
> 这意味着成功测试不能排除间歇性问题。哪些证据有助于你对比失败和成功的连接？

**06 [B] / Network Engineer：** We need precise timestamps, source addresses, destination ports, and connection identifiers. A targeted packet capture would also help if the issue happens again.
> 我们需要精确时间戳、源地址、目标端口和连接标识。如果问题再次发生，定向抓包也会有帮助。

**07 [A] / Ops Coordinator：** I can ask the application owner for those details. Can you prepare the capture now so we do not lose the next occurrence?
> 我可以请应用负责人提供这些信息。你能现在准备抓包吗？这样下次发生时就不会错失证据。

**08 [B] / Network Engineer：** Yes. I will use the approved capture procedure, limit the duration and endpoints, and store the output in the restricted incident evidence location.
> 可以。我会使用已批准的抓包流程，限制时长和端点，并将结果存入受限的故障证据位置。

**09 [A] / Ops Coordinator：** Good. What is your next checkpoint if the failure does not recur? We still need a useful update rather than an open-ended wait.
> 好的。如果故障没有复发，你的下一检查点是什么？我们仍需要有用的进展更新，而不是无限期等待。

**10 [B] / Network Engineer：** By 16:00 UTC, I will document the completed checks, capture readiness, and remaining hypotheses. I will also compare routing events around the original timestamps.
> 我会在16:00 UTC前记录已完成检查、抓包准备和剩余假设，也会对照原始时间戳检查路由事件。

**11 [A] / Ops Coordinator：** Please state which findings are confirmed and which are untested. I will add the application evidence by 15:30 UTC and link it to your task.
> 请区分已确认发现与未验证假设。我会在15:30 UTC前补充应用证据，并关联到你的任务。

**12 [B] / Network Engineer：** Agreed. I own the network investigation and the 16:00 UTC update. If we capture a new failure earlier, I will notify the incident coordinator immediately.
> 同意。网络排查和16:00 UTC的更新由我负责。如果更早捕获到新故障，我会立即通知故障协调员。

**可复用表达：**

- What evidence would help you compare the failed and successful connections? — 哪些证据有助于你对比失败和成功的连接？
- Please state which findings are confirmed and which are untested. — 请区分已确认发现与未验证假设。

**落实动作：** 运维协调员15:30 UTC前补充应用证据；网络工程师准备受限抓包，并在16:00 UTC前提交明确的排查更新。
**练习重点：** 把笼统进展追问为具体证据、下一步、负责人和更新时间。

---

<a id="s008"></a>
## 008 说明任务延期
**分类：** 跨国会议沟通  /  **英文词数：** 281
**音频：** [播放 MP3](audio/008.mp3)  /  [英文字幕](audio/008.srt)

**背景：** 预生产兼容性验证失败，升级无法按原计划推进。你向经理说明影响，并提出有条件的新计划和下一检查节点。

**角色：** A — Ops Engineer（运维工程师）；B — Operations Manager（运维经理）

**01 [A] / Ops Engineer：** The upgrade is behind schedule because the compatibility test failed in staging. We cannot complete the production readiness review with the current evidence.
> 由于预生产兼容性测试失败，升级工作延期。目前证据不足以完成生产准备评审。

**02 [B] / Operations Manager：** What failed, and how does it affect the production date? We have already reserved the maintenance window and informed several support teams.
> 什么测试失败了？会怎样影响生产日期？我们已经预留维护窗口，并通知了几个支持团队。

**03 [A] / Ops Engineer：** The reporting agent cannot connect after the library update. Core application checks passed, but reporting is a required function for the business owner.
> 库更新后，报表代理无法连接。核心应用检查已通过，但报表是业务负责人要求必须具备的功能。

**04 [B] / Operations Manager：** Could we proceed without that agent and repair it afterward, or would that create an unacceptable gap in the service?
> 能先不带这个代理上线，之后再修复吗？还是这会造成服务中不可接受的功能缺口？

**05 [A] / Ops Engineer：** The owner has not accepted that gap, and the current change approval includes working reports. We need another test cycle or an approved scope change.
> 业务负责人尚未接受这一缺口，而且当前变更审批包含报表正常运行的要求。我们需要再做一轮测试，或获得范围变更批准。

**06 [B] / Operations Manager：** Understood. Do you have a confirmed fix, or are we still estimating how much investigation remains? I need to explain the uncertainty clearly.
> 明白。你们有已确认的修复方案吗？还是仍在估算还需排查多少工作？我需要清楚说明不确定性。

**07 [A] / Ops Engineer：** The vendor suggested a compatible agent version, but we have not validated it. The application team will test installation, report generation, and rollback tomorrow.
> 供应商建议了一个兼容的代理版本，但我们尚未验证。应用团队明天会测试安装、报表生成和回退。

**08 [B] / Operations Manager：** What support would help that testing finish efficiently? Please identify a specific dependency instead of simply asking everyone to prioritize the upgrade.
> 哪些支持能帮助高效完成测试？请指出具体依赖，而不只是要求大家优先处理升级。

**09 [A] / Ops Engineer：** We need a reporting specialist for two hours and access to representative test data. I have requested both, but the specialist is not confirmed yet.
> 我们需要报表专家支持两小时，并访问有代表性的测试数据。我已提出这两项请求，但专家尚未确认。

**10 [B] / Operations Manager：** I will contact the reporting manager today. Please publish the blocker, its owner, and the validation criteria so the teams understand what must change.
> 我今天联系报表经理。请公布阻塞事项、负责人和验证标准，让各团队知道必须解决什么。

**11 [A] / Ops Engineer：** I will update the project record today and hold the next checkpoint tomorrow at 15:00 UTC. After validation, I will propose a revised production date.
> 我今天更新项目记录，明天15:00 UTC进行下一次检查。验证后，我会提出调整后的生产日期。

**12 [B] / Operations Manager：** Agreed. I will tell stakeholders the current window is at risk and confirm its status at that checkpoint, without promising an unvalidated completion date.
> 同意。我会通知相关方当前窗口存在风险，并在该检查节点确认状态，不承诺尚未验证的完成日期。

**可复用表达：**

- We cannot complete the production readiness review with the current evidence. — 目前证据不足以完成生产准备评审。
- After validation, I will propose a revised production date. — 验证后，我会提出调整后的生产日期。

**落实动作：** 经理协调报表专家；运维工程师今天记录阻塞项，明天15:00 UTC评审验证结果，再提出新的生产日期。
**练习重点：** 说明延期原因与实际影响，提出具体支持需求，避免提前承诺日期。

---

<a id="s009"></a>
## 009 无法承诺恢复时间
**分类：** 跨国会议沟通  /  **英文词数：** 290
**音频：** [播放 MP3](audio/009.mp3)  /  [英文字幕](audio/009.srt)

**背景：** 业务负责人要求确认三十分钟内恢复。你需要区分缓解措施、恢复验证和下次更新时间，持续提供可用信息。

**角色：** A — Ops Engineer（运维工程师）；B — Business Lead（业务负责人）

**01 [B] / Business Lead：** Can you confirm the service will be back within thirty minutes? Our operations team needs to decide whether to activate the manual order process.
> 你能确认服务会在三十分钟内恢复吗？业务运营团队需要决定是否启动手工订单流程。

**02 [A] / Ops Engineer：** We do not have a reliable recovery estimate yet. We are validating a mitigation, but we have not confirmed that it restores successful order processing.
> 目前还没有可靠的恢复时间预估。我们正在验证缓解措施，但还没确认它能恢复订单成功处理。

**03 [B] / Business Lead：** What does validating mean in this case? The dashboard looks better than it did ten minutes ago, so people are expecting a quick recovery.
> 这里的验证具体指什么？看板比十分钟前好一些，因此大家预期很快就会恢复。

**04 [A] / Ops Engineer：** The error rate has decreased, but some orders still time out. We need to check completed orders and payment status, not only server health.
> 错误率已经下降，但部分订单仍然超时。除了服务器健康状况，我们还需检查订单完成情况和付款状态。

**05 [B] / Business Lead：** If you cannot give a recovery time, what information can I use to decide whether our staff should switch to the manual process?
> 如果无法给出恢复时间，我可以依据哪些信息决定是否让员工切换到手工流程？

**06 [A] / Ops Engineer：** I can provide the current failure rate, affected functions, and trend. Your team should apply the documented business continuity criteria using those facts.
> 我可以提供当前失败率、受影响功能和变化趋势。你们应根据这些事实，应用既定业务连续性标准。

**07 [B] / Business Lead：** Our procedure says to consider manual handling after twenty minutes of sustained order failures. We have reached that point, but duplicate orders are a concern.
> 我们的流程要求订单持续失败二十分钟后考虑手工处理。现在已到这个节点，但我们担心重复订单。

**08 [A] / Ops Engineer：** Please coordinate with the order owner before entering replacements. A timed-out request may still complete, so reconciliation is necessary before retrying or manually recreating it.
> 录入替代订单前，请先与订单负责人协调。超时请求仍可能完成，所以重试或手工重建前需要核对。

**09 [B] / Business Lead：** Understood. When will you know more about the mitigation, and how will you tell us if the recovery estimate remains uncertain?
> 明白。什么时候能获得更多缓解措施的信息？如果恢复预估仍不确定，你会怎样告知我们？

**10 [A] / Ops Engineer：** We will update you by 14:30 UTC, even if there is no confirmed estimate. The update will include validation results and any change in business impact.
> 我们会在14:30 UTC前更新，即使仍没有确认的预估。更新会包含验证结果和业务影响的变化。

**11 [B] / Business Lead：** I will have the order owner review the manual procedure now and tell staff that the next update time is not a promised recovery time.
> 我现在让订单负责人复核手工流程，并告诉员工，下次更新时间并不代表承诺的恢复时间。

**12 [A] / Ops Engineer：** Thank you. I own the technical update and will notify you earlier if impact worsens or we confirm that normal order processing has recovered.
> 谢谢。技术更新由我负责。如果影响加重，或确认正常订单处理已恢复，我会提前通知你。

**可复用表达：**

- We do not have a reliable recovery estimate yet. — 目前还没有可靠的恢复时间预估。
- The next update time is not a promised recovery time. — 下次更新时间并不代表承诺的恢复时间。

**落实动作：** 运维工程师14:30 UTC前提供验证与影响更新；业务负责人依据连续性流程协调手工处理，并防止超时订单重复录入。
**练习重点：** 坦诚表达不确定性，提供决策所需事实，并区分恢复承诺和沟通承诺。

---

<a id="s010"></a>
## 010 会末总结决定
**分类：** 跨国会议沟通  /  **英文词数：** 290
**音频：** [播放 MP3](audio/010.mp3)  /  [英文字幕](audio/010.srt)

**背景：** 跨部门协调会即将结束。你逐项确认测试证据、回退准备、业务验证和上线决策节点，避免把准备完成误当成上线批准。

**角色：** A — Meeting Facilitator（会议主持人）；B — Application Lead（应用负责人）；C — Service Owner（服务负责人）

**01 [A] / Meeting Facilitator：** Before we close, let us confirm the decisions and action owners. I want the minutes to show what is agreed and what remains conditional.
> 结束前，我们确认一下决定和行动负责人。我希望会议纪要写清已达成共识的事项，以及仍附带条件的事项。

**02 [B] / Application Lead：** The application team will validate the fix in staging by November 12 at 17:00 UTC. That includes the original failure and the main regression checks.
> 应用团队会在11月12日17:00 UTC前在预生产环境验证修复，包括原始故障场景和主要回归检查。

**03 [A] / Meeting Facilitator：** Please attach the test results rather than only marking the task complete. Who will review whether the evidence covers the reported business impact?
> 请附上测试结果，不要只把任务标记为完成。谁来评审这些证据是否覆盖已报告的业务影响？

**04 [C] / Service Owner：** I will review the business scenarios with the application lead. We need successful report generation and confirmation that previously queued reports are handled correctly.
> 我会和应用负责人一起评审业务场景。我们需要确认报表成功生成，并且此前排队的报表得到正确处理。

**05 [B] / Application Lead：** Agreed. We will include both checks. Operations also needs to confirm the rollback approach because the release changes a database index used by reporting.
> 同意。我们会纳入这两项检查。发布会修改报表使用的数据库索引，因此运维还需确认回退方式。

**06 [A] / Meeting Facilitator：** Operations will prepare that plan with the database owner by 16:00 UTC on November 12, including compatibility checks and the conditions for stopping deployment.
> 运维会与数据库负责人在11月12日16:00 UTC前准备好方案，包括兼容性检查和停止部署的条件。

**07 [C] / Service Owner：** Does completing those tasks automatically authorize production, or do we need a separate readiness decision before the maintenance window begins?
> 完成这些任务是否就自动获得生产实施授权，还是需要在维护窗口开始前另行作出准备情况决策？

**08 [A] / Meeting Facilitator：** We need a readiness decision and the required change approval. We will review the evidence at 18:00 UTC; preparation alone does not authorize deployment.
> 我们需要作出准备情况决策，并获得必要的变更审批。我们将在18:00 UTC评审证据；仅完成准备并不代表获准部署。

**09 [B] / Application Lead：** If the staging test fails again, I will flag it immediately rather than wait for the meeting. We should not keep preparing around an unconfirmed fix.
> 如果预生产测试再次失败，我会立即提出，不会等到开会。我们不应围绕尚未确认的修复继续准备。

**10 [C] / Service Owner：** I will own the business acceptance result and stakeholder notice. Please send me any changed scope before I confirm the wording with regional teams.
> 我负责业务验收结果和相关方通知。范围如有变化，请在我与区域团队确认措辞前告知。

**11 [A] / Meeting Facilitator：** I will send the minutes within an hour, listing these owners, deadlines, evidence links, and the 18:00 UTC decision checkpoint. Please correct any misunderstanding.
> 我会在一小时内发送会议纪要，列出负责人、截止时间、证据链接和18:00 UTC决策节点。如有理解错误，请纠正。

**12 [B] / Application Lead：** That matches my understanding. I own the staging validation, the service owner owns acceptance, and operations coordinates rollback readiness and the final evidence review.
> 这与我的理解一致。我负责预生产验证，服务负责人负责验收，运维协调回退准备和最终证据评审。

**可复用表达：**

- Let us confirm the decisions and action owners. — 我们确认一下决定和行动负责人。
- Preparation alone does not authorize deployment. — 仅完成准备并不代表获准部署。

**落实动作：** 主持人一小时内发纪要；11月12日16:00 UTC前完成回退准备，17:00 UTC前完成预生产验证，18:00 UTC评审证据并确认审批状态。
**练习重点：** 会末复述责任、截止时间和依赖关系，区分准备完成与实施授权。

---

<a id="s011"></a>
## 011 服务器CPU持续升高
**分类：** Infrastructure 基础设施  /  **英文词数：** 293
**音频：** [播放 MP3](audio/011.mp3)  /  [英文字幕](audio/011.srt)

**背景：** 生产服务器CPU持续高于90%，请求延迟上升。团队需要确认高耗用进程，评估暂停定时任务的影响，并验证业务是否改善。

**角色：** A — Ops Engineer（运维工程师）；B — Infrastructure Lead（基础设施负责人）；C — Batch Job Owner（批处理任务负责人）

**01 [B] / Infrastructure Lead：** CPU usage has stayed above ninety percent for twenty minutes, and request latency is rising. Is incoming traffic higher than the normal morning baseline?
> CPU使用率已连续二十分钟超过90%，请求延迟也在上升。进入的流量是否高于平时早间基线？

**02 [A] / Ops Engineer：** Request volume is close to normal. A scheduled reconciliation process is consuming most of the CPU on the affected server, based on process metrics.
> 请求量接近平时水平。根据进程指标，一个定时对账进程占用了受影响服务器的大部分CPU。

**03 [B] / Infrastructure Lead：** Do we know why that process is busier today? I do not want to stop a critical business task without understanding its dependencies.
> 知道这个进程今天为什么更繁忙吗？我不想在不了解依赖的情况下停止关键业务任务。

**04 [C] / Batch Job Owner：** It is processing yesterday’s backlog as well as today’s records. It can pause at a checkpoint, but terminating it immediately could require a lengthy restart.
> 它同时在处理昨天积压的数据和今天的记录。可以在检查点暂停，但直接终止可能导致很长的重启处理。

**05 [A] / Ops Engineer：** Can you request a controlled pause through the documented procedure? We can then compare CPU and application latency before deciding whether further mitigation is needed.
> 你能按既定流程请求受控暂停吗？之后我们对比CPU和应用延迟，再决定是否需要进一步缓解。

**06 [C] / Batch Job Owner：** Yes. The next checkpoint should arrive within five minutes. Pausing there preserves progress, although the reconciliation report will be delayed until the job resumes.
> 可以。预计五分钟内到达下一检查点。在那里暂停能保留进度，但对账报表会延迟到任务恢复后。

**07 [B] / Infrastructure Lead：** Please notify the report owner about that delay and confirm whether any downstream task requires the report before we authorize the pause.
> 请通知报表负责人这一延迟，并在批准暂停前确认是否有下游任务需要该报表。

**08 [C] / Batch Job Owner：** I checked the dependency schedule. Nothing consumes the report until 18:00 UTC, and the owner accepts a temporary pause with an update by 12:00 UTC.
> 我检查了依赖时间表。18:00 UTC前没有任务使用该报表，负责人接受临时暂停，但要求12:00 UTC前更新。

**09 [A] / Ops Engineer：** With that confirmation, I will coordinate the pause and record the exact time. We will watch CPU, queue length, and successful customer requests afterward.
> 有了这个确认，我会协调暂停并记录准确时间，之后观察CPU、队列长度和客户请求成功情况。

**10 [B] / Infrastructure Lead：** If latency improves, treat that as evidence for contention, not the full root cause. We still need to explain why the backlog accumulated.
> 如果延迟改善，可将其视为资源争用的证据，但不是完整根因。我们仍需解释积压为何形成。

**11 [A] / Ops Engineer：** Agreed. I will review service recovery after ten minutes and post results by 11:30 UTC. The investigation will remain open even if performance returns to normal.
> 同意。我会在十分钟后评估服务恢复情况，并在11:30 UTC前发布结果。即使性能恢复正常，调查仍会继续。

**12 [C] / Batch Job Owner：** I own the backlog investigation and a throttled restart proposal. I will bring the completion forecast and report risk to the 12:00 UTC checkpoint.
> 积压调查和限速重启方案由我负责。我会在12:00 UTC检查节点提供完成预估和报表风险。

**可复用表达：**

- Request volume is close to normal. — 请求量接近平时水平。
- Treat that as evidence for contention, not the full root cause. — 将其视为资源争用的证据，但不是完整根因。

**落实动作：** 确认报表依赖并获准后受控暂停任务；运维工程师11:30 UTC前报告服务验证结果，任务负责人12:00 UTC提供积压调查与限速恢复方案。
**练习重点：** 说明性能证据，协调安全暂停，并区分暂时缓解与根因分析。

---

<a id="s012"></a>
## 012 内存压力与OOM
**分类：** Infrastructure 基础设施  /  **英文词数：** 290
**音频：** [播放 MP3](audio/012.mp3)  /  [英文字幕](audio/012.srt)

**背景：** 应用进程被系统终止后已重启。开发和运维讨论内存耗尽证据、临时容量方案，以及如何继续排查持续增长的内存占用。

**角色：** A — Ops Engineer（运维工程师）；B — Application Developer（应用开发工程师）

**01 [B] / Application Developer：** Was the overnight restart caused by an application crash or an operating system issue? The application log stops without showing a normal shutdown.
> 昨晚重启是应用崩溃还是操作系统问题造成的？应用日志中断了，没有正常关闭记录。

**02 [A] / Ops Engineer：** The system logs show that the out-of-memory killer terminated the process. That confirms memory exhaustion occurred, but it does not explain the underlying growth.
> 系统日志显示内存耗尽终止机制结束了该进程。这证实发生了内存耗尽，但不能解释内存增长的根本原因。

**03 [B] / Application Developer：** Should we increase the memory allocation immediately? The process has restarted, and I want to prevent another interruption while we investigate the application.
> 要立即增加内存配额吗？进程已经重启，我希望在排查应用期间避免再次中断。

**04 [A] / Ops Engineer：** We can assess temporary headroom, but first I need to distinguish the process limit from total host capacity and check other workloads on the server.
> 可以评估临时容量余量，但我首先要区分进程限制与主机总容量，并检查服务器上的其他工作负载。

**05 [B] / Application Developer：** The heap chart rises steadily after each restart and does not return to its earlier level. Could that be enough to call this a leak?
> 堆内存曲线在每次重启后持续上升，没有回到早先水平。这是否足以认定存在内存泄漏？

**06 [A] / Ops Engineer：** It suggests retained memory, but traffic patterns and caching could also contribute. We need allocation evidence before confirming a leak or choosing a permanent fix.
> 这提示可能有内存未释放，但流量模式和缓存也可能产生影响。确认泄漏或选择永久修复前，需要内存分配证据。

**07 [B] / Application Developer：** I can collect a supported memory profile from staging using the same workload. Capturing a large dump directly in production might add pressure.
> 我可以在预生产环境使用相同工作负载，采集受支持的内存分析数据。在生产环境直接抓取大型转储可能增加压力。

**08 [A] / Ops Engineer：** That sounds reasonable. I found enough host capacity for a limited increase, provided we keep the documented reserve for the other applications.
> 这样合理。我确认主机有足够容量进行有限增加，前提是保留其他应用所需的既定余量。

**09 [B] / Application Developer：** Let us request that temporary change and set an expiration for review. Additional memory may buy time, but growing usage could consume it again.
> 我们申请这项临时变更，并设定复审期限。增加内存可能争取时间，但持续增长仍可能再次耗尽它。

**10 [A] / Ops Engineer：** I will submit the change and monitor the memory trend, restart count, and successful transactions. We will not judge recovery solely by the process staying alive.
> 我会提交变更，监控内存趋势、重启次数和成功交易，不能仅凭进程存活就判断恢复。

**11 [B] / Application Developer：** I own the application investigation and will share the first profile comparison by 16:00 UTC. Please include the termination timestamp and process limit in the ticket.
> 应用调查由我负责，我会在16:00 UTC前分享首份分析对比。请在工单中加入终止时间戳和进程限制。

**12 [A] / Ops Engineer：** I will add those now and schedule a capacity review tomorrow. We will reassess the temporary allocation after we understand the growth rate.
> 我现在补充这些信息，并安排明天复审容量。了解增长速度后，我们会重新评估临时配额。

**可复用表达：**

- That confirms memory exhaustion occurred, but it does not explain the underlying growth. — 这证实发生了内存耗尽，但不能解释内存增长的根本原因。
- Additional memory may buy time, but growing usage could consume it again. — 增加内存可能争取时间，但持续增长仍可能再次耗尽它。

**落实动作：** 运维工程师申请有复审期限的临时内存变更并验证业务；开发工程师16:00 UTC前提供分析对比，明天共同复审容量。
**练习重点：** 区分已确认的终止机制、待验证的泄漏假设和临时扩容措施。

---

<a id="s013"></a>
## 013 磁盘空间即将耗尽
**分类：** Infrastructure 基础设施  /  **英文词数：** 291
**音频：** [播放 MP3](audio/013.mp3)  /  [英文字幕](audio/013.srt)

**背景：** 生产日志卷使用率达到92%，按近期增长趋势可能两小时内写满。团队需要同时处理容量风险、保留要求和异常增长来源。

**角色：** A — Ops Engineer（运维工程师）；B — Infrastructure Lead（基础设施负责人）

**01 [A] / Ops Engineer：** The log volume is ninety-two percent full. Based on the last thirty minutes of growth, it may reach capacity within about two hours.
> 日志卷使用率已达92%。按过去三十分钟的增长速度，可能约两小时内达到容量上限。

**02 [B] / Infrastructure Lead：** Can we delete the oldest files now? I would like to create breathing room before the volume fills and the application starts failing writes.
> 能现在删除最旧的文件吗？我希望在磁盘写满、应用写入失败前腾出一些空间。

**03 [A] / Ops Engineer：** I will check the retention policy and archive status first. Some older logs support an active investigation, so age alone does not establish deletion eligibility.
> 我会先检查保留策略和归档状态。有些旧日志支持正在进行的调查，因此不能仅凭文件年龄判断是否可删除。

**04 [B] / Infrastructure Lead：** Understood. What is driving the growth? If rotation stopped working, a one-time cleanup could give us only a short period of relief.
> 明白。是什么导致增长？如果轮转失效，一次清理可能只能短暂缓解。

**05 [A] / Ops Engineer：** The current log file is growing unusually fast, and yesterday’s rotation task failed. I am checking permissions and the task error before changing anything.
> 当前日志文件增长异常快，而且昨天的轮转任务失败了。我正在检查权限和任务错误，之后再决定修改。

**06 [B] / Infrastructure Lead：** Please also check whether debug logging was enabled during troubleshooting. We should ask the application owner before reducing anything needed for the investigation.
> 也请检查排障期间是否开启了调试日志。降低调查所需的日志记录前，应先询问应用负责人。

**07 [A] / Ops Engineer：** The owner confirmed extra debug logging can end now under the troubleshooting plan. I will restore the normal level and preserve the relevant evidence.
> 应用负责人确认按排障方案现在可以结束额外调试日志。我会恢复正常级别，并保留相关证据。

**08 [B] / Infrastructure Lead：** How much space can approved cleanup recover, and do we need expansion as well? Please account for the time required to grow the filesystem.
> 获准清理可以释放多少空间？是否还需扩容？请把扩展文件系统所需时间也考虑进去。

**09 [A] / Ops Engineer：** Verified archived files should release about fifteen percent of the volume. I am also checking online expansion support and available storage with the platform owner.
> 已确认归档的文件应可释放约15%的卷空间。我也在与平台负责人确认在线扩容支持和可用存储。

**10 [B] / Infrastructure Lead：** Use the approved cleanup procedure and avoid truncating files that are still open. If expansion is needed, submit the appropriate change with the capacity evidence.
> 请按获准清理流程操作，避免截断仍被打开的文件。如果需要扩容，提交相应变更并附容量证据。

**11 [A] / Ops Engineer：** I will report usage and the new growth rate by 13:00 UTC. After repairing rotation, I will verify the next cycle rather than assume it works.
> 我会在13:00 UTC前报告使用率和新的增长速度。修复轮转后，我会验证下一次轮转，而不直接假定已正常。

**12 [B] / Infrastructure Lead：** I will review the expansion recommendation at that checkpoint. Keep the application owner informed and verify normal log writes before closing the immediate capacity risk.
> 我会在该节点评审扩容建议。请持续通知应用负责人，并在关闭当前容量风险前验证日志写入正常。

**可复用表达：**

- Age alone does not establish deletion eligibility. — 不能仅凭文件年龄判断是否可删除。
- I will report usage and the new growth rate by 13:00 UTC. — 我会在13:00 UTC前报告使用率和新的增长速度。

**落实动作：** 运维工程师按保留要求清理获准日志、恢复正常日志级别并修复轮转，13:00 UTC前报告容量趋势；基础设施负责人评审扩容建议。
**练习重点：** 解释容量预测的依据，确认清理权限，并追踪异常增长来源。

---

<a id="s014"></a>
## 014 磁盘未满但无法写入
**分类：** Infrastructure 基础设施  /  **英文词数：** 298
**音频：** [播放 MP3](audio/014.mp3)  /  [英文字幕](audio/014.srt)

**背景：** 应用提示磁盘已满，但容量看板仍有空闲空间。你和开发工程师发现inode耗尽，需要安全处理大量临时文件并防止复发。

**角色：** A — Ops Engineer（运维工程师）；B — Application Developer（应用开发工程师）

**01 [B] / Application Developer：** Writes are failing with a no-space error, but the disk dashboard shows almost forty percent free. What else should we check before expanding storage?
> 写入报错说空间不足，但磁盘看板显示仍有近40%的空闲空间。扩容前还应检查什么？

**02 [A] / Ops Engineer：** We should check inode usage as well as bytes. The affected filesystem has exhausted its inodes because the application created millions of small temporary files.
> 除了字节容量，还应检查inode使用率。应用创建了数百万个小临时文件，耗尽了受影响文件系统的inode。

**03 [B] / Application Developer：** Does that mean larger files might still have room while creating a new file fails? That would explain why the usual capacity alert did not fire.
> 这是否意味着现有文件可能仍有空间变大，但无法创建新文件？这能解释普通容量告警为何没有触发。

**04 [A] / Ops Engineer：** Yes, creating a new file requires an available inode. We need to restore that resource and add monitoring so the byte-based dashboard does not hide the risk.
> 是的，创建新文件需要可用inode。我们要恢复这种资源，并增加监控，避免只看字节容量的看板掩盖风险。

**05 [B] / Application Developer：** Can we clear the temporary directory now? Most of those files are from old export jobs, but a few exports are still running.
> 现在能清理临时目录吗？大多数文件来自旧导出任务，但还有少量导出仍在运行。

**06 [A] / Ops Engineer：** Let us identify completed job directories and confirm the retention rule first. We should not remove active files or rely only on their modification time.
> 先识别已完成任务的目录，并确认保留规则。不能删除活动文件，也不能只依据修改时间判断。

**07 [B] / Application Developer：** I can compare the job registry with directory names and provide an approved list. The application normally removes those files after a successful export.
> 我可以把任务登记信息与目录名称对照，提供获准清理清单。应用通常会在导出成功后删除这些文件。

**08 [A] / Ops Engineer：** Please check failed and canceled jobs too. Their cleanup paths may differ, and the accumulation could continue even if successful jobs clean up correctly.
> 也请检查失败和已取消的任务。它们的清理流程可能不同，即使成功任务清理正常，文件仍可能持续积累。

**09 [B] / Application Developer：** I found that canceled exports skip cleanup. I will fix that behavior and add a scheduled cleanup proposal with safeguards for running jobs.
> 我发现已取消的导出会跳过清理。我会修复该行为，并提出定时清理方案，保护运行中的任务。

**10 [A] / Ops Engineer：** I will remove only the verified inactive directories in controlled batches, then check inode availability, file creation, and the completion of a small test export.
> 我会分批受控清理已确认不活动的目录，然后检查可用inode、文件创建，以及一个小型测试导出的完成情况。

**11 [B] / Application Developer：** I will send the approved directory list by 10:20 UTC and own the application correction. We should agree on a warning threshold based on growth.
> 我会在10:20 UTC前发送获准目录清单，并负责应用修正。我们应根据增长情况确定预警阈值。

**12 [A] / Ops Engineer：** Agreed. I will post the recovery checks by 10:40 UTC and create the inode alert task. We will track the application fix separately until validated.
> 同意。我会在10:40 UTC前发布恢复检查结果，并创建inode告警任务。应用修复会单独跟踪，直到验证通过。

**可复用表达：**

- We should check inode usage as well as bytes. — 除了字节容量，还应检查inode使用率。
- We should not remove active files or rely only on their modification time. — 不能删除活动文件，也不能只依据修改时间判断。

**落实动作：** 开发工程师10:20 UTC前提供安全清理清单；运维工程师分批清理并在10:40 UTC前验证文件与导出恢复，双方跟踪清理逻辑修复和inode告警。
**练习重点：** 解释空间与inode的区别，明确清理范围，验证业务并安排防复发措施。

<a id="s015"></a>
## 015 存储延迟升高
**分类：** Infrastructure 基础设施  /  **英文词数：** 299
**音频：** [播放 MP3](audio/015.mp3)  /  [英文字幕](audio/015.srt)

**背景：** 应用响应变慢，但CPU和内存正常。应用、运维和存储团队结合延迟、队列深度和备份时间线验证存储争用假设。

**角色：** A — Ops Engineer（运维工程师）；B — Application Lead（应用负责人）；C — Storage Engineer（存储工程师）

**01 [B] / Application Lead：** Application response times increased around 02:10 UTC, but CPU and memory remain near their normal levels. Could the database be waiting on storage?
> 应用响应时间从02:10 UTC左右开始上升，但CPU和内存仍接近正常水平。数据库是否在等待存储？

**02 [A] / Ops Engineer：** Disk latency and queue depth increased at the same time. That supports checking storage contention, although it does not identify which workload is responsible.
> 磁盘延迟和队列深度在同一时间上升。这支持检查存储争用，但还不能确定是哪个工作负载导致的。

**03 [C] / Storage Engineer：** A backup job started on the same storage pool at 02:00 UTC. Its throughput is higher than usual because it is catching up after a delay.
> 同一存储池在02:00 UTC启动了备份任务。由于此前延迟后正在追赶进度，其吞吐量高于平时。

**04 [B] / Application Lead：** The timing seems convincing. Can we stop the backup immediately, or could that leave us without the recovery point required by the business?
> 时间上似乎很吻合。能立即停止备份吗？还是这会导致无法满足业务要求的恢复点？

**05 [C] / Storage Engineer：** We should check the backup policy first. The last verified recovery point is still within the required interval, but stopping now would require a documented restart plan.
> 应先检查备份策略。上次已验证的恢复点仍在要求的时间间隔内，但现在停止需要有记录的重启方案。

**06 [A] / Ops Engineer：** Could we safely reduce backup throughput instead? A controlled reduction would preserve progress and help us observe whether application performance changes with the storage load.
> 能否安全降低备份吞吐量？受控降低可以保留进度，也有助于观察存储负载变化时应用性能是否随之变化。

**07 [C] / Storage Engineer：** Yes. The backup owner has approved a temporary throttle within the runbook. I will apply it and record the time and new throughput limit.
> 可以。备份负责人已按操作手册批准临时限速。我会实施，并记录时间和新的吞吐量限制。

**08 [B] / Application Lead：** I will compare transaction completion times before and after the adjustment. Some requests are queued, so the customer experience may recover later than the disk metrics.
> 我会对比调整前后的交易完成时间。有些请求在排队，因此客户体验可能比磁盘指标恢复得更晚。

**09 [A] / Ops Engineer：** Exactly. We will monitor storage latency, queue drain, and completed business transactions together. Lower disk latency alone will not establish full service recovery.
> 正是如此。我们会同时观察存储延迟、队列消退和已完成业务交易。仅磁盘延迟下降不能证明服务完全恢复。

**10 [C] / Storage Engineer：** I will also estimate the backup completion time under the throttle and flag any risk to the next required recovery point by 03:00 UTC.
> 我也会估算限速后的备份完成时间，并在03:00 UTC前指出下一要求恢复点是否有风险。

**11 [B] / Application Lead：** The application team will check for failed or unusually delayed transactions at that checkpoint. We should not retry completed work just because clients saw timeouts.
> 应用团队会在该节点检查失败或异常延迟的交易。不能仅因客户端超时，就重试已经完成的工作。

**12 [A] / Ops Engineer：** I will coordinate the 03:00 UTC review and document the evidence. After recovery, we will review backup scheduling and shared pool capacity with both owners.
> 我会协调03:00 UTC评审并记录证据。恢复后，我们会和双方负责人一起评审备份时间安排与共享存储池容量。

**可复用表达：**

- That supports checking storage contention, although it does not identify which workload is responsible. — 这支持检查存储争用，但还不能确定是哪个工作负载导致的。
- Lower disk latency alone will not establish full service recovery. — 仅磁盘延迟下降不能证明服务完全恢复。

**落实动作：** 存储工程师按获准流程临时限速并评估备份影响，应用团队核对交易，运维工程师03:00 UTC组织联合评审并跟进长期容量安排。
**练习重点：** 用可观测变化验证假设，兼顾备份恢复要求和业务恢复证据。

---

<a id="s016"></a>
## 016 服务器补丁维护
**分类：** Infrastructure 基础设施  /  **英文词数：** 294
**音频：** [播放 MP3](audio/016.mp3)  /  [英文字幕](audio/016.srt)

**背景：** 操作系统安全补丁需要重启。运维和服务负责人确定逐节点维护的容量前提、停止条件及无法正常恢复时的处置方式。

**角色：** A — Ops Engineer（运维工程师）；B — Service Owner（服务负责人）

**01 [A] / Ops Engineer：** The security patches require a reboot. Before confirming tonight’s maintenance, can the service tolerate one node being unavailable while the remaining nodes handle traffic?
> 安全补丁需要重启。确认今晚维护前，服务能否承受一个节点不可用，由剩余节点处理流量？

**02 [B] / Service Owner：** Yes, provided the remaining nodes have enough capacity. We have three application nodes, but I want current load figures rather than last month’s estimate.
> 可以，前提是剩余节点有足够容量。我们有三个应用节点，但我需要当前负载数据，而不是上个月的估算。

**03 [A] / Ops Engineer：** Yesterday’s peak and the latest load test support running on two nodes with reserve. I will recheck current traffic before we start the approved change.
> 昨天的峰值和最新负载测试表明，两节点运行仍有余量。我会在开始已批准的变更前复核当前流量。

**04 [B] / Service Owner：** How will you remove a node from service? Some requests can run for several minutes, and a sudden disconnect would interrupt customer exports.
> 你会怎样把节点移出服务？部分请求会持续几分钟，突然断开会中断客户导出。

**05 [A] / Ops Engineer：** We will stop new traffic to one node, wait for active requests to drain, and confirm the drain before applying patches and rebooting.
> 我们会停止向单个节点发送新流量，等待活动请求处理完，确认排空后再安装补丁并重启。

**06 [B] / Service Owner：** Please define what validation means before putting it back. A successful reboot and a green host check do not prove the application functions correctly.
> 请定义重新加入前的验证内容。重启成功和主机检查正常，并不证明应用功能正确。

**07 [A] / Ops Engineer：** We will check the patch version, application dependencies, and a representative export. Then we will restore limited traffic and observe errors and latency for ten minutes.
> 我们会检查补丁版本、应用依赖和一个有代表性的导出，然后恢复少量流量，并观察错误和延迟十分钟。

**08 [B] / Service Owner：** That works. Stop the rollout if checks fail or service errors exceed the agreed threshold. Do not patch another node while the first remains uncertain.
> 可以。如果检查失败或服务错误超过约定阈值，就停止推进。首个节点状态不明确时，不要继续处理其他节点。

**09 [A] / Ops Engineer：** Agreed. If the patched node cannot return safely, we will keep it out of traffic and follow the approved recovery plan with the platform owner.
> 同意。如果打过补丁的节点无法安全恢复，我们会继续把它隔离在流量之外，并与平台负责人按已批准恢复方案处理。

**10 [B] / Service Owner：** Is the previous system image still usable, or could restoring it conflict with application state? Please confirm the recovery method before the window begins.
> 之前的系统镜像还能使用吗？恢复它是否可能与应用状态冲突？请在窗口开始前确认恢复方式。

**11 [A] / Ops Engineer：** These nodes are stateless, and the platform owner will verify image compatibility and the rebuild procedure by 18:00 UTC. No database changes are included.
> 这些节点不保存业务状态，平台负责人会在18:00 UTC前确认镜像兼容性和重建流程。本次不包含数据库变更。

**12 [B] / Service Owner：** I will review that evidence at 18:30 UTC and confirm business coverage. Record each node’s validation before proceeding, and share the final service check afterward.
> 我会在18:30 UTC评审证据并确认业务支持安排。继续下个节点前记录本节点验证结果，结束后分享最终服务检查。

**可复用表达：**

- We will stop new traffic to one node, wait for active requests to drain, and confirm the drain before applying patches and rebooting. — 我们会停止向单个节点发送新流量，等待活动请求处理完，确认排空后再安装补丁并重启。
- Do not patch another node while the first remains uncertain. — 首个节点状态不明确时，不要继续处理其他节点。

**落实动作：** 平台负责人18:00 UTC前确认恢复兼容性，服务负责人18:30 UTC评审；维护按节点排空、打补丁、验证和观察，触发阈值就停止。
**练习重点：** 确认维护前提、逐节点验证步骤、停止条件和可执行恢复路径。

---

<a id="s017"></a>
## 017 虚拟机异常重启
**分类：** Infrastructure 基础设施  /  **英文词数：** 293
**音频：** [播放 MP3](audio/017.mp3)  /  [英文字幕](audio/017.srt)

**背景：** 一台虚拟机夜间发生非计划重启。你向基础设施负责人汇报业务验证进展，并区分客户机日志、宿主机事件和触发原因。

**角色：** A — Ops Engineer（运维工程师）；B — Infrastructure Lead（基础设施负责人）

**01 [B] / Infrastructure Lead：** Was the overnight reboot planned, or did the underlying host fail? I cannot find a maintenance notice, and the application team is asking for an explanation.
> 昨晚重启是计划内操作，还是底层宿主机故障？我找不到维护通知，应用团队正在询问原因。

**02 [A] / Ops Engineer：** It was not in our change calendar. The guest boot record shows a restart at 03:14 UTC, but we have not confirmed what triggered it.
> 变更日历里没有这次重启。客户机启动记录显示03:14 UTC发生重启，但我们尚未确认触发原因。

**03 [B] / Infrastructure Lead：** Has the application recovered, or are we reporting only that the operating system and application process have started again?
> 应用恢复了吗？还是目前只能报告操作系统和应用进程已重新启动？

**04 [A] / Ops Engineer：** The process is running and health checks pass. We are still validating a complete business transaction and checking whether the interrupted background jobs resumed correctly.
> 进程正在运行，健康检查通过。我们仍在验证完整业务交易，并检查中断的后台任务是否正确恢复。

**05 [B] / Infrastructure Lead：** Please prioritize that validation. For the reboot investigation, can we distinguish an operating system shutdown from a hypervisor reset or host-level event?
> 请优先完成验证。调查重启时，能否区分操作系统关闭、虚拟化平台重置以及宿主机层面事件？

**06 [A] / Ops Engineer：** The guest logs end abruptly without a normal shutdown entry. That supports looking outside the guest, but it does not prove the host failed.
> 客户机日志突然结束，没有正常关闭记录。这支持向客户机外部排查，但不能证明宿主机发生故障。

**07 [B] / Infrastructure Lead：** Have you checked platform automation and administrator actions as well? A restart request could come from a policy or a manual operation.
> 也检查了平台自动化和管理员操作吗？重启请求可能来自策略或人工操作。

**08 [A] / Ops Engineer：** I requested the hypervisor event history and audit records from the platform owner. I will compare them with the guest timestamp and note any clock difference.
> 我已向平台负责人索取虚拟化事件历史和审计记录。我会对照客户机时间戳，并记录任何时钟差异。

**09 [B] / Infrastructure Lead：** Good. Are other virtual machines on the same host affected? That may help define the scope without assuming they experienced the same failure.
> 好的。同一宿主机上的其他虚拟机是否受影响？这有助于界定范围，但不要假定它们经历了相同故障。

**10 [A] / Ops Engineer：** The platform team is checking that. So far, monitoring shows no confirmed restart on the two neighboring guests, but we need the host events to verify.
> 平台团队正在核查。目前监控未确认相邻两台客户机发生重启，但仍需宿主机事件验证。

**11 [B] / Infrastructure Lead：** Please give us a business recovery update by 04:00 UTC and a separate investigation update by 06:00 UTC, even if the trigger is still unknown.
> 请在04:00 UTC前更新业务恢复情况，06:00 UTC前单独更新调查进展，即使触发原因届时仍未知。

**12 [A] / Ops Engineer：** I own both updates and will involve the application owner for transaction checks. We will preserve the logs and keep the cause unconfirmed until evidence supports it.
> 这两次更新由我负责，我会请应用负责人参与交易检查。我们会保留日志，在证据充分前不确认原因。

**可复用表达：**

- We have not confirmed what triggered it. — 我们尚未确认触发原因。
- That supports looking outside the guest, but it does not prove the host failed. — 这支持向客户机外部排查，但不能证明宿主机发生故障。

**落实动作：** 运维工程师协调应用验证，04:00 UTC前更新业务恢复、06:00 UTC前更新原因调查；平台团队提供事件与审计记录。
**练习重点：** 区分启动成功与业务恢复，用多层证据描述非计划重启而不提前归因。

---

<a id="s018"></a>
## 018 机房硬件故障
**分类：** Infrastructure 基础设施  /  **英文词数：** 286
**音频：** [播放 MP3](audio/018.mp3)  /  [英文字幕](audio/018.srt)

**背景：** 机房一块物理磁盘故障，阵列降级但服务仍可用。团队核对硬件标识、备份与更换条件，并安排重建监控。

**角色：** A — Ops Engineer（运维工程师）；B — Data Center Technician（机房技术员）；C — Storage Owner（存储负责人）

**01 [A] / Ops Engineer：** A physical disk has failed in the production storage array. The service remains available, but redundancy is reduced. The hardware alert needs prompt attention.
> 生产存储阵列中一块物理磁盘故障。服务仍可用，但冗余能力下降，需要及时处理硬件告警。

**02 [B] / Data Center Technician：** We have a replacement available in the data center. Do you want us to replace the disk now, or should we wait for further checks?
> 机房有可用备件。需要现在更换磁盘，还是等待进一步检查？

**03 [C] / Storage Owner：** Please wait until we confirm the enclosure, slot, and serial number against the management console. We must also verify the replacement model and supported procedure.
> 请先等待我们在管理控制台核对机箱、槽位和序列号，还要确认备件型号和受支持的操作流程。

**04 [A] / Ops Engineer：** I will add the console evidence to the hardware ticket. Are there any other failing disks or warnings that would change the replacement risk?
> 我会把控制台证据加入硬件工单。是否还有其他故障磁盘或告警，会改变本次更换风险？

**05 [C] / Storage Owner：** There are no additional failures reported, but I am checking error trends. I will also confirm the latest verified backup before authorizing the replacement.
> 目前未报告其他故障，但我正在检查错误趋势。我也会在批准更换前确认最新已验证备份。

**06 [B] / Data Center Technician：** The spare matches the required model and capacity. I can have a second technician verify the physical labels and follow the approved replacement checklist.
> 备件符合要求的型号和容量。我可以请另一位技术员复核物理标签，并按获准更换清单操作。

**07 [A] / Ops Engineer：** Please record both verifications in the ticket. Once replacement begins, who will monitor rebuild progress and watch for application performance impact?
> 请在工单中记录两次核对。开始更换后，谁来监控重建进度和应用性能影响？

**08 [C] / Storage Owner：** I own rebuild monitoring. Rebuild activity may increase storage latency, so we will track array health together with application response time and errors.
> 重建监控由我负责。重建活动可能增加存储延迟，所以我们会同时跟踪阵列健康、应用响应时间和错误。

**09 [B] / Data Center Technician：** Should we tell the business team a fixed completion time? They are asking when the service will have its normal protection level again.
> 需要告诉业务团队一个固定完成时间吗？他们在问服务何时恢复正常保护水平。

**10 [C] / Storage Owner：** Not yet. The controller estimate can change with workload and disk performance. I will share the initial estimate as provisional after rebuild starts.
> 暂时不要。控制器估算会随工作负载和磁盘性能变化。重建开始后，我会分享初步预估，并注明是暂定值。

**11 [A] / Ops Engineer：** I will coordinate the first status check at 14:00 UTC. We should distinguish service availability from restored redundancy in every update until the rebuild completes.
> 我会协调14:00 UTC的首次状态检查。重建完成前，每次更新都应区分服务可用和冗余恢复。

**12 [B] / Data Center Technician：** Understood. We will proceed only after your checks and authorization are recorded. I own the physical replacement and will report any unexpected indication immediately.
> 明白。检查和授权记录完成后，我们才会操作。物理更换由我负责，出现任何异常指示会立即报告。

**可复用表达：**

- The service remains available, but redundancy is reduced. — 服务仍可用，但冗余能力下降。
- The controller estimate can change with workload and disk performance. — 控制器估算会随工作负载和磁盘性能变化。

**落实动作：** 核实硬件、备件和备份并获授权后，由机房技术员更换；存储负责人监控重建，运维工程师14:00 UTC协调状态检查。
**练习重点：** 准确表达降级风险，进行硬件双重核对，并避免对重建时长作无依据承诺。

---

<a id="s019"></a>
## 019 时间同步异常
**分类：** Infrastructure 基础设施  /  **英文词数：** 300
**音频：** [播放 MP3](audio/019.mp3)  /  [英文字幕](audio/019.srt)

**背景：** 跨系统日志看似顺序混乱，部分认证请求失败。你和工程师核查时钟偏差、同步服务及安全校时方式。

**角色：** A — Ops Engineer（运维工程师）；B — Systems Engineer（系统工程师）

**01 [B] / Systems Engineer：** These events appear out of order across the application and authentication logs. Are all servers using the same approved time source and reporting timestamps consistently?
> 应用日志和认证日志之间的事件看起来顺序混乱。所有服务器是否使用相同的获准时间源，并一致地记录时间戳？

**02 [A] / Ops Engineer：** One application server is about four minutes behind the reference time. I recorded the offset and am checking its synchronization service and network access.
> 一台应用服务器比参考时间慢约四分钟。我已记录偏差，正在检查其同步服务和网络访问。

**03 [B] / Systems Engineer：** Could that explain the authentication errors? Some tokens appear expired in one log even though the issuing service records them as valid.
> 这能解释认证错误吗？有些令牌在一份日志中似乎已过期，而签发服务记录它们仍有效。

**04 [A] / Ops Engineer：** It could contribute, but we have not confirmed it as the cause. We need to compare token validity windows and error details with the measured offset.
> 这可能有影响，但还未确认是原因。我们需要把令牌有效时间窗口和错误详情与测得的偏差进行比较。

**05 [B] / Systems Engineer：** Can we set the clock directly to the current time? That seems faster than waiting for the synchronization service to correct several minutes of drift.
> 可以直接把时钟设置为当前时间吗？这似乎比等待同步服务纠正几分钟偏差更快。

**06 [A] / Ops Engineer：** An abrupt clock change can affect scheduled jobs and time-sensitive applications. We should follow the approved correction method and involve the service owner before proceeding.
> 时钟突然跳变可能影响定时任务和时间敏感应用。我们应遵循获准校时方式，并在操作前让服务负责人参与。

**07 [B] / Systems Engineer：** I found the synchronization service was stopped after maintenance. The configured sources are correct, but we need to check why it did not start automatically.
> 我发现同步服务在维护后处于停止状态。配置的时间源正确，但需要检查它为何没有自动启动。

**08 [A] / Ops Engineer：** Please preserve the service logs and maintenance record. I will ask the platform owner whether controlled adjustment requires temporarily draining this node from traffic.
> 请保留服务日志和维护记录。我会询问平台负责人，受控调整是否需要暂时将该节点从流量中移出。

**09 [B] / Systems Engineer：** The runbook calls for draining this application node before a correction of that size. The other nodes have enough capacity, and the owner approved the procedure.
> 操作手册要求这种幅度的校时先排空应用节点。其他节点容量足够，而且负责人已经批准该流程。

**10 [A] / Ops Engineer：** Then we will drain it, correct the clock, and verify synchronization stability before restoring traffic. We will recheck authentication using fresh test tokens afterward.
> 那我们先排空节点、校时，确认同步稳定后再恢复流量，之后使用新的测试令牌重新检查认证。

**11 [B] / Systems Engineer：** I will complete the system checks by 11:00 UTC and propose monitoring for clock offset and synchronization status. The original logs will retain their recorded timestamps.
> 我会在11:00 UTC前完成系统检查，并提出时钟偏差和同步状态监控方案。原始日志保留其记录时的时间戳。

**12 [A] / Ops Engineer：** I will publish a timeline with the offset clearly annotated and compare authentication results at 11:15 UTC. We will keep investigating if failures continue after correction.
> 我会发布明确标注偏差的时间线，并在11:15 UTC对比认证结果。如果校时后仍失败，我们会继续排查。

**可复用表达：**

- It could contribute, but we have not confirmed it as the cause. — 这可能有影响，但还未确认是原因。
- We should follow the approved correction method and involve the service owner before proceeding. — 我们应遵循获准校时方式，并在操作前让服务负责人参与。

**落实动作：** 按手册先排空节点再校时；系统工程师11:00 UTC前完成同步检查，运维工程师11:15 UTC比较认证结果，并给原始时间线标注偏差。
**练习重点：** 谨慎表达因果关系，说明时钟跳变风险，并验证校时后的业务行为。

---

<a id="s020"></a>
## 020 容量与成本评审
**分类：** Infrastructure 基础设施  /  **英文词数：** 303
**音频：** [播放 MP3](audio/020.mp3)  /  [英文字幕](audio/020.srt)

**背景：** 月度容量评审中，预测显示六周后将触及内部容量阈值。运维、财务和基础设施负责人比较资源优化、错峰和渐进扩容方案。

**角色：** A — Ops Engineer（运维工程师）；B — Finance Partner（财务业务伙伴）；C — Infrastructure Lead（基础设施负责人）

**01 [A] / Ops Engineer：** At the current growth rate, we expect to reach our planning threshold in six weeks. That threshold preserves reserve capacity rather than marking total exhaustion.
> 按当前增长速度，预计六周后达到规划阈值。这个阈值用于保留容量余量，并不意味着资源完全耗尽。

**02 [B] / Finance Partner：** Does that mean we need to double the infrastructure budget now? I need to understand the spending request before next month’s forecast is finalized.
> 这是否意味着现在就要把基础设施预算翻倍？在下月预算预测定稿前，我需要了解支出请求。

**03 [A] / Ops Engineer：** Not necessarily. We will compare right-sizing, moving flexible batch work away from peak periods, and adding capacity incrementally where the measured bottlenecks justify it.
> 不一定。我们会比较规格优化、把灵活批任务移出高峰时段，以及在测得的瓶颈确有需要时逐步增加容量。

**04 [C] / Infrastructure Lead：** What assumptions support the six-week estimate? Average utilization can hide peak pressure, and any proposal must preserve service capacity when one node is unavailable.
> 六周预估基于什么假设？平均使用率可能掩盖峰值压力，而且任何方案都必须在一个节点不可用时保留服务容量。

**05 [A] / Ops Engineer：** The estimate uses eight weeks of peak usage and the announced customer rollout. I will show a baseline forecast and a higher-demand case, including node failure.
> 预估依据八周的峰值用量和已公布的客户推广计划。我会展示基线预测和更高需求情景，并纳入节点故障。

**06 [B] / Finance Partner：** Please separate confirmed demand from assumptions. I would also like to see one-time implementation costs, ongoing charges, and the confidence range for any projected savings.
> 请区分已确认需求和假设。我也希望看到一次性实施成本、持续费用，以及预计节省金额的可信范围。

**07 [C] / Infrastructure Lead：** Before reducing server sizes, check workload variability and recovery requirements. A machine that looks idle on average may provide needed capacity during a failure.
> 缩小服务器规格前，请检查工作负载波动和恢复要求。平均看似空闲的机器可能在故障时提供必要容量。

**08 [A] / Ops Engineer：** Agreed. We will identify candidates from workload history and test changes individually. We will also check database limits so extra application instances do not shift the bottleneck.
> 同意。我们会根据工作负载历史筛选候选对象，逐项测试调整，并检查数据库限制，避免新增应用实例只是转移瓶颈。

**09 [B] / Finance Partner：** Could the proposal include a staged spending option? Releasing funds against measured demand would be easier to approve than buying the entire forecast upfront.
> 方案能包含分阶段支出选项吗？根据实测需求释放资金，比按全部预测一次性购买更容易获批。

**10 [A] / Ops Engineer：** Yes. I will define capacity triggers, expected lead times, and owners for each stage. We must order early enough to avoid consuming the required reserve.
> 可以。我会为每个阶段定义容量触发条件、预计准备时间和负责人。必须足够提前订购，避免耗用必要余量。

**11 [C] / Infrastructure Lead：** I will validate technical feasibility and resilience assumptions by November 17 at 16:00 UTC. Please bring comparable options with the same demand and availability targets.
> 我会在11月17日16:00 UTC前验证技术可行性和韧性假设。请提供基于相同需求和可用性目标的可比方案。

**12 [B] / Finance Partner：** I will review the cost ranges on November 18 at 16:00 UTC. We can choose an initial stage then, with later spending conditional on the agreed capacity triggers.
> 我会在11月18日16:00 UTC评审成本范围。届时可以选择第一阶段，之后的支出取决于约定容量条件是否触发。

**可复用表达：**

- That threshold preserves reserve capacity rather than marking total exhaustion. — 这个阈值用于保留容量余量，并不意味着资源完全耗尽。
- Please separate confirmed demand from assumptions. — 请区分已确认需求和假设。

**落实动作：** 运维工程师提交同等目标下的分阶段方案；基础设施负责人11月17日16:00 UTC前核实技术假设，财务伙伴11月18日16:00 UTC评审成本并决定首阶段投入。
**练习重点：** 解释容量预测与阈值，比较可比选项，并兼顾成本、交付周期和故障余量。

---

<a id="s021"></a>
## 021 DNS解析失败
**分类：** 网络与访问  /  **英文词数：** 270
**音频：** [播放 MP3](audio/021.mp3)  /  [英文字幕](audio/021.srt)

**背景：** 欧洲部分同事无法解析生产服务域名。运维、网络与区域负责人一起区分应用可用性和公司内部DNS故障。

**角色：** A — Ops Engineer（运维工程师）；B — Regional Lead（区域负责人）；C — Network Engineer（网络工程师）

**01 [B] / Regional Lead：** Our European support team cannot resolve the service hostname. Customers are reporting access failures, but the application dashboard still shows healthy instances.
> 欧洲支持团队无法解析服务域名。客户报告访问失败，但应用看板仍显示实例健康。

**02 [A] / Ops Engineer：** A controlled test reaches the application through its IP address with the correct hostname. That supports application availability, but it does not validate DNS.
> 受控测试使用正确主机名，通过IP地址可以访问应用。这说明应用可用，但不能证明DNS正常。

**03 [C] / Network Engineer：** Which resolvers did those affected devices use? We should compare corporate resolvers with an approved external resolver before changing any records.
> 受影响设备使用了哪些解析器？修改记录前，应把公司内部解析器与获准使用的外部解析器进行比较。

**04 [B] / Regional Lead：** We have six examples from two offices. All six use corporate DNS, while the same hostname resolves successfully from our external monitoring service.
> 我们收集了两个办公室的六个样本，均使用公司DNS，而外部监控服务能成功解析同一主机名。

**05 [A] / Ops Engineer：** Please attach their timestamps and resolver addresses to the incident. We will check the authoritative answers, cached responses, and recent configuration changes.
> 请把时间戳和解析器地址附到故障记录中。我们会检查权威应答、缓存响应以及近期配置变更。

**06 [C] / Network Engineer：** The authoritative record is correct. One corporate forwarding rule changed at 09:15 UTC, and queries through that path now return a server failure.
> 权威记录正确。公司内部一条转发规则在UTC 09:15发生变更，经过该路径的查询现在返回服务器错误。

**07 [B] / Regional Lead：** Can we tell everyone to change their laptop DNS settings? The support team needs a practical workaround while you investigate the forwarding rule.
> 能让大家修改笔记本电脑的DNS设置吗？调查转发规则期间，支持团队需要可行的临时办法。

**08 [A] / Ops Engineer：** Individual changes could break internal resolution and create inconsistent results. I recommend restoring the previous forwarding configuration through the incident change process.
> 单独修改可能破坏内部域名解析，并导致结果不一致。我建议通过故障变更流程恢复之前的转发配置。

**09 [C] / Network Engineer：** I can prepare that rollback now. It affects only the new forwarding rule, and the previous configuration passed validation earlier this morning.
> 我现在可以准备回退。范围仅限这条新转发规则，之前的配置今天早上已通过验证。

**10 [A] / Ops Engineer：** After approval, please apply it and repeat queries from both offices. I will check login and search through the normal customer access path.
> 审批通过后，请执行回退并从两个办公室重复查询。我会通过正常客户访问路径检查登录和搜索。

**11 [B] / Regional Lead：** I will coordinate those office checks and update support. Let us review resolution results and customer transactions together at 10:00 UTC.
> 我会协调办公室验证并更新支持团队。我们在UTC 10:00一起检查解析结果和客户交易。

**12 [A] / Ops Engineer：** Agreed. We will report recovery only after those checks pass, then investigate why the forwarding change escaped testing and improve the coverage.
> 同意。检查通过后才宣布恢复，随后调查为什么测试没有发现转发变更的问题，并完善覆盖范围。

**可复用表达：**

- That supports application availability, but it does not validate DNS. — 这能支持应用可用性的判断，但不能证明DNS正常。
- We will report recovery only after those checks pass. — 这些检查通过后，我们才会报告恢复。

**落实动作：** 网络工程师按故障变更流程申请回退转发规则；运维与区域负责人在UTC 10:00核对解析和客户交易。
**练习重点：** 区分DNS故障与应用故障，用证据缩小影响范围并确认业务恢复。

---

<a id="s022"></a>
## 022 防火墙端口申请
**分类：** 网络与访问  /  **英文词数：** 275
**音频：** [播放 MP3](audio/022.mp3)  /  [英文字幕](audio/022.srt)

**背景：** 新应用需要访问内部数据库，运维在变更评审中说明最小访问范围，并与安全、网络团队确认验证办法。

**角色：** A — Ops Engineer（运维工程师）；B — Security Reviewer（安全审核人员）；C — Network Engineer（网络工程师）

**01 [A] / Ops Engineer：** The reporting application needs to reach the internal database on TCP port 5432. I have opened a firewall request for the production environment.
> 报表应用需要通过TCP 5432端口访问内部数据库。我已经为生产环境提交防火墙申请。

**02 [B] / Security Reviewer：** Please provide the exact source range, destination address, and business justification. We also need an owner and a review date for this access.
> 请提供确切的源地址范围、目标地址和业务理由。还需要注明访问权限的负责人及复核日期。

**03 [A] / Ops Engineer：** The source is the reporting subnet, and the destination is the database service address. The approved data flow shows scheduled revenue reports.
> 源地址是报表子网，目标地址是数据库服务地址。已批准的数据流说明其用于定时生成收入报表。

**04 [C] / Network Engineer：** That subnet contains another application. Can we restrict the source to the reporting security group instead, assuming the network platform supports that reference?
> 这个子网还有另一个应用。如果网络平台支持引用安全组，能把源限定为报表应用的安全组吗？

**05 [A] / Ops Engineer：** Yes, that would match the actual consumers more closely. I will update the request and attach the application inventory before the review.
> 可以，这样更贴合实际使用方。我会在审核前更新申请，并附上应用清单。

**06 [B] / Security Reviewer：** Will this connection require a database administrator account? Network reachability should not grant broader database permissions than the reporting task actually needs.
> 这条连接需要数据库管理员账号吗？网络连通不应让报表任务获得超出实际所需的数据库权限。

**07 [A] / Ops Engineer：** The application will use a dedicated read-only account from the approved secret store. Database permissions and encrypted connections are covered by separate checks.
> 应用会使用批准的密钥存储中的专用只读账号。数据库权限与加密连接另有检查。

**08 [C] / Network Engineer：** Once security approves the revised scope, I can schedule the rule change for 14:00 UTC. We should test both allowed and denied traffic.
> 安全团队批准修订范围后，我可以把规则变更安排在UTC 14:00。我们应验证允许和拒绝的流量。

**09 [A] / Ops Engineer：** I will run the reporting connection test and a small query. Could you confirm that an unrelated application still cannot reach the database?
> 我会测试报表连接并执行一条小查询。能请你确认无关应用仍无法访问该数据库吗？

**10 [B] / Security Reviewer：** That is appropriate. Please capture connection results without account secrets, and make sure the ticket references the approved change and access owner.
> 这样合适。请记录连接结果，但不要记录账号密钥，并确保工单关联已批准变更和访问负责人。

**11 [C] / Network Engineer：** If the validation fails, I will remove the new rule and compare the firewall logs with your test timestamps before proposing another change.
> 如果验证失败，我会删除新规则，对照测试时间戳检查防火墙日志，再提出后续变更。

**12 [A] / Ops Engineer：** I will own the final verification and attach evidence by 14:30 UTC. We will close the request only after the reporting job completes successfully.
> 我负责最终验证，并在UTC 14:30前附上证据。报表任务成功完成后，我们才关闭申请。

**可复用表达：**

- Can we restrict the source to the reporting security group instead? — 能把源地址范围改为限定在报表应用安全组吗？
- We should test both allowed and denied traffic. — 应同时验证应放行和应拒绝的流量。

**落实动作：** 运维修订源范围并补充依据；安全批准后，网络工程师于UTC 14:00实施，运维在14:30前完成正向与反向访问验证。
**练习重点：** 清楚说明源、目的、端口与业务理由，并确认审批和验证责任。

---

<a id="s023"></a>
## 023 跨区域网络延迟
**分类：** 网络与访问  /  **英文词数：** 281
**音频：** [播放 MP3](audio/023.mp3)  /  [英文字幕](audio/023.srt)

**背景：** 美国团队访问亚洲服务的速度明显变慢。运维协调相同条件下的跨区域测量，避免把所有延迟都归因于网络距离。

**角色：** A — Ops Engineer（运维工程师）；B — US Engineer（美国团队工程师）；C — Network Engineer（网络工程师）

**01 [B] / US Engineer：** The customer search page takes almost two seconds from our US office. Our Singapore colleague sees the same page load in about half a second.
> 美国办公室打开客户搜索页面接近两秒。新加坡同事打开同一页面大约只需半秒。

**02 [A] / Ops Engineer：** Let us compare the same request type and time window. Different search terms or cached responses could make the regional comparison misleading.
> 我们比较相同请求类型和时间段。不同搜索词或缓存响应可能让区域比较产生误导。

**03 [C] / Network Engineer：** Please collect request IDs, UTC timestamps, and source locations. I can combine the application traces with approved network measurements from both locations.
> 请收集请求ID、UTC时间戳和来源位置。我可以结合应用调用链与两地获准开展的网络测量。

**04 [B] / US Engineer：** I have ten requests using the same test account and search terms. The browser timings show most of the delay before the first response byte.
> 我有十个使用同一测试账号和搜索词的请求。浏览器计时显示，大部分延迟出现在收到首个响应字节之前。

**05 [A] / Ops Engineer：** That includes network time and server processing. The matching traces show similar processing times, around 180 milliseconds, so we should inspect the connection path.
> 这段时间包含网络传输和服务端处理。对应调用链的处理时间相近，约180毫秒，因此应检查连接路径。

**06 [C] / Network Engineer：** The US office traffic crosses the corporate inspection proxy before reaching Asia. A direct approved monitoring probe has substantially lower connection establishment time.
> 美国办公室流量会先经过公司检查代理，再到亚洲。获准使用的直连监控探针建立连接的时间明显更短。

**07 [B] / US Engineer：** Does that mean we should remove the proxy for everyone? Sales is asking whether customers outside the office are experiencing the same slowdown.
> 这是否意味着应让所有人绕过代理？销售团队在问，办公室以外的客户是否也遇到同样变慢。

**08 [A] / Ops Engineer：** We do not have evidence of broad customer impact yet. External probes remain near baseline, and changing the proxy path requires a separate review.
> 目前还没有证据表明客户普遍受影响。外部探针仍接近基线，调整代理路径需要单独审核。

**09 [C] / Network Engineer：** I found elevated connection queue time on the US proxy pool. I will check its capacity and recent policy changes with the network owner.
> 我发现美国代理池建立连接的排队时间升高。我会与网络负责人检查其容量和近期策略变更。

**10 [A] / Ops Engineer：** Please preserve those measurements before any adjustment. We can repeat the ten-request comparison afterward and verify that the inspection controls still function.
> 任何调整前请保留测量数据。调整后可以重复这十个请求的比较，并验证流量检查控制仍然有效。

**11 [B] / US Engineer：** I will coordinate the office retest at 16:00 UTC and ask support for any matching customer reports, including timestamps and affected locations.
> 我会协调UTC 16:00的办公室复测，并向支持团队索取吻合的客户报告，包括时间戳和受影响位置。

**12 [A] / Ops Engineer：** At that checkpoint, we will share the measured impact and next action. We should describe the proxy evidence without declaring a root cause prematurely.
> 到检查时间，我们会分享测得的影响和下一步行动。描述代理相关证据时，不应过早宣布根因。

**可复用表达：**

- Let us compare the same request type and time window. — 我们比较相同的请求类型和时间段。
- We do not have evidence of broad customer impact yet. — 目前还没有证据表明客户普遍受到影响。

**落实动作：** 网络工程师检查代理排队及策略变化；美国团队工程师在UTC 16:00协调同条件复测，运维对比外部与办公室影响。
**练习重点：** 控制比较条件，区分网络与应用耗时，并准确表达证据和不确定性。

---

<a id="s024"></a>
## 024 负载均衡健康检查
**分类：** 网络与访问  /  **英文词数：** 281
**音频：** [播放 MP3](audio/024.mp3)  /  [英文字幕](audio/024.srt)

**背景：** 负载均衡器把一个仍在运行的应用实例移出流量。运维与应用负责人检查就绪接口和依赖状态，判断是否能安全恢复转发。

**角色：** A — Ops Engineer（运维工程师）；B — Application Lead（应用负责人）

**01 [B] / Application Lead：** The application process is running, and a local status check succeeds. Why did the load balancer stop sending requests to this instance?
> 应用进程在运行，本机状态检查也成功了。负载均衡器为什么停止向这个实例发送请求？

**02 [A] / Ops Engineer：** Its readiness endpoint returns HTTP 503 to the load balancer. The local check you ran only confirms that the process can answer requests.
> 它的就绪接口向负载均衡器返回HTTP 503。你运行的本机检查只能确认进程能够响应请求。

**03 [B] / Application Lead：** Could the readiness endpoint depend on the recommendation service? That service is degraded, but users should still be able to place orders without recommendations.
> 就绪接口是否依赖推荐服务？推荐服务性能下降，但用户应当仍能在没有推荐的情况下下单。

**04 [A] / Ops Engineer：** The response body names that dependency. Let us compare the endpoint behavior with the documented readiness contract and confirm which dependencies are essential.
> 响应正文列出了该依赖。我们对照文档中的就绪约定检查接口行为，并确认哪些依赖是必需的。

**05 [B] / Application Lead：** The contract says database access is essential, while recommendations are optional. Last week, a library update added all downstream checks to the readiness result.
> 约定中数据库访问是必需的，推荐服务是可选的。上周一次库更新把所有下游检查都计入了就绪结果。

**06 [A] / Ops Engineer：** That could explain the removal, but we still need functional evidence. Can the application handle an unavailable recommendation service without blocking checkout?
> 这可能解释实例被移出的原因，但我们仍需要功能证据。推荐服务不可用时，应用能否不阻塞结算？

**07 [B] / Application Lead：** Staging tests show checkout succeeds and displays a fallback message. I will repeat that test with the current production version and capture the results.
> 预生产测试显示，结算成功并显示降级提示。我会使用当前生产版本重复测试并记录结果。

**08 [A] / Ops Engineer：** Please include order creation and payment confirmation. We should correct the readiness implementation through a reviewed change, while leaving the failing dependency visible in monitoring.
> 请包含订单创建和支付确认。我们应通过评审后的变更修正就绪实现，同时让监控继续显示依赖故障。

**09 [B] / Application Lead：** I can prepare a small patch that separates optional dependency health from readiness. Would changing the load balancer to accept every response be faster?
> 我可以准备一个小补丁，把可选依赖健康情况与就绪判定分开。让负载均衡器接受所有响应会不会更快？

**10 [A] / Ops Engineer：** That would also admit genuinely unready instances. Keep the current success criteria, and validate the patch on one instance before restoring its traffic gradually.
> 那也会让真正未就绪的实例接收流量。应保留当前成功标准，先在一个实例验证补丁，再逐步恢复其流量。

**11 [B] / Application Lead：** I will own the patch and request the incident change review. The test evidence will be ready for our 11:30 UTC checkpoint.
> 我负责补丁并申请故障变更评审。测试证据会在UTC 11:30的检查节点前准备好。

**12 [A] / Ops Engineer：** I will watch error rates and completed orders during that rollout. We can confirm recovery when readiness and customer transactions remain healthy together.
> 上线期间我会观察错误率和完成订单量。就绪状态与客户交易同时保持正常后，才能确认恢复。

**可复用表达：**

- Let us compare the endpoint behavior with the documented readiness contract. — 我们对照文档中的就绪约定检查接口行为。
- We still need functional evidence. — 我们仍然需要功能层面的证据。

**落实动作：** 应用负责人准备就绪检查补丁并申请评审，UTC 11:30提供测试证据；运维单实例验证后逐步恢复流量并检查订单。
**练习重点：** 解释存活与就绪的差别，讨论依赖降级和业务验证。

---

<a id="s025"></a>
## 025 TLS证书即将过期
**分类：** 网络与访问  /  **英文词数：** 285
**音频：** [播放 MP3](audio/025.mp3)  /  [英文字幕](audio/025.srt)

**背景：** 生产入口证书将在七天后过期。运维推动平台和应用团队提前完成续期、兼容性测试与到期监控验证。

**角色：** A — Ops Engineer（运维工程师）；B — Platform Engineer（平台工程师）；C — Application Lead（应用负责人）

**01 [A] / Ops Engineer：** The production gateway certificate expires on November 18 at 12:00 UTC, seven days from now. Who owns renewal, installation, and client validation?
> 生产网关证书将在七天后的11月18日UTC 12:00过期。续期、安装和客户端验证分别由谁负责？

**02 [B] / Platform Engineer：** Platform owns renewal and gateway installation. The certificate authority request is ready, but we need the application team to confirm the required hostnames.
