> 平台团队负责续期和网关安装。证书机构申请已准备好，但需要应用团队确认所需主机名。

**03 [C] / Application Lead：** The customer portal and mobile API use this certificate. I will verify the hostname list against the current routing configuration and mobile client settings.
> 客户门户和移动API使用这张证书。我会根据当前路由配置和移动客户端设置核对主机名列表。

**04 [A] / Ops Engineer：** Please also identify supported clients with special trust requirements. A browser test alone would not cover older mobile versions or integrated partner systems.
> 也请找出对信任有特殊要求的受支持客户端。仅测试浏览器无法覆盖较早的移动版本或集成的合作伙伴系统。

**05 [B] / Platform Engineer：** The new certificate will use the approved issuing chain. I will check the private key match, validity dates, and intermediate certificates before installation.
> 新证书会使用已批准的签发链。安装前我会检查私钥是否匹配、有效期及中间证书。

**06 [C] / Application Lead：** One partner pins a certificate authority rather than our leaf certificate. I will ask their technical owner to validate the proposed chain today.
> 一个合作伙伴固定信任某证书机构，而不是我们的终端证书。我今天会请其技术负责人验证拟使用的证书链。

**07 [A] / Ops Engineer：** Can we complete staging validation tomorrow and install before the weekend? That would leave time to address compatibility issues while the current certificate remains valid.
> 能否明天完成预生产验证，并在周末前安装？这样在当前证书仍有效时，还有时间处理兼容性问题。

**08 [B] / Platform Engineer：** Yes, subject to change approval. I propose November 13 at 09:00 UTC, starting with one gateway and comparing the served chain before continuing.
> 可以，但需变更审批。我建议11月13日UTC 09:00先更新一个网关，比较实际提供的证书链后再继续。

**09 [C] / Application Lead：** I will test browser login, mobile API access, and the partner connection. If a supported client fails, we will pause and capture its error.
> 我会测试浏览器登录、移动API访问和合作伙伴连接。如果某个受支持客户端失败，我们会暂停并记录错误。

**10 [A] / Ops Engineer：** Keep the existing certificate available during the validation period. Any rollback must happen before its expiry and follow the same controlled deployment process.
> 验证期间请保留现有证书。任何回退都必须在它过期前进行，并遵循同样受控的部署流程。

**11 [B] / Platform Engineer：** I will attach the installation evidence and verify every gateway serves the new certificate. I will also confirm the expiry monitor discovers the new date.
> 我会附上安装证据，并验证每个网关都提供新证书，也会确认到期监控识别出新日期。

**12 [A] / Ops Engineer：** Let us review readiness tomorrow at 15:00 UTC. Platform owns deployment, application owns client checks, and I will track the approval and final evidence.
> 我们明天UTC 15:00评审准备情况。平台负责部署，应用团队负责客户端检查，我负责跟踪审批和最终证据。

**可复用表达：**

- Who owns renewal, installation, and client validation? — 谁负责续期、安装和客户端验证？
- That would leave time to address compatibility issues. — 这样可以留出时间处理兼容性问题。

**落实动作：** 平台负责续期与部署，应用团队核对客户端兼容性；次日UTC 15:00确认准备情况，拟于11月13日09:00受控安装并验证告警。
**练习重点：** 明确证书全生命周期责任，表达时间余量和分阶段验证要求。

---

<a id="s026"></a>
## 026 VPN连接与访问范围
**分类：** 网络与访问  /  **英文词数：** 284
**音频：** [播放 MP3](audio/026.mp3)  /  [英文字幕](audio/026.srt)

**背景：** 海外工程师已连接VPN，却无法打开一个内部管理页面。运维根据其已批准的访问配置检查路由和策略。

**角色：** A — Ops Engineer（运维工程师）；B — Remote Engineer（远程工程师）；C — Network Engineer（网络工程师）

**01 [B] / Remote Engineer：** My VPN connection shows as active, but the production management page times out. I need to review a deployment before the release meeting.
> 我的VPN显示已连接，但生产管理页面超时。我需要在发布会议前检查一个部署。

**02 [A] / Ops Engineer：** Can you reach other internal services through this connection? Please tell me whether the failure affects one hostname, the entire management subnet, or everything.
> 你通过这条连接能访问其他内部服务吗？请确认故障影响一个主机名、整个管理子网，还是所有内部服务。

**03 [B] / Remote Engineer：** The internal documentation site and code repository work. Two management hosts in the same subnet time out, although both names resolve to expected addresses.
> 内部文档站和代码仓库正常。同一子网的两台管理主机都超时，但两个域名解析出的地址符合预期。

**04 [A] / Ops Engineer：** That narrows the scope. Please share the VPN profile name and failure timestamps through the support ticket, without including credentials or connection secrets.
> 这样缩小了范围。请通过支持工单提供VPN配置名称和失败时间戳，不要包含凭据或连接密钥。

**05 [C] / Network Engineer：** Your approved profile includes read-only access to that management network. I will compare the assigned routes and policy groups with the access approval.
> 你已批准的配置包含对该管理网络的只读访问。我会把分配的路由和策略组与审批记录进行比较。

**06 [B] / Remote Engineer：** I moved to the new VPN client yesterday. Could reinstalling the client help, or should I try adding the missing route manually first?
> 我昨天换用了新版VPN客户端。重新安装是否有用，还是应该先尝试手动添加缺失路由？

**07 [A] / Ops Engineer：** Please leave the client configuration unchanged while we collect evidence. A manual route could hide the provisioning problem and might not match your approved access.
> 收集证据期间请保持客户端配置不变。手动路由可能掩盖下发问题，也可能不符合已批准的访问范围。

**08 [C] / Network Engineer：** The new client received the standard profile instead of the approved operations profile. Its route list does not include the management subnet.
> 新客户端收到了标准配置，而不是已批准的运维配置。它的路由列表不包含管理子网。

**09 [A] / Ops Engineer：** Can you correct the profile assignment through the normal access process? We should verify that the update grants only the management destinations already approved.
> 能否通过正常访问流程修正配置分配？应确认更新只授予已批准管理目标的访问权限。

**10 [C] / Network Engineer：** Yes. I will apply the approved mapping and ask you to reconnect. We will check the route table and gateway logs at 13:30 UTC.
> 可以。我会应用已批准的映射并请你重新连接。我们在UTC 13:30检查路由表和网关日志。

**11 [B] / Remote Engineer：** I will reconnect at that time and test the read-only management page. I will also confirm that the documentation site and repository still work.
> 我会在那个时间重新连接并测试只读管理页面，也会确认文档站和代码仓库仍然正常。

**12 [A] / Ops Engineer：** I will document the results and hand the client migration issue to the VPN owner. Successful connection alone will not be our closure criterion.
> 我会记录结果，把客户端迁移问题交给VPN负责人。我们不会仅凭VPN连接成功就关闭工单。

**可复用表达：**

- That narrows the scope. — 这样就缩小了排查范围。
- Please leave the client configuration unchanged while we collect evidence. — 收集证据期间，请保持客户端配置不变。

**落实动作：** 网络工程师修正已批准的VPN配置映射；UTC 13:30重新连接并检查路由、策略和管理页面，运维跟进迁移配置问题。
**练习重点：** 从目标范围切入排查，区分VPN连通、路由和授权策略。

---

<a id="s027"></a>
## 027 SSO登录与权限不足
**分类：** 网络与访问  /  **英文词数：** 286
**音频：** [播放 MP3](audio/027.mp3)  /  [英文字幕](audio/027.srt)

**背景：** 同事能够通过单点登录认证，却无法访问生产控制台。运维和身份团队核对已批准的只读角色及权限下发记录。

**角色：** A — Ops Engineer（运维工程师）；B — Application Engineer（应用工程师）；C — Identity Engineer（身份系统工程师）

**01 [B] / Application Engineer：** I can sign in through SSO, but the production console says access denied. My manager approved a read-only role yesterday for release verification.
> 我能通过单点登录，但生产控制台提示访问被拒绝。经理昨天批准了只读角色，用于发布验证。

**02 [A] / Ops Engineer：** Authentication succeeded, but authorization appears to have failed. Please share the approval ticket and console error reference so we can verify the expected role.
> 身份认证成功了，但授权似乎失败。请提供审批工单和控制台错误参考编号，以便核对预期角色。

**03 [B] / Application Engineer：** The ticket is approved, and it names the production observer role. I can access staging, but I cannot open any production resource pages.
> 工单已批准，角色名称是生产观察者。我可以访问预生产，但无法打开任何生产资源页面。

**04 [C] / Identity Engineer：** I will check the identity group mapping and provisioning history. An approved request does not necessarily mean the console has received the membership update.
> 我会检查身份组映射和权限下发历史。申请已批准并不一定意味着控制台已收到成员关系更新。

**05 [A] / Ops Engineer：** Please compare the exact account identifier too. We have seen confusion when people use a contractor identity and an employee identity with similar names.
> 也请核对确切账号标识。我们遇到过承包商账号与员工账号名称相似而造成混淆的情况。

**06 [B] / Application Engineer：** I am using the employee account listed in the request. Should I ask someone to lend me a working administrator session for the meeting?
> 我使用的是申请中列出的员工账号。要不要请别人借我一个可用的管理员会话参加会议？

**07 [A] / Ops Engineer：** Keep access tied to your own identity and approved role. I can share the relevant read-only deployment evidence through the ticket while we investigate.
> 访问应始终关联你本人的身份和已批准角色。调查期间，我可以通过工单分享相关的只读部署证据。

**08 [C] / Identity Engineer：** The directory group is correct, but the console provisioning job rejected one mapping. I found a role name mismatch introduced in yesterday’s configuration update.
> 目录组正确，但控制台权限下发任务拒绝了一条映射。我发现昨天配置更新引入了角色名称不匹配。

**09 [A] / Ops Engineer：** Can you repair that mapping with the identity change owner and rerun provisioning? We should retain the original approval and record the correction.
> 能否与身份变更负责人修复映射并重新执行下发？我们应保留原审批，并记录修正过程。

**10 [C] / Identity Engineer：** Yes. After the reviewed correction, I will confirm the assigned role in the audit log. Please start a new session once I notify you.
> 可以。修正经评审后，我会在审计日志中确认分配的角色。收到我的通知后，请启动新会话。

**11 [B] / Application Engineer：** I will test access at 15:00 UTC and confirm that production details are visible. I will also verify that write controls remain unavailable.
> 我会在UTC 15:00测试访问，确认可查看生产详情，也会验证写入操作仍然不可用。

**12 [A] / Ops Engineer：** I will close the support ticket after those checks and attach the audit reference. The identity owner will separately track prevention of future mapping failures.
> 这些检查通过后，我会关闭支持工单并附上审计参考记录。身份系统负责人会另行跟进如何避免映射再次失败。

**可复用表达：**

- Authentication succeeded, but authorization appears to have failed. — 身份认证成功，但授权似乎失败。
- Keep access tied to your own identity and approved role. — 访问应关联你本人的身份和已批准角色。

**落实动作：** 身份工程师修复并审核角色映射，重新下发权限；申请人在UTC 15:00使用新会话验证只读权限及写入限制。
**练习重点：** 准确区分认证、授权和权限下发，说明角色边界与替代协作方式。

---

<a id="s028"></a>
## 028 服务账号密钥轮换
**分类：** 网络与访问  /  **英文词数：** 286
**音频：** [播放 MP3](audio/028.mp3)  /  [英文字幕](audio/028.srt)

**背景：** 三个应用及一个定时任务可能使用同一服务凭据。运维、应用和安全团队确认依赖后安排有重叠期的轮换。

**角色：** A — Ops Engineer（运维工程师）；B — Application Lead（应用负责人）；C — Security Engineer（安全工程师）

**01 [A] / Ops Engineer：** The shared service credential is due for rotation next week. Have we identified every consumer, including background jobs that might run less frequently?
> 共用服务凭据下周需要轮换。是否已经找出所有使用方，包括运行频率较低的后台任务？

**02 [B] / Application Lead：** Three applications are confirmed. A monthly reconciliation job may also use it, but its owner has not verified the current configuration yet.
> 三个应用已经确认。一个月度对账任务也可能使用它，但负责人还未核实当前配置。

**03 [C] / Security Engineer：** We should resolve that uncertainty before revocation. Does the provider support two active credentials so we can migrate consumers without an immediate shared cutover?
> 撤销前应先消除这个不确定性。供应方是否支持同时启用两组凭据，以便逐步迁移使用方，而不是立即统一切换？

**04 [A] / Ops Engineer：** It supports two active credentials with separate identifiers. I propose creating the replacement in the approved secret store and tracking migration by consumer.
> 它支持两组分别带有标识的有效凭据。我建议在批准的密钥存储中创建新凭据，并按使用方跟踪迁移。

**05 [B] / Application Lead：** The reconciliation owner just confirmed the job uses this account. We can run its validation mode today without posting any financial updates.
> 对账负责人刚确认任务使用这个账号。今天可以运行其验证模式，不会提交任何财务更新。

**06 [C] / Security Engineer：** Good. Record the credential identifier and test result, never the secret itself. Please also verify that the replacement has the same approved permission scope.
> 很好。记录凭据标识和测试结果，绝不能记录密钥本身。也请核对新凭据具有相同的已批准权限范围。

**07 [A] / Ops Engineer：** I will check permissions and update one application first through the change process. We will observe authentication errors and completed transactions before migrating the others.
> 我会检查权限，并通过变更流程先更新一个应用。观察认证错误和已完成交易后，再迁移其他使用方。

**08 [B] / Application Lead：** How will we know the monthly job is no longer using the old credential? Its next scheduled run is after the rotation deadline.
> 怎样确认月度任务不再使用旧凭据？它下一次计划运行在轮换截止日期之后。

**09 [A] / Ops Engineer：** We will validate its updated secret reference and run the approved validation mode. Provider audit logs should show the replacement identifier for that execution.
> 我们会核对更新后的密钥引用，并运行获准的验证模式。供应方审计日志应显示该次执行使用新凭据标识。

**10 [C] / Security Engineer：** Do not revoke based only on silence in the logs. Require an explicit successful test from all four consumers and owner confirmation of the inventory.
> 不要仅因日志没有动静就撤销凭据。必须取得全部四个使用方明确的成功测试，以及负责人对清单的确认。

**11 [B] / Application Lead：** I will collect the application and job evidence by 17:00 UTC tomorrow. Any failed test will block revocation and trigger a review of the migration.
> 我会在明天UTC 17:00前收集应用与任务证据。任何测试失败都会阻止撤销，并触发迁移复核。

**12 [A] / Ops Engineer：** After security approves the evidence, I will revoke the old credential and repeat key checks. We will then update the rotation schedule and ownership record.
> 安全团队批准证据后，我会撤销旧凭据并重复关键检查。随后更新轮换计划和责任人记录。

**可复用表达：**

- Have we identified every consumer? — 是否已找出所有使用方？
- Any failed test will block revocation and trigger a review of the migration. — 任何测试失败都会阻止撤销并触发迁移复核。

**落实动作：** 运维分批迁移新凭据，应用负责人于次日UTC 17:00前收集全部四个使用方的成功证据；安全批准后撤销旧凭据并复测。
**练习重点：** 清点低频使用方，解释重叠轮换、显式验证和撤销门槛。

---

<a id="s029"></a>
## 029 WAF误拦截业务请求
**分类：** 网络与访问  /  **英文词数：** 286
**音频：** [播放 MP3](audio/029.mp3)  /  [英文字幕](audio/029.srt)

**背景：** WAF规则更新后，一类合法结算请求被拒绝。应用、运维与安全团队基于请求特征制定限时例外，而非关闭整套防护。

**角色：** A — Ops Engineer（运维工程师）；B — Application Lead（应用负责人）；C — Security Engineer（安全工程师）

**01 [B] / Application Lead：** Since the WAF policy update, some legitimate checkout requests return HTTP 403. The application logs show no matching requests reaching the checkout handler.
> WAF策略更新后，一些合法结算请求返回HTTP 403。应用日志没有显示相应请求到达结算处理程序。

**02 [A] / Ops Engineer：** Please send a few request IDs and UTC timestamps through the incident ticket. I will correlate them with WAF events and the deployment timeline.
> 请通过故障工单提供几个请求ID和UTC时间戳。我会把它们与WAF事件和部署时间线进行关联。

**03 [C] / Security Engineer：** Do not attach complete payment payloads. Sanitized field names, rule identifiers, and the request path should let us investigate without exposing customer information.
> 不要附上完整支付请求内容。脱敏后的字段名、规则标识和请求路径应足以调查，同时避免暴露客户信息。

**04 [A] / Ops Engineer：** The matched events point to one new rule inspecting an optional delivery note. Other checkout requests pass, and the affected pattern uses valid punctuation.
> 匹配事件指向一条检查可选配送备注的新规则。其他结算请求通过，受影响的请求特征包含合法标点符号。

**05 [B] / Application Lead：** Our support team reproduced it with a normal delivery instruction. Could we temporarily switch the entire checkout policy to monitoring mode to restore sales?
> 支持团队用正常配送说明复现了问题。能否暂时把整个结算策略切到监控模式来恢复销售？

**06 [C] / Security Engineer：** That would remove broader protections than necessary. Let us define an exception for this rule, endpoint, and field, with an expiry and active monitoring.
> 那会移除超出必要范围的防护。我们应针对这条规则、这个接口和字段定义例外，并设置到期时间及持续监控。

**07 [A] / Ops Engineer：** I can draft the exception request using the verified examples. We should keep inspection on other fields and preserve the remaining checkout rules.
> 我可以根据已验证样本起草例外申请。其他字段的检查和其余结算规则应继续保留。

**08 [B] / Application Lead：** Will narrowing the exception to that field still allow malicious content there? I want to understand what compensating validation the application provides.
> 把例外限定在该字段后，是否仍会放过其中的恶意内容？我想了解应用提供哪些补充校验。

**09 [C] / Security Engineer：** We need that assessment before approval. Please confirm length limits and safe handling, then test legitimate examples and our approved security test cases.
> 审批前需要评估这一点。请确认长度限制和安全处理方式，再测试合法样本及已批准的安全测试用例。

**10 [A] / Ops Engineer：** After approval, I will apply the exception for two hours and compare blocked requests, checkout errors, and completed orders. We will review at 12:30 UTC.
> 批准后，我会应用两小时的例外，并比较被阻止请求、结算错误和完成订单量。我们在UTC 12:30复核。

**11 [B] / Application Lead：** I will own functional validation and provide results within fifteen minutes of the change. Support will confirm whether the reported customer cases can complete checkout.
> 我负责功能验证，并在变更后十五分钟内提供结果。支持团队会确认所报告客户案例能否完成结算。

**12 [C] / Security Engineer：** I will own the permanent rule correction. If review is incomplete before expiry, we must request an extension explicitly rather than leave the exception unattended.
> 我负责永久规则修正。如果到期前复核尚未完成，必须明确申请延期，不能任由例外无人管理。

**可复用表达：**

- Let us define an exception for this rule, endpoint, and field. — 我们为这条规则、这个接口和字段定义例外。
- I want to understand what compensating validation the application provides. — 我想了解应用提供哪些补充校验。

**落实动作：** 运维提交两小时限时例外，安全评估补充校验后批准；应用验证结算，UTC 12:30复核恢复情况并跟进永久修正。
**练习重点：** 用证据解释误拦截，协商最小例外、有效期和业务验证。

---

<a id="s030"></a>
## 030 第三方链路与代理超时
**分类：** 网络与访问  /  **英文词数：** 289
**音频：** [播放 MP3](audio/030.mp3)  /  [英文字幕](audio/030.srt)

**背景：** 供应商接口在开发电脑上可用，生产环境却超时。运维比较真实出站路径，与供应商共同核对代理出口和白名单。

**角色：** A — Ops Engineer（运维工程师）；B — Application Developer（应用开发工程师）；C — Vendor Engineer（供应商工程师）

**01 [B] / Application Developer：** The vendor API responds from my laptop, but production calls time out after ten seconds. The application code and requested endpoint are the same.
> 供应商API在我的笔记本上有响应，但生产调用十秒后超时。应用代码和请求接口相同。

**02 [A] / Ops Engineer：** The connection path differs between your laptop and production. Production uses an outbound proxy, so we need to compare DNS, proxy routing, and destination access.
> 你的笔记本与生产环境的连接路径不同。生产使用出站代理，因此需要比较DNS、代理路由和目标访问情况。

**03 [C] / Vendor Engineer：** Please provide the failed request time and the observed public source IP through our support ticket. We will check the corresponding access records.
> 请通过我们的支持工单提供失败请求时间和实际公网源IP。我们会检查对应的访问记录。

**04 [A] / Ops Engineer：** I will share the approved egress details there. First, I am checking whether the timeout occurs during proxy connection, TLS negotiation, or the API response.
> 我会在那里分享获准提供的出口信息。首先，我正在检查超时发生在代理连接、TLS协商，还是API响应阶段。

**05 [B] / Application Developer：** The trace reaches the proxy, but it shows no completed TLS session with the vendor. Should we increase the application timeout to thirty seconds?
> 调用链到达了代理，但没有与供应商完成TLS会话。是否应该把应用超时增加到三十秒？

**06 [A] / Ops Engineer：** A longer timeout would not resolve a blocked connection and could tie up more workers. Let us identify the failed connection stage before changing it.
> 延长超时无法解决连接被阻止的问题，还可能占用更多工作线程。修改前应先确定失败的连接阶段。

**07 [C] / Vendor Engineer：** I see connection attempts from an address outside your registered range. Did the production egress address change during your recent proxy maintenance?
> 我看到来自已登记范围之外某个地址的连接尝试。最近的代理维护是否改变了生产出口地址？

**08 [A] / Ops Engineer：** The maintenance added a second approved proxy gateway, but its public address is absent from the vendor request. I will verify both active gateways.
> 维护新增了第二个已批准的代理网关，但供应商申请中没有它的公网地址。我会核实两个在用网关。

**09 [B] / Application Developer：** Can we force production through the original gateway until the update is approved? We need to consider whether that would reduce capacity or redundancy.
> 更新获批前，能让生产强制经过原网关吗？需要考虑这是否会降低容量或冗余。

**10 [A] / Ops Engineer：** We will assess that option with the network owner. The preferred fix is to update the vendor allowlist through the existing approval process.
> 我们会与网络负责人评估这个方案。首选修复是通过现有审批流程更新供应商白名单。

**11 [C] / Vendor Engineer：** Once approved, I can add the exact new address and coordinate a test at 18:00 UTC. Please send requests through each gateway separately.
> 批准后，我可以添加确切的新地址，并协调UTC 18:00的测试。请分别通过每个网关发送请求。

**12 [A] / Ops Engineer：** I will coordinate those tests and verify successful business responses, not just open connections. We will attach results and add vendor checks to the maintenance checklist.
> 我会协调测试并验证成功的业务响应，而不只是连接能打开。我们会附上结果，并把供应商检查加入维护清单。

**可复用表达：**

- The connection path differs between your laptop and production. — 你的笔记本与生产环境的连接路径不同。
- Let us identify the failed connection stage before changing it. — 修改之前，我们先确定连接在哪个阶段失败。

**落实动作：** 运维核实两个出口网关并提交白名单更新；供应商批准后在UTC 18:00联合测试，逐个网关验证实际业务响应。
**练习重点：** 比较环境的真实连接路径，解释超时阶段并协调外部依赖。

---

<a id="s031"></a>
## 031 云配额阻止扩容
**分类：** 云平台、容器与自动化  /  **英文词数：** 288
**音频：** [播放 MP3](audio/031.mp3)  /  [英文字幕](audio/031.srt)

**背景：** 高峰前的云实例扩容失败，负责人以为预算充足就能扩容。运维解释区域配额、真实容量与替代方案的区别。

**角色：** A — Ops Engineer（运维工程师）；B — Platform Lead（平台负责人）

**01 [B] / Platform Lead：** The scale-out request failed this morning, but our monthly budget has plenty of room. What is preventing the additional instances from starting?
> 今天早上的扩容申请失败了，但月度预算还很充足。是什么阻止了新增实例启动？

**02 [A] / Ops Engineer：** We reached the regional compute quota for this instance family. Budget controls spending, while this quota limits the amount of compute we can provision.
> 我们达到了这个实例系列的区域计算配额。预算控制支出，而该配额限制可创建的计算资源量。

**03 [B] / Platform Lead：** The traffic peak begins tomorrow afternoon. Can the provider raise the limit today, and does a higher quota guarantee that the requested capacity exists?
> 流量高峰从明天下午开始。供应商今天能提升限额吗？提高配额是否保证所需容量实际存在？

**04 [A] / Ops Engineer：** I have submitted an urgent increase request, but there is no confirmed completion time. Quota approval also does not guarantee available capacity in each zone.
> 我已提交紧急提升申请，但没有确认完成时间。配额获批也不保证每个可用区都有足够实际容量。

**05 [B] / Platform Lead：** What alternatives can we prepare while waiting? We have some idle development instances, and another instance family appears to have unused quota.
> 等待期间可以准备哪些替代方案？我们有一些空闲开发实例，另一个实例系列似乎也有剩余配额。

**06 [A] / Ops Engineer：** Development uses a different account, so stopping those instances will not release this production quota. The other family needs compatibility and performance validation first.
> 开发使用不同账号，停止那些实例无法释放这个生产配额。另一个实例系列需要先验证兼容性和性能。

**07 [B] / Platform Lead：** Could we move everything into the zone that still has room? I want to understand whether our current distribution is blocking a practical workaround.
> 能否把所有实例移到仍有空间的可用区？我想了解当前分布是否阻碍了可行的临时方案。

**08 [A] / Ops Engineer：** That would concentrate risk and would not change the regional quota. We should preserve the approved distribution and assess capacity across the required zones.
> 那会集中风险，也不会改变区域配额。我们应保留已批准的分布，并评估所需各可用区的容量。

**09 [B] / Platform Lead：** Please test the alternative family today, then. Can the current fleet handle tomorrow’s forecast if the increase is delayed or the tests fail?
> 那请今天测试替代实例系列。如果提升延迟或测试失败，现有实例能否承受明天的预测流量？

**10 [A] / Ops Engineer：** The latest load test supports normal traffic, but the forecast exceeds our tested headroom. We can defer approved background work and review demand controls.
> 最近负载测试支持正常流量，但预测流量超过已测试的余量。我们可以推迟获准延期的后台任务，并评审需求控制措施。

**11 [B] / Platform Lead：** I will coordinate those business tradeoffs and contact our provider representative. Please bring quota status, test results, and remaining risk to the 16:00 UTC review.
> 我会协调这些业务取舍，并联系供应商代表。请在UTC 16:00评审中提供配额状态、测试结果和剩余风险。

**12 [A] / Ops Engineer：** I will own that update and keep the deployment plan conditional on evidence. We will not describe extra capacity as available until provisioning and validation succeed.
> 我负责更新，部署计划将以证据为条件。只有创建和验证成功后，才会宣布新增容量已可用。

**可复用表达：**

- There is no confirmed completion time. — 目前没有确认的完成时间。
- Quota approval also does not guarantee available capacity in each zone. — 配额获批也不保证各可用区实际有可用容量。

**落实动作：** 运维跟踪紧急配额申请并测试替代实例系列，平台负责人协调业务取舍；UTC 16:00基于容量证据评审高峰准备情况。
**练习重点：** 解释预算、配额与实际容量的区别，避免承诺未确认的扩容时间。

---

<a id="s032"></a>
## 032 可用区故障与流量转移
**分类：** 云平台、容器与自动化  /  **英文词数：** 302
**音频：** [播放 MP3](audio/032.mp3)  /  [英文字幕](audio/032.srt)

**背景：** 一个可用区故障影响生产请求。故障负责人、运维与数据库团队在确认剩余容量和写入安全后，按手册分阶段切流。

**角色：** A — Ops Engineer（运维工程师）；B — Incident Lead（故障负责人）；C — Database Engineer（数据库工程师）

**01 [B] / Incident Lead：** Zone A is failing health checks, and customer errors are increasing. Can we move its remaining traffic to zone B immediately under the failover runbook?
> A可用区健康检查失败，客户错误在增加。能否按故障切换手册立即把剩余流量移到B区？

**02 [A] / Ops Engineer：** Before shifting traffic, we need to verify capacity and dependencies. Zone B has application instances ready, but I still need confirmation of database write availability.
> 转移流量前，需要验证容量和依赖。B区已有就绪应用实例，但我还需要确认数据库写入可用。

**03 [C] / Database Engineer：** The database primary moved to zone B through the managed failover process. Replication was synchronous before the event, and the current writer health check passes.
> 数据库主节点已通过托管故障切换流程转移到B区。事件前是同步复制，当前写入节点健康检查通过。

**04 [B] / Incident Lead：** Can we state that no data was lost and service is recovered? The executive update is due in ten minutes, and they need a clear status.
> 能否宣布没有数据丢失、服务已恢复？十分钟后需要向管理层更新，他们需要明确状态。

**05 [A] / Ops Engineer：** We can report that database failover completed, but customer recovery still needs validation. Data integrity requires transaction checks; healthy infrastructure alone does not prove it.
> 可以报告数据库切换已完成，但仍需验证客户业务恢复。数据完整性需要交易检查，仅基础设施健康无法证明。

**06 [C] / Database Engineer：** I am checking recent committed orders against the payment records and database logs. I will also confirm that applications reconnect to the current writer endpoint.
> 我正在用支付记录和数据库日志核对最近已提交订单，也会确认应用重新连接到当前写入端点。

**07 [A] / Ops Engineer：** Zone B can handle the forecast load within the tested limit. Its queue, cache, and secret services are available, and background processing can be deferred.
> B区可以在已测试的限额内承受预测负载。其队列、缓存和密钥服务可用，后台处理可以延后。

**08 [B] / Incident Lead：** Proceed with the runbook checkpoints under my incident authority. What measurements will tell us to pause before sending all remaining traffic to zone B?
> 按我的故障处置授权执行手册检查节点。哪些指标会提示我们在全部剩余流量转到B区前暂停？

**09 [A] / Ops Engineer：** We will increase traffic in two steps, watching checkout success, database saturation, and request latency. Crossing the runbook thresholds means pausing and reassessing capacity.
> 我们分两步增加流量，观察结算成功率、数据库饱和度和请求延迟。超过手册阈值就暂停并重新评估容量。

**10 [C] / Database Engineer：** The transaction samples match, and writer connections are stable. I will remain on the bridge during each step and watch lock waits and connection limits.
> 交易样本一致，写入连接稳定。每一步我都会留在故障会议中，观察锁等待和连接限额。

**11 [B] / Incident Lead：** I will tell stakeholders that failover validation is underway, with reduced zone redundancy. Our next checkpoint is 10:20 UTC, regardless of whether the shift is complete.
> 我会告知相关方切换验证正在进行，当前可用区冗余降低。无论切流是否完成，下次检查时间都是UTC 10:20。

**12 [A] / Ops Engineer：** I will own traffic changes and record the evidence. We will confirm recovery against business checks and plan restoration of zone redundancy as separate follow-up work.
> 我负责流量变更并记录证据。我们根据业务检查确认恢复，并另行安排可用区冗余恢复工作。

**可复用表达：**

- Customer recovery still needs validation. — 客户业务恢复仍需要验证。
- Crossing the runbook thresholds means pausing and reassessing capacity. — 超过手册阈值意味着暂停并重新评估容量。

**落实动作：** 数据库团队确认写入与交易数据，运维按授权分两步切流；UTC 10:20汇总业务验证结果，并单独跟进可用区冗余恢复。
**练习重点：** 在紧急场景下说明切换前提、暂停阈值和恢复证据。

---

<a id="s033"></a>
## 033 Pod持续重启
**分类：** 云平台、容器与自动化  /  **英文词数：** 293
**音频：** [播放 MP3](audio/033.mp3)  /  [英文字幕](audio/033.srt)

**背景：** 新版本Pod持续重启，但集群其他工作负载稳定。运维与开发根据历史容器日志和配置差异定位启动失败。

**角色：** A — Ops Engineer（运维工程师）；B — Application Developer（应用开发工程师）

**01 [B] / Application Developer：** The new pods keep restarting with CrashLoopBackOff. Is the Kubernetes cluster unstable, or did the deployment introduce a problem with our application?
> 新Pod持续重启，显示CrashLoopBackOff。是Kubernetes集群不稳定，还是部署给应用引入了问题？

**02 [A] / Ops Engineer：** Other workloads on the same nodes are stable. These containers exit within three seconds, and their last logs report a missing required configuration value.
> 相同节点上的其他工作负载稳定。这些容器在三秒内退出，最后日志报告缺少一个必需配置值。

**03 [B] / Application Developer：** Please share the previous container logs and exit code. The live logs disappear too quickly, and I want to compare them with the startup changes.
> 请提供上一个容器的日志和退出码。实时日志消失太快，我想与启动逻辑变更进行比较。

**04 [A] / Ops Engineer：** I attached sanitized previous logs and the deployment revision to the incident. The exit code is one, and there is no recorded memory kill.
> 我已把脱敏后的历史日志和部署修订版附到故障记录。退出码为一，没有内存不足导致的终止记录。

**05 [B] / Application Developer：** The new version requires a queue endpoint variable. It exists in staging, but the production deployment template does not reference the corresponding configuration key.
> 新版本需要一个队列端点变量。预生产有该变量，但生产部署模板没有引用对应配置键。

**06 [A] / Ops Engineer：** That matches the error, but let us verify the intended production endpoint before adding it. We should not copy staging values into production.
> 这与错误吻合，但添加前先核实预期生产端点。我们不应把预生产的值复制到生产。

**07 [B] / Application Developer：** The application configuration owner confirms the approved endpoint. I can update the template and run validation to check that every required startup value is present.
> 应用配置负责人确认了已批准的端点。我可以更新模板并运行验证，检查所有必需启动值是否齐全。

**08 [A] / Ops Engineer：** How is customer traffic affected right now? The old replicas are still running, so we need their capacity and error rates before choosing the next step.
> 现在客户流量受到什么影响？旧副本仍在运行，因此选择下一步前需要了解其容量和错误率。

**09 [B] / Application Developer：** The rollout stopped after two new pods failed. The old replicas retain full serving capacity, and we have not observed an increase in customer errors.
> 两个新Pod失败后，发布已停止。旧副本保留完整服务容量，我们没有观察到客户错误增加。

**10 [A] / Ops Engineer：** Keep the rollout paused while the template correction is reviewed. After approval, validate one new pod’s startup, readiness, queue access, and a representative business transaction.
> 模板修正评审期间保持发布暂停。批准后，验证一个新Pod的启动、就绪、队列访问和一笔代表性业务交易。

**11 [B] / Application Developer：** I will own the correction and provide the test evidence at 14:00 UTC. If validation fails, we will stop and retain the currently serving revision.
> 我负责修正，并在UTC 14:00提供测试证据。如果验证失败，我们会停止并保留当前提供服务的修订版。

**12 [A] / Ops Engineer：** I will monitor the remaining rollout and record the configuration gap. We should add a required-value check to deployment validation before the next release.
> 我会监控后续发布并记录配置缺口。下次发布前，应在部署验证中加入必需配置值检查。

**可复用表达：**

- Other workloads on the same nodes are stable. — 相同节点上的其他工作负载稳定。
- Keep the rollout paused while the template correction is reviewed. — 模板修正评审期间，保持发布暂停。

**落实动作：** 开发负责人修正生产配置引用并于UTC 14:00提供证据；运维先验证一个新Pod及业务交易，通过后监控后续发布。
**练习重点：** 利用历史日志、退出码和部署差异缩小问题范围，同时确认现有服务容量。

---

<a id="s034"></a>
## 034 Pod无法调度
**分类：** 云平台、容器与自动化  /  **英文词数：** 296
**音频：** [播放 MP3](audio/034.mp3)  /  [英文字幕](audio/034.srt)

**背景：** 发布的新Pod一直Pending。发布负责人希望降低资源请求，运维结合调度事件、内存峰值和节点约束评估更稳妥的处理方式。

**角色：** A — Ops Engineer（运维工程师）；B — Release Lead（发布负责人）

**01 [B] / Release Lead：** The replacement pods have been pending for fifteen minutes. We have free memory elsewhere in the cluster, so why can the scheduler not place them?
> 替换Pod已Pending十五分钟。集群其他地方有空闲内存，为什么调度器无法放置它们？

**02 [A] / Ops Engineer：** The scheduler reports insufficient requested memory on eligible nodes. These pods require the production application pool, and the spare memory is in a different pool.
> 调度器提示符合条件的节点没有足够可分配内存。这些Pod要求生产应用节点池，而空闲内存在另一个池。

**03 [B] / Release Lead：** Could we lower the memory requests to make them fit? The dashboard shows only moderate usage, and the release window ends in an hour.
> 能否降低内存请求，让它们放得下？看板显示使用量不高，发布窗口还有一小时结束。

**04 [A] / Ops Engineer：** We need peak demand and startup measurements, not just the current average. Lower requests could cause overpacking and leave the workload vulnerable during load spikes.
> 我们需要峰值需求和启动阶段数据，而不只是当前均值。降低请求可能造成过度堆放，让工作负载在流量突增时更脆弱。

**05 [B] / Release Lead：** The new version uses the same requests as the previous one. Is the rolling update temporarily asking for more capacity because old replicas remain active?
> 新版本与上一版使用相同资源请求。是否因为旧副本仍运行，滚动更新暂时需要更多容量？

**06 [A] / Ops Engineer：** Yes, the configured surge adds two pods while keeping existing replicas available. I also checked placement rules; the application must remain distributed across two zones.
> 是的，配置的额外副本数会增加两个Pod，同时保留现有副本可用。我也检查了放置规则，应用必须分布在两个可用区。

**07 [B] / Release Lead：** What is the lowest-risk path within this window? We could change the update strategy, add eligible nodes, or postpone the release until capacity is ready.
> 在当前窗口内风险最低的方式是什么？可以修改更新策略、增加符合条件的节点，或等容量就绪后再发布。

**08 [A] / Ops Engineer：** The node pool has approved quota for one additional node per zone. Provisioning them preserves the current availability settings, provided the nodes become ready in time.
> 该节点池有已批准配额，可在每个可用区新增一个节点。只要节点及时就绪，就能保留当前可用性设置。

**09 [B] / Release Lead：** Please start the approved capacity change. At what point should we stop waiting and defer the release rather than compress the validation period?
> 请启动已批准的容量变更。应在什么时间停止等待并推迟发布，避免压缩验证时间？

**10 [A] / Ops Engineer：** If both nodes are not ready by 15:30 UTC, we will defer. Ready means cluster registration, network checks, and sufficient allocatable memory all pass.
> 如果两个节点在UTC 15:30前都未就绪，就推迟发布。就绪意味着集群注册、网络检查及足够可分配内存全部通过。

**11 [B] / Release Lead：** I will communicate that checkpoint to the release team. Once scheduling succeeds, we still need application readiness and the planned business validation before proceeding.
> 我会把这个检查节点通知发布团队。调度成功后，仍需应用就绪以及计划中的业务验证才能继续。

**12 [A] / Ops Engineer：** I will own node validation and capture the scheduler events. After this release, we will include rollout surge capacity in our routine capacity planning.
> 我负责节点验证并记录调度事件。这次发布后，我们会把滚动更新的额外容量纳入日常容量规划。

**可复用表达：**

- We need peak demand and startup measurements. — 我们需要峰值需求和启动阶段的测量数据。
- We will include rollout surge capacity in our routine capacity planning. — 我们会把滚动更新所需额外容量纳入日常容量规划。

**落实动作：** 运维按批准配额增加两个可用区的节点；UTC 15:30仍未通过就绪检查则推迟发布，发布负责人保留完整业务验证时间。
**练习重点：** 解释可调度容量与总空闲资源的差别，讨论资源请求及发布容量取舍。

---

<a id="s035"></a>
## 035 节点维护与工作负载迁移
**分类：** 云平台、容器与自动化  /  **英文词数：** 301
**音频：** [播放 MP3](audio/035.mp3)  /  [英文字幕](audio/035.srt)

**背景：** 维护前发现某工作负载有不健康副本，中断预算阻止排空。运维和平台团队先恢复冗余，再按节点逐一维护。

**角色：** A — Ops Engineer（运维工程师）；B — Platform Engineer（平台工程师）

**01 [A] / Ops Engineer：** We need to drain node seven for operating system maintenance. Have we checked workload disruption budgets, replacement capacity, and any storage tied to this node?
> 需要排空七号节点进行操作系统维护。是否检查了工作负载中断预算、替代容量和绑定该节点的存储？

**02 [B] / Platform Engineer：** One application has three replicas, but only two are healthy. Its disruption budget requires two available replicas, so evicting the local healthy pod is blocked.
> 一个应用有三个副本，但仅两个健康。它的中断预算要求两个可用副本，因此驱逐本机健康Pod被阻止了。

**03 [A] / Ops Engineer：** Let us restore redundancy before proceeding with maintenance. Do we know why the third replica is unhealthy, and is the application owner already involved?
> 继续维护前先恢复冗余。我们知道第三个副本为什么不健康吗？应用负责人是否已经参与？

**04 [B] / Platform Engineer：** The third replica cannot connect to its required queue because of a configuration mistake. The application owner is preparing a correction through the change process.
> 第三个副本因配置错误无法连接必需队列。应用负责人正在通过变更流程准备修正。

**05 [A] / Ops Engineer：** Please keep the drain paused. We should not override the disruption budget simply to meet the maintenance schedule, especially while the service has reduced redundancy.
> 请保持排空暂停。不应仅为赶上维护时间而绕过中断预算，尤其是服务冗余已降低时。

**06 [B] / Platform Engineer：** Agreed. The remaining nodes have capacity for the evicted workloads. I also confirmed that this application uses shared storage and has no node-local persistent data.
> 同意。其余节点有容量承载被驱逐工作负载。我也确认这个应用使用共享存储，没有节点本地持久化数据。

**07 [A] / Ops Engineer：** After the correction, wait until all three replicas are ready and stable. Can the application owner run a representative request before we resume?
> 修正后，等三个副本都就绪且稳定再继续。恢复操作前，应用负责人能否运行一个代表性请求？

**08 [B] / Platform Engineer：** Yes. They will verify queue processing and a completed customer operation. We will retain the evidence in the maintenance ticket rather than rely only on pod status.
> 可以。他们会验证队列处理和一项已完成客户操作。我们会把证据保存在维护工单中，而不只看Pod状态。

**09 [A] / Ops Engineer：** Once that passes, cordon the node and drain through normal eviction handling. Watch replacement readiness and stop if another replica becomes unhealthy during the move.
> 通过后，将节点设为不可调度并通过正常驱逐机制排空。观察替代副本就绪情况，如果迁移中又有副本不健康就停止。

**10 [B] / Platform Engineer：** I will perform the drain and maintenance one node at a time. After reboot, I will validate networking, storage access, and node health before uncordoning.
> 我会逐个节点执行排空和维护。重启后，先验证网络、存储访问和节点健康，再恢复节点可调度状态。

**11 [A] / Ops Engineer：** Let us use 19:00 UTC as the readiness checkpoint. If redundancy is not restored by then, we will reschedule this node and notify the maintenance owner.
> 以UTC 19:00作为准备情况检查节点。如果到时冗余尚未恢复，就重新安排该节点维护并通知维护负责人。

**12 [B] / Platform Engineer：** I will update the ticket at that checkpoint. We will close only after workload health remains stable and the application owner confirms the post-maintenance checks.
> 我会在那个检查时间更新工单。工作负载健康持续稳定，且应用负责人确认维护后检查通过，才会关闭工单。

**可复用表达：**

- Let us restore redundancy before proceeding with maintenance. — 继续维护前，我们先恢复冗余。
- Please keep the drain paused. — 请保持排空操作暂停。

**落实动作：** 先由应用负责人修正配置并验证三副本健康；UTC 19:00确认准备情况，通过后平台逐节点排空维护，否则重新排期。
**练习重点：** 解释中断预算与冗余的关系，明确维护前提、停止条件和业务验收。

---

<a id="s036"></a>
## 036 自动扩容未触发
**分类：** 云平台、容器与自动化  /  **英文词数：** 298
**音频：** [播放 MP3](audio/036.mp3)  /  [英文字幕](audio/036.srt)

**背景：** 业务请求排队且延迟升高，但按CPU配置的自动扩容没有动作。运维与应用负责人先检查扩容逻辑和下游瓶颈。

**角色：** A — Ops Engineer（运维工程师）；B — Application Lead（应用负责人）

**01 [B] / Application Lead：** Request latency has doubled, and the queue keeps growing. Why has the autoscaler stayed at six replicas when its configured maximum is twelve?
> 请求延迟翻倍，队列持续增长。最大副本数配置为十二，自动扩容为什么仍停在六个副本？

**02 [A] / Ops Engineer：** It scales on average CPU utilization, which is forty percent against a seventy percent target. The controller is receiving valid metrics and behaving as configured.
> 它按平均CPU利用率扩容，目前是百分之四十，目标为百分之七十。控制器收到有效指标，行为符合配置。

**03 [B] / Application Lead：** The workers may be waiting on database connections instead of using CPU. Could queue depth be a more useful scaling signal for this service?
> 工作线程可能在等待数据库连接，而不是消耗CPU。队列深度是否更适合作为这个服务的扩容指标？

**04 [A] / Ops Engineer：** The CPU signal does not reflect this waiting time. Before choosing queue depth, let us inspect dependency capacity so additional replicas do not increase database pressure.
> CPU指标没有反映这段等待时间。选择队列深度前，我们先检查依赖容量，避免增加副本后加重数据库压力。

**05 [B] / Application Lead：** The traces show long connection waits, and the database connection pool is almost full. Application CPU dropped as the connection waits started rising.
> 调用链显示连接等待很长，数据库连接池几乎满了。连接等待开始上升时，应用CPU反而下降了。

**06 [A] / Ops Engineer：** That supports a downstream constraint. I will ask the database owner to inspect active queries and locks, while we check request concurrency and retry volume.
> 这支持下游受限的判断。我会请数据库负责人检查活跃查询和锁，同时我们检查请求并发和重试量。

**07 [B] / Application Lead：** We found a reporting query holding locks longer than usual. The database owner is reviewing it; meanwhile, should we increase connection limits to clear the queue?
> 我们发现一个报表查询持锁时间异常偏长。数据库负责人正在检查；与此同时，是否应提高连接上限来清理队列？

**08 [A] / Ops Engineer：** More connections could intensify contention. Keep the approved limits while the owner assesses the query, and use existing admission controls to protect critical requests.
> 更多连接可能加剧争用。负责人评估查询期间应保留已批准的上限，并用现有准入控制保护关键请求。

**09 [B] / Application Lead：** I will verify that client retries follow our backoff policy. Once database performance recovers, we can measure whether the current workers drain the backlog safely.
> 我会核实客户端重试是否遵循退避策略。数据库性能恢复后，再测量当前工作线程能否安全消化积压。

**10 [A] / Ops Engineer：** For a future queue-based policy, we need load testing, dependency headroom, and a safe replica ceiling. That should be a separate reviewed change after recovery.
> 未来若采用基于队列的策略，需要负载测试、依赖余量和安全副本上限。恢复后应作为单独变更接受评审。

**11 [B] / Application Lead：** I will own application checks and report queue age, successful transactions, and retry volume at 11:00 UTC. The database owner will report the query findings.
> 我负责应用检查，并在UTC 11:00报告队列中请求等待时长、成功交易和重试量。数据库负责人汇报查询调查结果。

**12 [A] / Ops Engineer：** I will coordinate that checkpoint and track latency against baseline. We will confirm recovery when critical transactions succeed and the backlog returns to a healthy level.
> 我会协调检查节点，并将延迟与基线比较。关键交易成功且积压恢复到健康水平后，才确认恢复。

**可复用表达：**

- The controller is receiving valid metrics and behaving as configured. — 控制器收到有效指标，行为符合配置。
- More connections could intensify contention. — 更多连接可能加剧争用。

**落实动作：** 运维协调数据库负责人调查长持锁查询，应用负责人检查重试及关键业务；UTC 11:00核对积压和交易，恢复后单独评审扩容指标。
**练习重点：** 解释自动扩容触发条件，区分信号问题与下游瓶颈并评估放大效应。

---

<a id="s037"></a>
## 037 镜像拉取失败
**分类：** 云平台、容器与自动化  /  **英文词数：** 303
**音频：** [播放 MP3](audio/037.mp3)  /  [英文字幕](audio/037.srt)

**背景：** 镜像构建成功且标签存在，但生产Pod拉取失败。开发与运维逐层核对镜像引用、仓库连通和命名空间凭据。

**角色：** A — Ops Engineer（运维工程师）；B — Application Developer（应用开发工程师）

**01 [B] / Application Developer：** The build completed successfully, and I can see the image tag in the registry. Why is production stuck in ImagePullBackOff instead of starting the containers?
> 构建成功了，我也能在仓库里看到镜像标签。为什么生产卡在ImagePullBackOff，无法启动容器？

**02 [A] / Ops Engineer：** The artifact exists, but access from production still needs validation. I am checking the exact image reference, registry reachability, and credentials used by this namespace.
> 制品存在，但仍需验证生产环境能否访问。我正在检查确切镜像引用、仓库连通性和这个命名空间使用的凭据。

**03 [B] / Application Developer：** The deployment points to the expected repository and tag. Could the image have been overwritten, or might production be contacting a different registry endpoint?
> 部署指向预期仓库和标签。镜像是否被覆盖，或生产是否访问了不同的仓库端点？

**04 [A] / Ops Engineer：** The recorded digest matches the release artifact. Node events show an authorization rejection from the correct registry, rather than a name resolution or network timeout.
> 记录的摘要与发布制品匹配。节点事件显示正确仓库返回授权拒绝，并非域名解析问题或网络超时。

**05 [B] / Application Developer：** This namespace uses a separate image pull secret from staging. I suspect the repository moved, but the production service account kept its old credential reference.
> 这个命名空间使用的镜像拉取密钥与预生产不同。我怀疑仓库迁移了，但生产服务账号保留了旧凭据引用。

**06 [A] / Ops Engineer：** Let us verify the secret reference and repository read permissions through the registry owner. Please share identifiers and audit events, without copying the credential into chat.
> 我们与仓库负责人核对密钥引用和仓库读取权限。请提供标识和审计事件，不要把凭据复制到聊天里。

**07 [B] / Application Developer：** The owner confirms that the existing identity only reads the previous repository. An approved production identity already has read access to the new release repository.
> 负责人确认，现有身份只能读取旧仓库。另一个已批准的生产身份已经拥有新发布仓库的读取权限。

**08 [A] / Ops Engineer：** Then propose the correct secret reference through the deployment process. We should not grant broad registry administration rights just to make an image pull succeed.
> 那就通过部署流程申请使用正确密钥引用。不应只为拉取镜像成功而授予宽泛的仓库管理权限。

**09 [B] / Application Developer：** The old replicas still serve traffic, and the rollout is paused. I will prepare the reference correction and retain the existing release digest and verification checks.
> 旧副本仍在服务流量，发布已暂停。我会准备引用修正，并保留现有发布摘要和验证检查。

**10 [A] / Ops Engineer：** After approval, test one pod on a production node. Confirm image pull, startup, readiness, and a representative request before allowing the remaining rollout to continue.
> 批准后，在生产节点测试一个Pod。确认镜像拉取、启动、就绪及代表性请求成功后，再允许剩余发布继续。

**11 [B] / Application Developer：** I will own that correction and post sanitized results by 13:00 UTC. If pulling still fails, we will keep the rollout paused and inspect the new events.
> 我负责修正，并在UTC 13:00前发布脱敏结果。如果仍拉取失败，就保持发布暂停并检查新事件。

**12 [A] / Ops Engineer：** I will monitor customer errors during validation. Afterward, we should add a production identity access check whenever a release moves to a different repository.
> 验证期间我会监控客户错误。之后，每当发布迁移到其他仓库时，都应增加生产身份访问检查。

**可复用表达：**

- The artifact exists, but access from production still needs validation. — 制品存在，但仍需验证生产环境能否访问。
- Please share identifiers and audit events, without copying the credential into chat. — 请提供标识和审计事件，不要把凭据复制到聊天里。

**落实动作：** 开发负责人通过发布流程修正已批准的密钥引用；先在生产节点测试一个Pod，UTC 13:00前提供证据，运维监控业务错误。
**练习重点：** 按镜像引用、网络和授权分层排查，保持凭据最小权限。

---

<a id="s038"></a>
## 038 环境配置不一致
**分类：** 云平台、容器与自动化  /  **英文词数：** 292
**音频：** [播放 MP3](audio/038.mp3)  /  [英文字幕](audio/038.srt)

**背景：** 同一版本在预生产通过，在生产却报错。运维与应用负责人发现功能开关和依赖地址差异，先判断哪些是预期配置。

**角色：** A — Ops Engineer（运维工程师）；B — Application Lead（应用负责人）

**01 [B] / Application Lead：** The release passed staging yesterday, but production validation is failing. What differs between the environments besides the customer data and normal scale differences?
> 该版本昨天在预生产通过，但生产验证失败。除了客户数据和正常规模差异，两个环境还有什么不同？

**02 [A] / Ops Engineer：** The configuration comparison shows a different dependency endpoint and one feature flag enabled only in production. We need to confirm whether both differences are intentional.
> 配置比较显示，依赖端点不同，而且一个功能开关仅在生产启用。我们需要确认这两处差异是否都出于预期。

**03 [B] / Application Lead：** The dependency endpoint should differ because production uses a separate account. I do not recognize the enabled flag; it activates the new asynchronous validation path.
> 依赖端点应当不同，因为生产使用单独账号。那个启用的开关我不熟悉，它会激活新的异步校验路径。

**04 [A] / Ops Engineer：** Where is the approved flag state documented, and who owns it? We should compare against that source rather than make production identical to staging.
> 批准的开关状态记录在哪里，由谁负责？应与该依据比较，而不是让生产与预生产完全相同。

**05 [B] / Application Lead：** The rollout document says the flag must remain disabled until partner testing finishes. The application team owns it, and partner approval is still pending.
> 发布文档要求在合作伙伴测试结束前保持开关关闭。应用团队负责该配置，合作伙伴审批尚未完成。

**06 [A] / Ops Engineer：** The deployment history shows the production override was omitted from the new template. The application then used its default value, which enables the feature.
> 部署历史显示，新模板遗漏了生产覆盖配置，应用因此使用默认值，而默认值会启用该功能。

**07 [B] / Application Lead：** That explains the unexpected path, but could the endpoint also be wrong? I want to rule out a second issue before we propose the correction.
> 这解释了意外路径，但端点是否也可能有问题？提出修正前，我想排除第二个问题。

**08 [A] / Ops Engineer：** The endpoint matches the approved production inventory, and connectivity checks pass. Keep that intentional difference; correct the flag override through the reviewed configuration change.
> 端点与批准的生产清单一致，连通性检查通过。应保留这项预期差异，通过评审后的配置变更修正开关覆盖值。

**09 [B] / Application Lead：** The flag affects only new requests. I will verify whether any asynchronous requests are still pending and define how they complete without being submitted twice.
> 该开关只影响新请求。我会核查是否仍有异步请求待处理，并确定如何完成处理且避免重复提交。

**10 [A] / Ops Engineer：** After approval, restore the documented flag state on one instance first. We should validate the normal path and inspect the pending requests before expanding.
> 批准后，先在一个实例恢复文档规定的开关状态。扩大范围前，应验证正常路径并检查待处理请求。

**11 [B] / Application Lead：** I will own the configuration correction and request tracking. The validation evidence and pending-request status will be ready at our 16:30 UTC checkpoint.
> 我负责配置修正和请求跟踪。验证证据及待处理请求状态会在UTC 16:30检查节点前准备好。

**12 [A] / Ops Engineer：** I will compare error rates and completed transactions with baseline. We should also add explicit production flag values and required override checks to release validation.
> 我会把错误率和已完成交易与基线比较，也应在发布验证中加入明确生产开关值和必需覆盖配置检查。

**可复用表达：**

- We need to confirm whether both differences are intentional. — 我们需要确认两处差异是否都符合预期。
- Keep that intentional difference. — 保留这项符合预期的差异。

**落实动作：** 应用负责人修正遗漏的生产开关覆盖值并检查在途请求；UTC 16:30提交证据，运维比较业务指标并补充配置验证要求。
**练习重点：** 区分合理环境差异和配置漂移，依据批准配置选择修正范围。

---

<a id="s039"></a>
## 039 Terraform计划出现意外删除
**分类：** 云平台、容器与自动化  /  **英文词数：** 303
**音频：** [播放 MP3](audio/039.mp3)  /  [英文字幕](audio/039.srt)

**背景：** Terraform评审发现，本应只更新标签的变更计划删除生产数据库。运维要求暂停执行，并与平台工程师排查资源地址和状态映射。

**角色：** A — Ops Engineer（运维工程师）；B — Platform Engineer（平台工程师）

**01 [A] / Ops Engineer：** The Terraform plan includes deleting a production database and creating another one. The request only mentions a tag update; is this resource replacement expected?
> Terraform计划包含删除生产数据库并创建另一个。申请只提到更新标签；这次资源替换符合预期吗？

**02 [B] / Platform Engineer：** No, the database must remain in place. I updated the tag and reorganized a module, but I did not intend any change to the database itself.
> 不符合。数据库必须保留。我更新了标签并重组了一个模块，但不打算对数据库本身做任何修改。

**03 [A] / Ops Engineer：** Let us stop before applying and inspect the resource addresses, configuration diff, and state mapping. Please confirm that no execution has started in another pipeline.
> 我们先不要执行，检查资源地址、配置差异和状态映射。请确认其他流水线没有开始执行。

**04 [B] / Platform Engineer：** No apply is running, and the deployment gate remains closed. The module refactor changed the database resource address, but the state still contains the old address.
> 没有正在运行的apply，部署关卡仍关闭。模块重构改变了数据库资源地址，但状态仍保存旧地址。

**05 [A] / Ops Engineer：** That may explain the planned replacement. Does this refactor need an explicit move declaration so Terraform recognizes the existing database under its new configuration address?
> 这可能解释计划中的替换。该重构是否需要显式移动声明，让Terraform识别新配置地址对应现有数据库？

**06 [B] / Platform Engineer：** Yes. I omitted the move declaration. I will add it and check provider documentation to confirm that the revised configuration preserves the same underlying resource.
> 是的，我遗漏了移动声明。我会补上，并检查提供程序文档，确认修订配置保留同一个底层资源。

**07 [A] / Ops Engineer：** Please use our controlled state workflow and retain a protected backup. We should avoid ad hoc state edits or removing the database merely to silence the plan.
> 请使用受控状态流程并保留受保护备份。不要临时随意修改状态，也不要仅为让计划不报删除而移除数据库。

**08 [B] / Platform Engineer：** I will regenerate the plan against the correct production workspace after adding the declaration. The expected result is the address move and intended tag update only.
> 添加声明后，我会针对正确生产工作区重新生成计划。预期结果仅包含地址移动和计划中的标签更新。

**09 [A] / Ops Engineer：** Check every resource action, including replacement indicators and outputs. A shorter summary alone is not enough to establish that the production database will remain untouched.
> 请检查每个资源操作，包括替换标记和输出。仅凭更短的摘要，无法确认生产数据库不受影响。

**10 [B] / Platform Engineer：** Understood. I will attach the full sanitized plan and request a second review by 17:00 UTC. There should be no resource creation or destruction.
> 明白。我会在UTC 17:00前附上完整脱敏计划并申请第二次评审。计划不应创建或销毁任何资源。

**11 [A] / Ops Engineer：** The reviewed plan must match the configuration and state used for execution. If either changes afterward, regenerate the plan and repeat the required review.
> 已评审计划必须与执行使用的配置和状态一致。如果之后任一发生变化，应重新生成计划并再次完成所需评审。

**12 [B] / Platform Engineer：** I will keep the apply blocked until that review passes. After the approved change, I will verify the database identifier, connectivity, and final state mapping.
> 评审通过前我会保持apply被阻止。批准的变更执行后，我会验证数据库标识、连通性和最终状态映射。

**可复用表达：**

- Let us stop before applying. — 先不要执行。
- If either changes afterward, regenerate the plan and repeat the required review. — 如果之后任一发生变化，应重新生成计划并再次完成所需评审。

**落实动作：** 平台工程师补充资源地址移动声明并在UTC 17:00前提交完整新计划；第二次评审通过前保持apply关闭，执行后核对数据库与状态映射。
**练习重点：** 明确停止非预期删除，追问状态映射并要求重新生成和评审计划。

---

<a id="s040"></a>
## 040 自动化脚本执行范围
**分类：** 云平台、容器与自动化  /  **英文词数：** 298
**音频：** [播放 MP3](audio/040.mp3)  /  [英文字幕](audio/040.srt)

**背景：** 经理希望当晚用脚本批量修正生产配置。运维提出试运行、小批验证、安全续跑与回退方案，并设置明确扩批门槛。

**角色：** A — Ops Engineer（运维工程师）；B — Operations Manager（运维经理）

**01 [B] / Operations Manager：** Can we run the configuration repair script across all 120 production servers tonight? Completing them together would clear a large maintenance backlog before tomorrow’s review.
> 今晚能在全部120台生产服务器上运行配置修复脚本吗？一起完成可以在明天评审前清掉大量维护积压。

**02 [A] / Ops Engineer：** I recommend a dry run, then a small representative batch. We need to confirm the target inventory, expected changes, and service impact before expanding.
> 我建议先试运行，再选一小批有代表性的机器。扩大前需要确认目标清单、预期变更和服务影响。

**03 [B] / Operations Manager：** What does the dry run verify, and how will you select the first servers? Our application hosts and queue workers use different configuration layouts.
> 试运行验证什么，又怎样选择首批服务器？应用主机与队列工作节点的配置布局不同。

**04 [A] / Ops Engineer：** It will list proposed changes without writing files. We will select one host from each layout and verify that critical redundant partners are outside the batch.
> 它只列出拟进行的变更，不写文件。我们从每种布局选择一台主机，并确认关键冗余伙伴不在同一批。

**05 [B] / Operations Manager：** What happens if the script stops halfway through a host or the remote session drops? I do not want inconsistent configuration or duplicate changes.
> 如果脚本在某台主机上执行到一半停止，或者远程会话断开，会怎样？我不希望出现配置不一致或重复变更。

**06 [A] / Ops Engineer：** The script must write changes atomically, record per-host completion, and compare current state before retrying. We will test interruption and safe reruns in staging first.
> 脚本必须以原子方式写入变更，记录每台主机完成情况，并在重试前比较当前状态。我们会先在预生产测试中断和安全重跑。

**07 [B] / Operations Manager：** Please also cover rollback. If the updated configuration causes an application failure, can we restore the previous file without introducing another compatibility problem?
> 也请考虑回退。如果更新后的配置导致应用故障，能否恢复之前的文件而不引入新的兼容性问题？

**08 [A] / Ops Engineer：** We will preserve protected configuration snapshots and test restoration with the deployed application version. Any required service restart must be included in the approved procedure.
> 我们会保留受保护的配置快照，并用已部署应用版本测试恢复。任何必需的服务重启都必须纳入已批准流程。

**09 [B] / Operations Manager：** That sounds reasonable. What evidence do you need from the first batch before you recommend proceeding, and what would automatically stop the remaining work?
> 这样合理。建议继续前，需要首批提供什么证据？哪些情况会自动停止剩余工作？

**10 [A] / Ops Engineer：** We need the expected file differences, healthy service checks, and successful business requests. Unexpected changes, failed checks, or elevated errors will stop the next batch.
> 我们需要符合预期的文件差异、健康的服务检查及成功业务请求。意外变更、检查失败或错误升高都会阻止下一批。

**11 [B] / Operations Manager：** I will approve the staged schedule once those prerequisites are documented. Let us review the dry-run results and interruption tests at 18:00 UTC before production execution.
> 这些前提记录清楚后，我会批准分批计划。我们在生产执行前，于UTC 18:00评审试运行结果和中断测试。

**12 [A] / Ops Engineer：** I will own execution and maintain a host-by-host record. We will expand only after each checkpoint passes and report completed, unchanged, failed, and pending hosts separately.
> 我负责执行并维护逐主机记录。只有各检查节点通过后才扩大范围，并分别报告已完成、无须变更、失败和待处理主机。

**可复用表达：**

- We need to confirm the target inventory, expected changes, and service impact before expanding. — 扩大范围前，需要确认目标清单、预期变更和服务影响。
- We will expand only after each checkpoint passes. — 只有各检查节点通过后，我们才扩大范围。

**落实动作：** 运维完成试运行、中断、安全重跑及回退测试；UTC 18:00由经理评审，批准后按代表性小批执行，验证通过再扩批。
**练习重点：** 清楚说明自动化范围、幂等与续跑要求，并定义停止条件和扩批依据。

---

<a id="s041"></a>
## 041 应用启动失败
**分类：** Application 应用与依赖  /  **英文词数：** 266
**音频：** [播放 MP3](audio/041.mp3)  /  [英文字幕](audio/041.srt)

**背景：** 发布任务已结束，但新版应用未通过就绪检查。团队需要查明启动失败原因，并确认现有实例是否仍能提供服务。

**角色：** A — Ops Engineer（运维工程师）；B — Release Lead（发布负责人）；C — Developer（开发工程师）

**01 [B] / Release Lead：** The deployment completed, but the new instances never became ready. Are we dealing with a slow startup or an actual failure?
> 部署已经完成，但新实例一直没有就绪。这是启动慢，还是确实启动失败了？

**02 [A] / Ops Engineer：** The process exits after thirty seconds. Startup logs identify a missing configuration value for the inventory service URL, without exposing any credentials.
> 进程在三十秒后退出。启动日志指出缺少库存服务地址这一配置值，日志没有暴露任何凭据。

**03 [C] / Developer：** That value became mandatory in this release. It exists in our approved configuration repository, but the production deployment references an older configuration revision.
> 这个值在本次版本中变成了必填项。获准使用的配置库里有这个值，但生产部署引用的是旧版配置。

**04 [B] / Release Lead：** Can we update the configuration directly, or do we need to stop the release and restore the previous application version first?
> 我们能直接更新配置吗，还是需要先停止发布并恢复之前的应用版本？

**05 [A] / Ops Engineer：** The previous instances are still serving traffic, so I'll pause the rollout. We can verify the correction before starting any replacements.
> 之前的实例仍在承接流量，所以我会暂停滚动发布。我们可以先核实修正内容，再开始替换实例。

**06 [C] / Developer：** I'll compare the required settings against the release manifest and check the destination address. We should avoid discovering another missing value afterward.
> 我会根据发布清单比较必需配置，并检查目标地址，避免之后又发现其他缺失值。

**07 [B] / Release Lead：** Please also confirm that the existing version can use this configuration. We need a workable rollback path if the new instances fail again.
> 也请确认现有版本能使用这份配置。如果新实例再次失败，我们需要有可行的回退路径。

**08 [C] / Developer：** The setting is additive, and the previous version ignores it. I'll attach that compatibility check and the configuration difference to the change record.
> 这是新增配置，之前的版本会忽略它。我会把兼容性检查和配置差异附到变更记录中。

**09 [A] / Ops Engineer：** Once the release approval is recorded, I'll deploy one instance and check readiness, dependency access, and a test inventory lookup before proceeding.
> 发布批准记录完成后，我会先部署一个实例，检查就绪状态、依赖访问和测试库存查询，再继续推进。

**10 [B] / Release Lead：** What would make us stop at that stage, even if the readiness check turns green? I want the decision criteria to be clear.
> 在那个阶段，即使就绪检查变绿，什么情况仍会让我们停止？我想把决策标准说清楚。

**11 [A] / Ops Engineer：** Any failed functional check or elevated errors will stop expansion. I'll post the first validation results in ten minutes, without committing to completion yet.
> 任何功能检查失败或错误率升高都会阻止扩大部署。我会在十分钟后发布首轮验证结果，目前不承诺完成时间。

**12 [B] / Release Lead：** Agreed. Keep the healthy instances available, and have the release owner confirm the full rollout only after the application checks pass.
> 同意。保持健康实例可用，应用检查通过后，再由发布负责人确认是否完成全部发布。

**可复用表达：**

- The previous instances are still serving traffic. — 之前的实例仍在承接流量。
- We need a workable rollback path. — 我们需要有可行的回退路径。

**落实动作：** 运维暂停发布并保留健康实例；开发核对配置和版本兼容性，获批后先验证单个实例，十分钟后汇报检查结果。
**练习重点：** 说明发布完成与应用就绪的区别，确认配置来源、回退条件和验证责任。

---

<a id="s042"></a>
## 042 API错误率突然上升
**分类：** Application 应用与依赖  /  **英文词数：** 271
**音频：** [播放 MP3](audio/042.mp3)  /  [英文字幕](audio/042.srt)

**背景：** 新版本上线后，创建订单接口的服务器错误率上升。团队需要按接口确认影响，并在保存证据后决定是否回退。

**角色：** A — Ops Engineer（运维工程师）；B — Developer（开发工程师）；C — Release Lead（发布负责人）

**01 [A] / Ops Engineer：** The API's server error rate rose immediately after the release. Order creation is affected, while product searches and order history remain healthy.
> API的服务器错误率在发布后立即上升。创建订单受到影响，商品搜索和订单历史仍然正常。

**02 [B] / Developer：** How large is the increase, and does it affect all order creation requests or a specific combination of fields in the payload?
> 增幅有多大？是所有创建订单请求都受影响，还是只影响请求内容中特定字段的组合？

**03 [A] / Ops Engineer：** Eight percent of order creation requests failed over the last five minutes. The failing requests include a discount code; the others usually succeed.
> 过去五分钟，百分之八的创建订单请求失败。失败请求都包含折扣码，其他请求通常成功。

**04 [C] / Release Lead：** That exceeds our release rollback threshold. Before we decide, can you preserve the relevant logs and confirm whether failed requests created any orders?
> 这超过了我们的发布回退阈值。作决定前，能否保存相关日志，并确认失败请求是否创建了订单？

**05 [B] / Developer：** I'll trace a sample using request IDs. The exception appears before the database write, but I need to verify that across the affected path.
> 我会用请求ID追踪一组样本。异常看起来发生在数据库写入前，但我需要核实整条受影响链路。

**06 [A] / Ops Engineer：** I'll preserve the error traces and compare both versions. Correlation with deployment is strong, although we haven't established the exact defect yet.
> 我会保存错误调用链并比较两个版本。问题与部署的关联很强，但我们还没有确定具体缺陷。

**07 [C] / Release Lead：** Was there a database migration in this release? I don't want an application rollback to introduce a second problem with stored data.
> 本次发布包含数据库迁移吗？我不希望应用回退给已存数据带来第二个问题。

**08 [B] / Developer：** There was no schema or data migration. The previous application version accepts the current data format, and the rollback package passed our compatibility check.
> 没有表结构或数据迁移。之前的应用版本能接受当前数据格式，回退包也通过了我们的兼容性检查。

**09 [C] / Release Lead：** Then I'll authorize the rollback under the release procedure. Keep the evidence for investigation, and tell support which order operation is affected.
> 那我会按发布流程批准回退。保留证据用于调查，并告知支持团队具体哪个订单操作受影响。

**10 [A] / Ops Engineer：** I'll run the rollback and monitor order creation by payload type. A lower overall error rate alone could hide continued discount failures.
> 我会执行回退，并按请求内容类型监测创建订单。仅看整体错误率下降，可能会漏掉折扣请求持续失败的情况。

**11 [B] / Developer：** I'll validate a discounted test order and check its database record. We'll also confirm that failed attempts did not leave partial orders.
> 我会验证一笔带折扣的测试订单并检查数据库记录。我们也会确认失败尝试没有留下不完整订单。

**12 [A] / Ops Engineer：** I'll update the incident in ten minutes with rollback status and business checks. The defect investigation will continue separately after recovery is confirmed.
> 我会在十分钟后更新故障记录，说明回退状态和业务检查结果。确认恢复后，缺陷调查会继续单独推进。

**可复用表达：**

- Order creation is affected, while product searches and order history remain healthy. — 创建订单受到影响，商品搜索和订单历史仍然正常。
- Correlation with deployment is strong. — 问题与部署的关联很强。

**落实动作：** 发布负责人按流程批准回退；运维保留调用链并按请求类型监控，开发验证折扣订单和数据完整性，十分钟后更新。
**练习重点：** 用请求比例描述影响，区分发布关联、故障原因和业务恢复证据。

<a id="s043"></a>
## 043 API超时与上游依赖
**分类：** Application 应用与依赖  /  **英文词数：** 266
**音频：** [播放 MP3](audio/043.mp3)  /  [英文字幕](audio/043.srt)

**背景：** 网关频繁返回超时，开发建议提高等待时间。团队通过调用链分析上游耗时，并讨论超时预算和并发压力。

**角色：** A — Ops Engineer（运维工程师）；B — Developer（开发工程师）

**01 [B] / Developer：** The gateway returns timeout errors after five seconds. Could we raise its timeout to fifteen seconds so more requests have time to finish?
> 网关在五秒后返回超时错误。能否把超时提高到十五秒，让更多请求有时间完成？

**02 [A] / Ops Engineer：** That might reduce visible errors temporarily, but it would also hold connections longer. The upstream pricing service has become much slower.
> 这可能暂时减少可见错误，但也会让连接占用更久。上游定价服务已经明显变慢。

**03 [B] / Developer：** Do the traces show where those five seconds go? We should separate network delay from time spent waiting inside the pricing service.
> 调用链能显示这五秒花在哪里吗？我们应该区分网络延迟和定价服务内部的等待时间。

**04 [A] / Ops Engineer：** Most of the time is inside pricing. Its request queue grew sharply, while connection establishment stayed near its normal twenty milliseconds.
> 大部分时间花在定价服务内部。它的请求队列明显增长，而连接建立时间仍接近正常的二十毫秒。

**05 [B] / Developer：** The pricing team says database calls are slow. Would adding application instances help clear the queue while they investigate the queries?
> 定价团队说数据库调用很慢。他们调查查询期间，增加应用实例有助于清理队列吗？

**06 [A] / Ops Engineer：** More instances could increase database concurrency and make that bottleneck worse. We need their capacity assessment before changing either concurrency or instance count.
> 更多实例可能增加数据库并发，让瓶颈恶化。调整并发或实例数量前，需要他们评估容量。

**07 [B] / Developer：** Understood. Our client also retries once after a timeout. Could the first attempt still be running when that retry reaches the service?
> 明白。我们的客户端在超时后还会重试一次。重试到达服务时，第一次尝试是否仍可能在运行？

**08 [A] / Ops Engineer：** Yes, cancellation doesn't currently propagate reliably. I'll check overlapping attempts and ask the pricing owner which requests can safely be retried.
> 有可能，目前取消信号不能可靠地传递。我会检查重叠尝试，并询问定价负责人哪些请求可以安全重试。

**09 [B] / Developer：** We have an approved fallback for optional price suggestions, but confirmed checkout prices must remain accurate. Can we isolate the optional requests?
> 可选价格建议有获准的降级方案，但结算确认价格必须保持准确。能否单独处理这些可选请求？

**10 [A] / Ops Engineer：** Yes. Let's route only the optional suggestions to that fallback through the incident change process, then watch pricing load and checkout latency.
> 可以。我们通过故障变更流程，仅让可选建议使用该降级方案，然后观察定价负载和结算延迟。

**11 [B] / Developer：** I'll coordinate that change and review timeout budgets with both service owners. We should leave enough time for the gateway to return a controlled response.
> 我会协调变更，并与两位服务负责人检查超时预算。应当为网关返回受控响应留出足够时间。

**12 [A] / Ops Engineer：** I'll share traces and updated queue measurements in fifteen minutes. We'll reconsider timeout settings after understanding the upstream limit and the fallback results.
> 我会在十五分钟后分享调用链和最新队列数据。了解上游限制和降级效果后，我们再重新考虑超时设置。

**可复用表达：**

- The upstream pricing service has become much slower. — 上游定价服务已经明显变慢。
- We need their capacity assessment before changing either concurrency or instance count. — 调整并发或实例数量前，需要他们评估容量。

**落实动作：** 运维核查调用耗时和重叠重试；开发协调可选请求降级并检查超时预算，十五分钟后基于队列和业务延迟再决策。
**练习重点：** 解释延长超时的代价，用调用链证据定位瓶颈，并限定降级范围。

---

<a id="s044"></a>
## 044 前端正常但业务操作失败
**分类：** Application 应用与依赖  /  **英文词数：** 277
**音频：** [播放 MP3](audio/044.mp3)  /  [英文字幕](audio/044.srt)

**背景：** 客户可以打开网站首页，但提交订单持续失败。业务与运维需要统一故障影响和恢复标准。

**角色：** A — Ops Engineer（运维工程师）；B — Business Lead（业务负责人）

**01 [B] / Business Lead：** The homepage loads on my laptop, and the website status check is green. Why are we still treating this as an active incident?
> 我的电脑能打开首页，网站状态检查也是绿色。为什么我们仍把它当作未恢复的故障？

**02 [A] / Ops Engineer：** Those checks only confirm that the page is available. The checkout journey is failing when customers submit orders, so the business impact continues.
> 那些检查只确认页面可用。客户提交订单时，结算流程失败，所以业务影响仍在持续。

**03 [B] / Business Lead：** Can you quantify that impact? We need to explain it clearly to customer support without suggesting that every visitor is affected.
> 能量化影响吗？我们需要向客户支持清楚解释，又不能让人以为每位访客都受到影响。

**04 [A] / Ops Engineer：** During the last ten minutes, thirty percent of order submission requests failed. That is a request measure; we haven't calculated unique affected customers yet.
> 过去十分钟，百分之三十的订单提交请求失败。这是按请求统计的指标；我们还没有计算受影响的独立客户数。

**05 [B] / Business Lead：** Thank you for separating those figures. Are customers being charged when the order fails, or is the failure happening before payment begins?
> 谢谢你区分这两个数字。订单失败时，客户会被扣款吗，还是在支付开始前就已经失败了？

**06 [A] / Ops Engineer：** The traces suggest the failure occurs before payment authorization, but finance is reconciling a sample. We shouldn't rule out payment impact until that finishes.
> 调用链显示失败可能发生在支付授权之前，但财务还在核对样本。在核对完成前，不应排除支付影响。

**07 [B] / Business Lead：** The application team has applied a correction. If the submission endpoint returns a successful response now, can we announce that checkout has recovered?
> 应用团队已经实施修正。如果提交接口现在返回成功响应，我们能宣布结算恢复了吗？

**08 [A] / Ops Engineer：** We need to validate the full journey: create an order, confirm payment status, verify the stored order, and check that the confirmation reaches the customer.
> 我们需要验证完整流程：创建订单、确认支付状态、核实已存订单，并检查确认信息是否送达客户。

**09 [B] / Business Lead：** I can assign a business tester to the approved test account. Should we also check both standard delivery and the regional collection option?
> 我可以安排业务测试人员使用获准的测试账户。是否也应该检查标准配送和区域自提这两种方式？

**10 [A] / Ops Engineer：** Yes, both were affected. I'll compare their submission success rates and latency during a ten-minute observation period after the test transactions succeed.
> 需要，两种方式都受到了影响。测试交易成功后，我会在十分钟观察期内比较它们的提交成功率和延迟。

**11 [B] / Business Lead：** I'll own the business validation and ask support to keep the incident message active until we have those results and the payment reconciliation.
> 我负责业务验证，并要求支持团队在取得这些结果和支付核对结果之前，继续保留故障通知。

**12 [A] / Ops Engineer：** I'll gather the technical evidence and report at the next checkpoint in fifteen minutes. We'll confirm recovery together once both sets of checks pass.
> 我会收集技术证据，在十五分钟后的下一个检查点汇报。两类检查都通过后，我们共同确认恢复。

**可复用表达：**

- The business impact continues. — 业务影响仍在持续。
- We need to validate the full journey. — 我们需要验证完整流程。

**落实动作：** 业务负责人安排两类履约方式的测试与支付核对；运维验证交易记录、通知和成功率，十五分钟后共同判断恢复。
**练习重点：** 把页面可用性与完整业务链路关联起来，准确区分请求数据和客户人数。

---

<a id="s045"></a>
## 045 连接池耗尽
**分类：** Application 应用与依赖  /  **英文词数：** 273
**音频：** [播放 MP3](audio/045.mp3)  /  [英文字幕](audio/045.srt)

**背景：** 应用出现大量获取数据库连接超时，开发建议扩大连接池。运维和数据库管理员共同检查连接总量及持有时间。

**角色：** A — Ops Engineer（运维工程师）；B — Developer（开发工程师）；C — Database Administrator（数据库管理员）

**01 [B] / Developer：** Requests are timing out while waiting for database connections. Should we double the application connection pool to give them more room?
> 请求在等待数据库连接时超时。我们要不要把应用连接池扩大一倍，给它们更多余量？

**02 [A] / Ops Engineer：** Let's check the total connection budget first. We have eight application instances, so doubling each pool would add hundreds of potential database connections.
> 我们先检查总连接预算。目前有八个应用实例，因此把每个连接池翻倍，可能会增加数百个数据库连接。

**03 [C] / Database Administrator：** The database currently has seven hundred sixty connections against a limit of eight hundred. Several other services share that limit with this application.
> 数据库当前有七百六十个连接，上限是八百。其他几个服务也与这个应用共用这一上限。

**04 [B] / Developer：** Then a larger pool isn't safe. Are connections staying busy because queries are slow, or are we failing to return them after requests finish?
> 那扩大连接池并不安全。连接一直被占用，是因为查询慢，还是因为请求结束后没有归还连接？

**05 [A] / Ops Engineer：** Both need checking. Pool metrics show the hold time increased from two seconds to twenty, while active requests grew only slightly after deployment.
> 两方面都需要检查。连接池指标显示，持有时间从两秒增至二十秒，而部署后的活跃请求量只略有增加。

**06 [C] / Database Administrator：** I see many idle connections inside open transactions. They aren't running queries, but they still hold resources and may retain database locks.
> 我看到很多处于未关闭事务中的空闲连接。它们没有执行查询，但仍占用资源，也可能保留数据库锁。

**07 [B] / Developer：** A new error path may skip transaction cleanup. I'll inspect that code and correlate affected requests with connection identifiers before claiming a leak.
> 新的异常处理路径可能跳过了事务清理。我会检查代码，并把受影响请求与连接标识关联起来，再判断是否存在泄漏。

**08 [A] / Ops Engineer：** Meanwhile, can we reduce the affected optional workload through the approved control? That could protect core transactions while we investigate connection release.
> 同时，我们能通过获准的控制措施减少受影响的可选工作负载吗？这样可以在排查连接释放期间保护核心事务。

**09 [C] / Database Administrator：** Yes, provided we monitor active transactions and lock waits. Please avoid restarting all instances together; that could produce a sudden reconnection surge.
> 可以，前提是监控活跃事务和锁等待。请避免同时重启所有实例，否则可能出现突然的重连高峰。

**10 [B] / Developer：** If a targeted restart becomes necessary, I'll first confirm request draining and transaction handling. The code fix will need a separate reviewed deployment.
> 如果确实需要针对性重启，我会先确认请求排空和事务处理。代码修复则需要单独经过审核的发布。

**11 [A] / Ops Engineer：** I'll coordinate the workload reduction and collect pool measurements every minute. Let's reconvene in ten minutes with connection trends and the code findings.
> 我会协调减少工作负载，并每分钟采集连接池指标。十分钟后，我们带着连接趋势和代码发现再次讨论。

**12 [C] / Database Administrator：** I'll watch database headroom and identify the transaction owners. We can call the mitigation effective only when core requests complete without growing lock waits.
> 我会监测数据库余量并确认事务负责人。只有核心请求能够完成且锁等待不再增长，才能认为缓解措施有效。

**可复用表达：**

- Let's check the total connection budget first. — 我们先检查总连接预算。
- Please avoid restarting all instances together. — 请避免同时重启所有实例。

**落实动作：** 保持连接池上限，按流程减少可选负载；开发核查异常路径清理逻辑，数据库管理员跟踪事务和锁，十分钟后复核。
**练习重点：** 说明单实例调整对总连接数的影响，区分慢查询、长事务和连接未释放。

---

<a id="s046"></a>
## 046 线程池与请求排队
**分类：** Application 应用与依赖  /  **英文词数：** 268
**音频：** [播放 MP3](audio/046.mp3)  /  [英文字幕](audio/046.srt)

**背景：** CPU使用率不高，但应用请求队列持续增长。团队需要结合线程等待和下游延迟解释处理瓶颈。

**角色：** A — Ops Engineer（运维工程师）；B — Application Lead（应用负责人）；C — Developer（开发工程师）

**01 [B] / Application Lead：** CPU is only forty percent, but response time has tripled and requests are queuing. Why can't the application use the remaining capacity?
> CPU只有百分之四十，但响应时间增加到三倍，而且请求在排队。为什么应用不能利用剩余容量？

**02 [A] / Ops Engineer：** Low CPU doesn't necessarily mean spare processing capacity. Worker threads may be occupied while waiting for a dependency rather than doing calculations.
> CPU低不一定表示还有处理余量。工作线程可能正被依赖等待占用，而不是在执行计算。

**03 [C] / Developer：** Can you capture thread information during the slowdown? We should verify where the workers are waiting before we change the pool settings.
> 能在变慢期间采集线程信息吗？调整线程池设置前，应该先核实工作线程在等待哪里。

**04 [A] / Ops Engineer：** I'll use our approved diagnostic capture with a short sampling window. I'll also align the timestamps with queue depth and dependency latency.
> 我会使用获准的诊断采集方式，并采用较短的采样窗口。我还会把时间戳与队列深度和依赖延迟对齐。

**05 [B] / Application Lead：** Would increasing the worker count help temporarily? The incoming request rate is normal, and our queue has reached two thousand pending requests.
> 临时增加工作线程数量会有帮助吗？传入请求速率正常，但队列已经达到两千个待处理请求。

**06 [C] / Developer：** Only if the dependency has room for more concurrent work. Otherwise, extra workers could send more requests into the same bottleneck.
> 只有依赖能承担更多并发处理才有帮助。否则，额外的工作线程可能把更多请求送进同一个瓶颈。

**07 [A] / Ops Engineer：** The first capture shows most workers blocked on the document service. Its latency rose from one second to twelve seconds before our queue grew.
> 首次采集显示，大部分工作线程阻塞在文档服务上。在我们的队列增长前，它的延迟已经从一秒增至十二秒。

**08 [B] / Application Lead：** Document previews are optional, but document downloads are essential. Can we reduce preview work without interrupting downloads or losing any user data?
> 文档预览是可选功能，但下载是必要功能。能否减少预览处理，同时不中断下载，也不丢失用户数据？

**09 [C] / Developer：** The application supports a temporary preview fallback. I'll verify that downloads use a separate path and get the service owner's approval to enable it.
> 应用支持临时预览降级。我会确认下载使用独立链路，并取得服务负责人的批准后启用。

**10 [A] / Ops Engineer：** I'll monitor queue age as well as depth. A smaller queue isn't enough if old requests remain stuck or clients keep timing out.
> 我会同时监测队列中请求的等待时间和数量。如果旧请求仍然卡住，或客户端持续超时，仅队列变小还不够。

**11 [B] / Application Lead：** Please coordinate with the document team and give us another update in ten minutes. Keep any thread captures in the restricted diagnostic location.
> 请与文档团队协调，并在十分钟后再次更新。线程采集文件都保存在访问受限的诊断位置。

**12 [A] / Ops Engineer：** Agreed. We'll confirm improvement through completed downloads, lower wait times, and falling dependency latency, then investigate why document processing slowed down.
> 同意。我们会通过下载完成情况、等待时间下降和依赖延迟降低来确认改善，再调查文档处理变慢的原因。

**可复用表达：**

- Low CPU doesn't necessarily mean spare processing capacity. — CPU低不一定表示还有处理余量。
- I'll monitor queue age as well as depth. — 我会同时监测队列中请求的等待时间和数量。

**落实动作：** 运维采集受控线程信息并关联队列指标；开发确认预览降级边界并申请批准，十分钟后检查下载完成率和等待时间。
**练习重点：** 用等待状态解释低CPU下的拥塞，讨论增加线程的前提与业务降级边界。

---

<a id="s047"></a>
## 047 重试放大流量
**分类：** Application 应用与依赖  /  **英文词数：** 279
**音频：** [播放 MP3](audio/047.mp3)  /  [英文字幕](audio/047.srt)

**背景：** 依赖服务出错后，内部调用量增至原来的三倍，但用户活动没有明显变化。团队排查多层重试是否造成流量放大。

**角色：** A — Ops Engineer（运维工程师）；B — Incident Lead（故障负责人）；C — Developer（开发工程师）

**01 [B] / Incident Lead：** Calls to the inventory dependency tripled, but customer activity stayed flat. Is this a traffic spike, or are our systems generating extra work?
> 库存依赖的调用量增至三倍，但客户活动没有变化。这是流量高峰，还是我们的系统产生了额外处理？

**02 [A] / Ops Engineer：** The edge request rate is stable. Traces show the same operation retried by the application client and again by an internal proxy.
> 边缘请求速率稳定。调用链显示，同一操作先由应用客户端重试，再由内部代理重试。

**03 [C] / Developer：** Each layer allows two retries, so one customer request can trigger several downstream attempts. The timeout settings may also make those attempts overlap.
> 每层都允许重试两次，因此一个客户请求可能引发多次下游尝试。超时设置还可能让这些尝试重叠。

**04 [B] / Incident Lead：** Should we disable every retry while inventory recovers? We need to lower the load quickly, but I don't want to lose recoverable requests.
> 库存服务恢复期间，我们应该关闭所有重试吗？我们需要迅速减轻负载，但我不希望丢掉本可恢复的请求。

**05 [A] / Ops Engineer：** Let's choose one retry owner and set a bounded policy. Different operations need different treatment, especially if they can change inventory or create reservations.
> 我们先选定一个重试负责层，并设置有上限的策略。不同操作需要不同处理，尤其是会修改库存或创建预留的操作。

**06 [C] / Developer：** Availability checks are read-only. Reservations use an idempotency key, but we must verify that the same key survives every retry before relying on it.
> 库存可用性检查是只读的。预留操作使用幂等键，但依赖它之前，必须核实每次重试都保留相同的键。

**07 [B] / Incident Lead：** Which change can we safely make first under the incident procedure? Please include a measurable target so we can judge whether it helps.
> 按故障流程，我们首先能安全实施哪项变更？请给出可衡量的目标，便于判断是否有效。

**08 [A] / Ops Engineer：** We can remove duplicate proxy retries for availability checks and keep one client retry with backoff and jitter, using the approved configuration control.
> 我们可以通过获准的配置控制，移除可用性检查在代理层的重复重试，保留客户端一次带退避和随机间隔的重试。

**09 [C] / Developer：** I'll review reservation traces separately and hold its retry policy unchanged until key propagation is verified. We should also respect the dependency's retry guidance.
> 我会单独检查预留操作的调用链，在核实幂等键传递前保持其重试策略不变。我们也应该遵循依赖服务的重试指导。

**10 [A] / Ops Engineer：** The target is fewer downstream attempts per incoming request, alongside improving success rates. A lower call count alone could simply mean more requests fail immediately.
> 目标是每个传入请求对应的下游尝试减少，同时成功率提高。仅调用数下降，也可能只是更多请求立即失败。

**11 [B] / Incident Lead：** Proceed with that scoped mitigation after approval. I'll ask the inventory owner to report capacity and errors at our checkpoint in ten minutes.
> 批准后就执行这个限定范围的缓解措施。我会请库存负责人在十分钟后的检查点报告容量和错误情况。

**12 [C] / Developer：** I'll document the retry chain and propose a single coordinated policy afterward. We'll keep monitoring reservations for duplicates while validating customer recovery.
> 我会记录重试链路，并在后续提出统一协调的策略。验证客户侧恢复期间，我们会继续监测是否出现重复预留。

**可复用表达：**

- Let's choose one retry owner and set a bounded policy. — 我们先选定一个重试负责层，并设置有上限的策略。
- A lower call count alone could simply mean more requests fail immediately. — 仅调用数下降，也可能只是更多请求立即失败。

**落实动作：** 先按流程移除只读检查的重复代理重试；开发核实预留操作的幂等键传递，十分钟后结合调用放大比和成功率复核。
**练习重点：** 解释跨层重试放大，按操作类型评估幂等性，并用成功率验证缓解效果。

---

<a id="s048"></a>
## 048 功能开关紧急降级
**分类：** Application 应用与依赖  /  **英文词数：** 283
**音频：** [播放 MP3](audio/048.mp3)  /  [英文字幕](audio/048.srt)

**背景：** 新推荐功能导致结算变慢，产品希望暂时关闭推荐。团队需确认功能开关的降级行为和执行后的业务效果。

**角色：** A — Ops Engineer（运维工程师）；B — Product Owner（产品负责人）；C — Developer（开发工程师）

**01 [B] / Product Owner：** Can we disable the recommendation feature while keeping checkout available? Customers should still be able to buy items even if suggestions disappear.
> 我们能关闭推荐功能，同时保持结算可用吗？即使推荐不再显示，客户也应该仍能购买商品。

**02 [A] / Ops Engineer：** The feature flag supports that, but we need to confirm the fallback behavior. Does checkout continue normally when the recommendation response is absent?
> 功能开关支持这样做，但需要确认降级行为。没有推荐响应时，结算能正常继续吗？

**03 [C] / Developer：** The checkout page uses a default empty list when the flag is off. It doesn't need recommendation data to calculate prices or submit orders.
> 开关关闭后，结算页面会默认使用空列表。计算价格或提交订单都不需要推荐数据。

**04 [B] / Product Owner：** Was that path tested with the version now in production? A fallback from an earlier release might behave differently after the recent checkout changes.
> 这个链路用当前生产版本测试过吗？最近结算发生了变更，早期版本中的降级逻辑现在可能表现不同。

**05 [C] / Developer：** Yes, the release tests cover checkout with the flag disabled. I'll attach those results and repeat the key flow in our matching test environment.
> 测试过，发布测试覆盖了关闭开关后的结算。我会附上结果，并在版本匹配的测试环境中重新执行关键流程。

**06 [A] / Ops Engineer：** I'll check the flag's scope and propagation behavior. We need to know whether it applies to every region and how quickly instances refresh it.
> 我会检查开关范围和传播行为。我们需要知道它是否覆盖所有区域，以及实例多快会刷新配置。

**07 [B] / Product Owner：** Please include mobile checkout. It's acceptable to lose recommendations temporarily, but we shouldn't display a blank error panel or interrupt saved shopping carts.
> 请把移动端结算包括进去。暂时没有推荐可以接受，但不应出现空白错误面板，也不应影响已保存的购物车。

**08 [C] / Developer：** Mobile clients also accept an empty recommendation list. Existing carts remain stored separately. I'll verify those points before the change owner requests approval.
> 移动客户端也能接受空的推荐列表。现有购物车单独存储。我会在变更负责人申请批准前核实这些内容。

**09 [A] / Ops Engineer：** After approval, I'll switch the flag off and confirm the effective value on running instances. Then we'll test checkout across the affected channels.
> 批准后，我会关闭开关，并确认运行中实例的实际生效值。随后我们会测试受影响渠道的结算。

**10 [B] / Product Owner：** What measures will tell us the mitigation worked? Recommendation errors may disappear automatically when we stop calling that service, even if checkout still fails.
> 哪些指标能说明缓解有效？停止调用推荐服务后，它的错误可能自动消失，但结算仍可能失败。

**11 [A] / Ops Engineer：** We'll track successful order submissions and checkout latency, supported by test transactions. I'll share an initial comparison ten minutes after the flag change takes effect.
> 我们会跟踪订单提交成功情况和结算延迟，并用测试交易佐证。开关变更生效十分钟后，我会分享初步对比。

**12 [B] / Product Owner：** Agreed. I'll confirm the temporary product impact and own the decision to restore recommendations after the team validates a fix under normal change review.
> 同意。我会确认临时的产品影响；团队按正常变更审核验证修复后，由我负责决定何时恢复推荐。

**可复用表达：**

- Can we disable the recommendation feature while keeping checkout available? — 我们能关闭推荐功能，同时保持结算可用吗？
- Please include mobile checkout. — 请把移动端结算包括进去。

**落实动作：** 开发核实当前版本的网页与移动端降级逻辑；运维获批后关闭开关并验证生效，十分钟后报告结算指标，产品负责人决定后续恢复。
**练习重点：** 确认功能开关的适用范围，用核心交易指标衡量降级，而不是只看依赖错误消失。

---

<a id="s049"></a>
## 049 第三方接口故障
**分类：** Application 应用与依赖  /  **英文词数：** 277
**音频：** [播放 MP3](audio/049.mp3)  /  [英文字幕](audio/049.srt)

**背景：** 支付链路出现异常，业务希望判断是否由外部供应商造成。团队准备证据升级，同时继续验证我方请求和配置。

**角色：** A — Ops Engineer（运维工程师）；B — Business Lead（业务负责人）；C — Vendor Manager（供应商经理）

**01 [B] / Business Lead：** Is this our issue or the payment provider's issue? Support needs a clear explanation because customers are reporting failed payment attempts.
> 这是我们的问题，还是支付服务商的问题？支持团队需要清楚的解释，因为客户正在反馈支付尝试失败。

**02 [A] / Ops Engineer：** We see failures on the provider path, but we're validating both sides before assigning the cause. Their public status page still reports normal service.
> 我们看到服务商链路上有失败，但会先核实双方情况再判断原因。他们的公开状态页面仍显示服务正常。
