**03 [C] / Vendor Manager：** What evidence do you have so far? I can open a priority support case, but the provider will ask for specific examples.
> 目前有哪些证据？我可以发起优先支持工单，但服务商会要求具体案例。

**04 [A] / Ops Engineer：** Twelve percent of authorization requests returned gateway errors over fifteen minutes. Our request format, credentials, and network reachability checks passed for the sampled calls.
> 十五分钟内，百分之十二的授权请求返回网关错误。抽样调用的请求格式、凭据和网络可达性检查均已通过。

**05 [B] / Business Lead：** Does that mean those customers weren't charged? We need to avoid telling them to retry if the first attempt might still complete.
> 这是否意味着这些客户没有被扣款？如果第一次尝试仍可能完成，我们就不能让他们直接重试。

**06 [A] / Ops Engineer：** Not necessarily. Some responses are ambiguous, so we'll check transaction status using the provider's documented process and reconcile against our order records.
> 不一定。部分响应无法明确判断，所以我们会按服务商文档中的流程检查交易状态，并与我们的订单记录核对。

**07 [C] / Vendor Manager：** Please send sanitized request IDs, UTC timestamps, response codes, and the completed checks. I'll use the approved support channel and exclude payment details.
> 请提供脱敏请求ID、UTC时间戳、响应码和已完成的检查。我会使用获准的支持渠道，并排除支付详情。

**08 [A] / Ops Engineer：** I'll include examples from successful and failed calls, plus the affected region. That comparison may help them distinguish routing trouble from account configuration.
> 我会同时提供成功和失败调用的例子，以及受影响区域。这样的对比可能帮助他们区分路由故障和账户配置问题。

**09 [B] / Business Lead：** Can we route customers to our other payment method while the case is open? It's already available, but we need to understand its capacity.
> 工单处理期间，能否引导客户使用另一种支付方式？它已经上线，但我们需要了解它的容量。

**10 [A] / Ops Engineer：** I'll ask its service owner to assess headroom and confirm the approved customer flow. We shouldn't switch pending transactions without checking their current status.
> 我会请该服务负责人评估余量，并确认获准的客户流程。没有检查当前状态前，不应切换待确认交易。

**11 [C] / Vendor Manager：** I'll own the provider escalation and request an acknowledgement. I'll update the incident in fifteen minutes even if they haven't confirmed a cause or recovery estimate.
> 我负责向服务商升级并要求确认收到。即使对方尚未确认原因或预计恢复时间，我也会在十五分钟后更新故障。

**12 [B] / Business Lead：** I'll coordinate the customer message: some payments are failing, pending transactions are being checked, and the next update will follow that checkpoint.
> 我会协调客户通知：部分支付失败，待确认交易正在核查，下次更新会在这个检查点之后提供。

**可复用表达：**

- Some responses are ambiguous. — 部分响应无法明确判断。
- Please send sanitized request IDs, UTC timestamps, response codes, and the completed checks. — 请提供脱敏请求ID、UTC时间戳、响应码和已完成的检查。

**落实动作：** 运维整理脱敏证据并核对支付状态；供应商经理负责升级并在十五分钟后更新，业务协调客户通知和备用支付评估。
**练习重点：** 避免过早归因，说明支付状态不确定性，并清楚提出供应商升级所需证据。

---

<a id="s050"></a>
## 050 版本与接口兼容性
**分类：** Application 应用与依赖  /  **英文词数：** 281
**音频：** [播放 MP3](audio/050.mp3)  /  [英文字幕](audio/050.srt)

**背景：** 服务升级后，仍使用旧响应格式的客户端调用失败。团队需要盘点受影响版本，并在客户端升级期间提供兼容方案。

**角色：** A — Ops Engineer（运维工程师）；B — Developer（开发工程师）；C — Application Lead（应用负责人）

**01 [B] / Developer：** The new API passes our integration tests. Which clients are failing, and do they all receive the same response from the server?
> 新API通过了我们的集成测试。哪些客户端失败了，它们收到的服务器响应都一样吗？

**02 [A] / Ops Engineer：** Clients using the previous response format fail when parsing the result. The server returns success, so the usual server error dashboard misses this impact.
> 使用旧响应格式的客户端在解析结果时失败。服务器返回成功，所以常规服务器错误看板没有反映这部分影响。

**03 [C] / Application Lead：** How much production traffic still comes from those clients? Can we coordinate an immediate upgrade, or are some versions outside our direct control?
> 这些客户端仍占多少生产流量？我们能协调立即升级吗，还是有些版本不在我们直接控制之下？

**04 [A] / Ops Engineer：** Older clients account for eighteen percent of requests. Some belong to external partners, and their release schedules mean an immediate upgrade isn't realistic.
> 旧客户端占请求量的百分之十八。部分属于外部合作方，按他们的发布安排，立即升级并不现实。

**05 [B] / Developer：** We replaced a text status field with a structured object. Our tests covered current clients, but they didn't include the older partner contract.
> 我们把一个文本状态字段替换成了结构化对象。测试覆盖了当前客户端，但没有包括合作方旧版接口契约。

**06 [C] / Application Lead：** Then we need a compatible mitigation while those clients are updated. Is restoring the old field possible without breaking clients already using the new format?
> 那么在客户端更新期间，我们需要兼容的缓解方案。能否恢复旧字段，同时不破坏已经使用新格式的客户端？

**07 [B] / Developer：** We can serve the previous format through the existing versioned endpoint. I'll verify that its routing and response behavior still match the documented contract.
> 我们可以通过现有的版本化接口提供旧格式。我会核实它的路由和响应行为仍然符合文档中的契约。

**08 [A] / Ops Engineer：** Please check how callers select that endpoint. We should avoid guessing client versions from incomplete headers or sending partners to an untested fallback.
> 请检查调用方如何选择该接口。我们应避免根据不完整的请求头猜测客户端版本，也不能让合作方使用未经测试的降级路径。

**09 [C] / Application Lead：** I'll have the integration owner confirm the affected client list and contact owners through the established process. What validation do you need before rollout?
> 我会让集成负责人确认受影响客户端清单，并按现有流程联系各负责人。上线前需要哪些验证？

**10 [B] / Developer：** Contract tests for both response versions, plus sample calls from an affected partner client. We also need to check that both paths preserve authorization behavior.
> 两种响应版本的契约测试，以及受影响合作方客户端的样本调用。还需要检查两条链路都保持原有授权行为。

**11 [A] / Ops Engineer：** I'll monitor client completion metrics as well as server responses. Let's review the test evidence in twenty minutes before approving the compatibility change.
> 我会同时监测客户端完成情况和服务器响应。二十分钟后先评审测试证据，再批准兼容性变更。

**12 [C] / Application Lead：** Agreed. I'll own the migration schedule and require confirmed client adoption before we set a retirement date for the older API version.
> 同意。我负责迁移计划，并要求先确认客户端已采用新版本，再设定旧API版本的停用日期。

**可复用表达：**

- Older clients account for eighteen percent of requests. — 旧客户端占请求量的百分之十八。
- What validation do you need before rollout? — 上线前需要哪些验证？

**落实动作：** 开发验证旧版接口及双版本契约；运维监测客户端完成率，二十分钟后评审证据，应用负责人负责合作方迁移和旧版退役条件。
**练习重点：** 解释服务器成功与客户端成功的区别，提出有依据的兼容措施和迁移责任。

---

<a id="s051"></a>
## 051 数据库慢查询
**分类：** 数据库、中间件与批处理  /  **英文词数：** 277
**音频：** [播放 MP3](audio/051.mp3)  /  [英文字幕](audio/051.srt)

**背景：** 数据库延迟在发布后明显增加，应用负责人提出扩容。运维和数据库管理员利用查询统计及执行计划寻找针对性改进。

**角色：** A — Ops Engineer（运维工程师）；B — Application Lead（应用负责人）；C — Database Administrator（数据库管理员）

**01 [B] / Application Lead：** Database response time has doubled since the release. Should we move to a larger instance before the next peak traffic period begins?
> 发布后数据库响应时间翻倍。下一个流量高峰到来前，我们是否应该换用更大的实例？

**02 [A] / Ops Engineer：** A small set of queries accounts for most of the increase. We're reviewing their execution plans before deciding whether more resources would help.
> 少量查询造成了大部分增幅。决定增加资源是否有帮助前，我们正在检查它们的执行计划。

**03 [C] / Database Administrator：** One customer search query now scans two million rows instead of twenty thousand. The release added a filter that changed the access plan.
> 一个客户搜索查询现在扫描两百万行，而之前是两万行。本次发布增加了一个筛选条件，改变了数据访问计划。

**04 [B] / Application Lead：** Is that conclusion based on a production sample or a test run? We should make sure the slower query explains the customer impact.
> 这个结论基于生产样本还是测试运行？我们应该确认较慢的查询确实能够解释客户侧影响。

**05 [A] / Ops Engineer：** Production traces link it to the affected search requests. Other database operations remain near baseline, and overall request volume hasn't changed significantly.
> 生产调用链显示它对应受影响的搜索请求。其他数据库操作仍接近基线，整体请求量没有明显变化。

**06 [C] / Database Administrator：** I'll compare the old and new plans using representative parameters in the test environment. Data distribution matters, so one fast sample won't be sufficient.
> 我会在测试环境使用有代表性的参数，对比新旧执行计划。数据分布很重要，所以一个快速样本还不够。

**07 [B] / Application Lead：** Could a new index solve this faster than changing the application query? We need a practical mitigation as well as a longer-term fix.
> 增加索引是否比修改应用查询更快解决问题？我们既需要切实可行的缓解措施，也需要长期修复。

**08 [C] / Database Administrator：** Possibly, but index creation has storage, locking, and write costs. I'll assess those alongside a query rewrite instead of assuming an index is harmless.
> 有可能，但建立索引有存储、锁和写入方面的成本。我会把这些与查询改写一起评估，不能假定增加索引没有代价。

**09 [A] / Ops Engineer：** We can temporarily limit the optional bulk search feature through its approved control. That should reduce pressure while preserving individual account lookups.
> 我们可以通过获准的控制措施，临时限制可选的批量搜索功能。这样应能减轻压力，同时保留单个账户查询。

**10 [B] / Application Lead：** I'll confirm that tradeoff with the business owner. What evidence will we review before choosing and approving the targeted database or application change?
> 我会与业务负责人确认这一取舍。选择并批准针对性的数据库或应用变更前，我们会评审哪些证据？

**11 [C] / Database Administrator：** Compare query latency, rows examined, write performance, and resource use across representative cases. I'll bring the results and deployment risks to our thirty-minute checkpoint.
> 比较有代表性场景下的查询延迟、扫描行数、写入性能和资源使用。我会在三十分钟后的检查点提供结果和发布风险。

**12 [A] / Ops Engineer：** I'll track search completion and database health during the mitigation. After the fix, we'll compare against the same baseline before considering the issue resolved.
> 缓解期间，我会跟踪搜索完成情况和数据库健康状态。修复后，我们会对照相同基线，再决定问题是否解决。

**可复用表达：**

- A small set of queries accounts for most of the increase. — 少量查询造成了大部分增幅。
- Data distribution matters, so one fast sample won't be sufficient. — 数据分布很重要，所以一个快速样本还不够。

**落实动作：** 运维监测并协调临时限制批量搜索；数据库管理员比较执行计划及修复成本，三十分钟后带证据评审，业务确认临时取舍。
**练习重点：** 用查询证据回应扩容建议，说明索引和查询改写的成本，并明确验证基线。

---

<a id="s052"></a>
## 052 锁等待与长事务
**分类：** 数据库、中间件与批处理  /  **英文词数：** 285
**音频：** [播放 MP3](audio/052.mp3)  /  [英文字幕](audio/052.srt)

**背景：** 数据库更新因锁等待阻塞，团队考虑终止阻塞会话。需要先确认长事务归属和回滚代价，再决定恢复步骤。

**角色：** A — Ops Engineer（运维工程师）；B — Developer（开发工程师）；C — Database Administrator（数据库管理员）

**01 [B] / Developer：** Several account updates have been waiting for six minutes. Can we terminate the blocking session now and let those requests move forward?
> 几个账户更新已经等待六分钟。我们能现在终止阻塞会话，让这些请求继续处理吗？

**02 [A] / Ops Engineer：** The database administrator identified a long-running transaction at the head of the blocking chain. We need to assess its rollback impact before terminating it.
> 数据库管理员找到了阻塞链头部的长事务。终止前，我们需要评估它的回滚影响。

**03 [C] / Database Administrator：** It belongs to the reconciliation job and has modified about four hundred thousand rows. Termination could trigger a lengthy rollback while locks remain held.
> 它属于对账任务，已经修改约四十万行。终止可能触发耗时回滚，期间锁可能继续被持有。

**04 [B] / Developer：** So killing the session wouldn't necessarily release everything immediately. Do we know whether the job is still making progress or waiting on something else?
> 所以终止会话不一定马上释放所有资源。我们知道任务仍在推进，还是在等待其他东西吗？

**05 [C] / Database Administrator：** Its processed row count is increasing, and it isn't waiting on another lock. However, the current transaction is much larger than the job's usual batches.
> 已处理行数仍在增加，而且它没有等待其他锁。不过，当前事务明显大于这个任务通常的批次。

**06 [A] / Ops Engineer：** I'll contact the job owner to understand the batch change and expected remaining work. We shouldn't invent a completion time from one progress sample.
> 我会联系任务负责人，了解批次变更和预计剩余工作。我们不应根据单次进度样本编造完成时间。

**07 [B] / Developer：** Can we pause new reconciliation work without interrupting the active transaction? That might keep the problem from repeating while we assess the options.
> 能否暂停新的对账处理，而不中断当前事务？这样可以在评估方案期间避免问题重复发生。

**08 [C] / Database Administrator：** Yes, the scheduler supports pausing future batches. The owner must confirm that control, and we should also limit automatic retries from blocked application requests.
> 可以，调度器支持暂停后续批次。负责人必须确认该控制方式，我们还应该限制被阻塞应用请求的自动重试。

**09 [A] / Ops Engineer：** I'll give the incident lead two options: allow monitored completion or authorize termination with a potentially long rollback. We'll include current business impact for each.
> 我会向故障负责人提供两个方案：持续监控直至完成，或批准终止并承担可能长时间回滚的代价。每个方案都附上当前业务影响。

**10 [B] / Developer：** I'll check whether the blocked requests can expire safely and confirm that customers won't receive duplicate updates when they try again later.
> 我会检查被阻塞请求是否能安全超时，并确认客户稍后重试时不会产生重复更新。

**11 [C] / Database Administrator：** I'll collect progress and lock measurements for the next five minutes. If termination is chosen, I'll execute it under approval and monitor rollback explicitly.
> 我会在接下来五分钟收集进度和锁指标。如果决定终止，我会在批准后执行，并专门监控回滚。

**12 [A] / Ops Engineer：** Let's review those measurements with the job owner and incident lead at that checkpoint. Recovery requires completed account updates, not just a disappeared session.
> 我们在那个检查点与任务负责人和故障负责人一起评审数据。恢复需要账户更新真正完成，不能只看会话消失。

**可复用表达：**

- We need to assess its rollback impact before terminating it. — 终止前，我们需要评估它的回滚影响。
- Recovery requires completed account updates, not just a disappeared session. — 恢复需要账户更新真正完成，不能只看会话消失。

**落实动作：** 运维联系对账负责人并向故障负责人列出选择；开发检查请求超时与重试安全，数据库管理员五分钟内采集进度，终止须获批并监控回滚。
**练习重点：** 解释终止长事务不等于立即恢复，结合阻塞链、业务影响和回滚代价比较方案。

---

<a id="s053"></a>
## 053 数据库复制延迟
**分类：** 数据库、中间件与批处理  /  **英文词数：** 280
**音频：** [播放 MP3](audio/053.mp3)  /  [英文字幕](audio/053.srt)

**背景：** 区域用户修改账户资料后仍看到旧值。团队确认读副本延迟，并评估将部分读取临时转到主库的风险。

**角色：** A — Ops Engineer（运维工程师）；B — Regional Lead（区域负责人）；C — Database Administrator（数据库管理员）

**01 [B] / Regional Lead：** Users save their account changes successfully, but some still see the old details afterward. Are the writes failing, or are we showing outdated data?
> 用户成功保存账户修改后，有些人仍然看到旧资料。这是写入失败，还是我们显示了过时数据？

**02 [A] / Ops Engineer：** Sample writes are present on the primary. The regional read replica is ninety seconds behind, and the affected reads appear to use that replica.
> 抽样写入已存在于主库。区域读副本落后九十秒，受影响的读取似乎都使用该副本。

**03 [C] / Database Administrator：** I'll confirm the routing with request traces and compare replication positions. We should distinguish replication delay from a separate application cache problem.
> 我会通过请求调用链确认路由，并比较复制位点。我们应该把复制延迟与独立的应用缓存问题区分开。

**04 [B] / Regional Lead：** Could we send every regional read to the primary until the replica catches up? The inconsistency is confusing customers and increasing support contacts.
> 能否在副本追上之前，把所有区域读取发到主库？这种不一致让客户困惑，也增加了支持咨询。

**05 [A] / Ops Engineer：** We'll assess capacity and consistency requirements before changing read routing. The primary also handles writes, so an unrestricted switch could affect more customers.
> 调整读路由前，我们会评估容量和一致性要求。主库还处理写入，因此不受限制地切换可能影响更多客户。

**06 [C] / Database Administrator：** The primary has some headroom, but not enough for all regional reporting traffic. Account confirmation reads are small and could be considered separately.
> 主库有一些余量，但不足以承接所有区域报表流量。账户确认读取量小，可以单独考虑。

**07 [B] / Regional Lead：** Then prioritize the screen immediately after a customer saves changes. Historical reports can tolerate delayed data if we show the data timestamp clearly.
> 那就优先处理客户保存修改后的页面。历史报表可以容忍延迟数据，前提是清楚显示数据时间戳。

**08 [A] / Ops Engineer：** I'll ask the application owner to verify that we can route those confirmation reads independently. We also need to monitor cross-region latency.
> 我会请应用负责人核实，能否独立路由这些确认读取。我们还需要监控跨区域延迟。

**09 [C] / Database Administrator：** The replica is applying a large backlog after a storage slowdown. I'll investigate that separately and watch whether the lag is shrinking or growing.
> 存储变慢后，副本正在应用大量积压。我会单独调查，并观察延迟是在缩小还是扩大。

**10 [B] / Regional Lead：** Please tell support that saved changes are present in the checked examples. Avoid promising an exact catch-up time until you have a stable processing trend.
> 请告诉支持团队，已检查案例中的修改确实已保存。在处理趋势稳定之前，避免承诺确切的追平时间。

**11 [A] / Ops Engineer：** After the routing change is approved, I'll verify saved details through the user flow and track primary load. We'll review results in fifteen minutes.
> 读路由变更获批后，我会通过用户流程验证已保存资料，并跟踪主库负载。十五分钟后评审结果。

**12 [C] / Database Administrator：** I'll own replica recovery and define a lag threshold with the application owner before returning reads. We'll confirm correctness as well as capacity.
> 我负责副本恢复，并在把读取切回前，与应用负责人确定延迟阈值。我们既要确认数据正确，也要确认容量。

**可复用表达：**

- We should distinguish replication delay from a separate application cache problem. — 我们应该把复制延迟与独立的应用缓存问题区分开。
- We also need to monitor cross-region latency. — 我们还需要监控跨区域延迟。

**落实动作：** 数据库管理员核实复制状态并恢复副本；运维评估仅将保存后确认读取转到主库，获批后监测负载和正确性，十五分钟后复核。
**练习重点：** 澄清写入成功与读取陈旧，按一致性需求限定流量切换并评估主库容量。

---

<a id="s054"></a>
## 054 数据库主备切换
**分类：** 数据库、中间件与批处理  /  **英文词数：** 283
**音频：** [播放 MP3](audio/054.mp3)  /  [英文字幕](audio/054.srt)

**背景：** 2026年11月12日，数据库主库异常，团队准备提升备库。切换前需隔离旧主库、说明潜在数据风险，并明确应用恢复验证。

**角色：** A — Ops Engineer（运维工程师）；B — Incident Lead（故障负责人）；C — Database Administrator（数据库管理员）

**01 [B] / Incident Lead：** The primary stopped responding at 10:15 UTC on November 12, 2026. Is the standby ready, and what must we check before authorizing failover?
> 主库在2026年11月12日10:15 UTC停止响应。备库准备好了吗，批准切换前还必须检查什么？

**02 [C] / Database Administrator：** I need to confirm replication state and fence the old primary. We must prevent it from accepting writes if it becomes reachable again.
> 我需要确认复制状态并隔离旧主库。必须防止它重新可达后继续接受写入。

**03 [A] / Ops Engineer：** I'll prepare connection routing and identify the application checks. I'll wait for your promotion confirmation before sending any write traffic to the standby.
> 我会准备连接路由并确定应用检查项。收到你确认备库提升完成后，我才会把写流量发过去。

**04 [B] / Incident Lead：** Please explain potential data loss against the approved objectives. Our service allows a thirty-second recovery point gap and targets recovery within fifteen minutes.
> 请对照已批准的目标说明潜在数据丢失。该服务允许三十秒的恢复点差距，目标是在十五分钟内恢复。

**05 [C] / Database Administrator：** The standby has applied commits through 10:14:42 UTC. The latest confirmed primary commit is at 10:15:00, leaving an eighteen-second interval requiring reconciliation.
> 备库已应用截至10:14:42 UTC的提交。最后确认的主库提交在10:15:00，中间有十八秒的时间区间需要核对。

**06 [B] / Incident Lead：** Eighteen seconds is the observed replication gap, but it doesn't bound all possible loss. Can you establish the final writable point before approval?
> 十八秒是观察到的复制差距，但不能据此界定所有潜在丢失。批准前，你能确认最后可写时间点吗？

**07 [C] / Database Administrator：** Storage fencing completed at 10:15:00 UTC. Durable commit records show no later writes, so that eighteen-second interval bounds the current data exposure.
> 存储隔离在10:15:00 UTC完成。持久化提交记录显示之后没有写入，因此当前数据风险范围限定在这十八秒内。

**08 [A] / Ops Engineer：** The business owner can reconcile affected orders using durable request records and partner confirmations. I'll preserve those references without retrying uncertain transactions automatically.
> 业务负责人可以利用持久化请求记录和合作方确认信息核对受影响订单。我会保留这些关联信息，不自动重试状态不明确的事务。

**09 [C] / Database Administrator：** The infrastructure team confirmed the fence blocks every old-primary write path. Replication checks are complete, and I'm ready to promote once approval is recorded.
> 基础设施团队确认，隔离阻断了旧主库的所有写入路径。复制检查已完成，批准记录完成后我就可以提升备库。

**10 [B] / Incident Lead：** I approve the failover with that documented data risk. Keep the recovery timer running; meeting the time target depends on application recovery, too.
> 我批准在记录该数据风险的前提下切换。继续计算恢复耗时；达到时间目标也取决于应用是否恢复。

**11 [A] / Ops Engineer：** After promotion, I'll refresh connections gradually and test account reads and order writes. We'll verify persistence, error rates, and business reconciliation before declaring recovery.
> 提升后，我会逐步刷新连接，并测试账户读取和订单写入。宣布恢复前，要验证数据持久性、错误率和业务核对情况。

**12 [C] / Database Administrator：** I'll report database status at 10:20 UTC and keep the old primary isolated. Reintroducing it will require a separate, reviewed resynchronization plan.
> 我会在10:20 UTC报告数据库状态，并继续隔离旧主库。让它重新加入需要单独审核重新同步方案。

**可复用表达：**

- We must prevent it from accepting writes if it becomes reachable again. — 必须防止它重新可达后继续接受写入。
- Keep the recovery timer running. — 继续计算恢复耗时。

**落实动作：** 数据库管理员确认隔离后按批准提升备库，并在10:20 UTC更新；运维逐步恢复连接并验证业务，业务负责人核对十八秒潜在风险区间内的交易。
**练习重点：** 用恢复时间和恢复点目标讨论切换，区分潜在暴露区间与已确认丢失，并明确旧主库隔离。

---

<a id="s055"></a>
## 055 备份成功与恢复验证
**分类：** 数据库、中间件与批处理  /  **英文词数：** 290
**音频：** [播放 MP3](audio/055.mp3)  /  [英文字幕](audio/055.srt)

**背景：** 备份看板显示任务全部成功，经理希望确认服务可恢复。团队安排隔离恢复演练，以实测结果验证恢复时间和数据目标。

**角色：** A — Ops Engineer（运维工程师）；B — Manager（经理）；C — Database Administrator（数据库管理员）

**01 [B] / Manager：** All backup jobs are green this month. Can we tell the service owner that recovery is guaranteed within the documented time and data objectives?
> 本月所有备份任务都是绿色。我们能告诉服务负责人，一定能在文档规定的时间和数据目标内恢复吗？

**02 [A] / Ops Engineer：** A successful backup does not by itself prove recoverability. We still need a restore test, usable recovery credentials, and validation by the application team.
> 仅有备份成功，并不能证明可恢复。我们仍需要恢复测试、可用的恢复凭据，以及应用团队的验证。

**03 [C] / Database Administrator：** I'll restore a selected backup and its transaction logs into an isolated environment. The test will use approved access and protect the production data.
> 我会把选定备份及其事务日志恢复到隔离环境。测试会使用获准的访问方式，并保护生产数据。

**04 [B] / Manager：** Our objectives are one hour for service recovery and no more than five minutes of lost data. How will you measure those separately?
> 我们的目标是在一小时内恢复服务，数据丢失不超过五分钟。你们会如何分别衡量这两个目标？

**05 [A] / Ops Engineer：** We'll record when recovery is requested and when the application is usable. Separately, we'll identify the latest recovered transaction and compare its timestamp with the simulated incident.
> 我们会记录提出恢复请求的时间和应用可用的时间。另外，识别最新恢复的事务，并将其时间戳与模拟故障时间比较。

**06 [C] / Database Administrator：** For this exercise, the simulated incident is at 04:00 UTC on November 12, 2026. I'll document which backup and logs support that point.
> 本次演练的模拟故障时间是2026年11月12日04:00 UTC。我会记录支持恢复到该时间点的备份和日志。

**07 [B] / Manager：** Please include access approval and environment preparation in the recovery timeline. Starting the clock only when data copying begins would hide important delays.
> 请把访问批准和环境准备计入恢复时间线。只从开始复制数据时计时，会掩盖重要延迟。

**08 [A] / Ops Engineer：** Agreed. We'll use the service's documented measurement boundaries and show each stage. Any pre-provisioned resources will be listed so the result has clear limits.
> 同意。我们会使用服务文档规定的计量边界，并展示各个阶段。所有预先准备的资源都会列出，让结果的适用范围清楚。

**09 [C] / Database Administrator：** After restoration, I'll check database integrity and the latest committed records. A running database alone won't prove that all required application data is available.
> 恢复后，我会检查数据库完整性和最新提交记录。仅数据库能运行，并不能证明所有必需的应用数据都可用。

**10 [B] / Manager：** I'll arrange an application tester to verify login, order lookup, and a controlled transaction. Please avoid sending notifications or payments from the test environment.
> 我会安排应用测试人员验证登录、订单查询和受控交易。请避免从测试环境发送通知或支付。

**11 [A] / Ops Engineer：** External side effects will be isolated in the test setup. I'll publish the measured recovery time, recovered data point, and functional results after the exercise.
> 测试配置会隔离外部副作用。演练后，我会发布实测恢复时间、恢复的数据时间点和功能结果。

**12 [B] / Manager：** Let's review that evidence tomorrow before updating the assurance statement. If either objective is missed, assign an improvement owner and schedule another measured test.
> 我们明天先评审这些证据，再更新保障说明。任一目标未达到，都要指定改进负责人并安排再次实测。

**可复用表达：**

- A successful backup does not by itself prove recoverability. — 仅有备份成功，并不能证明可恢复。
- Please include access approval and environment preparation in the recovery timeline. — 请把访问批准和环境准备计入恢复时间线。

**落实动作：** 数据库管理员实施隔离恢复，运维按规定边界记录全过程耗时和恢复数据点；经理安排业务功能验证，次日评审目标达成证据。
**练习重点：** 把备份成功、恢复成功和业务可用区分开，清楚衡量恢复时间与可恢复数据点。

---

<a id="s056"></a>
## 056 Redis缓存命中率下降
**分类：** 数据库、中间件与批处理  /  **英文词数：** 287
**音频：** [播放 MP3](audio/056.mp3)  /  [英文字幕](audio/056.srt)

**背景：** Redis重启后缓存命中率降低，数据库承受额外查询。团队讨论受控预热，并避免再次清空缓存或把压力全部推给数据库。

**角色：** A — Ops Engineer（运维工程师）；B — Developer（开发工程师）；C — Application Lead（应用负责人）

**01 [B] / Developer：** Database traffic increased sharply after the Redis restart, and customers report slower page loads. Should we clear the cache again in case entries are inconsistent?
> Redis重启后数据库流量明显上升，客户反馈页面变慢。是否应该再次清空缓存，以防缓存项不一致？

**02 [A] / Ops Engineer：** The hit rate fell from ninety-five percent to fifteen percent because the cache is cold. Another flush could prolong the database load spike.
> 命中率从百分之九十五降到百分之十五，是因为缓存尚未预热。再次清空可能延长数据库负载高峰。

**03 [C] / Application Lead：** Do we have evidence of incorrect values, or is this mainly missing entries? We shouldn't treat a performance problem as data corruption without checking.
> 我们有错误值的证据吗，还是主要缺少缓存项？在检查前，不应把性能问题当成数据损坏。

**04 [A] / Ops Engineer：** Sampled values match the database, and misses account for most additional queries. I'll keep checking correctness while we investigate why warming is slow.
> 抽样值与数据库一致，大部分新增查询来自缓存未命中。我会继续检查正确性，同时调查预热为何缓慢。

**05 [B] / Developer：** Our usual warm-up job loads popular product records, but it's currently disabled. Can we start it now and let customer requests fill the rest?
> 常规预热任务会加载热门商品记录，但目前被停用了。我们能现在启动它，让客户请求补齐其余数据吗？

**06 [A] / Ops Engineer：** Let's use the approved warm-up method with a rate limit. The database is already busy, so unrestricted warming could make customer response times worse.
> 我们使用获准的预热方式，并设置速率限制。数据库已经很忙，无限制预热可能让客户响应时间更差。

**07 [C] / Application Lead：** Please define a stop condition before starting. We also need to make sure the job doesn't cache account-specific responses under shared product keys.
> 请在开始前定义停止条件。我们还需要确保该任务不会把账户专属响应缓存到共享商品键下面。

**08 [B] / Developer：** The job only loads public product data. I'll verify the key namespace, expiration settings, and per-key request coordination to avoid simultaneous reloads of popular entries.
> 任务只加载公开商品数据。我会核实键命名空间、过期设置和单键请求协调机制，避免热门缓存项被同时重复加载。

**09 [A] / Ops Engineer：** We'll start below the approved warm-up rate and pause if database latency crosses our operating threshold. I'll monitor hit rate, evictions, and successful page requests.
> 我们会从低于获准预热速率的水平开始，数据库延迟超过运行阈值就暂停。我会监测命中率、淘汰量和页面请求成功情况。

**10 [C] / Application Lead：** Could short expiration times keep the cache cold even after loading? Please compare those settings with the previous healthy configuration before increasing the warm-up rate.
> 过短的过期时间会不会让缓存加载后仍持续处于冷状态？提高预热速率前，请与之前的健康配置比较。

**11 [B] / Developer：** I'll compare those settings and inspect memory headroom. We won't extend expiration blindly because product data has freshness requirements agreed with the business.
> 我会比较这些设置并检查内存余量。我们不会盲目延长过期时间，因为商品数据有与业务约定的新鲜度要求。

**12 [A] / Ops Engineer：** I'll post a trend comparison in ten minutes. Recovery requires stable database latency and normal page performance, alongside an improving cache hit rate.
> 我会在十分钟后发布趋势对比。恢复不仅需要缓存命中率改善，还需要数据库延迟稳定、页面性能正常。

**可复用表达：**

- Another flush could prolong the database load spike. — 再次清空可能延长数据库负载高峰。
- Please define a stop condition before starting. — 请在开始前定义停止条件。

**落实动作：** 开发核实预热数据范围、键和过期设置；运维按获准速率预热并设暂停阈值，十分钟后以缓存、数据库和页面指标共同复核。
**练习重点：** 说明冷缓存造成的负载，提出预热限速和停止条件，同时保护数据正确性。

---

<a id="s057"></a>
## 057 消息队列积压
**分类：** 数据库、中间件与批处理  /  **英文词数：** 289
**音频：** [播放 MP3](audio/057.mp3)  /  [英文字幕](audio/057.srt)

**背景：** 消息生产速率正常，但消费吞吐下降，积压持续增加。团队发现下游限流，需要限制压力并给出有条件的清理时间估算。

**角色：** A — Ops Engineer（运维工程师）；B — Service Owner（服务负责人）；C — Developer（开发工程师）

**01 [B] / Service Owner：** Queue depth keeps growing, and delayed notifications are becoming visible to customers. Can we add more consumers to catch up before the afternoon peak?
> 队列持续增长，客户已经发现通知延迟。能否增加消费者，在下午高峰前追上进度？

**02 [A] / Ops Engineer：** We have eighteen thousand queued messages. Producers add six hundred per minute, while consumers currently complete only four hundred fifty per minute.
> 目前有一万八千条消息积压。生产者每分钟新增六百条，消费者目前每分钟只完成四百五十条。

**03 [C] / Developer：** The downstream notification API is returning rate-limit errors. Retries consume part of our allocation, so more consumers could increase calls without improving successful processing.
> 下游通知API正在返回限流错误。重试占用了部分调用配额，因此增加消费者可能增加调用量，却无法提高成功处理量。

**04 [B] / Service Owner：** What capacity have we agreed with that API owner? We should work from the actual limit rather than increasing concurrency until something breaks.
> 我们与该API负责人约定了多少容量？我们应该以实际限制为依据，而不能一直加并发，直到某处出问题。

**05 [A] / Ops Engineer：** Our allocation is one thousand calls per minute. I'll confirm that it still applies and check whether all consumer instances share the same rate control.
> 我们的配额是每分钟一千次调用。我会确认它仍然适用，并检查所有消费者实例是否共用同一限速控制。

**06 [C] / Developer：** They currently enforce limits independently, which allows the total to exceed our allocation. I can apply the reviewed shared limit and bounded retry backoff.
> 它们目前各自限速，导致总量可能超过配额。我可以实施已审核的共享限速和有上限的重试退避。

**07 [B] / Service Owner：** Will delayed messages still be useful when they arrive? Some notifications refer to short-lived offers, so draining every message might produce misleading customer messages.
> 延迟消息到达时仍有用吗？部分通知涉及短期优惠，因此把所有消息都处理出去，可能会向客户发送误导信息。

**08 [A] / Ops Engineer：** We'll check message age and expiry rules with the product owner. Expired messages need their approved handling path, while valid messages should retain required ordering.
> 我们会与产品负责人核实消息等待时间和过期规则。过期消息需要走获准的处理路径，有效消息则应保持必要顺序。

**09 [C] / Developer：** After the rate fix, suppose we sustain nine hundred successful messages per minute. With six hundred arriving, the backlog would shrink by three hundred per minute.
> 限速修正后，假设能持续每分钟成功处理九百条。新增六百条时，积压就会每分钟减少三百条。

**10 [B] / Service Owner：** That suggests about an hour for eighteen thousand messages, provided those rates hold. Please present it as a conditional estimate rather than a guaranteed completion time.
> 这样看来，只要速率保持，一万八千条大约需要一小时。请把它表述为有条件的估算，而不是保证完成时间。

**11 [A] / Ops Engineer：** I'll measure actual throughput and oldest-message age after the approved change. We'll refresh the estimate at our fifteen-minute checkpoint and validate delivered notifications.
> 获准变更后，我会测量实际吞吐和最旧消息的等待时间。十五分钟后的检查点会更新估算，并验证通知实际送达。

**12 [B] / Service Owner：** I'll coordinate the product decision on expired messages and notify support of the delay. Keep concurrency unchanged until downstream headroom supports another increase.
> 我会协调过期消息的产品决策，并告知支持团队当前延迟。下游余量支持进一步增加之前，保持并发不变。

**可复用表达：**

- Please present it as a conditional estimate rather than a guaranteed completion time. — 请把它表述为有条件的估算，而不是保证完成时间。
- Retries consume part of our allocation. — 重试占用了部分调用配额。

**落实动作：** 开发获批后实施共享限速和退避；运维按实际净消化速率计算积压时间，十五分钟后更新，服务负责人协调过期消息处理。
**练习重点：** 比较生产、消费和净消化速率，解释下游限流，并给出带条件的时间估算。

---

<a id="s058"></a>
## 058 重复消息与幂等性
**分类：** 数据库、中间件与批处理  /  **英文词数：** 289
**音频：** [播放 MP3](audio/058.mp3)  /  [英文字幕](audio/058.srt)

**背景：** 消费者重试后出现重复订单记录，业务担心重复扣款。团队需要先控制新增重复，再核实幂等缺陷和对账方案。

**角色：** A — Ops Engineer（运维工程师）；B — Business Lead（业务负责人）；C — Developer（开发工程师）

**01 [B] / Business Lead：** Some orders appear twice in the business dashboard. Did the queue duplicate messages, and should we cancel every record that looks like a repeat?
> 业务看板里有些订单出现两次。是队列重复消息了吗？我们是否应该取消所有看起来重复的记录？

**02 [A] / Ops Engineer：** Delivery retries are possible, but a repeated delivery shouldn't create another order if deduplication works. We're comparing message IDs, business keys, and database records.
> 消息可能被重新投递，但如果去重正常，重复投递不应创建另一笔订单。我们正在比较消息ID、业务键和数据库记录。

**03 [C] / Developer：** I found one processing path that writes the order before saving the idempotency result. A crash between those steps can leave the retry unprotected.
> 我发现一条处理链路会先写订单，再保存幂等处理结果。两步之间如果崩溃，重试就可能失去保护。

**04 [B] / Business Lead：** Can we stop that path while keeping other orders moving? We also need to know whether duplicate records caused duplicate payments or shipments.
> 能否停止这条链路，同时让其他订单继续处理？我们还需要知道重复记录是否造成重复支付或发货。

**05 [A] / Ops Engineer：** I'll request approval to pause the affected consumer group and hold its messages durably. Other groups use a separate path, which we'll verify before leaving them running.
> 我会申请批准，暂停受影响的消费者组，并持久保留其消息。其他组使用独立链路，继续运行前我们会核实这一点。

**06 [C] / Developer：** I'll preserve the failing examples and fix the write boundary. The idempotency result and business write need a consistent atomic operation for this path.
> 我会保留失败案例，并修复写入边界。这条链路的幂等结果和业务写入，需要在一个一致的原子操作中完成。

**07 [B] / Business Lead：** How many duplicates have we confirmed so far? Please separate confirmed duplicate orders from repeated messages that were handled correctly without extra records.
> 目前确认了多少重复？请把已确认的重复订单，与已正确处理、没有新增记录的重复消息区分开。

**08 [A] / Ops Engineer：** We've confirmed fourteen duplicate order pairs in the sampled window. The full affected period and any payment or fulfillment impact are still under investigation.
> 在抽样时间窗口内，我们已经确认十四组重复订单。完整受影响时间段，以及支付或履约影响仍在调查中。

**09 [B] / Business Lead：** I'll assign finance and fulfillment owners to reconcile those pairs. Don't delete or cancel records automatically; we need to identify the valid order and downstream actions.
> 我会指定财务和履约负责人核对这些订单。不要自动删除或取消记录；我们需要确认有效订单及其下游处理。

**10 [C] / Developer：** Before restarting, I'll test duplicate delivery, a crash after the write, and recovery using the same business key. The fix also needs normal review.
> 重启前，我会测试重复投递、写入后崩溃，以及使用相同业务键恢复处理。修复也需要正常审核。

**11 [A] / Ops Engineer：** I'll keep replay disabled until the fix and reconciliation plan are approved. We'll review containment status and the affected-record list in twenty minutes.
> 修复和对账方案批准前，我会保持重放关闭。二十分钟后，我们评审控制情况和受影响记录清单。

**12 [B] / Business Lead：** Agreed. I'll own the business corrections and customer communication. We can resume controlled processing once technical validation and the record-handling decisions are complete.
> 同意。我负责业务纠正和客户沟通。技术验证以及记录处理决策完成后，可以恢复受控处理。

**可复用表达：**

- Please separate confirmed duplicate orders from repeated messages that were handled correctly without extra records. — 请把已确认的重复订单与已正确处理、未新增记录的重复消息区分开。
- Don't delete or cancel records automatically. — 不要自动删除或取消记录。

**落实动作：** 运维申请暂停受影响消费者并保留消息；开发修复并测试原子幂等处理，业务安排财务和履约对账，二十分钟后评审，批准前不重放。
**练习重点：** 区分重复投递与重复业务结果，明确控制、修复、对账和恢复处理的先后条件。

---

<a id="s059"></a>
## 059 死信队列消息处理
**分类：** 数据库、中间件与批处理  /  **英文词数：** 287
**音频：** [播放 MP3](audio/059.mp3)  /  [英文字幕](audio/059.srt)

**背景：** 开发修复缺陷后希望重放死信队列。团队先按失败原因和业务状态分类，再进行小批受控重放与对账。

**角色：** A — Ops Engineer（运维工程师）；B — Developer（开发工程师）；C — Business Operations Lead（业务运营负责人）

**01 [B] / Developer：** The parsing bug is fixed and deployed. Can we replay the entire dead-letter queue now? There are about eight thousand messages waiting.
> 解析缺陷已经修复并部署。现在能重放整个死信队列吗？大约有八千条消息在等待处理。

**02 [A] / Ops Engineer：** Let's classify the failures first. Some messages may still be invalid, and others may have completed a business action before they entered the queue.
> 我们先对失败分类。部分消息可能仍然无效，另一些可能在进入死信队列前已经完成了业务操作。

**03 [C] / Business Operations Lead：** Please check the business status before retrying. An old request to cancel an order could be unsafe if that order has already shipped.
> 重试前请检查业务状态。如果订单已经发货，旧的取消订单请求可能无法安全执行。

**04 [B] / Developer：** I'll group the messages by failure reason and schema version, then compare their business keys with processing records. The bug only affected one format.
> 我会按失败原因和结构版本给消息分组，再将业务键与处理记录比较。这个缺陷只影响一种格式。

**05 [A] / Ops Engineer：** Also verify idempotency across the replay path. A different replay tool must preserve the original business key rather than generate a fresh operation identity.
> 还要核实重放链路的幂等性。不同的重放工具也必须保留原业务键，不能为操作生成新的身份标识。

**06 [B] / Developer：** Our tool preserves that key and records the replay attempt separately. I'll demonstrate it with ten verified messages whose business actions are still valid.
> 我们的工具会保留业务键，并单独记录重放尝试。我会用十条已核实、且业务操作仍有效的消息演示。

**07 [C] / Business Operations Lead：** I'll approve that sample after checking the current order states. Keep invalid messages aside for correction, and retain a clear explanation for excluded records.
> 检查当前订单状态后，我会批准这组样本。无效消息先单独保留待修正，并清楚记录排除各条记录的原因。

**08 [A] / Ops Engineer：** We'll replay the sample at a controlled rate and compare outcomes with business records. We'll stop if duplicate actions, unexpected state changes, or renewed errors appear.
> 我们会以受控速率重放样本，并把结果与业务记录核对。如果出现重复操作、意外状态变化或错误再次出现，就停止。

**09 [B] / Developer：** Should successful messages be removed from the source queue immediately, or retained until reconciliation? I want to avoid losing evidence or processing them twice.
> 成功消息应立即从源队列删除，还是保留到对账完成？我想避免丢失证据，也避免再次处理。

**10 [A] / Ops Engineer：** Use the approved replay workflow: acknowledge only after confirmed processing, with durable audit records linking source and result. We'll retain evidence under the normal policy.
> 使用获准的重放流程：确认处理后才确认消息，并用持久审计记录关联源消息与结果。证据按常规政策保留。

**11 [C] / Business Operations Lead：** After the sample reconciles, I'll approve larger batches for eligible messages. Please check age limits and any required ordering before expanding the replay.
> 样本核对通过后，我会批准对符合条件的消息扩大批次。扩大重放前，请检查时效限制和必要顺序。

**12 [B] / Developer：** I'll prepare the classification summary and sample results for review in thirty minutes. Messages with unclear outcomes will stay isolated until their owners decide.
> 我会准备分类摘要和样本结果，在三十分钟后评审。结果不明确的消息将继续隔离，等待其负责人决定。

**可复用表达：**

- Let's classify the failures first. — 我们先对失败分类。
- Please check age limits and any required ordering before expanding the replay. — 扩大重放前，请检查时效限制和必要顺序。

**落实动作：** 开发分类消息并验证原业务键保留；业务运营批准十条有效样本，运维限速重放并留审计证据，三十分钟后按对账结果决定扩批。
**练习重点：** 说明修复缺陷并不意味着所有死信都可重放，清楚表达业务有效性、幂等和停止条件。

---

<a id="s060"></a>
## 060 夜间批处理超时
**分类：** 数据库、中间件与批处理  /  **英文词数：** 295
**音频：** [播放 MP3](audio/060.mp3)  /  [英文字幕](audio/060.srt)

**背景：** 2026年11月12日清晨，跨区夜间批处理因上游文件延迟，可能错过06:30 UTC的业务截止时间。团队需要评估处理时间，并协调备用报表审批。

**角色：** A — Ops Engineer（运维工程师）；B — Business Lead（业务负责人）；C — Data Engineer（数据工程师）

**01 [B] / Business Lead：** Will the overnight report be ready before our 06:30 UTC deadline on November 12, 2026? Regional teams need it before they start processing accounts.
> 夜间报表能在2026年11月12日06:30 UTC的截止时间前准备好吗？区域团队开始处理账户前需要这份报表。

**02 [A] / Ops Engineer：** The batch is waiting for an upstream file. We don't have a confirmed arrival time, so I can't commit to the report being ready.
> 批处理正在等待上游文件。我们没有确认的到达时间，因此无法承诺报表按时准备好。

**03 [C] / Data Engineer：** Once the file arrives and passes validation, processing normally takes twenty-five minutes. Larger files can take longer, and we still need the final reconciliation.
> 文件到达并通过校验后，处理通常需要二十五分钟。较大文件可能耗时更长，而且仍需要最终对账。

**04 [B] / Business Lead：** Can we publish yesterday's report as a fallback? It would let teams prepare, but I need to understand which decisions would become unsafe.
> 能否发布昨天的报表作为替代？这样团队可以准备工作，但我需要了解哪些决策会因此不安全。

**05 [A] / Ops Engineer：** The fallback needs business approval and a clear data timestamp. Yesterday's balances won't include overnight changes, so the permitted use must be explicit.
> 替代方案需要业务批准，并清楚标注数据时间戳。昨天的余额不包含夜间变化，因此必须明确允许的用途。

**06 [B] / Business Lead：** It can support staffing estimates, but not account adjustments or customer balance confirmations. I'll confirm that restriction with the regional operations owners.
> 它可以用于人员安排估算，但不能用于账户调整或向客户确认余额。我会与区域运营负责人确认这一限制。

**07 [C] / Data Engineer：** I'll contact the upstream owner for file status and check whether any partial delivery is usable. We won't silently combine incomplete data with yesterday's report.
> 我会联系上游负责人确认文件状态，并检查部分交付是否可用。我们不会不加说明地把不完整数据与昨天的报表合并。

**08 [A] / Ops Engineer：** Please also confirm whether the batch can resume safely after the delay. We need to avoid duplicate processing if the scheduler starts another attempt.
> 也请确认延迟后能否安全继续批处理。如果调度器启动另一次尝试，我们需要避免重复处理。

**09 [C] / Data Engineer：** The job tracks completed input files, but I'll verify the current checkpoint and keep retries under the documented control. The late file needs completeness checks.
> 任务会记录已处理的输入文件，但我会核实当前检查点，并按文档控制重试。迟到文件需要进行完整性检查。

**10 [B] / Business Lead：** I'll own approval of the restricted fallback and tell teams which activities must wait. Please give me another update by 06:00 UTC, even without an arrival estimate.
> 我负责批准限定用途的替代方案，并告知团队哪些活动必须等待。请在06:00 UTC前再次更新，即使仍没有到达时间预估。

**11 [A] / Ops Engineer：** At that checkpoint, I'll report file status, remaining processing steps, and the fallback decision. We'll label the report with its actual data cutoff, not its publication time.
> 在那个检查点，我会报告文件状态、剩余处理步骤和替代方案决定。报表会标明实际数据截止时间，而不是用发布时间代替。

**12 [C] / Data Engineer：** I'll own the batch restart and reconciliation. The final report will replace the fallback only after data checks pass and the business owner confirms release.
> 我负责批处理恢复执行和对账。只有数据检查通过、业务负责人确认发布后，最终报表才会替换备用报表。

**可复用表达：**

- The fallback needs business approval and a clear data timestamp. — 替代方案需要业务批准，并清楚标注数据时间戳。
- We don't have a confirmed arrival time. — 我们没有确认的到达时间。

**落实动作：** 数据工程师确认上游文件、检查点和完整性；业务负责人批准备用报表的限定用途，运维在06:00 UTC前更新并标明真实数据截止时间。
**练习重点：** 不承诺未经确认的完成时间，说明依赖与剩余步骤，并对陈旧数据的用途取得明确批准。

---

<a id="s061"></a>
## 061 告警阈值评审
**分类：** 监控、性能与可靠性  /  **英文词数：** 262
**音频：** [播放 MP3](audio/061.mp3)  /  [英文字幕](audio/061.srt)

**背景：** 团队评审频繁触发的CPU告警，希望减少无效呼叫，同时保留对真实容量风险的预警。

**角色：** A — Ops Engineer（运维工程师）；B — Service Owner（服务负责人）

**01 [B] / Service Owner：** We received twenty CPU pages last week, but only two coincided with slower responses. Can we make this alert less sensitive?
> 上周我们收到了二十次CPU告警呼叫，但只有两次同时出现响应变慢。能降低这条告警的敏感度吗？

**02 [A] / Ops Engineer：** Probably, but I want to compare CPU usage with request latency, error rates, and queue depth before we change the threshold.
> 应该可以，但调整阈值前，我想先对照CPU使用率、请求延迟、错误率和队列深度。

**03 [B] / Service Owner：** Most spikes lasted under a minute during scheduled imports. The two longer events happened when the request queue was also growing.
> 多数尖峰发生在计划导入期间，持续不到一分钟。两次较长的异常发生时，请求队列也在增长。

**04 [A] / Ops Engineer：** That suggests duration matters. We could require high CPU for five minutes and use queue growth to provide additional context for responders.
> 这说明持续时间很重要。我们可以要求高CPU持续五分钟，并用队列增长为响应人员提供补充信息。

**05 [B] / Service Owner：** Would that delay detection if a sudden traffic increase causes customer impact within the first minute? I do not want that gap.
> 如果流量突然增加，第一分钟就影响客户，这会不会延迟发现？我不希望留下这个缺口。

**06 [A] / Ops Engineer：** The separate latency and error alerts would still apply. We should replay all three rules against previous incidents to check detection times.
> 独立的延迟和错误告警仍然生效。我们应使用历史故障回放这三条规则，检查发现时间。

**07 [B] / Service Owner：** Please include the import failure from September. CPU stayed below our current threshold even though the queue took an hour to clear.
> 请把九月份的导入故障也纳入验证。当时CPU没有达到现有阈值，但队列花了一小时才清空。

**08 [A] / Ops Engineer：** Good point. That case may need a queue age alert rather than a different CPU threshold. I will assess both signals separately.
> 有道理。那个案例可能需要队列等待时长告警，而不只是调整CPU阈值。我会分别评估这两个信号。

**09 [B] / Service Owner：** Who will review the proposed rules, and can we observe their behavior before changing the production paging configuration for the service?
> 谁来评审拟议规则？更改这个服务的生产呼叫配置前，能先观察规则表现吗？

**10 [A] / Ops Engineer：** I will prepare the historical results by Thursday at 14:00 UTC. You and the monitoring owner can review a week of shadow results.
> 我会在周四14:00 UTC前准备好历史验证结果。你和监控负责人可以一起评审一周的影子运行结果。

**11 [B] / Service Owner：** That works. Keep the existing pages active during observation, and document any incident that the proposed rule detects later or misses completely.
> 可以。观察期间保持现有呼叫生效，并记录新规则发现较晚或完全漏掉的每一次故障。

**12 [A] / Ops Engineer：** Agreed. We will approve the final change only after comparing missed events, detection delay, and paging volume, with a documented rollback option.
> 同意。我们会比较漏报、发现延迟和呼叫数量，记录回退方案，再批准最终变更。

**可复用表达：**

- We should replay all three rules against previous incidents to check detection times. — 我们应使用历史故障回放这三条规则，检查发现时间。
- I will assess both signals separately. — 我会分别评估这两个信号。

**落实动作：** 运维周四14:00 UTC前提交历史回放结果；服务与监控负责人评审一周影子运行数据后决定是否调整，并保留回退方案。
**练习重点：** 练习用证据讨论阈值、持续时间、漏报风险及变更验收条件。

---

<a id="s062"></a>
## 062 重复告警与降噪
**分类：** 监控、性能与可靠性  /  **英文词数：** 265
**音频：** [播放 MP3](audio/062.mp3)  /  [英文字幕](audio/062.srt)

**背景：** 数据库异常引发大量重复呼叫，团队讨论告警分组、依赖抑制和路由规则。

**角色：** A — Ops Engineer（运维工程师）；B — On-call Engineer（值班工程师）；C — Monitoring Lead（监控负责人）

**01 [B] / On-call Engineer：** One database failure generated fifty pages overnight. I spent several minutes opening duplicate incidents before I could identify the shared dependency.
> 昨晚一次数据库故障触发了五十次呼叫。我花了几分钟打开重复故障记录，才找出共同依赖。

**02 [A] / Ops Engineer：** Let us separate repeated notifications from distinct symptoms. Which alerts shared the same database cluster, and which belonged to unrelated services?
> 我们先区分重复通知和不同症状。哪些告警属于同一个数据库集群，哪些来自无关服务？

**03 [B] / On-call Engineer：** Forty-two pages came from services using the east cluster. Eight came from a separate batch system and should remain independent notifications.
> 四十二次呼叫来自使用东部集群的服务。另有八次来自独立的批处理系统，应保留为独立通知。

**04 [C] / Monitoring Lead：** We can group the east-cluster alerts into one incident. Should the database alert also suppress every application alert associated with that cluster?
> 可以把东部集群的告警合并为一个故障。数据库告警还应该抑制所有相关应用告警吗？

**05 [A] / Ops Engineer：** Only where we have verified the dependency. An application may have another failure at the same time, so blanket suppression could hide impact.
> 只在依赖关系已经验证的地方这样做。应用可能同时发生其他故障，全面抑制会掩盖影响。

**06 [C] / Monitoring Lead：** Then we can suppress duplicate pages while keeping downstream events visible on the incident timeline. Critical user journey failures can retain separate routing.
> 那么我们可以抑制重复呼叫，同时让下游事件继续显示在故障时间线上。关键用户流程失败可保留独立路由。

**07 [B] / On-call Engineer：** What happens if the database alert clears first but checkout is still failing? I need the application problem to become actionable again.
> 如果数据库告警先恢复，但结算仍然失败，会怎样？我需要应用问题重新触发可处理的通知。

**08 [A] / Ops Engineer：** We should test that transition explicitly. Once suppression ends, unresolved application alerts must notify their owners according to the configured notification policy.
> 我们应专门测试这种状态转换。抑制结束后，未解决的应用告警必须按配置的通知政策通知负责人。

**09 [C] / Monitoring Lead：** I will create tests for dependency failure, recovery, missing labels, and simultaneous independent incidents. Incorrect labels will fall back to normal routing.
> 我会针对依赖故障、恢复、标签缺失和同时出现独立故障创建测试。标签错误时将回到正常路由。

**10 [B] / On-call Engineer：** Please also check the destination team. Last night the database page reached the application rotation, which added another ten minutes of delay.
> 也请检查接收团队。昨晚数据库告警发给了应用值班组，又增加了十分钟延迟。

**11 [A] / Ops Engineer：** I will verify ownership with both teams today. We can review test results tomorrow at 16:00 UTC before enabling any new suppression rules.
> 我今天会和两个团队确认责任归属。明天16:00 UTC评审测试结果后，再启用新的抑制规则。

**12 [C] / Monitoring Lead：** Agreed. After approval, we will monitor duplicate page counts and missed escalations for seven days, with the old routing available for rollback.
> 同意。批准后，我们会连续七天观察重复呼叫数量和遗漏升级，并保留旧路由以便回退。

**可复用表达：**

- Let us separate repeated notifications from distinct symptoms. — 我们先区分重复通知和不同症状。
- We should test that transition explicitly. — 我们应专门测试这种状态转换。

**落实动作：** 运维当天核实责任路由，监控负责人完成抑制转换测试；次日16:00 UTC评审，批准后观察七天。
**练习重点：** 练习澄清告警依赖、提出例外场景，并确认恢复后的通知行为。

---

<a id="s063"></a>
## 063 平均延迟与P95
**分类：** 监控、性能与可靠性  /  **英文词数：** 272
**音频：** [播放 MP3](audio/063.mp3)  /  [英文字幕](audio/063.srt)

**背景：** 平均响应时间看似正常，但一个区域持续有慢请求反馈，团队需要解释并排查长尾延迟。

**角色：** A — Ops Engineer（运维工程师）；B — Engineering Manager（工程经理）；C — Application Engineer（应用工程师）

**01 [B] / Engineering Manager：** The global average response time is still two hundred milliseconds. Why are customers in one region saying the application feels much slower?
> 全球平均响应时间仍是两百毫秒，为什么一个区域的客户说应用明显变慢了？

**02 [A] / Ops Engineer：** The average hides the slower requests. In the east region, the ninety-fifth percentile increased from eight hundred milliseconds to three seconds this morning.
> 平均值掩盖了较慢请求。今天上午，东部区域的第九十五百分位延迟从八百毫秒升到了三秒。

**03 [B] / Engineering Manager：** Does that mean ninety-five percent of customers are waiting three seconds, or are we talking about a smaller share of the traffic?
> 这是否意味着百分之九十五的客户都要等三秒，还是只涉及一小部分流量？

**04 [A] / Ops Engineer：** It describes requests, not individual customers. Roughly ninety-five percent completed within three seconds in that window; the remaining requests took longer.
> 它描述的是请求，不是独立客户。在该窗口内，约百分之九十五的请求在三秒内完成，其余更慢。

**05 [C] / Application Engineer：** The change is concentrated in product search. Other endpoints look stable, but search traffic also increased, so the comparison needs a consistent request mix.
> 变化集中在商品搜索。其他接口看起来稳定，但搜索流量也增加了，因此比较时需要保持请求组合一致。

**06 [A] / Ops Engineer：** Let us compare the same endpoint and region across matching fifteen-minute windows, then separate cached searches from requests that reach the database.
> 我们按相同接口和区域比较对应的十五分钟窗口，再区分缓存命中的搜索与访问数据库的请求。

**07 [B] / Engineering Manager：** Could the regional percentile simply reflect low traffic? I want to understand how much confidence we should place in this apparent increase.
> 区域分位数会不会只是低流量造成的？我想知道对这次看起来的升高应该有多大把握。

**08 [A] / Ops Engineer：** There were forty thousand search requests in the affected window. I will check histogram coverage and sample counts before relying on the comparison.
> 受影响窗口内有四万次搜索请求。我会先检查直方图覆盖情况和样本数量，再采用这个比较结果。

**09 [C] / Application Engineer：** The slower traces show database calls taking most of the time. That narrows the investigation, but it does not establish why those calls slowed.
> 较慢的调用链显示，大部分时间花在数据库调用上。这缩小了排查范围，但尚不能说明调用为何变慢。

**10 [A] / Ops Engineer：** Please compare query plans and connection wait times. I will check regional resource pressure and whether the traffic change affected cache hit rates.
> 请比较查询计划和连接等待时间。我会检查区域资源压力，以及流量变化是否影响了缓存命中率。

**11 [B] / Engineering Manager：** Bring the findings to our 13:30 UTC checkpoint. Include request counts and customer-facing response times so we can judge whether mitigation is needed.
> 请在13:30 UTC检查点汇报发现，并包含请求数量和面向客户的响应时间，便于判断是否需要缓解措施。

**12 [A] / Ops Engineer：** Understood. We will report the regional tail latency alongside the average, and clearly separate measured behavior from our current explanations for it.
> 明白。我们会同时报告区域长尾延迟和平均值，并明确区分测量到的现象与当前对原因的解释。

**可复用表达：**

- The average hides the slower requests. — 平均值掩盖了较慢请求。
- That narrows the investigation, but it does not establish why those calls slowed. — 这缩小了排查范围，但尚不能说明调用为何变慢。

**落实动作：** 运维和应用工程师分别检查区域资源、缓存、查询计划和连接等待；13:30 UTC带请求数量及分位数证据汇报。
**练习重点：** 练习解释分位数、区分请求与客户，以及按可比窗口拆分性能数据。

---

<a id="s064"></a>
## 064 SLI与SLO口径
**分类：** 监控、性能与可靠性  /  **英文词数：** 273
**音频：** [播放 MP3](audio/064.mp3)  /  [英文字幕](audio/064.srt)

**背景：** 业务团队报告结算失败，但技术看板仍显示高可用，双方需要统一指标含义与目标。

**角色：** A — Ops Engineer（运维工程师）；B — Business Lead（业务负责人）；C — Reliability Engineer（可靠性工程师）

**01 [B] / Business Lead：** The dashboard shows ninety-nine point nine percent availability, but customers could not finish checkout this morning. Those two messages seem inconsistent to me.
> 看板显示可用性为百分之九十九点九，但今天上午客户无法完成结算。对我来说，这两种说法似乎不一致。

**02 [A] / Ops Engineer：** We need to confirm what the indicator measures. The current chart counts successful endpoint responses, which may not represent completed customer checkouts.
> 我们需要确认指标具体衡量什么。目前图表统计接口成功响应，这未必能代表客户完成结算。

**03 [C] / Reliability Engineer：** The payment handoff returned a successful response before confirmation arrived. Our indicator treated that response as success even when confirmation later failed.
> 支付交接在确认结果到达前就返回了成功响应。即使之后确认失败，现有指标仍把这次响应算作成功。

**04 [B] / Business Lead：** Then can we define success as an order being confirmed and visible to the customer? That would be closer to the actual business outcome.
> 那能否把成功定义为订单已确认且客户可见？这样更接近实际业务结果。

**05 [A] / Ops Engineer：** Yes, provided we define the eligible attempts and completion deadline. We also need to explain how retries and abandoned sessions affect the calculation.
> 可以，前提是定义纳入统计的尝试和完成时限。还需要说明重试和放弃会话如何影响计算。

**06 [C] / Reliability Engineer：** I suggest counting each valid checkout attempt once, using its transaction identifier. A retry within that attempt should not create another successful outcome.
> 我建议按交易标识符，将每次有效结算尝试只统计一次。同一次尝试中的重试不应再增加一个成功结果。

**07 [B] / Business Lead：** How will you handle declined cards? A valid decline is different from our platform failing, but customers still do not receive an order.
> 银行卡被拒付怎么处理？有效拒付与平台故障不同，但客户同样没有拿到订单。

**08 [A] / Ops Engineer：** We will document expected business rejections separately and agree on exclusions with you. Unexpected processing failures must remain visible in the service indicator.
> 我们会单独记录预期的业务拒绝，并和你确认排除项。意外处理失败必须继续体现在服务指标中。

**09 [C] / Reliability Engineer：** For the service level objective, the proposed target is ninety-nine point nine percent over a rolling thirty-day window, subject to business approval.
> 服务等级目标建议定为滚动三十天窗口内达到百分之九十九点九，需经业务批准。

**10 [B] / Business Lead：** Before approving that target, I need to see results for last month and this morning's outage, including any missing transaction events.
> 批准目标前，我需要看到上个月和今天上午故障的回算结果，并说明可能缺失的交易事件。

**11 [A] / Ops Engineer：** I will backfill the proposed service level indicator and flag data gaps. We will review the definition and historical result Friday at 11:00 UTC.
> 我会回算拟议的服务等级指标，并标注数据缺口。周五11:00 UTC我们评审定义和历史结果。

**12 [C] / Reliability Engineer：** After approval, I will publish the denominator, exclusions, target, and measurement window beside the dashboard so future reports use the same definitions.
> 批准后，我会在看板旁公布分母、排除项、目标和统计窗口，让今后的报告采用相同定义。

**可复用表达：**

- We need to confirm what the indicator measures. — 我们需要确认指标具体衡量什么。
- That would be closer to the actual business outcome. — 这样更接近实际业务结果。

**落实动作：** 运维回算用户结算成功指标并标注数据缺口；周五11:00 UTC与业务评审纳入范围、排除项、窗口和目标。
**练习重点：** 练习澄清SLI与SLO定义、统计分母和业务成功标准。

---

<a id="s065"></a>
## 065 错误预算与发布节奏
**分类：** 监控、性能与可靠性  /  **英文词数：** 280
**音频：** [播放 MP3](audio/065.mp3)  /  [英文字幕](audio/065.srt)

**背景：** 近期故障消耗了大部分错误预算，产品与运维团队评审本周发布安排。

**角色：** A — Ops Engineer（运维工程师）；B — Product Owner（产品负责人）；C — Engineering Lead（工程负责人）

**01 [B] / Product Owner：** We have three feature releases planned this week. Can we maintain the schedule, or does the recent outage mean everything has to stop?
> 本周计划发布三项功能。我们还能按计划执行吗，还是近期故障意味着所有发布都必须停止？

**02 [A] / Ops Engineer：** The service has consumed ninety percent of its monthly error budget. Our agreed policy requires a reliability review before further production changes.
> 服务已消耗当月百分之九十的错误预算。按照既定政策，继续生产变更前需要进行可靠性评审。

**03 [B] / Product Owner：** Please explain the budget in practical terms. Does the remaining ten percent represent a safe amount of downtime that we can deliberately use?
> 请用实际业务语言解释这个预算。剩余的百分之十是否代表我们可以主动使用的一段安全停机时间？

**04 [A] / Ops Engineer：** No. It tracks how much unsuccessful service our objective permits over the measurement window. It is a decision signal, not planned outage allowance.
> 不是。它衡量统计窗口内服务目标允许的未成功服务量，是决策信号，不是计划停机额度。

**05 [C] / Engineering Lead：** The releases have different risks. One changes payment routing, one updates help text, and the third fixes the retry issue behind yesterday's overload.
> 这些发布风险不同。一个修改支付路由，一个更新帮助文本，第三个修复导致昨天过载的重试问题。

**06 [A] / Ops Engineer：** We should assess each change under the policy. The retry fix may reduce current risk, but it still needs evidence and a safe rollout.
> 我们应按政策逐项评估。重试修复可能降低当前风险，但仍需要证据和安全发布方案。

**07 [B] / Product Owner：** Can the help text proceed while payment routing waits? Marketing needs a clear answer today because the campaign announcement references the new payment option.
> 能否先发布帮助文本，暂缓支付路由？市场团队今天需要明确答复，因为活动公告提到了新的支付选项。

**08 [C] / Engineering Lead：** The text update is isolated, with no runtime configuration change. Payment routing has broader exposure and should wait for the review board's decision.
> 文本更新是独立的，不涉及运行配置变更。支付路由影响范围更广，应等待评审委员会决定。

**09 [A] / Ops Engineer：** For the retry fix, please provide test results, canary limits, rollback conditions, and an owner watching downstream load throughout the initial rollout.
> 对于重试修复，请提供测试结果、灰度范围、回退条件，并指定负责人在初期发布全程观察下游负载。

**10 [B] / Product Owner：** I will revise the campaign message if payment routing is deferred. When can we confirm the approved release list and the next budget checkpoint?
> 如果支付路由延期，我会调整活动文案。什么时候能确认获批发布清单和下一次预算检查点？

**11 [A] / Ops Engineer：** The review is today at 15:00 UTC. We will record the decision for each change and reassess the service indicators tomorrow at the same time.
> 评审在今天15:00 UTC。我们会记录每项变更的决定，并在明天同一时间重新评估服务指标。

**12 [C] / Engineering Lead：** Agreed. I will bring the evidence and reliability priorities, and we will communicate the resulting schedule together so teams receive one consistent message.
> 同意。我会带上证据和可靠性优先事项，并一起传达最终安排，确保各团队收到一致信息。

**可复用表达：**

- We should assess each change under the policy. — 我们应按政策逐项评估变更。
- It is a decision signal, not planned outage allowance. — 它是决策信号，不是计划停机额度。

**落实动作：** 今天15:00 UTC逐项评审发布风险和证据，记录批准决定；产品同步活动安排，次日同一时间复查服务指标。
**练习重点：** 练习解释错误预算、按风险讨论发布例外并明确决策时间。

---

<a id="s066"></a>
## 066 监控数据缺失
**分类：** 监控、性能与可靠性  /  **英文词数：** 280
**音频：** [播放 MP3](audio/066.mp3)  /  [英文字幕](audio/066.srt)

**背景：** 生产看板突然空白，团队需要区分服务故障与遥测链路异常，并维持临时观测。

**角色：** A — Ops Engineer（运维工程师）；B — Engineering Manager（工程经理）；C — Monitoring Engineer（监控工程师）

**01 [B] / Engineering Manager：** The service dashboard has shown no data for ten minutes. Is production down, and should we notify the business teams right away?
> 服务看板已经十分钟没有数据。生产服务宕机了吗，我们是否应立即通知业务团队？

**02 [A] / Ops Engineer：** We have not confirmed a service failure. I am checking an independent external probe and recent transactions while we investigate the missing telemetry.
> 我们尚未确认服务故障。我正在检查独立外部探测和近期交易，同时排查遥测数据缺失。

**03 [C] / Monitoring Engineer：** The external probe succeeds from three locations. Metric ingestion stopped at 09:20 UTC, and the collectors are reporting export failures to the monitoring gateway.
> 三个位置的外部探测均成功。指标接收在09:20 UTC停止，采集器报告向监控网关导出失败。

**04 [B] / Engineering Manager：** So can we tell everyone the service is healthy? I want to avoid unnecessary concern, but I also need a statement we can support.
> 那能告诉大家服务健康吗？我想避免不必要的担忧，但也需要一个有依据的说法。

**05 [A] / Ops Engineer：** We can say the independent checks are passing. They cover limited journeys, so we still need application logs and business confirmation before broader conclusions.
> 可以说独立检查正在通过。但它们只覆盖有限流程，因此得出更全面结论前仍需应用日志和业务确认。

**06 [C] / Monitoring Engineer：** The gateway certificate expired this morning. I have the renewal procedure, but I need to confirm whether that explains every collector failure.
> 网关证书今天上午到期了。我有续期流程，但还需要确认它是否解释了所有采集器故障。

**07 [A] / Ops Engineer：** Please verify the certificate errors and use the approved renewal process. Meanwhile, I will arrange temporary checks of transaction success and application error logs.
> 请核实证书错误并按批准的续期流程操作。同时，我会安排临时检查交易成功情况和应用错误日志。

**08 [B] / Engineering Manager：** What visibility will the on-call engineer have during the repair? The usual paging rules depend on the same data that has stopped arriving.
> 修复期间值班工程师能通过什么了解情况？平时的呼叫规则依赖的正是已经停止到达的数据。

**09 [A] / Ops Engineer：** We will keep external probes active and review independent logs every five minutes. I will also flag the monitoring gap in the operations channel.
> 我们会保持外部探测运行，每五分钟检查一次独立日志。我还会在运维频道标明监控缺口。

**10 [C] / Monitoring Engineer：** The certificate replacement is approved. After applying it, I will verify fresh timestamps, ingestion volume, and alert evaluation rather than just dashboard loading.
> 证书更换已获批准。执行后，我会验证最新时间戳、接收量和告警计算，而不只看页面是否加载。

**11 [B] / Engineering Manager：** Please give the next update at 09:45 UTC, even if the repair is incomplete. Include any customer impact found through the independent checks.
> 请在09:45 UTC更新，即使尚未修复也要更新。请包含独立检查发现的任何客户影响。

**12 [A] / Ops Engineer：** Understood. We will track the observation failure separately, retain the data-gap interval, and remove temporary checks only after normal monitoring is verified.
> 明白。我们会单独跟踪观测故障，保留数据缺失区间，并在确认正常监控恢复后再撤除临时检查。

**可复用表达：**

- We have not confirmed a service failure. — 我们尚未确认服务故障。
- We can say the independent checks are passing. — 我们可以说独立检查正在通过。

**落实动作：** 监控工程师按批准流程更换证书并验证采集与告警；运维每五分钟进行独立检查，09:45 UTC汇报。
**练习重点：** 练习在监控缺失时表达证据边界、建立临时检查并验证观测恢复。

---

<a id="s067"></a>
## 067 跨服务日志关联
**分类：** 监控、性能与可靠性  /  **英文词数：** 281
**音频：** [播放 MP3](audio/067.mp3)  /  [英文字幕](audio/067.srt)

**背景：** 多个服务出现相关错误，但追踪上下文传递不完整，团队需要谨慎关联同一笔交易。

**角色：** A — Ops Engineer（运维工程师）；B — Application Developer（应用开发工程师）；C — Payments Engineer（支付工程师）

**01 [B] / Application Developer：** I found an order error and a payment timeout within the same second. Can we treat them as parts of the same failed transaction?
> 我发现一个订单错误和一个支付超时发生在同一秒。能把它们视为同一笔失败交易的一部分吗？

**02 [A] / Ops Engineer：** Not from timing alone. Let us compare the request identifier, trace identifier, and UTC timestamps across the services before linking those events.
> 仅凭时间还不行。先比较各服务的请求标识符、追踪标识符和UTC时间戳，再关联这些事件。

**03 [C] / Payments Engineer：** The payment entry has a transaction identifier, but no trace identifier. Our gateway appears to drop the incoming correlation header before forwarding requests.
> 支付日志有交易标识符，却没有追踪标识符。网关似乎在转发请求前丢弃了传入的关联请求头。

**04 [B] / Application Developer：** The order service logs the transaction identifier after creating the payment request. Could that give us a reliable link for this particular sample?
> 订单服务在创建支付请求后会记录交易标识符。这能为这个样本提供可靠关联吗？

**05 [A] / Ops Engineer：** Yes, if the identifier is stable across both records and retries. Please confirm its semantics rather than assuming two fields with similar names match.
> 可以，前提是该标识符在两边记录和重试中保持一致。请确认它的含义，不要因为字段名称相似就认定匹配。

**06 [C] / Payments Engineer：** I confirmed the same identifier on both sides. There are three payment attempts, so the timeout may describe only one attempt, not the final outcome.
> 我已确认两边标识符一致。共有三次支付尝试，因此超时可能只描述其中一次，而非最终结果。

**07 [B] / Application Developer：** The final attempt succeeded, but the order update failed afterward. That explains why the customer saw an error despite a successful payment record.
> 最后一次尝试成功了，但之后的订单更新失败。这解释了为什么支付记录成功，客户却看到了错误。

**08 [A] / Ops Engineer：** That is supported for this sample. We should check additional affected transactions before concluding the same sequence explains the whole incident or its scope.
> 这个样本支持这种说法。但要认定同样的过程能解释整个故障及其范围，还需要检查更多受影响交易。

**09 [C] / Payments Engineer：** I will investigate the missing header and propose a gateway fix. Should we include a test that checks propagation through every retry path?
> 我会排查请求头缺失并提出网关修复方案。是否应加入测试，检查每条重试路径上的上下文传递？

**10 [A] / Ops Engineer：** Please do, and keep sensitive payment details out of shared logs. We need identifiers and processing states, not card data or authentication secrets.
> 请加入，也不要把敏感支付信息放进共享日志。我们需要标识符和处理状态，不需要卡片数据或认证秘密。

**11 [B] / Application Developer：** I will trace five more failed orders and document the evidence chain by 17:00 UTC, including where correlation remains incomplete or uncertain.
> 我会继续追踪五笔失败订单，在17:00 UTC前记录证据链，包括关联仍不完整或不确定的位置。

**12 [A] / Ops Engineer：** Great. At that checkpoint, we will separate confirmed transaction sequences from open gaps and assign owners for both the application failure and tracing repair.
> 好的。到那个检查点，我们会区分已确认的交易过程与未解决缺口，并分别指定应用故障和追踪修复负责人。

**可复用表达：**

- Not from timing alone. — 仅凭时间还不能判断。
- That is supported for this sample. — 这个样本支持这种说法。

**落实动作：** 应用工程师17:00 UTC前补查五笔订单，支付工程师提出上下文传递修复和重试路径测试；分别记录业务故障与追踪缺口。
**练习重点：** 练习建立跨服务证据链、区分重试与最终结果，并表达关联的不确定性。

---

<a id="s068"></a>
## 068 压测与真实流量差异
**分类：** 监控、性能与可靠性  /  **英文词数：** 278
**音频：** [播放 MP3](audio/068.mp3)  /  [英文字幕](audio/068.srt)

**背景：** 生产高峰出现拥塞，尽管之前的压测通过，团队复核测试模型与生产差异。

**角色：** A — Ops Engineer（运维工程师）；B — Engineering Manager（工程经理）；C — Performance Engineer（性能工程师）

**01 [B] / Engineering Manager：** The load test passed last week, yet production struggled during the sale. What did the test miss, and how can we trust the next result?
> 上周压测通过了，但促销期间生产系统仍出现异常。测试漏了什么，我们怎样才能信任下一次结果？

**02 [A] / Ops Engineer：** The test represented request volume reasonably well, but not the production request mix, data size, or limits of the shared search dependency.
> 测试对请求量的模拟比较合理，但没有充分体现生产请求组合、数据规模和共享搜索依赖的限制。

**03 [C] / Performance Engineer：** We used mostly cached reads and a small catalog. During the sale, searches with multiple filters increased and reached the database more often.
> 我们主要使用缓存读取和小规模商品目录。促销期间，多条件筛选搜索增加，访问数据库也更频繁。

**04 [B] / Engineering Manager：** Could we simply double the test traffic and add application replicas? That seems faster than rebuilding the whole workload model before the next event.
> 能否直接把测试流量加倍，再增加应用副本？这看起来比下一次活动前重建整个负载模型更快。

**05 [A] / Ops Engineer：** More replicas may increase pressure on the same dependency. We need to reproduce the actual bottleneck before deciding whether application capacity would help.
> 增加副本可能加重同一个依赖的压力。我们需要先复现实际瓶颈，再判断增加应用容量是否有帮助。

**06 [C] / Performance Engineer：** I can build an anonymized workload from production proportions, with realistic catalog size, cold-cache periods, and the current search quota represented explicitly.
> 我可以按生产比例构建匿名化负载，使用真实规模的商品目录，加入冷缓存阶段，并明确模拟当前搜索配额。

**07 [B] / Engineering Manager：** How long should it run? The previous test lasted twenty minutes, while the production slowdown appeared almost an hour into the campaign.
> 应该跑多久？之前的测试只有二十分钟，而生产变慢是在活动开始接近一小时后出现的。

**08 [A] / Ops Engineer：** We need a longer steady-load phase to expose queue growth and resource accumulation, plus controlled bursts and a recovery period after traffic falls.
> 需要更长的稳态负载阶段来暴露队列增长和资源累积，还要有受控突发流量和流量下降后的恢复阶段。

**09 [C] / Performance Engineer：** I propose a two-hour steady phase. Pass criteria will cover completed searches, checkout success, tail latency, dependency saturation, and how quickly queues drain.
> 我建议稳态阶段持续两小时。通过标准将覆盖搜索完成、结算成功、长尾延迟、依赖饱和度和队列消退速度。

**10 [B] / Engineering Manager：** Make the constraints visible in the report. A passing result should state the supported traffic profile, not imply unlimited readiness for any campaign.
> 请在报告中明确约束。通过结果应说明支持哪种流量模型，而不是让人以为任何活动都能无限承载。

**11 [A] / Ops Engineer：** We will agree on the workload and acceptance criteria Wednesday at 10:00 UTC. The test environment limits must also be documented before execution.
> 我们将在周三10:00 UTC确认负载和验收标准。执行前还必须记录测试环境限制。

**12 [C] / Performance Engineer：** I will prepare that proposal and identify production differences we cannot simulate. After the run, we will turn the measured bottlenecks into owned actions.
> 我会准备方案，并列出无法模拟的生产差异。执行后，我们会根据实测瓶颈制定有明确负责人的行动项。

**可复用表达：**

- More replicas may increase pressure on the same dependency. — 增加副本可能加重同一个依赖的压力。
- The test environment limits must also be documented before execution. — 执行前还必须记录测试环境限制。

**落实动作：** 性能工程师制定真实负载、两小时稳态阶段和恢复验收方案；周三10:00 UTC确认生产差异、测试约束及通过标准。
**练习重点：** 练习分析压测代表性、反问扩容假设，并限定测试结论适用范围。

---

<a id="s069"></a>
## 069 探测成功但客户仍报错
**分类：** 监控、性能与可靠性  /  **英文词数：** 285
**音频：** [播放 MP3](audio/069.mp3)  /  [英文字幕](audio/069.srt)

**背景：** 合成探测正常，但特定客户持续报错，运维与支持团队根据客户样本核实覆盖缺口。

**角色：** A — Ops Engineer（运维工程师）；B — Service Owner（服务负责人）；C — Support Engineer（支持工程师）

**01 [B] / Service Owner：** All synthetic checks are green, but one customer says file uploads keep failing. Could this be a problem with their network or browser?
> 所有合成探测都正常，但有位客户说文件上传一直失败。会不会是他们的网络或浏览器有问题？

**02 [A] / Ops Engineer：** That is possible, but our checks may not cover the affected account type, region, or upload path. We should examine their examples first.
> 有这种可能，但我们的检查未必覆盖受影响的账户类型、区域或上传路径。应先检查他们的样本。

**03 [C] / Support Engineer：** They supplied three failing request identifiers and UTC timestamps. The requests came from an enterprise account using large files through the regional gateway.
> 他们提供了三个失败请求标识符和UTC时间戳。这些请求来自企业账户，通过区域网关上传大文件。

**04 [B] / Service Owner：** Our probe uploads a small file through the global endpoint. It does not exercise that regional gateway or the larger enterprise upload limit.
> 我们的探测通过全球入口上传小文件，既不经过那个区域网关，也不会触及更大的企业上传限额。

**05 [A] / Ops Engineer：** Then the green result has limited relevance to this complaint. Let us trace those requests and compare gateway responses with the upload service logs.
> 那这个正常结果对当前投诉的参考价值有限。我们来追踪这些请求，对照网关响应和上传服务日志。

**06 [C] / Support Engineer：** The gateway returns a timeout after thirty seconds. The upload service continues processing, so a customer retry might create another copy of the file.
> 网关在三十秒后返回超时，但上传服务继续处理，因此客户重试可能产生文件的另一个副本。

**07 [A] / Ops Engineer：** Please check whether the request identifier prevents duplicate processing before recommending retries. We also need to confirm completed uploads remain accessible to the customer.
> 建议客户重试前，请先检查请求标识符是否能防止重复处理。还要确认已完成上传的文件对客户可访问。

**08 [B] / Service Owner：** Can we reproduce the timeout safely with a test enterprise account? I would prefer direct evidence before changing the gateway limit for all customers.
> 能用测试企业账户安全复现超时吗？在修改所有客户的网关限制前，我希望先获得直接证据。

**09 [A] / Ops Engineer：** Yes. Use a non-sensitive test file in the same region and record size, duration, and response codes. Any configuration change still needs normal approval.
> 可以。在同一区域使用不含敏感信息的测试文件，并记录大小、耗时和响应码。任何配置变更仍需正常批准。

**10 [C] / Support Engineer：** I will update the customer now with confirmed findings and avoid assigning blame. When should I promise the next progress update from our team?
> 我现在会向客户同步已确认发现，避免先归责。可以承诺团队什么时候提供下一次进展更新？

**11 [A] / Ops Engineer：** Give them 16:30 UTC as the next update time, not a recovery estimate. I will own reproduction, and the service owner will assess mitigation.
> 告知他们下次更新时间为16:30 UTC，不是预计恢复时间。我负责复现，服务负责人评估缓解措施。

**12 [B] / Service Owner：** Agreed. We will add representative regional upload coverage after validating the failure, and keep the customer case open until they confirm the workflow succeeds.
> 同意。验证故障后，我们会增加有代表性的区域上传检查，并在客户确认流程成功前保持支持工单开放。

**可复用表达：**

- Then the green result has limited relevance to this complaint. — 那这个正常结果对当前投诉的参考价值有限。
- Give them 16:30 UTC as the next update time, not a recovery estimate. — 告知他们下次更新时间为16:30 UTC，不是预计恢复时间。

**落实动作：** 运维用测试企业账户复现，服务负责人评估缓解；支持在16:30 UTC更新客户，并在客户确认成功后关闭工单。
**练习重点：** 练习承认探测覆盖限制、依据客户样本排查，并区分更新承诺与恢复承诺。

---

<a id="s070"></a>
## 070 容量预测与促销保障
**分类：** 监控、性能与可靠性  /  **英文词数：** 282
**音频：** [播放 MP3](audio/070.mp3)  /  [英文字幕](audio/070.srt)

**背景：** 跨国促销活动前，业务与技术团队核对流量预测、依赖上限和降级决策安排。

**角色：** A — Ops Engineer（运维工程师）；B — Business Lead（业务负责人）；C — Capacity Engineer（容量工程师）

**01 [B] / Business Lead：** The campaign forecast is twice our normal traffic. Can you confirm we are ready before the regional teams finalize their promotional messages tomorrow?
> 活动预计流量是平时的两倍。各区域团队明天确定促销信息前，你能确认我们准备好了吗？

**02 [A] / Ops Engineer：** We need more than a daily traffic total. Please share the regional launch schedule, peak concurrency forecast, and expected mix of browsing and transactions.
> 只有全天流量总数还不够。请提供各区域启动时间表、峰值并发预测，以及浏览和交易的预计比例。

**03 [B] / Business Lead：** Most advertisements launch at 12:00 UTC, and purchases may triple for thirty minutes. The rest of the day should remain near twice normal volume.
> 大部分广告在12:00 UTC上线，购买请求可能在三十分钟内达到平时三倍。其余时间应接近平时两倍。

**04 [C] / Capacity Engineer：** That concentrated peak matters. Application capacity has headroom, but the payment provider quota is only twenty percent above our previous transaction peak.
> 这种集中峰值很关键。应用容量有余量，但支付提供商的配额只比我们此前的交易峰值高百分之二十。

**05 [A] / Ops Engineer：** Adding application replicas will not remove that quota. We should request provider capacity and validate queue behavior under the forecast transaction rate.
> 增加应用副本无法消除那个配额限制。我们应向提供商申请容量，并按预测交易速率验证队列行为。

**06 [B] / Business Lead：** If the provider cannot increase the quota in time, could we stagger advertisements by region? I need to know the practical business alternatives.
> 如果提供商来不及提高配额，能否让各区域广告错峰上线？我需要了解实际可行的业务替代方案。

**07 [C] / Capacity Engineer：** Yes, if the revised schedule keeps the combined transaction peak within tested capacity. We must also reserve headroom for a component failure during the event.
> 可以，前提是调整后综合交易峰值不超过已验证容量。还必须预留活动期间某个组件故障时的容量余量。

**08 [A] / Ops Engineer：** We also need approved fallback options, trigger conditions, and a business contact who can authorize them. We should not invent those decisions during overload.
> 我们还需要获批的降级选项、触发条件，以及能授权的业务联系人。不应等到过载时临时决定这些事情。

**09 [B] / Business Lead：** I will own the regional schedule and authorize campaign changes. Please bring me a clear choice between the original launch and a staggered plan.
> 我负责区域时间表，并有权批准活动调整。请明确提供原定上线方案和错峰方案这两个选择。

**10 [C] / Capacity Engineer：** I will finish the quota check and peak-load evidence by 17:00 UTC today. Readiness will remain conditional until the provider confirms its limit.
> 我会在今天17:00 UTC前完成配额核查和峰值负载证据整理。提供商确认限额前，准备结论仍附带条件。

**11 [A] / Ops Engineer：** Let us review both plans at that checkpoint, including recovery capacity, alert coverage, and named on-call contacts for every critical dependency involved.
> 我们在那个检查点评审两套方案，内容包括恢复容量、告警覆盖，以及每个关键依赖的明确值班联系人。

**12 [B] / Business Lead：** Agreed. I will publish the approved schedule afterward and ensure regional leads know the escalation route and who can change the campaign if needed.
> 同意。之后我会公布批准的时间表，并确保区域负责人了解升级路径，以及必要时谁有权调整活动。

**可复用表达：**

- We need more than a daily traffic total. — 只有全天流量总数还不够。
- Readiness will remain conditional until the provider confirms its limit. — 提供商确认限额前，准备结论仍附带条件。

**落实动作：** 容量工程师今天17:00 UTC前核实支付配额及峰值承载；业务负责原计划与错峰方案决策，并公布降级授权和联系人。
**练习重点：** 练习将业务预测转为容量条件，并确认依赖配额、恢复余量和业务决策责任。

---

<a id="s071"></a>
## 071 收到告警后的初步确认
**分类：** Incident 故障响应  /  **英文词数：** 281
**音频：** [播放 MP3](audio/071.mp3)  /  [英文字幕](audio/071.srt)

**背景：** 生产错误率告警刚刚触发，值班人员开展第一轮确认，并约定五分钟后汇报事实与待验证问题。

**角色：** A — Ops Engineer（运维工程师）；B — On-call Lead（值班负责人）

**01 [B] / On-call Lead：** The checkout error alert fired at 10:05 UTC. Have you confirmed customer impact, or could this be an issue with the monitoring rule?
> 结算错误告警在10:05 UTC触发。确认客户影响了吗，还是可能只是监控规则的问题？

**02 [A] / Ops Engineer：** Not yet. I am checking an independent checkout probe, recent production responses, and whether the failures are limited to one region or endpoint.
> 还没有。我正在检查独立结算探测、近期生产响应，以及失败是否仅限某个区域或接口。

**03 [B] / On-call Lead：** Please check the deployment history too. A release finished shortly before the alert, but we should not assume the timing proves a connection.
> 也请检查部署历史。一次发布在告警前不久结束，但不应认为时间接近就证明存在关联。

**04 [A] / Ops Engineer：** The probe failed twice in the east region, and gateway logs show a six percent checkout error rate over the last five minutes.
> 东部区域的探测失败了两次，网关日志显示最近五分钟结算错误率为百分之六。

**05 [B] / On-call Lead：** That confirms a problem on the checkout path. Are those failed requests or distinct customers, and do we know whether payment processing is affected?
> 这确认了结算链路存在问题。那些是失败请求还是不同客户？知道支付处理是否受影响了吗？

**06 [A] / Ops Engineer：** They are failed requests, including possible retries. I have not established the number of customers or whether any payments completed without order confirmation.
> 它们是失败请求，可能包含重试。我还没确认客户数量，也没确认是否有支付完成但订单未确认的情况。

**07 [B] / On-call Lead：** I will contact the application on-call engineer and ask support to check incoming reports. Please preserve a few failed request identifiers for tracing.
> 我会联系应用值班工程师，并请支持团队检查收到的反馈。请保留几个失败请求标识符用于追踪。

**08 [A] / Ops Engineer：** Will do. The release affected both regions, while confirmed failures currently appear only in the east. That difference needs investigation before we blame the release.
> 好的。发布涉及两个区域，而目前确认的失败只在东部。归因于发布前，需要调查这个差异。

**09 [B] / On-call Lead：** Do we need to escalate immediately, or can we wait until the first investigation checkpoint? Please compare the impact with our incident policy.
> 需要立即升级，还是可以等到第一次排查检查点？请对照我们的故障政策评估影响。

**10 [A] / Ops Engineer：** The current evidence requires notifying the incident manager for assessment. I will send the measured impact and open questions without choosing a severity myself.
> 当前证据要求通知故障经理评估。我会发送实测影响和待确认问题，而不自行决定故障等级。

**11 [B] / On-call Lead：** Good. Give the first written update at 10:10 UTC, even if the investigation is incomplete. List the next checks and their owners clearly.
> 好的。请在10:10 UTC发送首次书面更新，即使调查尚未完成。明确列出下一步检查及负责人。

**12 [A] / Ops Engineer：** Understood. I will separate confirmed facts, working hypotheses, and unknowns, and include the next checkpoint rather than offering an unsupported recovery estimate.
> 明白。我会区分已确认事实、工作假设和未知项，并给出下次检查点，不提供没有依据的恢复时间。

**可复用表达：**

- They are failed requests, including possible retries. — 它们是失败请求，可能包含重试。
- That difference needs investigation before we blame the release. — 归因于发布前，需要调查这个差异。

**落实动作：** 运维10:10 UTC首次更新，保留请求样本并通知故障经理评估；值班负责人联系应用和支持团队，明确下一轮检查责任。
**练习重点：** 练习初步确认影响、限制结论范围，并用事实与未知项组织首次故障汇报。

---

<a id="s072"></a>
## 072 确定等级并升级
**分类：** Incident 故障响应  /  **英文词数：** 279
**音频：** [播放 MP3](audio/072.mp3)  /  [英文字幕](audio/072.srt)

**背景：** 多个区域的核心结算流程失败，运维向故障经理提供证据，按组织标准定级并启动升级。

**角色：** A — Ops Engineer（运维工程师）；B — Incident Manager（故障经理）；C — Application Lead（应用负责人）

**01 [A] / Ops Engineer：** Checkout failures are confirmed in two regions, with thirty percent of attempts failing over ten minutes. This appears to meet our major-incident criteria.
> 已确认两个区域结算失败，十分钟内百分之三十的尝试失败。这看来符合我们的重大故障标准。

**02 [B] / Incident Manager：** What evidence supports that assessment, and which parts of the customer journey remain available? I need the current scope before assigning the severity.
> 哪些证据支持这个判断？客户流程中哪些部分仍可用？确定等级前，我需要了解当前范围。

**03 [A] / Ops Engineer：** External checkout tests and production transaction records agree. Browsing and existing order lookup remain available; unique affected customer counts are still being validated.
> 外部结算测试和生产交易记录一致。浏览和已有订单查询仍可用；受影响独立客户数量仍在核实。

**04 [C] / Application Lead：** Application logs show payment confirmation timeouts. We have not determined whether the cause is in our code, the database, or the network path.
> 应用日志显示支付确认超时。我们尚未判断原因是在代码、数据库，还是网络链路。

**05 [B] / Incident Manager：** Under our documented impact criteria, I am declaring severity one and opening the major-incident bridge. Please record 14:12 UTC as the declaration time.
> 根据我们记录在案的影响标准，我宣布为一级故障并启动重大故障会议。请记录宣布时间为14:12 UTC。

**06 [A] / Ops Engineer：** I will page the application, database, and network on-call engineers through the escalation system, with the incident link and confirmed facts attached.
> 我会通过升级系统呼叫应用、数据库和网络值班工程师，并附上故障链接和已确认事实。

**07 [B] / Incident Manager：** Ask each team to acknowledge ownership, not simply join the call. If anyone misses the policy response window, use the documented backup escalation.
> 请每个团队确认承担责任，而不只是加入会议。若有人超过政策规定的响应窗口，按既定备用路径升级。

**08 [C] / Application Lead：** I accept the application investigation. I will compare failing and successful requests and check recent changes while preserving samples for the other teams.
> 我接受应用排查任务。我会比较失败和成功请求、检查近期变更，并保留样本供其他团队使用。

**09 [A] / Ops Engineer：** The database engineer has acknowledged; the network page is still pending. I am tracking both response times and will escalate if the window expires.
> 数据库工程师已确认，网络呼叫仍待响应。我正在记录响应时间，超过规定窗口就会升级。

**10 [B] / Incident Manager：** I will assign a communications lead and notify business operations through the incident process. Our first stakeholder update will focus on impact and response.
> 我会指定沟通负责人，并按故障流程通知业务运营。首次干系人更新将重点说明影响和响应措施。

**11 [C] / Application Lead：** Can we set the next technical checkpoint for 14:20 UTC? That gives each track time to gather evidence without delaying an urgent mitigation proposal.
> 能把下一次技术检查点定在14:20 UTC吗？这样各排查线有时间收集证据，也不妨碍随时提出紧急缓解方案。

**12 [B] / Incident Manager：** Agreed. Escalate material changes immediately between checkpoints, and reassess severity if the measured impact changes. Recovery time remains unconfirmed at this stage.
> 同意。检查点之间如有重大变化立即升级，实测影响变化时重新评估等级。目前恢复时间仍未确认。

**可复用表达：**

- This appears to meet our major-incident criteria. — 这看来符合我们的重大故障标准。
- Ask each team to acknowledge ownership, not simply join the call. — 请每个团队确认承担责任，而不只是加入会议。

**落实动作：** 故障经理按组织标准宣布一级故障并启动流程；运维跟踪各团队接单与备用升级，14:20 UTC召开技术检查点。
**练习重点：** 练习用影响证据支持定级、确认升级接单，并根据政策安排响应。

---

<a id="s073"></a>
## 073 明确故障会议角色
**分类：** Incident 故障响应  /  **英文词数：** 283
**音频：** [播放 MP3](audio/073.mp3)  /  [英文字幕](audio/073.srt)

**背景：** 故障会议已有多人加入，指挥需要分配排查、沟通与记录责任，避免多人重复操作。

**角色：** A — Ops Engineer（运维工程师）；B — Incident Commander（故障指挥）；C — Communications Lead（沟通负责人）

**01 [B] / Incident Commander：** We now have nine people on the bridge. I will coordinate the incident; let us assign clear owners before everyone starts checking the same systems.
> 故障会议现在有九个人。我负责协调故障；大家开始检查相同系统前，先明确各项负责人。

**02 [A] / Ops Engineer：** I can lead the infrastructure track, covering compute, network reachability, and database connectivity. The application lead has agreed to own application-level investigation.
> 我可以负责基础设施排查，包括计算资源、网络连通性和数据库连接。应用负责人已同意负责应用层调查。
