**03 [C] / Communications Lead：** I will handle stakeholder communications. Who is recording the timeline, and where should I find verified facts for the next business update?
> 我负责干系人沟通。谁记录时间线？下次业务更新需要的已核实事实应从哪里获取？

**04 [A] / Ops Engineer：** Our second on-call engineer will maintain the incident document. Every entry will include the UTC timestamp, action or finding, owner, and supporting evidence.
> 第二位值班工程师会维护故障文档。每条记录将包含UTC时间戳、操作或发现、负责人及支持证据。

**05 [B] / Incident Commander：** Please keep proposed actions separate from completed actions. I need to know what actually changed before approving anything that could interact with it.
> 请把拟议操作和已完成操作分开。批准任何可能与现有变更相互影响的操作前，我需要知道实际改了什么。

**06 [A] / Ops Engineer：** Understood. Each investigation track will post findings to the shared document, but production changes will come through you under the emergency change process.
> 明白。各排查线会把发现发到共享文档，但生产变更将按紧急变更流程提交给你。

**07 [C] / Communications Lead：** I will draft updates from confirmed impact and approved actions. Can technical owners review factual details without delaying the agreed fifteen-minute communication schedule?
> 我会根据已确认影响和获批操作起草更新。技术负责人能否审核事实细节，同时不耽误约定的每十五分钟更新？

**08 [B] / Incident Commander：** Yes. Send the draft two minutes before each checkpoint. If a fact is uncertain, label it clearly instead of waiting for a complete root-cause explanation.
> 可以。每个检查点前两分钟发草稿。若某个事实不确定，就明确标注，不必等到根因完全解释清楚。

**09 [A] / Ops Engineer：** We also need one person executing each change. Otherwise, two engineers might restart overlapping components and make the result impossible to interpret safely.
> 每次变更还需要明确一位执行人，否则两位工程师可能重启相互关联的组件，导致无法可靠解释结果。

**10 [B] / Incident Commander：** Agreed. The action log will name the executor and observer, with start time, validation criteria, and rollback conditions before a change begins.
> 同意。每次变更开始前，操作记录会注明执行人、观察人、开始时间、验证标准和回退条件。

**11 [C] / Communications Lead：** The next stakeholder update is at 11:30 UTC. I will own that deadline and ask you only about material impact changes or decisions requiring approval.
> 下次干系人更新在11:30 UTC。我负责按时发送，只向你确认重大影响变化或需要批准的决定。

**12 [A] / Ops Engineer：** I will confirm the infrastructure assignments now and provide the first findings at 11:25 UTC. Any urgent risk will be raised immediately on the bridge.
> 我现在确认基础设施任务分配，并在11:25 UTC提供首轮发现。任何紧急风险都会立即在会议中提出。

**可复用表达：**

- I will handle stakeholder communications. — 我负责干系人沟通。
- Please keep proposed actions separate from completed actions. — 请把拟议操作和已完成操作分开。

**落实动作：** 故障指挥统筹批准，运维负责基础设施，应用负责人负责应用，第二值班人员记录；11:25 UTC汇报技术发现，11:30 UTC对外更新。
**练习重点：** 练习分配故障会议角色、区分拟议与已完成操作，并确认沟通节奏。

---

<a id="s074"></a>
## 074 量化影响范围
**分类：** Incident 故障响应  /  **英文词数：** 288
**音频：** [播放 MP3](audio/074.mp3)  /  [英文字幕](audio/074.srt)

**背景：** 领导需要故障影响数字，运维明确请求、用户和交易的不同口径，并与业务核实时间窗口。

**角色：** A — Ops Engineer（运维工程师）；B — Operations Director（运营总监）；C — Business Analyst（业务分析师）

**01 [B] / Operations Director：** How many users are affected, and is this a global outage? I need a reliable figure for the leadership update in ten minutes.
> 有多少用户受影响，是全球故障吗？十分钟后的领导更新需要一个可靠数字。

**02 [A] / Ops Engineer：** We have confirmed failures in the Asia-Pacific region. From 08:00 to 08:20 UTC, twelve thousand of one hundred thousand checkout requests failed.
> 我们已确认亚太区域有失败。08:00至08:20 UTC，十万次结算请求中有一万两千次失败。

**03 [B] / Operations Director：** Should I describe that as twelve thousand affected customers, or does the request count include people trying the same checkout more than once?
> 我应该说有一万两千名客户受影响吗，还是请求数包含同一个人多次尝试结算？

**04 [A] / Ops Engineer：** It includes retries, so it is not a unique customer count. We are deduplicating account and transaction identifiers, with limitations for unauthenticated sessions.
> 它包含重试，所以不是独立客户数。我们正在按账户和交易标识符去重，但未登录会话的统计存在限制。

**05 [C] / Business Analyst：** So far, we have identified forty-eight hundred distinct checkout attempts associated with those failures. Some attempts later succeeded, which changes the final business outcome.
> 目前已识别出与这些失败相关的四千八百次不同结算尝试。其中部分后来成功了，因此最终业务结果不同。

**06 [B] / Operations Director：** Can we estimate lost sales from those attempts? Multiplying by the average order value would give me a quick number for the executive team.
> 能根据这些尝试估算销售损失吗？乘以平均订单金额，就能很快给管理层一个数字。

**07 [C] / Business Analyst：** That would be a rough exposure estimate, not confirmed lost revenue. We need to reconcile later completions, abandoned carts, and any payments already captured.
> 那只能算粗略风险敞口估计，不能算已确认收入损失。需要核对后续完成、购物车放弃和已收款情况。

**08 [A] / Ops Engineer：** For now, report the failed request rate and distinct affected attempts separately. We should also state that customer counts and revenue effects remain under validation.
> 目前请分开报告失败请求率和受影响的不同尝试数，也应说明客户数量及收入影响仍在核实。

**09 [B] / Operations Director：** What about Europe and the Americas? I want to avoid calling it regional simply because we have not looked carefully at the other regions.
> 欧洲和美洲情况如何？我不希望只是因为没有仔细检查其他区域，就把它称为区域故障。

**10 [A] / Ops Engineer：** Independent probes and regional transaction metrics show no abnormal failure rate there during the same window. That supports the current regional scope, with ongoing monitoring.
> 同一窗口内，独立探测和区域交易指标显示其他区域没有异常失败率。这支持目前的区域范围判断，监控仍在持续。

**11 [C] / Business Analyst：** I will reconcile the forty-eight hundred attempts by 09:00 UTC and provide confirmed completions, unresolved outcomes, and the remaining uncertainty in the business estimate.
> 我会在09:00 UTC前核对这四千八百次尝试，列出已确认完成、结果未定及业务估算中仍存在的不确定性。

**12 [B] / Operations Director：** Good. I will use the stated twenty-minute window and measurement definitions, and make clear that the next update may revise the preliminary business numbers.
> 好的。我会使用明确的二十分钟窗口和统计定义，并说明下次更新可能修订初步业务数字。

**可复用表达：**

- It includes retries, so it is not a unique customer count. — 它包含重试，所以不是独立客户数。
- That would be a rough exposure estimate, not confirmed lost revenue. — 那只能算粗略风险敞口估计，不能算已确认收入损失。

**落实动作：** 对外分别报告二十分钟内失败请求率与不同结算尝试数；业务分析师09:00 UTC前核对最终结果，明确估算限制。
**练习重点：** 练习准确解释影响数字、拒绝不可靠换算，并说明范围、窗口和不确定性。

---

<a id="s075"></a>
## 075 建立时间线并验证假设
**分类：** Incident 故障响应  /  **英文词数：** 289
**音频：** [播放 MP3](audio/075.mp3)  /  [英文字幕](audio/075.srt)

**背景：** 故障紧随配置变更出现，团队建立时间线，并通过对照组和受控缓解检验因果假设。

**角色：** A — Ops Engineer（运维工程师）；B — Application Developer（应用开发工程师）；C — Incident Commander（故障指挥）

**01 [B] / Application Developer：** The timeout errors started right after the configuration rollout. That must be the cause, so I think we should reverse the change immediately.
> 超时错误在配置发布后马上出现，那一定就是原因，所以我认为应立即撤销变更。

**02 [A] / Ops Engineer：** The timing makes it a strong hypothesis, but it does not establish causation. Let us compare the rollout timeline with when each instance began failing.
> 从时间上看，这是重要假设，但还不能证明因果关系。我们先把发布时间线与各实例开始失败的时间对照。

**03 [C] / Incident Commander：** What evidence would confirm or weaken the hypothesis quickly? We need useful checks that do not delay a safe opportunity to reduce customer impact.
> 什么证据能快速确认或削弱这个假设？我们需要有效检查，同时不能延误安全降低客户影响的机会。

**04 [A] / Ops Engineer：** Compare updated and unchanged instances under similar traffic. Check the actual configuration values and error signatures, rather than relying only on deployment completion times.
> 比较流量相近的已更新和未更新实例。检查实际配置值及错误特征，不要只依赖部署完成时间。

**05 [B] / Application Developer：** Updated instances show eighteen percent timeouts; unchanged instances show two percent. The new configuration lowers the connection timeout from five seconds to one second.
> 已更新实例超时率为百分之十八，未更新实例为百分之二。新配置把连接超时从五秒降到一秒。

**06 [A] / Ops Engineer：** That supports a link, but traffic distribution may differ. We should check dependency latency and test the previous timeout on a small, controlled group.
> 这支持存在关联，但流量分布可能不同。应检查依赖延迟，并在小范围受控实例组上测试旧超时值。

**07 [C] / Incident Commander：** What are the risks of that test? Longer timeouts might hold connections longer, so I need capacity limits and clear stopping conditions before approval.
> 这个测试有什么风险？更长的超时可能更久占用连接，所以批准前我需要容量限制和明确停止条件。

**08 [A] / Ops Engineer：** We will limit the group to five percent of traffic, watch connection usage, and stop if saturation rises. The previous value remains within tested limits.
> 我们会把该组限制在百分之五流量，观察连接使用，饱和度升高就停止。旧值仍在已测试范围内。

**09 [B] / Application Developer：** I will execute the test after your emergency approval and record exact start times. The observer can compare outcomes against the unchanged control group.
> 获得紧急批准后我会执行测试，并记录准确开始时间。观察人员可以把结果与未改动的对照组比较。

**10 [C] / Incident Commander：** Approved under the incident change process. Review the first five minutes at 12:40 UTC, and raise any stopping condition immediately rather than waiting.
> 按故障变更流程批准。12:40 UTC评审前五分钟结果，出现任何停止条件立即提出，不要等检查点。

**11 [A] / Ops Engineer：** If errors fall in the test group, we can propose broader mitigation. We will still investigate why dependency latency increased beyond the new timeout.
> 如果测试组错误下降，可以提议扩大缓解范围。我们仍会调查依赖延迟为何升高并超过新超时值。

**12 [B] / Application Developer：** Understood. I will record the configuration change as a tested contributor only if the evidence supports it, and keep the deeper cause investigation open.
> 明白。只有证据支持时，我才会把配置变更记为经验证的促成因素，并继续调查更深层原因。

**可复用表达：**

- The timing makes it a strong hypothesis, but it does not establish causation. — 时间使它成为重要假设，但不能证明因果关系。
- Compare updated and unchanged instances under similar traffic. — 比较流量相近的已更新和未更新实例。

**落实动作：** 故障指挥批准百分之五流量受控测试，开发执行、运维观察连接饱和度；12:40 UTC评审，并继续调查依赖变慢原因。
**练习重点：** 练习区别相关与因果、提出对照验证，并区分缓解效果与完整根因。

---

<a id="s076"></a>
## 076 优先恢复与并行排查
**分类：** Incident 故障响应  /  **英文词数：** 285
**音频：** [播放 MP3](audio/076.mp3)  /  [英文字幕](audio/076.srt)

**背景：** 团队尚未确定根因，但发现可暂停非关键任务以降低业务压力，需要并行推进恢复与调查。

**角色：** A — Ops Engineer（运维工程师）；B — Application Engineer（应用工程师）；C — Incident Commander（故障指挥）

**01 [B] / Application Engineer：** We still do not know the exact root cause. Should we keep investigating before changing anything, or try to restore checkout service now?
> 我们仍不知道确切根因。应该继续调查后再做变更，还是现在就尝试恢复结算服务？

**02 [A] / Ops Engineer：** We can investigate in parallel while assessing a safe mitigation. The scheduled export workers are consuming resources in the same cluster as checkout.
> 可以并行调查，同时评估安全的缓解措施。计划导出任务正在与结算共用的集群中消耗资源。

**03 [C] / Incident Commander：** Pausing exports might help, but what evidence supports that option? I also need to understand the business cost and whether the pause is reversible.
> 暂停导出也许有帮助，但有什么证据支持？我还需要了解业务代价，以及暂停是否可逆。

**04 [A] / Ops Engineer：** Checkout queue growth began as export concurrency increased. That is not a confirmed cause, but a previous capacity test showed these workloads can compete.
> 结算队列开始增长时，导出并发也在上升。这不是已确认原因，但之前的容量测试显示两类负载可能争用资源。

**05 [B] / Application Engineer：** Exports are noncritical for the next hour and can resume from saved checkpoints. I will verify current checkpoints before proposing a pause of the workers.
> 未来一小时内导出并非关键任务，可以从保存的检查点恢复。我会先核实当前检查点，再提议暂停工作进程。

**06 [C] / Incident Commander：** Who will approve the delayed exports from the business side, and how will we tell whether pausing them actually improves the customer journey?
> 业务侧谁有权批准延迟导出？我们又将如何判断暂停后是否真的改善了客户流程？

**07 [A] / Ops Engineer：** The reporting owner has accepted a thirty-minute delay. We will measure checkout completion, queue age, and cluster pressure, with independent test orders as confirmation.
> 报表负责人已接受三十分钟延迟。我们会测量结算完成率、队列等待时长和集群压力，并用独立测试订单确认。

**08 [B] / Application Engineer：** I can lead the mitigation, while another developer preserves logs and investigates resource contention. We will capture evidence before the workers are paused.
> 我可以负责缓解，另一位开发保留日志并调查资源争用。暂停工作进程前，我们会先采集证据。

**09 [C] / Incident Commander：** Approved through the emergency process, with the reporting agreement recorded. Start with the documented pause procedure and stop if checkpoint integrity cannot be verified.
> 按紧急流程批准，并记录报表侧同意意见。使用既定暂停流程；如果无法确认检查点完整性，就停止操作。

**10 [A] / Ops Engineer：** I will observe the result for ten minutes and report at 13:10 UTC. If checkout does not improve, we will reconsider rather than assume success.
> 我会观察十分钟，并在13:10 UTC汇报。如果结算没有改善，我们会重新评估，而不是假定成功。

**11 [B] / Application Engineer：** If the service improves, should I resume exports immediately at lower concurrency, or wait until we agree on recovery and the next operating limits?
> 如果服务改善，应立即降低并发恢复导出，还是等大家确认恢复状态和下一步运行限制？

**12 [C] / Incident Commander：** Wait for that decision. Service recovery, export resumption, and root-cause confirmation are separate milestones, and each needs an owner and supporting evidence.
> 等待决定。服务恢复、导出重启和根因确认是不同里程碑，每项都需要负责人和支持证据。

**可复用表达：**

- We can investigate in parallel while assessing a safe mitigation. — 可以并行调查，同时评估安全的缓解措施。
- If checkout does not improve, we will reconsider rather than assume success. — 如果结算没有改善，我们会重新评估，而不是假定成功。

**落实动作：** 应用工程师按批准流程暂停导出并保留检查点，另一开发继续调查；运维观察十分钟，13:10 UTC验证结算恢复。
**练习重点：** 练习在根因未知时评估可逆缓解、拆分责任，并设立恢复验证检查点。

---

<a id="s077"></a>
## 077 故障期间决定回退
**分类：** Incident 故障响应  /  **英文词数：** 288
**音频：** [播放 MP3](audio/077.mp3)  /  [英文字幕](audio/077.srt)

**背景：** 新版本发布后故障指标达到回退阈值，团队核实数据库、配置和制品兼容性后申请紧急回退。

**角色：** A — Ops Engineer（运维工程师）；B — Release Lead（发布负责人）；C — Incident Commander（故障指挥）

**01 [B] / Release Lead：** The new release has exceeded the documented error threshold for rollback. Can we restore the previous version now, or are there additional checks?
> 新版本已超过既定回退错误阈值。现在能恢复旧版本吗，还是还需要额外检查？

**02 [A] / Ops Engineer：** We need to confirm database and configuration compatibility first. A binary rollback is unsafe if the older version cannot read data already written today.
> 需要先确认数据库和配置兼容性。如果旧版本无法读取今天已写入的数据，只回退程序是不安全的。

**03 [B] / Release Lead：** The schema change only added an optional column, and compatibility tests passed. I will verify that today's writes did not introduce unsupported data values.
> 结构变更只新增了一个可选字段，兼容性测试已通过。我会核实今天写入的数据没有引入旧版不支持的值。

**04 [C] / Incident Commander：** Please also confirm the previous artifact and configuration are available. I need a specific execution plan, not just confidence that rollback should work.
> 也请确认旧版制品和配置可用。我需要具体执行计划，不能只是相信回退应该有效。

**05 [A] / Ops Engineer：** The signed artifact is available and its integrity check passed. We will keep the additive schema change and avoid reversing the database migration during recovery.
> 签名制品可用，完整性检查已通过。恢复期间将保留新增结构，不逆向执行数据库迁移。

**06 [B] / Release Lead：** I verified the new records against the older reader and checked feature settings. The previous application accepts them without losing required fields or changing payment status.
> 我用旧版读取逻辑验证了新记录，并检查了功能配置。旧版可以处理，不会丢失必要字段或改变支付状态。

**07 [C] / Incident Commander：** What will the initial scope be, and what result would stop the rollback? We should avoid replacing the whole fleet before seeing evidence.
> 初始范围多大，什么结果会导致停止回退？看到证据前，应避免替换全部实例。

**08 [A] / Ops Engineer：** Start with ten percent of traffic. Stop if errors increase, data validation fails, or payment outcomes become inconsistent; retain the current version for controlled recovery.
> 先覆盖百分之十流量。错误增加、数据验证失败或支付结果不一致时停止；保留当前版本，供受控恢复使用。

**09 [B] / Release Lead：** I will execute, and the database on-call engineer will observe compatibility. We will compare completed checkouts and regional error rates for five minutes.
> 我执行，数据库值班工程师观察兼容性。我们会对比五分钟的结算完成情况和区域错误率。

**10 [C] / Incident Commander：** Approved under the emergency change process. Record the checks and approval in the incident log, then report the first result at 18:20 UTC.
> 按紧急变更流程批准。请在故障日志中记录检查和批准，然后在18:20 UTC汇报首轮结果。

**11 [A] / Ops Engineer：** We will expand only if the initial group meets the validation criteria. A successful rollback would support mitigation, but would not complete the root-cause analysis.
> 只有初始组符合验证标准才会扩大范围。回退成功能支持缓解判断，但不代表根因分析已经完成。

**12 [B] / Release Lead：** Understood. I will announce each stage on the bridge, preserve version-level evidence, and keep unrelated deployments paused until the commander reviews service stability.
> 明白。我会在会议中通报各阶段，保留版本级证据，并在指挥评审服务稳定性前暂停无关部署。

**可复用表达：**

- We need to confirm database and configuration compatibility first. — 需要先确认数据库和配置兼容性。
- We will expand only if the initial group meets the validation criteria. — 只有初始组符合验证标准才会扩大范围。

**落实动作：** 发布负责人确认旧版兼容新数据，运维验证制品及配置；批准后先回退百分之十流量，18:20 UTC评审再决定扩展。
**练习重点：** 练习提出回退前置条件、明确紧急批准，并区分回退缓解与根因确认。

---

<a id="s078"></a>
## 078 面向管理层的简明更新
**分类：** Incident 故障响应  /  **英文词数：** 287
**音频：** [播放 MP3](audio/078.mp3)  /  [英文字幕](audio/078.srt)

**背景：** 全球领导要求简明故障更新，运维围绕业务影响、当前措施、未知项和更新时间作答。

**角色：** A — Ops Engineer（运维工程师）；B — Executive Sponsor（管理层负责人）

**01 [B] / Executive Sponsor：** Please give me a concise update I can use with the regional leaders. Start with what customers cannot do and what remains available.
> 请给我一份可用于向区域领导同步的简明更新。先说明客户无法做什么，以及哪些功能仍可用。

**02 [A] / Ops Engineer：** Some customers in the Asia-Pacific region cannot create new orders. Browsing and existing order lookup remain available; we have not confirmed impact elsewhere.
> 亚太区域部分客户无法创建新订单。浏览和已有订单查询仍可用；尚未确认其他区域受到影响。

**03 [B] / Executive Sponsor：** How large is the impact, and are the figures stable enough to share? I want to avoid revising an unexplained headline number later.
> 影响有多大，数字是否足够稳定，可以分享？我希望避免之后修订一个没有说明口径的关键数字。

**04 [A] / Ops Engineer：** Twenty percent of order creation requests failed during the last fifteen minutes. That includes retries, so we are still validating the affected customer count.
> 最近十五分钟，百分之二十的订单创建请求失败。其中包含重试，所以受影响客户数量仍在核实。

**05 [B] / Executive Sponsor：** What are we doing to restore service, and is there anything you need from leadership to help the response move forward more quickly?
> 我们正在采取什么措施恢复服务？需要管理层提供什么，才能帮助响应更快推进？

**06 [A] / Ops Engineer：** The team is validating a controlled rollback after compatibility checks. Incident roles and technical support are covered; we do not need an additional decision yet.
> 团队完成兼容性检查后，正在验证受控回退。故障角色和技术支持已安排，目前还不需要额外决策。

**07 [B] / Executive Sponsor：** Can we tell customers they should retry shortly? Sales teams are asking for a recovery estimate and advice they can share without causing more confusion.
> 可以告诉客户稍后重试吗？销售团队正在询问恢复时间和可分享的建议，希望不要引起更多困惑。

**08 [A] / Ops Engineer：** Recovery time is unconfirmed. Support is preparing approved guidance, including how customers can check an order before retrying and potentially creating a duplicate.
> 恢复时间尚未确认。支持团队正在准备获批指引，包括客户如何在重试前检查订单，避免可能产生重复订单。

**09 [B] / Executive Sponsor：** Is there evidence of lost data or unexpected charges? Those concerns will matter more to the business than the details of the technical change.
> 有没有数据丢失或异常扣款的证据？业务团队会比技术变更细节更关心这些问题。

**10 [A] / Ops Engineer：** We have not confirmed either, but transaction reconciliation is ongoing. We will state that as an open validation item rather than give an unsupported assurance.
> 目前两者都未确认，但交易核对仍在进行。我们会将其列为待验证事项，不做没有依据的保证。

**11 [B] / Executive Sponsor：** Please send the next update at 15:00 UTC, even if the recovery time is still unknown. Escalate immediately if the scope or customer risk increases.
> 请在15:00 UTC发送下次更新，即使恢复时间仍未知。若范围或客户风险扩大，立即升级。

**12 [A] / Ops Engineer：** Understood. I will report current impact, progress on mitigation, remaining uncertainty, and the next checkpoint, using the same definitions in every leadership update.
> 明白。我会报告当前影响、缓解进展、剩余不确定性和下一检查点，并在每次领导更新中使用相同口径。

**可复用表达：**

- Recovery time is unconfirmed. — 恢复时间尚未确认。
- We will state that as an open validation item rather than give an unsupported assurance. — 我们会将其列为待验证事项，不做没有依据的保证。

**落实动作：** 运维15:00 UTC向管理层更新影响、缓解和未知项；支持提供获批重试指引，影响或客户风险扩大时立即升级。
**练习重点：** 练习用业务语言简明汇报，清晰表达恢复时间未定和交易核对状态。

---

<a id="s079"></a>
## 079 验证服务真正恢复
**分类：** Incident 故障响应  /  **英文词数：** 296
**音频：** [播放 MP3](audio/079.mp3)  /  [英文字幕](audio/079.srt)

**背景：** 缓解后告警恢复正常，但团队仍需验证关键业务流程，并单独跟踪延迟订单核对工作。

**角色：** A — Ops Engineer（运维工程师）；B — Application Engineer（应用工程师）；C — Business Lead（业务负责人）

**01 [B] / Application Engineer：** The alerts cleared after the mitigation, and error rates are back near baseline. Can we announce that the incident is resolved and end the bridge?
> 缓解后告警已消失，错误率也接近基线。可以宣布故障解决并结束会议了吗？

**02 [A] / Ops Engineer：** Let us verify critical user journeys, regional metrics, and backlog behavior first. An alert clearing does not prove that customers can complete their work.
> 先验证关键用户流程、区域指标和积压情况。告警消失并不能证明客户可以完成业务操作。

**03 [C] / Business Lead：** Test orders now succeed in both affected regions, and support has confirmed two customers completed checkout. However, some delayed orders still need transaction reconciliation.
> 两个受影响区域的测试订单都成功了，支持也确认两位客户完成了结算。但部分延迟订单仍需交易核对。

**04 [A] / Ops Engineer：** Please check order visibility and payment confirmation as well as creation. We need to verify the whole journey, not only the first successful response.
> 除了创建，还请检查订单可见性和支付确认。需要验证完整流程，不能只看第一个成功响应。

**05 [B] / Application Engineer：** Those checks pass. The processing queue is draining steadily, but forty orders remain in an uncertain state out of the original six hundred delayed orders.
> 这些检查都通过了。处理队列正在稳定消退，但最初六百笔延迟订单中，还有四十笔状态不确定。

**06 [C] / Business Lead：** Can we safely replay those forty orders? Customers are asking whether they should submit again, and I want to avoid duplicate orders or payments.
> 能安全重放这四十笔订单吗？客户在问是否应重新提交，我希望避免重复订单或重复付款。

**07 [A] / Ops Engineer：** Do not replay them blindly. Reconcile each order against payment records, use the approved recovery procedure, and confirm duplicate protection before any retry.
> 不要盲目重放。应逐笔与支付记录核对，使用批准的恢复流程，并在任何重试前确认防重复机制。

**08 [B] / Application Engineer：** The payments engineer will own that reconciliation, with a second reviewer. I will provide the remaining order list and current processing state in the incident record.
> 支付工程师负责核对，并安排第二位审核人员。我会在故障记录中提供剩余订单清单和当前处理状态。

**09 [A] / Ops Engineer：** For service recovery, we need the agreed thirty-minute stable period. Watch successful checkouts, regional latency, and queue age, including any new orders entering the system.
> 确认服务恢复需要满足约定的三十分钟稳定期。请观察结算成功、区域延迟和队列等待时长，包括新进入系统的订单。

**10 [C] / Business Lead：** Then the customer message can distinguish restored checkout service from the outstanding delayed orders. Please include a clear contact for people still waiting on confirmation.
> 那么客户通知可以区分结算服务已恢复与延迟订单仍待处理。请给仍在等待确认的客户提供明确联系人。

**11 [B] / Application Engineer：** The observation period ends at 16:10 UTC. I will bring the regional results then, while the payments engineer provides a separate reconciliation progress update.
> 观察期在16:10 UTC结束。我届时提供区域结果，支付工程师另行更新核对进度。

**12 [A] / Ops Engineer：** Agreed. If validation passes, we can report service recovery and keep the forty-order cleanup open with ownership, checkpoints, and customer communication until every outcome is resolved.
> 同意。如果验证通过，就可以报告服务恢复，同时继续跟踪四十笔订单清理，明确负责人、检查点和客户沟通，直至全部结果确定。

**可复用表达：**

- An alert clearing does not prove that customers can complete their work. — 告警消失并不能证明客户可以完成业务操作。
- Do not replay them blindly. — 不要盲目重放。

**落实动作：** 16:10 UTC完成三十分钟稳定观察后决定是否宣布服务恢复；支付工程师及复核人继续逐笔核对四十笔订单并同步客户。
**练习重点：** 练习验证端到端业务恢复，并把服务可用性与积压、数据核对工作分开报告。

---

<a id="s080"></a>
## 080 结束故障会议与观察交接
**分类：** Incident 故障响应  /  **英文词数：** 298
**音频：** [播放 MP3](audio/080.mp3)  /  [英文字幕](audio/080.srt)

**背景：** 服务通过观察期后准备结束持续故障会议，交接人员确认监控责任、升级条件和后续复盘安排。

**角色：** A — Ops Engineer（运维工程师）；B — Incident Commander（故障指挥）；C — Incoming On-call Engineer（接班值班工程师）

**01 [B] / Incident Commander：** The service has remained stable through the agreed sixty-minute observation period. Before we close the bridge, can we confirm monitoring ownership and remaining work?
> 服务已在约定的六十分钟观察期内保持稳定。结束会议前，能否确认监控责任和剩余工作？

**02 [A] / Ops Engineer：** Critical journeys are passing in all affected regions, and queues are back to baseline. The open reconciliation tasks have separate owners and documented checkpoints.
> 所有受影响区域的关键流程均通过，队列恢复基线。未完成核对任务已有独立负责人和书面检查点。

**03 [C] / Incoming On-call Engineer：** I am taking the next on-call shift. Please walk me through the temporary settings, what to watch, and which conditions require reopening the incident bridge.
> 我接下一班值班。请说明临时设置、需要观察的内容，以及哪些条件要求重新开启故障会议。

**04 [A] / Ops Engineer：** The export concurrency limit remains reduced under the approved mitigation. Watch checkout failures, queue age, and cluster saturation; the incident record links the exact alert thresholds.
> 根据获批缓解方案，导出并发限制仍处于降低状态。观察结算失败、队列等待时长和集群饱和度；故障记录链接了准确告警阈值。

**05 [B] / Incident Commander：** If a threshold is crossed, follow the documented escalation process and reassess impact under our policy. Do not wait for the next scheduled status update.
> 如果越过阈值，按既定升级流程处理，并根据政策重新评估影响。不要等下一次计划状态更新。

**06 [C] / Incoming On-call Engineer：** I have opened the dashboard and runbook, and I can access the incident record. Who can approve restoring the normal export settings later tonight?
> 我已打开看板和操作手册，也能访问故障记录。今晚稍后谁能批准恢复正常导出设置？

**07 [A] / Ops Engineer：** The reporting owner and service owner must review the staged resumption plan through the change process. Their contacts and the capacity evidence are attached.
> 报表负责人和服务负责人必须通过变更流程评审分阶段恢复方案。联系人和容量证据都已附上。

**08 [B] / Incident Commander：** Who will coordinate the post-incident review, and when will the team receive the evidence? I want clear follow-up ownership before people leave the call.
> 谁协调故障复盘，团队何时收到证据？大家退出会议前，我希望明确后续责任。

**09 [A] / Ops Engineer：** I will coordinate the review under our incident policy and circulate the timeline, decisions, and open hypotheses tomorrow by 12:00 UTC, with proposed attendees.
> 我会按故障政策协调复盘，明天12:00 UTC前分发时间线、决定和未验证假设，并提供建议参会人。

**10 [C] / Incoming On-call Engineer：** I accept monitoring ownership from 18:00 UTC. I will post an observation update at 19:00 UTC and escalate immediately if the documented triggers occur.
> 我接受自18:00 UTC起的监控责任。我会在19:00 UTC发布观察更新，出现既定触发条件则立即升级。

**11 [B] / Incident Commander：** With that handoff accepted, I am closing the live bridge and recording service recovery. Reconciliation and preventive actions will remain tracked until their owners complete them.
> 交接已被接受，我将结束实时故障会议并记录服务恢复。核对及预防行动仍会持续跟踪，直至负责人完成。

**12 [A] / Ops Engineer：** I will publish the closure note with the handoff, escalation contacts, and outstanding actions. The review invitation will follow once the required participants confirm availability.
> 我会发布结束说明，包含交接、升级联系人和未完成行动。必要参会人确认时间后，再发复盘邀请。

**可复用表达：**

- I accept monitoring ownership from 18:00 UTC. — 我接受自18:00 UTC起的监控责任。
- Do not wait for the next scheduled status update. — 不要等下一次计划状态更新。

**落实动作：** 接班人员18:00 UTC接受监控责任，19:00 UTC更新；指挥结束会议，运维次日12:00 UTC前分发复盘证据并持续跟踪剩余行动。
**练习重点：** 练习故障结束交接、复述再次升级条件，并明确复盘证据和责任时间。

---

<a id="s081"></a>
## 081 CAB变更评审
**分类：** 变更、发布、安全与灾备  /  **英文词数：** 258
**音频：** [播放 MP3](audio/081.mp3)  /  [英文字幕](audio/081.srt)

**背景：** 变更委员会评审应用运行环境升级，运维需要解释实施范围、风险证据与回退条件。

**角色：** A — Ops Engineer（运维工程师）；B — Change Manager（变更经理）

**01 [B] / Change Manager：** What exactly are you changing in production, and which customer functions could be affected during the proposed maintenance window?
> 这次具体要修改生产环境的什么内容？在拟定的维护窗口内，哪些客户功能可能受到影响？

**02 [A] / Ops Engineer：** We're upgrading the application runtime on six checkout servers. We'll replace one server at a time while the remaining five serve traffic.
> 我们要升级六台结账服务器的应用运行环境，每次替换一台，其余五台继续承接流量。

**03 [B] / Change Manager：** Have you demonstrated that five servers can handle the expected load, or does that assumption depend on traffic staying unusually low?
> 你们是否证明五台服务器能够承受预期负载，还是这个假设依赖流量始终异常低？

**04 [A] / Ops Engineer：** The staging load test reached projected peak traffic with five servers. CPU stayed below sixty percent, and checkout latency remained within our target.
> 预生产压测用五台服务器达到了预测峰值流量，CPU保持在百分之六十以下，结账延迟也符合目标。

**05 [B] / Change Manager：** Staging evidence helps, but production dependencies may behave differently. What will you check after replacing the first server before continuing?
> 预生产证据有帮助，但生产依赖可能表现不同。替换第一台服务器后，继续实施前会检查什么？

**06 [A] / Ops Engineer：** We'll observe it for fifteen minutes, comparing errors, latency, payment callbacks, and completed orders against the unchanged servers and the production baseline.
> 我们会观察十五分钟，将错误率、延迟、支付回调和已完成订单与未变更服务器及生产基线比较。

**07 [B] / Change Manager：** What stops the change, and can the old runtime still operate with the configuration that the upgraded version will use?
> 什么情况会停止变更？旧运行环境还能使用升级版本所用的配置吗？

**08 [A] / Ops Engineer：** A sustained breach of our approved thresholds stops the rollout. Configuration remains compatible, and we tested restoring the previous image in staging.
> 持续超过已批准的阈值就会停止推进。配置保持兼容，我们已在预生产测试恢复旧镜像。

**09 [B] / Change Manager：** Who can initiate rollback, and do you have the application owner available to confirm business recovery if that becomes necessary?
> 谁有权启动回退？如果需要回退，应用负责人是否在场确认业务恢复？

**10 [A] / Ops Engineer：** I own execution, and the designated change lead authorizes rollback under the approved plan. The application owner will validate checkout and reconciliation.
> 我负责执行，指定的变更负责人依据批准方案授权回退。应用负责人会验证结账和对账情况。

**11 [B] / Change Manager：** Attach those test results and the named contacts by November 12 at 15:00 UTC. Approval remains pending until I review the complete record.
> 请在11月12日15:00 UTC前附上测试结果和具体联系人。在我审阅完整记录之前，审批仍处于待定状态。

**12 [A] / Ops Engineer：** I'll update the change record before that checkpoint and confirm the review outcome. We won't schedule execution until the required approval is recorded.
> 我会在该检查点前更新变更记录并确认评审结果，必需审批记录齐全后才安排执行。

**可复用表达：**

- What stops the change? — 什么情况会停止变更？
- Approval remains pending until I review the complete record. — 在完整记录审阅完毕之前，审批仍待定。

**落实动作：** 运维于11月12日15:00 UTC前补齐测试、阈值和联系人；变更经理审阅后明确审批结果。
**练习重点：** 说明变更范围，以容量与业务验证证据回答风险质疑，并明确审批状态。

---

<a id="s082"></a>
## 082 发布前Go/No-Go
**分类：** 变更、发布、安全与灾备  /  **英文词数：** 258
**音频：** [播放 MP3](audio/082.mp3)  /  [英文字幕](audio/082.srt)

**背景：** 生产发布准入检查中仍缺少必需的业务验证，各团队讨论是否能够开始发布。

**角色：** A — Ops Engineer（运维工程师）；B — Release Lead（发布负责人）；C — Business Validator（业务验证负责人）

**01 [A] / Ops Engineer：** Before we decide whether to start, can each owner confirm the required approvals, monitoring coverage, recovery prerequisites, and business validation evidence?
> 在决定是否开始前，请各负责人确认必需审批、监控覆盖、恢复前提以及业务验证证据。

**02 [B] / Release Lead：** The technical checks are complete, the backup verification passed, and the previous application package is available. We're still missing business signoff.
> 技术检查已完成，备份验证通过，上一版应用包也可用。目前仍缺少业务签核。

**03 [A] / Ops Engineer：** Then we haven't met the agreed entry criteria. Which business checks remain, and who is responsible for providing the missing evidence?
> 那么我们还未满足约定准入条件。还剩哪些业务检查？由谁负责提供缺失的证据？

**04 [C] / Business Validator：** I own that validation. Standard purchases passed, but the finance team hasn't confirmed refunds involving the new tax calculation for international orders.
> 我负责这项验证。普通购买已通过，但财务团队尚未确认使用新税额计算的国际订单退款。

**05 [B] / Release Lead：** Could we release now and check refunds afterward? The feature team has already arranged support coverage for this deployment window.
> 能否先发布，再检查退款？功能团队已经为这个发布窗口安排了支持人员。

**06 [A] / Ops Engineer：** Refund validation is an explicit entry requirement because this release changes that behavior. Support coverage doesn't establish that the calculation is correct.
> 退款验证是明确的准入要求，因为本次发布改变了相关行为。安排支持人员不能证明计算正确。

**07 [C] / Business Validator：** Finance can review the examples at 13:30 UTC today. I'll provide the expected amounts, actual results, and their acceptance decision.
> 财务今天13:30 UTC可以审查这些案例。我会提供预期金额、实际结果以及他们的验收决定。

**08 [B] / Release Lead：** Our approved window closes at 15:00 UTC. If the evidence arrives late, do we still have enough time for deployment and observation?
> 已批准的窗口在15:00 UTC结束。如果证据晚到，我们还有足够时间完成部署和观察吗？

**09 [A] / Ops Engineer：** The plan requires ninety minutes including observation and rollback allowance. We'll reassess the remaining window; we cannot simply shorten those safeguards.
> 方案需要九十分钟，包含观察和预留回退时间。我们会重新评估剩余窗口，不能直接缩短这些保障环节。

**10 [C] / Business Validator：** If any refund case fails, I'll mark validation incomplete and explain the discrepancy. I won't treat a scheduled review as a passing result.
> 如果任何退款案例失败，我会标注验证未完成并说明差异，不会把已安排评审当作验证通过。

**11 [B] / Release Lead：** Agreed. The release remains on hold. I'll keep the support team informed and arrange another decision checkpoint at 13:45 UTC today.
> 同意，发布继续暂停。我会告知支持团队，并安排今天13:45 UTC再次进行决策检查。

**12 [A] / Ops Engineer：** At that checkpoint, we'll review the evidence and available time together, then record either an authorized start or a rescheduled release.
> 届时我们共同审查证据和剩余时间，记录授权启动或重新安排发布的决定。

**可复用表达：**

- We haven't met the agreed entry criteria. — 我们还未满足约定的准入条件。
- The release remains on hold. — 发布继续处于暂停状态。

**落实动作：** 业务验证负责人提交退款证据；13:45 UTC重新判断准入条件和窗口余量，未满足则改期。
**练习重点：** 在进度压力下说明准入标准，区分已安排验证与验证通过。

---

<a id="s083"></a>
## 083 灰度发布观察
**分类：** 变更、发布、安全与灾备  /  **英文词数：** 267
**音频：** [播放 MP3](audio/083.mp3)  /  [英文字幕](audio/083.srt)

**背景：** 新版本仅承接少量生产流量，团队需要判断是否已有足够证据扩大灰度范围。

**角色：** A — Ops Engineer（运维工程师）；B — Developer（开发工程师）

**01 [B] / Developer：** The canary has been running for twenty minutes without an alert. Can we move from five percent to all production traffic now?
> 灰度版本运行二十分钟，没有触发告警。能否从百分之五直接扩大到全部生产流量？

**02 [A] / Ops Engineer：** Let's review the agreed rollout stages first. We need representative traffic, comparable baseline metrics, and successful business checks before increasing its share.
> 先看约定的发布阶段。增加流量比例前，需要有代表性的流量、可比较的基线指标以及通过的业务检查。

**03 [B] / Developer：** The dashboard shows only two hundred requests on the new version. This region is quiet, and most requests are simple account lookups.
> 看板显示新版本只收到两百个请求。该区域流量较低，而且大多数是简单的账户查询。

**04 [A] / Ops Engineer：** That sample tells us little about order creation or refunds. How many completed business transactions have we observed on the canary?
> 这些样本无法充分说明订单创建或退款的情况。我们在灰度版本上观察到多少笔已完成的业务交易？

**05 [B] / Developer：** Only nine orders and no refunds. The request error rate looks normal, but one payment callback took much longer than usual.
> 只有九笔订单，没有退款。请求错误率看起来正常，但有一次支付回调比平时慢很多。

**06 [A] / Ops Engineer：** Please compare that callback with unchanged servers and check the trace. A shared payment delay would require a different interpretation than a canary regression.
> 请与未变更服务器的回调比较，并检查链路。支付系统共有的延迟，与灰度版本退化，需要作不同解读。

**07 [B] / Developer：** The same delay appears on the baseline servers, and the payment provider reported a short slowdown. Both versions completed those orders successfully.
> 基线服务器也有相同延迟，支付服务商报告了一次短暂变慢。两个版本都成功完成了相关订单。

**08 [A] / Ops Engineer：** Good. Keep that evidence in the rollout record, but continue the five percent stage until the agreed transaction sample and observation period are met.
> 好的，把证据保留在发布记录中，但继续维持百分之五阶段，直到满足约定交易样本量和观察时长。

**09 [B] / Developer：** Would synthetic refund tests help while we wait, provided they use our approved test accounts and avoid changing real customer orders?
> 等待期间可以使用合成退款测试吗？前提是使用批准的测试账户，不改动真实客户订单。

**10 [A] / Ops Engineer：** Yes, they can cover that path, alongside real traffic observations. We'll still review representativeness before approving the next twenty percent stage.
> 可以，它们能覆盖该路径，并与真实流量观察结合。批准下一阶段百分之二十之前，仍需审查样本代表性。

**11 [B] / Developer：** I'll run the approved checks and attach transaction counts and traces. Let's review again at 10:30 UTC rather than promise full rollout today.
> 我会执行批准的检查，附上交易数量和链路记录。我们10:30 UTC再评审，不承诺今天完成全量发布。

**12 [A] / Ops Engineer：** Agreed. I'll watch the stop thresholds and keep the baseline available. Any expansion will be a separate decision supported by the new evidence.
> 同意。我会关注停止阈值，保留基线版本。任何扩大发布的动作都要依据新增证据单独决策。

**可复用表达：**

- We need representative traffic. — 我们需要有代表性的流量。
- Any expansion will be a separate decision supported by the new evidence. — 任何扩大发布都需依据新增证据单独决策。

**落实动作：** 维持百分之五灰度，开发补充交易和链路证据，10:30 UTC决定是否进入百分之二十阶段。
**练习重点：** 比较灰度与基线，说明样本局限，并避免将无告警等同于可全量发布。

---

<a id="s084"></a>
## 084 数据库结构变更
**分类：** 变更、发布、安全与灾备  /  **英文词数：** 281
**音频：** [播放 MP3](audio/084.mp3)  /  [英文字幕](audio/084.srt)

**背景：** 新版本计划给大表增加字段，团队评估锁风险、分阶段迁移和应用回退的兼容性。

**角色：** A — Ops Engineer（运维工程师）；B — Database Administrator（数据库管理员）；C — Developer（开发工程师）

**01 [C] / Developer：** The new application needs an extra column on the orders table. Can we add it during deployment and remove the old column afterward?
> 新应用需要在订单表中增加一列。能否在部署时增加，然后删除旧列？

**02 [A] / Ops Engineer：** Let's separate those steps. We need the database review to cover locking, expected duration, replication impact, and compatibility with both application versions.
> 我们把这些步骤拆开。数据库评审需要涵盖锁行为、预计耗时、复制影响，以及对新旧应用版本的兼容性。

**03 [B] / Database Administrator：** The table contains eighty million rows. Our database supports an online operation for this addition, but acquiring the initial lock can still block traffic.
> 表里有八千万行。这个加列操作支持在线执行，但获取初始锁时仍可能阻塞业务流量。

**04 [C] / Developer：** What testing would show whether that lock is acceptable? A small development database completes the change almost immediately, so it hasn't revealed much.
> 怎样测试才能判断该锁是否可接受？小型开发数据库几乎立即完成变更，所以没暴露多少问题。

**05 [B] / Database Administrator：** I'll test on a representative copy with realistic concurrent writes. We'll measure lock waits, replica lag, disk usage, and the operation's cancellation behavior.
> 我会在有代表性的数据副本上模拟实际并发写入，测量锁等待、复制延迟、磁盘占用，以及取消操作时的行为。

**06 [A] / Ops Engineer：** Please define a lock timeout and explicit stop conditions. If migration stops halfway, what state should the deployment team expect to find?
> 请定义锁超时和明确的停止条件。如果迁移中途停止，部署团队应预期数据库处于什么状态？

**07 [B] / Database Administrator：** The column addition and data backfill are separate. The backfill will use restartable batches with progress records; we must inspect state before retrying.
> 加列和历史数据回填分开执行。回填使用可重启批次并记录进度；重试前必须检查当前状态。

**08 [C] / Developer：** I can make this release accept both schemas and read the new field only when populated. We'll keep writes compatible with the previous version.
> 我可以让这个版本兼容两种结构，只在新字段有值时读取它，同时保持写入与旧版本兼容。

**09 [A] / Ops Engineer：** Then application rollback can leave the added column in place. Removing the old column must wait until older versions and dependent jobs no longer need it.
> 这样应用回退时可以保留新增列。必须等旧版本及依赖任务都不再需要旧列，才能删除它。

**10 [B] / Database Administrator：** Agreed. A backup is useful recovery evidence, but restoring this entire database would be a separate, disruptive operation requiring its own recovery decision.
> 同意。备份可以提供恢复依据，但恢复整个数据库是另一项有较大影响的操作，需要独立的恢复决策。

**11 [C] / Developer：** I'll submit the compatibility tests and revised migration sequence by November 13 at 12:00 UTC, including the behavior when some rows remain unfilled.
> 我会在11月13日12:00 UTC前提交兼容性测试和修订后的迁移顺序，包括部分行尚未回填时的行为。

**12 [A] / Ops Engineer：** We'll review those results with the database tests before approval. The production plan will name a migration owner and a separate business validation owner.
> 审批前我们会结合数据库测试审阅这些结果。生产方案会指定迁移负责人和独立的业务验证负责人。

**可复用表达：**

- Let's separate those steps. — 我们把这些步骤拆开。
- We must inspect state before retrying. — 重试前必须检查当前状态。

**落实动作：** 开发与数据库管理员分别验证兼容性及锁风险，审批前明确迁移、回填、停止条件和业务验证责任。
**练习重点：** 讨论大表变更的部分完成状态，区分应用回退与数据库恢复。

---

<a id="s085"></a>
## 085 紧急变更授权
**分类：** 变更、发布、安全与灾备  /  **英文词数：** 277
**音频：** [播放 MP3](audio/085.mp3)  /  [英文字幕](audio/085.srt)

**背景：** 故障中需要修改生产超时配置，团队通过紧急变更流程明确授权、执行范围和验证方式。

**角色：** A — Ops Engineer（运维工程师）；B — Application Engineer（应用工程师）；C — Incident Commander（故障指挥官）

**01 [B] / Application Engineer：** The timeout setting looks wrong. It's just one configuration value; can I update production directly while we have everyone on the call?
> 超时配置似乎有问题。只改一个配置值，既然大家都在会上，我能直接修改生产吗？

**02 [A] / Ops Engineer：** We should use the emergency change process. A small edit can still affect many requests, so let's record the risk, authorization, and recovery steps.
> 我们应走紧急变更流程。小改动也可能影响大量请求，因此要记录风险、授权和恢复步骤。

**03 [C] / Incident Commander：** Prepare the minimum evidence for a prompt review. Under our emergency policy, I'm the designated approver, but I need a clearly bounded proposal.
> 请准备便于快速评审的最低必要证据。按本组织紧急流程，我是指定批准人，但需要范围明确的方案。

**04 [B] / Application Engineer：** The current timeout is shorter than the documented dependency requirement. I'd change one instance first and compare completed requests with the unchanged instances.
> 当前超时短于依赖服务文档要求。我建议先改一个实例，比较它和未变更实例的请求完成情况。

**05 [A] / Ops Engineer：** Increasing it could also hold connections longer. Have we checked connection capacity and whether the dependency is actually responding within the proposed limit?
> 延长超时也可能让连接占用更久。我们检查过连接容量，以及依赖是否确实能在拟议时限内响应吗？

**06 [B] / Application Engineer：** Recent traces show successful responses within that limit, and connection usage is below the reviewed threshold. I'll attach those graphs and the original value.
> 最近链路显示成功响应都在该时限内，连接使用量也低于评审阈值。我会附上图表和原始配置值。

**07 [C] / Incident Commander：** What would make you stop the trial, and who will verify that recovered requests correspond to successful business transactions rather than repeated retries?
> 什么情况会让你停止试验？谁来确认恢复的请求对应成功业务交易，而不是不断重复的重试？

**08 [A] / Ops Engineer：** We'll stop if connection saturation or transaction failures breach the documented limits. I'll monitor capacity, and the application owner will validate transaction results.
> 连接饱和度或交易失败超出记录的限值就停止。我监控容量，应用负责人验证交易结果。

**09 [B] / Application Engineer：** The previous configuration is captured, and I tested the reversal in staging. I'll record the exact execution time and avoid unrelated changes.
> 原配置已保存，我也在预生产测试了恢复操作。我会记录准确执行时间，不夹带无关修改。

**10 [C] / Incident Commander：** I approve the single-instance trial described in the emergency record. Expanding it requires a review of the observed results, not an automatic rollout.
> 我批准紧急变更记录中描述的单实例试验。扩大范围必须审查观察结果，不能自动推广。

**11 [A] / Ops Engineer：** I'll enter your approval and start with the engineer after that record is complete. Our first evidence review is at 14:20 UTC today.
> 记录完整后，我会登记您的批准，再与工程师开始执行。今天14:20 UTC进行第一次证据评审。

**12 [B] / Application Engineer：** Understood. I'll post the measured outcome at that checkpoint, and we'll retain the record for the required review after the incident is stabilized.
> 明白。我会在该检查点公布实测结果，并保留记录，用于故障稳定后按要求开展的评审。

**可复用表达：**

- A small edit can still affect many requests. — 小改动也可能影响大量请求。
- Expanding it requires a review of the observed results. — 扩大范围必须审查观察结果。

**落实动作：** 指定批准人授权单实例试验；运维登记后执行，14:20 UTC审查容量和业务结果再决定下一步。
**练习重点：** 在紧急情况下压缩流程耗时，同时清楚表达授权边界和验证标准。

---

<a id="s086"></a>
## 086 维护窗口通知
**分类：** 变更、发布、安全与灾备  /  **英文词数：** 283
**音频：** [播放 MP3](audio/086.mp3)  /  [英文字幕](audio/086.srt)

**背景：** 运维与全球业务负责人核对维护通知，避免把整个维护窗口误写为持续中断时间。

**角色：** A — Ops Engineer（运维工程师）；B — Business Lead（业务负责人）

**01 [A] / Ops Engineer：** Maintenance is planned for December 5, 2026, from 01:00 to 02:00 UTC. I'd like to confirm the business impact before we send the notice.
> 维护计划在2026年12月5日01:00至02:00 UTC进行。发送通知前，我想确认业务影响。

**02 [B] / Business Lead：** Will customers lose access for the entire hour? Our regional teams need a clear answer because some customers will still be working.
> 客户会整整一小时无法访问吗？部分客户届时仍在工作，各区域团队需要明确答复。

**03 [A] / Ops Engineer：** The hour is the maintenance window. We expect one interruption of up to five minutes during the connection switch, although actual duration could vary.
> 这一小时是维护窗口。预计连接切换期间会中断一次，最长约五分钟，但实际持续时间可能不同。

**04 [B] / Business Lead：** Which functions will be affected? Saying the whole platform is unavailable would cause unnecessary cancellations if most services remain accessible throughout the work.
> 哪些功能会受影响？如果大部分服务始终可用，宣称整个平台不可用会造成不必要的取消。

**05 [A] / Ops Engineer：** New order submission and order updates may fail briefly. Product browsing and existing order history should remain available, and we'll monitor those functions too.
> 新订单提交和订单更新可能短暂失败。商品浏览和历史订单查询预计仍可用，我们也会监控这些功能。

**06 [B] / Business Lead：** What should users do if they see an error after submitting an order? Repeated submissions could create confusion or duplicate business requests.
> 如果用户提交订单后看到错误，该怎么处理？反复提交可能造成混乱或重复业务请求。

**07 [A] / Ops Engineer：** The notice will ask them to check order history first and contact support if the result is unclear, rather than repeatedly submitting the same order.
> 通知会请用户先检查订单历史，结果不明确时联系支持，而不是重复提交相同订单。

**08 [B] / Business Lead：** Please include verified regional times as well. Last time, a copied conversion was wrong because one region had changed its daylight saving schedule.
> 请同时附上核实过的各区域时间。上次复制的时间换算有误，因为其中一个区域调整了夏令时。

**09 [A] / Ops Engineer：** I'll use the exact UTC date and window as the reference. Regional coordinators will verify local conversions before those versions are distributed.
> 我会以准确的UTC日期和窗口为准，各区域协调人会在发送对应版本前核实当地时间换算。

**10 [B] / Business Lead：** Who will provide updates if the interruption exceeds expectations, and where should business teams check whether the service is ready to use again?
> 如果中断超过预期，谁负责更新？业务团队应在哪里确认服务可以重新使用？

**11 [A] / Ops Engineer：** The service desk will publish updates on the approved status page. I'll coordinate technical updates, and the business validator will confirm successful order submission.
> 服务台会在批准的状态页面发布更新。我协调技术信息，业务验证负责人确认订单能够成功提交。

**12 [B] / Business Lead：** Good. Send me the revised draft by November 30 at 12:00 UTC. I'll confirm regional contacts and approve distribution after we resolve any conflicts.
> 好的。请在11月30日12:00 UTC前发来修订稿。我会确认区域联系人，解决冲突后批准发送。

**可复用表达：**

- The hour is the maintenance window. — 这一小时是维护窗口。
- I'd like to confirm the business impact before we send the notice. — 发送通知前，我想确认业务影响。

**落实动作：** 运维在11月30日12:00 UTC前提交通知稿，业务负责人核对区域时间、联系人及冲突后批准发送。
**练习重点：** 区分窗口和预计中断，准确说明受影响功能、用户操作和更新渠道。

---

<a id="s087"></a>
## 087 高危漏洞修复安排
**分类：** 变更、发布、安全与灾备  /  **英文词数：** 289
**音频：** [播放 MP3](audio/087.mp3)  /  [英文字幕](audio/087.srt)

**背景：** 安全团队要求修复新披露的严重漏洞，运维需要先核实受影响资产，再按风险安排补丁和例外。

**角色：** A — Ops Engineer（运维工程师）；B — Security Engineer（安全工程师）

**01 [B] / Security Engineer：** A critical vulnerability was disclosed this morning. Management wants a patching schedule today, especially for systems reachable from the internet or partner networks.
> 今天早上披露了一个严重漏洞。管理层希望今天拿到补丁计划，尤其关注互联网或合作伙伴网络可访问的系统。

**02 [A] / Ops Engineer：** We're matching the vendor advisory against our asset inventory and package versions. Severity alone doesn't tell us which systems have the vulnerable component enabled.
> 我们正用厂商公告核对资产清单和软件包版本。仅凭严重级别，无法知道哪些系统启用了存在漏洞的组件。

**03 [B] / Security Engineer：** Understood. Prioritize confirmed exposure and service impact, and flag anything that cannot meet the remediation deadline defined in our security policy.
> 明白。请按已确认的暴露面和服务影响排序，对无法满足本组织安全政策整改期限的情况作出标记。

**04 [A] / Ops Engineer：** We found twelve potentially affected hosts so far. Four serve external traffic; eight are internal, and we're still verifying whether the affected feature is active.
> 目前发现十二台可能受影响的主机。其中四台承接外部流量，八台在内部，我们仍在核实受影响功能是否启用。

**05 [B] / Security Engineer：** Can you patch the external hosts first without interrupting all traffic, and what evidence will show the fix is actually effective after installation?
> 能否优先修补外部主机，同时避免全部流量中断？安装后用什么证据证明修复确实有效？

**06 [A] / Ops Engineer：** We'll test the vendor package, then use an approved rolling plan where capacity allows. We'll verify versions, approved vulnerability checks, and normal business transactions.
> 我们先测试厂商补丁包，再在容量允许时按批准方案滚动实施，并核验版本、批准的漏洞检查及正常业务交易。

**07 [B] / Security Engineer：** One internal host supports an older reporting application. The owner says the vendor patch isn't certified yet, so that may need a formal exception.
> 有台内部主机支持旧版报表应用。负责人说厂商补丁尚未完成兼容认证，因此可能需要正式例外。

**08 [A] / Ops Engineer：** I'll ask that owner for the compatibility issue and test timeline. Any temporary control needs a security review, a named risk owner, and an expiration date.
> 我会请负责人说明兼容问题和测试时间。任何临时控制都需要安全评审、指定风险负责人和到期日。

**09 [B] / Security Engineer：** We can review restricted network access as a temporary mitigation if it fits the advisory. That would reduce exposure but would not count as permanent remediation.
> 如果符合公告建议，我们可以评审限制网络访问作为临时缓解。这能减少暴露面，但不能算永久整改。

**10 [A] / Ops Engineer：** Agreed. We'll also check newly discovered assets against the same inventory criteria, so an incomplete initial list doesn't become a false completion report.
> 同意。我们也会按同样清单标准检查新发现资产，避免把初始清单不完整误报为全部完成。

**11 [B] / Security Engineer：** Please provide the first asset-level status at 16:00 UTC today, including unknowns, patch owners, validation results, and any exception requests needing my review.
> 请在今天16:00 UTC提供首份逐资产状态，包含未知项、补丁负责人、验证结果及需要我审查的例外申请。

**12 [A] / Ops Engineer：** I'll deliver that checkpoint and update the schedule as testing confirms feasibility. We will report remaining exposure explicitly until remediation or approved exception handling is complete.
> 我会按时提交，并随测试确认可行性更新计划。在整改或批准的例外处置完成前，我们会明确报告剩余暴露风险。

**可复用表达：**

- Prioritize confirmed exposure and service impact. — 按已确认的暴露面和服务影响确定优先级。
- That would reduce exposure but would not count as permanent remediation. — 这能减少暴露面，但不能算永久整改。

**落实动作：** 16:00 UTC提交逐资产整改状态；优先处理确认暴露资产，对暂缓补丁记录风险负责人、临时控制及例外到期日。
**练习重点：** 描述风险排序和资产未知项，区分补丁修复、临时缓解与正式例外。

---

<a id="s088"></a>
## 088 疑似安全事件协调
**分类：** 变更、发布、安全与灾备  /  **英文词数：** 286
**音频：** [播放 MP3](audio/088.mp3)  /  [英文字幕](audio/088.srt)

**背景：** 一台生产主机出现异常登录及可疑进程，运维联系安全响应人员协调证据保全和受控隔离。

**角色：** A — Ops Engineer（运维工程师）；B — Security Responder（安全响应人员）；C — Service Owner（服务负责人）

**01 [A] / Ops Engineer：** We detected unusual logins and an unexpected process on one production host at 09:10 UTC. I have opened a restricted security incident record.
> 09:10 UTC，我们在一台生产主机发现异常登录和非预期进程。我已建立限制访问的安全事件记录。

**02 [B] / Security Responder：** Please preserve the relevant evidence and avoid uncoordinated changes. Have you recorded the host identifier, alert source, and timestamps without sharing sensitive details broadly?
> 请保留相关证据，避免未经协调的修改。是否已记录主机标识、告警来源和时间戳，并避免广泛传播敏感细节？

**03 [A] / Ops Engineer：** Yes. Those details are in the approved incident channel. I haven't rebooted the host or removed files because that could affect the investigation.
> 已经记录，信息都在批准的事件渠道中。我没有重启主机或删除文件，因为那可能影响调查。

**04 [C] / Service Owner：** That host handles part of our order traffic. If isolation is needed, can we remove it from service while the remaining hosts support demand?
> 该主机处理部分订单流量。如果需要隔离，能否先将其移出服务，由其余主机支撑需求？

**05 [A] / Ops Engineer：** Current capacity suggests that is feasible, but I'll verify active connections and pending work first. We also need to identify any unique local state.
> 当前容量看起来允许，但我会先核查活动连接和待处理任务，也需要确认有无独有的本地状态。

**06 [B] / Security Responder：** Follow the evidence collection steps in our response runbook using the approved tooling. I will decide the containment sequence with the incident lead.
> 请使用批准的工具，按响应手册执行证据采集步骤。我会与事件负责人决定控制措施的顺序。

**07 [C] / Service Owner：** The service team can monitor order completion during the change. Please tell us which customer symptoms to watch and whether any queued work needs reconciliation.
> 服务团队可以在操作期间监控订单完成情况。请说明应关注哪些客户症状，以及是否有排队任务需要对账。

**08 [A] / Ops Engineer：** I'll track failed submissions, completion delays, and the affected host's pending jobs. We won't replay transactions until we know their original processing status.
> 我会跟踪提交失败、完成延迟及该主机待处理任务。确认原交易处理状态前，不会重放交易。

**09 [B] / Security Responder：** The initial assessment supports isolating this host under our response procedure. Preserve access through the approved investigation path, and record each containment action.
> 初步评估支持按响应流程隔离该主机。保留批准的调查访问路径，并记录每项控制动作。

**10 [C] / Service Owner：** I acknowledge the service impact and will coordinate traffic removal with Ops. We'll validate that customers can still complete orders after the isolation.
> 我确认服务影响，并会与运维协调移除流量。隔离后，我们验证客户是否仍能完成订单。

**11 [A] / Ops Engineer：** I'll execute the coordinated steps and log the results. Our next restricted update is at 09:40 UTC, including capacity, business checks, and pending evidence tasks.
> 我会执行协调后的步骤并记录结果。09:40 UTC在受限渠道更新容量、业务检查和待完成证据任务。

**12 [B] / Security Responder：** Thank you. We'll communicate only confirmed facts and keep the scope under investigation. Suspicious activity alone does not establish the full extent of compromise.
> 谢谢。我们只传达已确认事实，事件范围仍在调查中。仅凭可疑活动无法确定受入侵的完整范围。

**可复用表达：**

- Please preserve the relevant evidence and avoid uncoordinated changes. — 请保留相关证据，避免未经协调的修改。
- We'll communicate only confirmed facts. — 我们只传达已确认事实。

**落实动作：** 按安全响应授权执行证据保全和单主机隔离，验证订单业务连续性，09:40 UTC在受限渠道更新。
**练习重点：** 用确认事实报告安全异常，协调证据、隔离权限和业务影响。

---

<a id="s089"></a>
## 089 灾备演练与RTO/RPO
**分类：** 变更、发布、安全与灾备  /  **英文词数：** 291
**音频：** [播放 MP3](audio/089.mp3)  /  [英文字幕](audio/089.srt)

**背景：** 全球团队准备灾备演练，明确恢复时间、可恢复数据时间点、业务验收及回切要求。

**角色：** A — Ops Engineer（运维工程师）；B — Recovery Manager（灾备负责人）；C — Business Lead（业务负责人）

**01 [B] / Recovery Manager：** What evidence will demonstrate a successful disaster recovery exercise? I want criteria that cover business service, not just servers starting in the recovery region.
> 什么证据能证明灾备演练成功？我希望标准覆盖业务服务，而不仅是灾备区域服务器启动。

**02 [A] / Ops Engineer：** The approved objectives are a sixty-minute recovery time objective and a five-minute recovery point objective. We'll measure both using the documented exercise boundaries.
> 批准目标是恢复时间目标六十分钟、恢复点目标五分钟。我们会按记录的演练边界衡量这两个指标。

**03 [C] / Business Lead：** Please explain when that recovery clock starts and stops. Business teams may define recovery differently if customers can log in but cannot finish transactions.
> 请解释恢复计时的起止点。如果客户可以登录却无法完成交易，业务团队对恢复的定义可能不同。

**04 [A] / Ops Engineer：** For this exercise, timing starts at the simulated disaster declaration and stops when the agreed business checks pass, including order submission and payment confirmation.
> 本次演练从模拟宣布灾难时开始计时，到约定业务检查通过时结束，包括订单提交和支付确认。

**05 [B] / Recovery Manager：** How will we verify the recovery point? A healthy replication indicator alone may miss transactions that were acknowledged but never reached the recovery site.
> 如何验证恢复点？复制状态健康这一指标可能遗漏已确认、却从未到达灾备站点的交易。

**06 [A] / Ops Engineer：** We'll compare transaction identifiers and durable timestamps with the source record captured at declaration. Any gap must be reconciled and evaluated against the five-minute objective.
> 我们会将交易标识和持久化时间戳与宣布灾难时保存的源记录比较。任何缺口都要对账，并对照五分钟目标评估。

**07 [C] / Business Lead：** My team will validate representative orders and check downstream accounting. Who will verify identity access, network routing, external integrations, and monitoring in the recovery region?
> 我的团队验证有代表性的订单并检查下游记账。谁来验证灾备区域的身份访问、网络路由、外部集成和监控？

**08 [A] / Ops Engineer：** Each dependency has a named owner in the exercise checklist. We'll confirm their availability before starting and record limitations where the test uses simulated integrations.
> 演练清单为每个依赖指定了负责人。开始前确认他们可参与，并记录使用模拟集成时的测试局限。

**09 [B] / Recovery Manager：** We also need a safe return plan. Switching back before data synchronization is verified could create inconsistent records or lose transactions written during recovery operations.
> 我们还需要安全回切方案。在数据同步验证前回切，可能导致记录不一致，或丢失灾备运行期间写入的交易。

**10 [A] / Ops Engineer：** Agreed. Failback requires separate checks, reconciliation, and authorization. We'll keep the recovered service active until the return conditions in the runbook are satisfied.
> 同意。回切需要独立检查、对账和授权。在满足手册规定的回切条件前，我们保持灾备服务运行。

**11 [C] / Business Lead：** Please send the checklist by November 18 at 14:00 UTC. I'll confirm the business validators and the exact transactions they need to inspect.
> 请在11月18日14:00 UTC前发出清单。我会确认业务验证人员及其需要检查的具体交易。

**12 [B] / Recovery Manager：** We'll review readiness at that checkpoint. After the exercise, report actual recovery time, measured data gaps, business results, and any limits on what was demonstrated.
> 届时评审准备情况。演练后报告实际恢复耗时、测得的数据缺口、业务结果以及所证明能力的局限。

**可复用表达：**

- Please explain when that recovery clock starts and stops. — 请解释恢复计时的起止点。
- Failback requires separate checks, reconciliation, and authorization. — 回切需要独立检查、对账和授权。

**落实动作：** 11月18日14:00 UTC评审演练清单，依据明确定义的RTO、RPO和业务检查衡量恢复，回切另行验证授权。
**练习重点：** 清楚解释RTO与RPO的测量口径，区分技术启动、业务恢复和安全回切。

---

<a id="s090"></a>
## 090 审计证据与权限复核
**分类：** 变更、发布、安全与灾备  /  **英文词数：** 286
**音频：** [播放 MP3](audio/090.mp3)  /  [英文字幕](audio/090.srt)

**背景：** 审计抽样核查生产访问的审批、复核与撤销证据，运维发现一项撤销记录存在缺口。

**角色：** A — Ops Engineer（运维工程师）；B — Auditor（审计人员）

**01 [B] / Auditor：** For these five sampled production accounts, can you show who approved access, what role was assigned, and when the entitlement was last reviewed?
> 针对抽样的五个生产账户，能否证明谁批准了访问、分配了什么角色，以及最近何时复核权限？

**02 [A] / Ops Engineer：** I'll map each account to its access request, named approver, assigned role, review history, and relevant access logs for the audit period.
> 我会把每个账户对应到访问申请、具体批准人、所分配角色、复核历史，以及审计期间相关的访问日志。

**03 [B] / Auditor：** Please distinguish the approved role from the permissions actually active during the sample period. An approval document alone doesn't establish that the implementation matched it.
> 请区分获批角色和抽样期间实际生效的权限。仅有审批文档不能证明实施情况与批准内容一致。

**04 [A] / Ops Engineer：** Understood. I'll include the role assignment history and compare it with the authorized scope. Where historical evidence is unavailable, I'll identify the gap explicitly.
> 明白。我会附上角色分配历史，与授权范围比较。如果历史证据不可用，我会明确标明缺口。

**05 [B] / Auditor：** How do you remove expired contractor access, and how do you verify that disabling an account also ends existing production sessions where required?
> 如何移除已到期的外包人员访问？在有要求的情况下，如何验证禁用账户也终止了现有生产会话？

**06 [A] / Ops Engineer：** The offboarding workflow triggers account suspension and role removal. The access team also applies the required session revocation and records its verification results.
> 离职流程触发账户停用和角色移除。权限团队还会执行规定的会话撤销，并记录验证结果。

**07 [B] / Auditor：** One sampled contractor ended work on October 30, but I only see a ticket closed on November 2. What happened between those dates?
> 有位抽样外包人员10月30日结束工作，但我只看到11月2日关闭的工单。这两天之间发生了什么？

**08 [A] / Ops Engineer：** The account was disabled on October 30, according to the identity log. However, I haven't found evidence confirming the separate production role removal time.
> 身份系统日志显示账户在10月30日已禁用。但我还没找到证据确认独立生产角色的移除时间。

**09 [B] / Auditor：** Please investigate that distinction. Don't infer that a late ticket closure means late revocation, but don't assume all permissions ended with the account either.
> 请调查这个区别。不要因为工单关闭晚就推断撤权晚，也不要假设所有权限都随账户禁用而结束。

**10 [A] / Ops Engineer：** I'll ask the access owner to retrieve the system records. We'll document any actual control failure and corrective action without backdating approvals or removal evidence.
> 我会请权限负责人调取系统记录。如存在实际控制失效，我们会记录并安排整改，不倒填审批或撤权证据。

**11 [B] / Auditor：** Provide the evidence index through the restricted audit folder by November 13 at 16:00 UTC. Limit unrelated personal information and preserve the original records.
> 请在11月13日16:00 UTC前通过受限审计文件夹提交证据索引，减少无关个人信息并保留原始记录。

**12 [A] / Ops Engineer：** I'll deliver the indexed sample and a separate list of unresolved gaps by that deadline. The control owner will own follow-up on any confirmed finding.
> 我会在期限前提交带索引的样本，以及单独的未解决缺口清单。控制措施负责人负责跟进已确认发现。

**可复用表达：**

- I'll identify the gap explicitly. — 我会明确标明缺口。
- Please distinguish the approved role from the permissions actually active during the sample period. — 请区分获批角色和抽样期间实际生效的权限。

**落实动作：** 运维在11月13日16:00 UTC前提供受限证据索引，权限负责人核实撤权时间并负责已确认缺口的整改。
**练习重点：** 依据记录说明权限全生命周期，区分审批、实际权限、账户禁用和撤权证据。

---

<a id="s091"></a>
## 091 故障复盘与根因说明
**分类：** Follow-up 复盘与闭环  /  **英文词数：** 290
**音频：** [播放 MP3](audio/091.mp3)  /  [英文字幕](audio/091.srt)

**背景：** 配置故障恢复后，团队复盘触发因素、影响扩大的条件，以及如何验证防复发措施有效。

**角色：** A — Ops Engineer（运维工程师）；B — Review Lead（复盘负责人）

**01 [B] / Review Lead：** What triggered yesterday's connection failures, and which conditions allowed the impact to spread? Please separate confirmed evidence from assumptions in the incident review.
> 什么触发了昨天的连接失败？哪些条件让影响扩大？请在复盘中区分已确认的证据和假设。

**02 [A] / Ops Engineer：** A configuration change replaced the database hostname with an invalid value. The deployment record and failed connection logs establish the sequence across affected instances.
> 一次配置变更把数据库主机名替换为无效值。部署记录和连接失败日志证明了受影响实例上的事件顺序。

**03 [B] / Review Lead：** Was that simply an editing mistake, or did a missing safeguard allow an otherwise detectable error to reach production and affect every instance?
> 这只是编辑错误，还是缺少防护措施，让本来可检测的错误进入生产并影响了所有实例？

**04 [A] / Ops Engineer：** The editor introduced the value, but our validation checked only file syntax. It didn't verify the destination, and the deployment updated all instances together.
> 编辑时引入了该值，但验证只检查文件语法，没有校验目标地址，而且部署同时更新了所有实例。

**05 [B] / Review Lead：** How confident are we that reverting the configuration explains recovery? Were any unrelated changes made at approximately the same time that could confuse that conclusion?
> 我们有多大把握认为恢复来自配置还原？同一时间附近是否有其他变更，可能影响这个结论？

**06 [A] / Ops Engineer：** The timeline shows no other production changes during recovery. Connections resumed after the previous value was restored, and staging reproduced the same failure with the invalid value.
> 时间线显示恢复期间没有其他生产变更。恢复旧值后连接恢复，预生产也用无效值重现了同样故障。

**07 [B] / Review Lead：** That supports the causal explanation. What delayed detection, and why didn't the deployment stop before the configuration reached the remaining instances in production?
> 这支持因果解释。什么延误了发现？配置到达其余生产实例前，部署为何没有停止？

**08 [A] / Ops Engineer：** The existing check tested process health without a database operation. We also lacked a staged rollout gate, so healthy processes masked failed customer requests.
> 原有检查只测试进程健康，没有数据库操作。我们也缺少分阶段发布关卡，因此进程健康掩盖了客户请求失败。

**09 [B] / Review Lead：** Let's define separate improvements for configuration validation, deployment controls, and monitoring. Naming a person who made the edit won't address those system gaps.
> 我们分别定义配置验证、部署控制和监控改进。指出谁编辑了配置，无法解决这些系统性缺口。

**10 [A] / Ops Engineer：** I'll own destination validation and a staged production check. The monitoring owner will add a representative transaction probe, with acceptance based on replaying this failure.
> 我负责目标地址验证和分阶段生产检查。监控负责人增加有代表性的交易探测，以重放本次故障作为验收依据。

**11 [B] / Review Lead：** Please attach the reproduction evidence and draft acceptance criteria by November 16 at 15:00 UTC. We'll confirm owners and dates at that review.
> 请在11月16日15:00 UTC前附上重现证据和验收标准草案，届时确认负责人及期限。

**12 [A] / Ops Engineer：** I'll also document the residual risks. We can say the cause is supported by evidence, while prevention remains pending until the new controls pass validation.
> 我还会记录剩余风险。可以说明原因有证据支持，但新控制通过验证之前，防复发工作仍未完成。

**可复用表达：**

- Please separate confirmed evidence from assumptions. — 请区分已确认的证据和假设。
- Naming a person who made the edit won't address those system gaps. — 指出谁编辑了配置，无法解决这些系统性缺口。

**落实动作：** 11月16日15:00 UTC审阅因果证据和验收标准，分别落实配置、发布与监控改进，并通过故障重放验证。
**练习重点：** 说明触发因素和系统缺口，用时间线与重现支撑因果判断，避免归咎个人。

---

<a id="s092"></a>
## 092 根因尚未确认
**分类：** Follow-up 复盘与闭环  /  **英文词数：** 279
**音频：** [播放 MP3](audio/092.mp3)  /  [英文字幕](audio/092.srt)

**背景：** 重启恢复了服务，但没有证据证明内存泄漏，团队需要给出诚实且有期限的调查计划。

**角色：** A — Ops Engineer（运维工程师）；B — Engineering Manager（工程经理）

**01 [B] / Engineering Manager：** The restart restored service, and memory usage dropped afterward. Can we write memory leakage as the root cause and finish the incident report?
> 重启恢复了服务，之后内存使用也下降了。能否把内存泄漏写成根因，完成故障报告？

**02 [A] / Ops Engineer：** Not yet. A restart clears several types of state, so the lower memory usage doesn't establish a leak. The underlying cause remains unconfirmed.
> 还不能。重启会清除多种状态，所以内存下降不能证明泄漏。底层原因仍未确认。

**03 [B] / Engineering Manager：** What do we know for certain, and how can we communicate progress without leaving readers with the impression that we have no investigation plan?
> 哪些情况已经确定？我们怎样传达进展，避免让读者以为我们没有调查计划？

**04 [A] / Ops Engineer：** We confirmed rising response times, stalled worker threads, and recovery after restarting two instances. We haven't established why those workers stopped completing requests.
> 已确认响应时间升高、工作线程停滞，以及两个实例重启后恢复，但尚未确定这些线程为何无法完成请求。

**05 [B] / Engineering Manager：** Do the available logs support other hypotheses, such as a blocked dependency or connection pool exhaustion, rather than an issue with memory allocation?
> 现有日志是否支持其他假设，比如依赖阻塞或连接池耗尽，而不是内存分配问题？

**06 [A] / Ops Engineer：** Both remain plausible. Connection waits increased, but the logs don't show whether that was the initiating problem or a consequence of stalled workers.
> 两种情况仍有可能。连接等待增加了，但日志无法说明这是初始问题，还是线程停滞带来的结果。

**07 [B] / Engineering Manager：** What evidence should we collect next time, and can we gather it without delaying recovery for users if the same symptoms return?
> 下次应采集什么证据？如果相同症状再次出现，能否在不延误用户恢复的情况下完成采集？

**08 [A] / Ops Engineer：** We'll add approved thread and memory diagnostics with bounded collection time. The runbook will prioritize recovery and specify when to stop collecting evidence.
> 我们会增加批准的线程和内存诊断，并限制采集时长。手册优先保障恢复，也会说明何时停止采集证据。

**09 [B] / Engineering Manager：** Please make the investigation finite. Who will examine the existing evidence, and when should we decide whether additional testing is worth the effort?
> 请给调查设定期限。谁负责检查现有证据？何时判断是否值得继续投入额外测试？

**10 [A] / Ops Engineer：** The application owner will review thread samples, and I'll correlate dependency metrics. We'll present findings and a recommendation on November 17 at 14:00 UTC.
> 应用负责人审阅线程样本，我关联依赖指标。我们会在11月17日14:00 UTC提交发现及建议。

**11 [B] / Engineering Manager：** Until then, the report should state that service recovered, monitoring continues, and root-cause analysis remains open. Avoid promising a confirmed cause by that date.
> 在此之前，报告应说明服务已恢复、监控持续进行、根因分析仍未结束。不要承诺到该日期一定能确认原因。

**12 [A] / Ops Engineer：** Agreed. I'll document the confirmed symptoms, effective mitigation, competing hypotheses, and evidence gaps. At the checkpoint, we'll decide the next investigation step explicitly.
> 同意。我会记录已确认症状、有效缓解、不同假设和证据缺口，并在检查点明确决定下一步调查动作。

**可复用表达：**

- The underlying cause remains unconfirmed. — 底层原因仍未确认。
- We haven't established why those workers stopped completing requests. — 我们尚未确定这些线程为何无法完成请求。

**落实动作：** 应用负责人和运维分工分析线程及依赖证据，11月17日14:00 UTC汇报并决定是否继续调查。
**练习重点：** 准确表达未知状态，避免把重启有效等同于根因确认，并设置调查检查点。

---

<a id="s093"></a>
## 093 落实行动负责人和期限
**分类：** Follow-up 复盘与闭环  /  **英文词数：** 288
**音频：** [播放 MP3](audio/093.mp3)  /  [英文字幕](audio/093.srt)

**背景：** 复盘提出告警与操作手册改进，运维推动团队把方向变成可验收、有负责人和期限的独立行动。

**角色：** A — Ops Engineer（运维工程师）；B — Review Lead（复盘负责人）；C — Monitoring Lead（监控负责人）

**01 [B] / Review Lead：** We've agreed to improve alerting and update the recovery runbook. Can I record one action saying the operations team will handle both items?
> 我们已同意改进告警并更新恢复手册。我能否记成一项行动，由运维团队处理两项内容？

**02 [A] / Ops Engineer：** Let's split them into separate actions with one accountable owner each. Otherwise, a partial update could make the whole improvement appear complete.
> 我们把它们拆成独立行动，每项指定一名最终负责人，否则部分更新可能让整项改进看起来已经完成。

**03 [C] / Monitoring Lead：** I'll own the alerting action. The current rule reacts only when the queue is already full, so I propose detecting sustained processing delays earlier.
> 告警行动由我负责。当前规则只在队列已满时响应，所以我建议更早检测持续的处理延迟。

**04 [A] / Ops Engineer：** What proves completion? We should specify the failure to replay, the maximum detection time, and how we confirm the notification reaches the correct on-call engineer.
> 用什么证明完成？应明确重放哪种故障、最长发现时间，以及如何确认通知到达正确的值班工程师。

**05 [C] / Monitoring Lead：** In the test, we'll stop a worker and replay representative traffic. The alert must fire within two minutes and reach the assigned on-call route.
> 测试中我们会停止一个工作进程并重放有代表性的流量。告警必须在两分钟内触发，并到达指定值班通知渠道。

**06 [B] / Review Lead：** That covers detection, but could the rule also page constantly during normal low traffic? Please include evidence that it handles expected quiet periods appropriately.
> 这涵盖了发现能力，但规则会不会在正常低流量时不断通知？也请证明它能够正确处理预期的低谷时段。

**07 [C] / Monitoring Lead：** Agreed. I'll test the quiet-period behavior and attach both results. I can commit to a reviewed rule by November 18 at 12:00 UTC.
> 同意。我会测试低谷时段的行为，并附上两类结果。我承诺11月18日12:00 UTC前提交完成评审的规则。

**08 [A] / Ops Engineer：** I'll own the runbook action separately. Acceptance will require another engineer to follow the steps, identify the stop condition, and complete the recovery check.
> 手册行动单独由我负责。验收要求另一名工程师照步骤操作、识别停止条件，并完成恢复检查。

**09 [B] / Review Lead：** Who will review that exercise, and does your due date depend on the alerting change being finished first? We should show any sequencing explicitly.
> 谁来审阅这次演练？你的期限是否取决于先完成告警修改？我们应明确显示先后依赖关系。

**10 [A] / Ops Engineer：** The service lead will review it. The recovery steps can be validated independently, but I'll link the final alert reference after the monitoring review.
> 服务负责人负责审阅。恢复步骤可独立验证，监控评审后我再链接最终告警引用。

**11 [C] / Monitoring Lead：** I will share the proposed alert details early so the runbook doesn't wait unnecessarily. Any change to our commitment will be recorded with the reason.
> 我会提前分享拟定告警的详细信息，避免手册无谓等待。如承诺有变化，会连同原因一起记录。

**12 [B] / Review Lead：** I'll create two tracked actions with those owners, criteria, and dependencies. We'll check progress on November 16 and keep completion evidence attached to each record.
> 我会创建两项跟踪行动，写明负责人、标准和依赖。11月16日检查进展，每项记录都附上完成证据。

**可复用表达：**

- What proves completion? — 用什么证明完成？
- Let's split them into separate actions with one accountable owner each. — 我们把它们拆成独立行动，每项指定一名最终负责人。

**落实动作：** 复盘负责人创建告警和手册两个行动项，11月16日检查进度；监控负责人11月18日12:00 UTC前提交规则及测试证据。
**练习重点：** 把模糊改进转成独立任务，确认最终负责人、期限、依赖和验收证据。

---

<a id="s094"></a>
## 094 礼貌催办逾期任务
**分类：** Follow-up 复盘与闭环  /  **英文词数：** 290
**音频：** [播放 MP3](audio/094.mp3)  /  [英文字幕](audio/094.srt)

**背景：** 跨区域团队负责的告警改进已经逾期，运维礼貌追踪阻塞并明确新的检查节点。

**角色：** A — Ops Engineer（运维工程师）；B — Regional Engineer（区域工程师）

**01 [A] / Ops Engineer：** The alerting action was due yesterday, November 10. Could you share the current status and any blockers so we can keep the review record accurate?
> 告警改进行动昨天，也就是11月10日到期。能否说明当前状态和阻塞，让我们准确更新复盘记录？

**02 [B] / Regional Engineer：** The rule change is ready, but the test environment is unavailable because another team extended its maintenance. I haven't run the required failure replay.
> 规则修改已完成，但另一个团队延长维护，导致测试环境不可用。我还没有执行必需的故障重放。

**03 [A] / Ops Engineer：** Thanks for explaining. Is the remaining work limited to validation, or are there implementation changes that might still affect the revised delivery date?
> 谢谢说明。剩下的工作只有验证，还是仍有实施修改，可能影响调整后的交付日期？

**04 [B] / Regional Engineer：** Validation is the known remaining step. If replay exposes a problem, we'll need another revision, so I can't honestly promise completion before testing.
> 目前已知剩余步骤是验证。如果重放发现问题，还需要再次修改，因此测试前无法如实承诺完成时间。

**05 [A] / Ops Engineer：** What support would unblock the test, and who can help secure an environment? We should avoid silently moving the due date without recording the dependency.
> 需要什么支持才能解除测试阻塞？谁能帮助获得环境？我们应记录依赖，避免悄悄修改到期日期。

**06 [B] / Regional Engineer：** I need the platform coordinator to confirm a slot. I'll contact them today and provide a definite scheduling update by November 12 at 12:00 UTC.
> 需要平台协调人确认时段。我今天联系他们，并在11月12日12:00 UTC前提供明确的排期更新。

**07 [A] / Ops Engineer：** Could another approved test environment provide equivalent coverage, or does this replay depend on a configuration available only in the unavailable environment?
> 其他批准的测试环境能否提供等效覆盖，还是此次重放依赖只有当前不可用环境才具备的配置？

**08 [B] / Regional Engineer：** A shared environment might work, but we'd need isolation and realistic queue settings. I will ask the platform coordinator to assess it rather than assume equivalence.
> 共享环境可能可行，但需要隔离和真实的队列配置。我会请平台协调人评估，不直接假设两者等效。

**09 [A] / Ops Engineer：** Until validation is complete, the original detection gap remains. Is the interim monitoring coverage still active, and does the on-call team understand its limitations?
> 验证完成前，原有检测缺口仍然存在。临时监控是否仍有效？值班团队是否理解其局限？

**10 [B] / Regional Engineer：** Yes. The manual check is assigned during each shift, and its limitations are documented. I'll reconfirm that coverage in today's regional handover.
> 是的，每班都指定了人工检查，局限也已记录。我会在今天的区域交接中再次确认覆盖。

**11 [A] / Ops Engineer：** I'll retain the original due date and mark the action blocked, with your scheduling checkpoint. Please escalate early if the platform team cannot offer an option.
> 我会保留原到期日期，将行动标为受阻，并写入你的排期检查点。如果平台团队无法提供方案，请尽早升级。

**12 [B] / Regional Engineer：** Understood. My November 12 update will include the confirmed test slot or the unresolved dependency, plus a revised completion estimate supported by that information.
> 明白。11月12日的更新会包含已确认的测试时段或未解决依赖，并依据这些信息给出修订的完成时间估计。

**可复用表达：**

- What support would unblock the test? — 需要什么支持才能解除测试阻塞？
- I'll retain the original due date and mark the action blocked. — 我会保留原到期日期，并将行动标为受阻。

**落实动作：** 区域工程师联系平台协调人，11月12日12:00 UTC更新测试排期；运维保留原期限并记录阻塞与临时监控风险。
**练习重点：** 礼貌催办时区分实施完成与验证完成，用可承诺的检查点替代无依据的完成承诺。

---

<a id="s095"></a>
## 095 修复验收与关闭工单
**分类：** Follow-up 复盘与闭环  /  **英文词数：** 286
**音频：** [播放 MP3](audio/095.mp3)  /  [英文字幕](audio/095.srt)

**背景：** 开发希望代码合并后关闭复盘行动，运维要求核对部署状态、原故障回归和业务验收。

**角色：** A — Ops Engineer（运维工程师）；B — Developer（开发工程师）

**01 [B] / Developer：** The retry fix has been merged and passed the unit tests. Can we close the action item before this week's reliability review?
> 重试修复已合并，单元测试也通过了。能否在本周可靠性评审前关闭行动项？

**02 [A] / Ops Engineer：** We still need deployment confirmation and evidence that the original failure no longer occurs. Code completion and accepted operational recovery are different milestones.
> 还需要部署确认，以及原故障不再发生的证据。代码完成和运维恢复通过验收，是不同的里程碑。

**03 [B] / Developer：** The release is scheduled for tonight, and the pipeline normally deploys automatically. Would a successful pipeline result be enough evidence to close it afterward?
> 发布安排在今晚，流水线通常自动部署。之后有成功的流水线结果，是否足以作为关闭证据？

**04 [A] / Ops Engineer：** It would establish part of the deployment history, but we must verify the running version and the agreed failure replay, not just pipeline status.
> 它能证明部分部署历史，但我们必须核实实际运行版本和约定的故障重放，而不能只看流水线状态。

**05 [B] / Developer：** The original issue created duplicate payments when a response was lost. We added a stable request key and reuse the previous result for matching retries.
> 原问题是在响应丢失时产生重复支付。我们增加了稳定的请求键，对匹配的重试复用先前结果。

**06 [A] / Ops Engineer：** Then acceptance should include a lost-response test, repeated retries, and reconciliation showing one charge for one business order. Have those scenarios been reviewed?
> 那么验收应包括响应丢失、重复重试，以及通过对账证明一笔业务订单只扣款一次。这些场景评审过了吗？

**07 [B] / Developer：** Yes, the service owner approved them. We'll also check that different orders are processed separately and that our logs don't expose sensitive payment information.
> 评审过，服务负责人已批准。我们还会检查不同订单独立处理，以及日志没有暴露敏感支付信息。

**08 [A] / Ops Engineer：** Good. Use the approved test accounts and attach the transaction references. What observation period did we agree for the production checks after deployment?
> 好的。请使用批准的测试账户并附上交易引用。我们约定部署后的生产检查观察多长时间？

**09 [B] / Developer：** Two hours of representative traffic, plus the controlled test results. If traffic is too low, we'll extend observation rather than claim the sample is sufficient.
> 两小时有代表性的流量，加上受控测试结果。如果流量太低，我们会延长观察，不宣称样本已经足够。

**10 [A] / Ops Engineer：** I'll keep the action open with implementation marked complete. Any remaining historical duplicates also need a linked reconciliation task so they aren't lost during closure.
> 我会让行动保持开放，将实施状态标为完成。历史重复交易也需关联对账任务，避免关闭时遗漏。

**11 [B] / Developer：** I'll attach the deployment record and regression evidence by November 14 at 09:00 UTC, and identify the finance owner handling any historical corrections.
> 我会在11月14日09:00 UTC前附上部署记录及回归证据，并明确负责历史更正的财务负责人。

**12 [A] / Ops Engineer：** Once the agreed criteria pass, I'll ask the service owner to accept the results and record closure, including any separate work that remains open.
> 约定标准通过后，我会请服务负责人验收结果并记录关闭，同时说明仍开放的其他工作。

**可复用表达：**

- We still need deployment confirmation. — 我们还需要部署确认。
- I'll keep the action open with implementation marked complete. — 我会让行动保持开放，并将实施状态标为完成。

**落实动作：** 开发在11月14日09:00 UTC前提交部署和回归证据；满足业务验收后由服务负责人确认关闭，历史对账单独跟踪。
**练习重点：** 区分代码合并、实际部署、生产验证和行动关闭，明确历史数据处置责任。

---

<a id="s096"></a>
## 096 全球值班交接
**分类：** Follow-up 复盘与闭环  /  **英文词数：** 289
**音频：** [播放 MP3](audio/096.mp3)  /  [英文字幕](audio/096.srt)

**背景：** 亚太值班将一个已缓解但尚未关闭的订单处理故障交给欧洲值班，双方核对积压、升级阈值及沟通责任。

**角色：** A — APAC Ops Engineer（亚太运维工程师）；B — EMEA Ops Engineer（欧洲中东非洲运维工程师）

**01 [A] / APAC Ops Engineer：** The order service has been stable for forty minutes after we reduced batch concurrency. Root-cause investigation and backlog reconciliation remain open at this handover.
> 降低批处理并发后，订单服务已稳定四十分钟。交接时，根因调查和积压对账仍未完成。

**02 [B] / EMEA Ops Engineer：** Before I take ownership, please summarize current customer impact, the remaining backlog, and whether any temporary controls need attention during my shift.
> 接手前，请总结当前客户影响、剩余积压，以及我值班期间需要关注哪些临时控制。

**03 [A] / APAC Ops Engineer：** New orders are completing normally. About four thousand eight hundred older orders remain queued, and processing is averaging three hundred per minute without a completion guarantee.
> 新订单完成正常，约四千八百笔旧订单仍在队列中，平均每分钟处理三百笔，但无法保证完成时间。

**04 [B] / EMEA Ops Engineer：** Are those counts from the latest snapshot, and how will I distinguish normal drain progress from a recurrence that requires escalation to the incident lead?
> 这些数量来自最新快照吗？如何区分正常积压消退与需要向故障负责人升级的复发情况？

**05 [A] / APAC Ops Engineer：** They are from 07:55 UTC. The ticket defines escalation if queue age rises for ten minutes or order failures exceed the approved incident threshold.
> 数据来自07:55 UTC。工单规定，队列年龄连续十分钟上升，或订单失败超过批准的故障阈值时升级。

**06 [B] / EMEA Ops Engineer：** I'll use those documented thresholds. Is the lower concurrency setting still required, and does changing it need approval from the current incident lead?
> 我会采用记录中的阈值。较低并发配置是否仍需要保留？修改它是否需要当前故障负责人批准？

**07 [A] / APAC Ops Engineer：** Yes. Keep it in place until the dependency owner reviews the evidence. Raising concurrency could overload that dependency again, so any adjustment needs coordinated approval.
> 需要保留，直到依赖负责人审阅证据。提高并发可能再次压垮依赖，因此任何调整都需协调批准。

**08 [B] / EMEA Ops Engineer：** Who owns reconciliation, and are there orders with uncertain outcomes that we must not replay automatically while working through the remaining processing backlog?
> 谁负责对账？是否有处理结果不确定的订单，在处理剩余积压时不能自动重放？

**09 [A] / APAC Ops Engineer：** The application owner is checking uncertain transactions against payment records. Their list is linked in the ticket; those orders are excluded from automated replay.
> 应用负责人正在对照支付记录检查结果不确定的交易，工单中已链接清单；这些订单被排除在自动重放之外。

**10 [B] / EMEA Ops Engineer：** I have the ticket, dashboards, restricted evidence links, and escalation contacts open. The next stakeholder update is at 08:30 UTC; I'll lead that update.
> 我已打开工单、看板、受限证据链接和升级联系人。下一次干系人更新在08:30 UTC，由我主持。

**11 [A] / APAC Ops Engineer：** Correct. Please explicitly confirm ownership from 08:00 UTC. I'll remain reachable during the agreed overlap if anything in the handover needs clarification.
> 正确。请明确确认从08:00 UTC起接手责任。如果交接内容需要澄清，我会在约定的重叠时段保持可联系。

**12 [B] / EMEA Ops Engineer：** I've reviewed the handover and accept ownership from 08:00 UTC. I'll monitor recovery, coordinate the pending investigation, and record the next reconciliation checkpoint.
> 我已审阅交接内容，并从08:00 UTC起接手责任。我会监控恢复、协调待完成调查，并记录下次对账检查点。

**可复用表达：**

- I've reviewed the handover and accept ownership from 08:00 UTC. — 我已审阅交接内容，并从08:00 UTC起接手责任。
- Root-cause investigation and backlog reconciliation remain open. — 根因调查和积压对账仍未完成。

**落实动作：** 欧洲中东非洲值班08:00 UTC接手，保持限并发控制，08:30 UTC主持更新并跟进调查和对账。
**练习重点：** 交接确认现状、临时控制、阈值、待办和责任起点，并取得明确接手回应。
