<a id="s097"></a>
## 097 周度运维健康汇报
**分类：** Follow-up 复盘与闭环  /  **英文词数：** 287
**音频：** [播放 MP3](audio/097.mp3)  /  [英文字幕](audio/097.srt)

**背景：** 全球周会上汇报稳定性趋势，运维说明故障次数减少但影响时长增加，并请求容量决策。

**角色：** A — Ops Engineer（运维工程师）；B — Global Lead（全球团队负责人）

**01 [B] / Global Lead：** What changed in service health this week, and which issue needs a leadership decision before next week's expected increase in reporting traffic?
> 本周服务健康状况有什么变化？在下周报表流量预计增加前，哪项问题需要管理层决策？

**02 [A] / Ops Engineer：** Incident count fell from six to four, but total customer-impact duration increased from thirty to seventy-five minutes because one outage lasted much longer.
> 故障次数从六次降为四次，但一次较长中断使总客户影响时长从三十分钟增至七十五分钟。

**03 [B] / Global Lead：** Are we using the same measurement across both weeks? I want to avoid celebrating fewer tickets if business disruption actually became worse overall.
> 两周的衡量口径相同吗？如果业务中断总体变严重，我不希望因为工单数量减少就庆祝。

**04 [A] / Ops Engineer：** Yes. We sum each incident's customer-impact duration using the same start and recovery criteria. Affected-user counts and failed transaction counts are reported separately.
> 是的，我们按相同的开始和恢复标准，累计每次故障的客户影响时长。受影响用户数和失败交易数单独报告。

**05 [B] / Global Lead：** What made the longer outage difficult to restore, and have the immediate recovery actions reduced the chance of another disruption during next week's workload?
> 什么导致较长故障难以恢复？即时恢复措施是否降低了下周工作负载下再次中断的可能性？

**06 [A] / Ops Engineer：** Reporting jobs saturated storage throughput on the shared database. Staggering those jobs restored normal operation, but it leaves limited margin for the projected workload.
> 报表任务耗尽了共享数据库的存储吞吐能力。错开任务后恢复正常，但对预测负载的余量仍然有限。

**07 [B] / Global Lead：** What is the proposed capacity action, and what evidence suggests it addresses the measured bottleneck rather than simply increasing application servers without a benefit?
> 拟议容量措施是什么？什么证据表明它能解决测得的瓶颈，而不是无实际收益地增加应用服务器？

**08 [A] / Ops Engineer：** We propose increasing provisioned storage throughput. The database team's controlled test improved job completion time while holding application capacity constant, and the vendor confirmed availability.
> 我们建议提高预配存储吞吐量。数据库团队的受控测试在应用容量不变时改善了任务完成时间，供应商也确认资源可用。

**09 [B] / Global Lead：** What decision do you need from me, and what risk remains if we delay it until the next regular budget review later this month?
> 需要我做什么决定？如果拖到本月后续的常规预算评审，仍会有什么风险？

**10 [A] / Ops Engineer：** We need budget approval by Friday, November 13, at 16:00 UTC to request the change window. Delaying would extend dependence on manual batch scheduling.
> 需要在11月13日星期五16:00 UTC前批准预算，以申请变更窗口。延迟会让我们更久依赖人工排批。

**11 [B] / Global Lead：** Send the test results, cost, and risk summary today. I'll decide by that checkpoint, while the database owner retains responsibility for technical change approval.
> 今天请发来测试结果、费用和风险摘要。我会在该检查点前决策，技术变更审批仍由数据库负责人负责。

**12 [A] / Ops Engineer：** I'll send that package and keep the interim schedule active. Next week's review will report actual workload, capacity margin, and whether the approved action took effect.
> 我会发送材料并保持临时排期。下周评审会报告实际负载、容量余量，以及获批措施是否已经生效。

**可复用表达：**

- We need budget approval by Friday, November 13, at 16:00 UTC. — 我们需要在11月13日星期五16:00 UTC前获得预算批准。
- Delaying would extend dependence on manual batch scheduling. — 延迟会让我们更久依赖人工排批。

**落实动作：** 运维当天提交测试、成本及风险材料，全球负责人11月13日16:00 UTC前决定预算；数据库负责人另行审批技术变更。
**练习重点：** 使用统一口径解释相反趋势，连接容量证据、风险和明确的决策请求。

---

<a id="s098"></a>
## 098 更新Runbook与知识转移
**分类：** Follow-up 复盘与闭环  /  **英文词数：** 294
**音频：** [播放 MP3](audio/098.mp3)  /  [英文字幕](audio/098.srt)

**背景：** 故障暴露手册过时，团队通过新同事实际演练验证修订内容，并建立长期维护责任。

**角色：** A — Ops Engineer（运维工程师）；B — Team Lead（团队负责人）；C — New Engineer（新加入工程师）

**01 [B] / Team Lead：** The recovery runbook still refers to the retired platform, and our new colleague couldn't follow it during the incident. How will we make it usable?
> 恢复手册仍引用已退役平台，新同事在故障中无法照着执行。我们怎样让它真正可用？

**02 [A] / Ops Engineer：** I'll update the procedure for the current platform, assign a permanent maintainer, and validate the draft with an engineer who didn't handle this incident.
> 我会按当前平台更新流程，指定长期维护人，并请未参与本次故障的工程师验证草稿。

**03 [C] / New Engineer：** I can test it. Last time, I couldn't tell which environment the commands targeted, or what output meant the check had actually succeeded.
> 我可以测试。上次我无法判断命令针对哪个环境，也不知道什么输出才表示检查真正成功。

**04 [A] / Ops Engineer：** Each step will state its purpose, target environment, required access, expected result, and stop condition. Any command examples will use clearly labeled placeholders.
> 每一步都会说明目的、目标环境、所需权限、预期结果和停止条件。命令示例会使用明确标注的占位符。

**05 [B] / Team Lead：** Please include escalation contacts and prerequisites. A procedure that depends on an unavailable permission or an unknown service owner won't help the on-call team.
> 请包括升级联系人和前提条件。如果流程依赖无法获得的权限或不明确的服务负责人，就帮不了值班团队。

**06 [C] / New Engineer：** Can the exercise also include an unexpected result? Following the happy path won't show whether I know when to stop and ask for help.
> 演练能否包含非预期结果？只走顺利路径，无法证明我知道何时应停止并求助。

**07 [A] / Ops Engineer：** Yes. We'll use an approved test environment and simulate a failed dependency check. You should stop before the recovery action and use the documented escalation path.
> 可以。我们使用批准的测试环境，模拟依赖检查失败。你应在恢复操作前停止，并走记录的升级路径。

**08 [B] / Team Lead：** Who will maintain the document after this revision? We need a review trigger when platform changes affect the procedure, as well as a scheduled review date.
> 本次修订后谁维护文档？除了定期复查日期，还需要在平台变更影响流程时触发复查。

**09 [A] / Ops Engineer：** The service's operations owner will maintain it. Relevant change records must include a documentation check, and the first scheduled review will be December 15.
> 由服务运维负责人维护。相关变更记录必须包含文档检查，首次定期复查安排在12月15日。

**10 [C] / New Engineer：** I'll record every unclear step during the exercise instead of filling gaps from memory. That should show whether another new engineer could follow it independently.
> 演练时我会记录每个不清楚的步骤，不凭记忆补全缺口。这样能看出另一位新工程师能否独立执行。

**11 [A] / Ops Engineer：** I'll deliver the draft by November 16 at 10:00 UTC. We'll run the exercise that afternoon and attach the results to the document's revision history.
> 我会在11月16日10:00 UTC前提交草稿，当天下午演练，并把结果附在文档修订历史中。

**12 [B] / Team Lead：** Agreed. Publish the reviewed version after the gaps are resolved, then cover it in the next handover. We'll measure usability through the exercise evidence.
> 同意。解决缺口后发布评审通过的版本，再在下一次交接中讲解。我们通过演练证据衡量可用性。

**可复用表达：**

- Each step will state its purpose, target environment, required access, expected result, and stop condition. — 每一步都会说明目的、目标环境、所需权限、预期结果和停止条件。
- I'll record every unclear step during the exercise. — 我会记录演练中每个不清楚的步骤。

**落实动作：** 运维11月16日10:00 UTC前完成草稿，当天下午由新工程师演练；解决缺口后发布，12月15日首次定期复查。
**练习重点：** 解释手册的可执行性标准，通过陌生读者演练检验知识转移，并落实维护责任。

---

<a id="s099"></a>
## 099 跨团队责任分歧
**分类：** Follow-up 复盘与闭环  /  **英文词数：** 298
**音频：** [播放 MP3](audio/099.mp3)  /  [英文字幕](audio/099.srt)

**背景：** 应用和平台负责人对重试风暴后的改进行动归属有分歧，运维协调按控制措施拆分责任。

**角色：** A — Ops Engineer（运维工程师）；B — Platform Lead（平台负责人）；C — Application Lead（应用负责人）

**01 [B] / Platform Lead：** This action belongs to the application team. The platform ran as configured; we can't own every failure caused by how an application uses it.
> 这项行动应归应用团队。平台按配置运行，我们不能负责所有由应用使用方式引发的故障。

**02 [C] / Application Lead：** We agree the retry behavior needs a code fix, but the shared platform didn't detect the traffic surge early enough to protect neighboring services.
> 我们同意重试行为需要代码修复，但共享平台没有足够早地发现流量激增，无法保护相邻服务。

**03 [A] / Ops Engineer：** Let's map the actions to the controls that need to change. We can assign ownership without requiring one team to accept responsibility for the entire incident.
> 我们按需要修改的控制措施划分行动。分配工作责任，不需要某个团队承担整个故障的全部责任。

**04 [B] / Platform Lead：** What specific platform control are you proposing? A broad action such as prevent overload could become an unlimited commitment that we cannot reasonably accept.
> 具体提议什么平台控制？像“防止过载”这样宽泛的行动，可能变成我们无法合理接受的无限承诺。

**05 [A] / Ops Engineer：** The first platform action is a detection rule for sustained saturation, with notification routing and a tested threshold. The application action is bounded retries.
> 第一项平台行动是针对持续饱和的检测规则，包含通知路由和经过测试的阈值。应用行动是限制重试。

**06 [C] / Application Lead：** I'll be accountable for the retry change, including a maximum attempt count and delay behavior. We'll share representative traffic patterns for testing the detection rule.
> 我负责重试修改，包括最大尝试次数和延迟行为，并分享有代表性的流量模式，支持检测规则测试。

**07 [B] / Platform Lead：** I can own the detection rule if the application team supplies those patterns. My team will document what the platform signal detects and its limitations.
> 如果应用团队提供这些模式，我可以负责检测规则。我的团队会记录平台信号能检测什么，以及相关局限。

**08 [A] / Ops Engineer：** Good. Each action will list one accountable owner and the supporting team. We'll also create a joint validation task with me accountable for coordinating evidence.
> 好的。每项行动会列一名最终负责人和支持团队。另外建立联合验证任务，由我负责协调证据。

**09 [C] / Application Lead：** Our test patterns will be ready by November 17 at 11:00 UTC. Can we set the integration test after both individual changes pass their reviews?
> 我们的测试模式在11月17日11:00 UTC前准备好。能否在两项修改分别评审通过后安排集成测试？

**10 [B] / Platform Lead：** Yes. I'll provide a rule review date once I see the patterns. We should record that dependency rather than promise an unsupported delivery date today.
> 可以。看到模式后我会给出规则评审日期。我们应记录依赖，而不是今天承诺没有依据的交付日期。

**11 [A] / Ops Engineer：** At the November 17 checkpoint, we'll confirm the remaining dates and test scope. Any unresolved control boundary will go to the service owner for a decision.
> 11月17日检查时，我们确认剩余日期和测试范围。任何未解决的控制边界问题都交给服务负责人决策。

**12 [C] / Application Lead：** That works. We'll keep the incident analysis factual and track the code, platform rule, and joint validation as separate commitments with linked completion evidence.
> 可以。我们会让故障分析以事实为依据，把代码、平台规则和联合验证作为独立承诺跟踪，并关联完成证据。

**可复用表达：**

- Let's map the actions to the controls that need to change. — 我们按需要修改的控制措施划分行动。
- Each action will list one accountable owner and the supporting team. — 每项行动列一名最终负责人和支持团队。

**落实动作：** 应用负责人11月17日11:00 UTC前提供测试模式；平台负责人据此确定规则评审时间，运维协调联合验证及争议升级。
**练习重点：** 把团队归责争论转成具体控制与支持关系，明确单项负责人和跨团队依赖。

---

<a id="s100"></a>
## 100 月度复盘与持续改进
**分类：** Follow-up 复盘与闭环  /  **英文词数：** 292
**音频：** [播放 MP3](audio/100.mp3)  /  [英文字幕](audio/100.srt)

**背景：** 月度管理评审关注可靠性改进是否真实有效，运维结合可比指标、受控验证和证据局限提出下一轮重点。

**角色：** A — Ops Engineer（运维工程师）；B — Operations Director（运维总监）

**01 [B] / Operations Director：** We closed eighteen improvement actions this month. What evidence shows that reliability actually improved, rather than simply showing that our action tracker became shorter?
> 本月关闭了十八项改进行动。什么证据能证明可靠性确实改善，而不只是行动清单变短？

**02 [A] / Ops Engineer：** We'll compare recurrence, detection time, restoration time, and customer impact across comparable periods. We'll also review controlled tests of the specific failures those actions addressed.
> 我们会在可比周期内比较复发、发现耗时、恢复耗时和客户影响，同时审阅针对这些行动所解决故障的受控测试。

**03 [B] / Operations Director：** What makes the periods comparable? This month's traffic was lower, so fewer incidents might reflect lighter demand instead of stronger controls or better recovery.
> 怎样保证周期可比？本月流量较低，故障更少可能是需求变轻，而非控制更强或恢复更好。

**04 [A] / Ops Engineer：** We'll report traffic volume, peak concurrency, and release frequency alongside the incident metrics. For workload-sensitive measures, we'll compare similar load bands and disclose remaining differences.
> 我们会同时报告流量、峰值并发和发布频次。对负载敏感的指标，比较相近负载区间，并披露剩余差异。

**05 [B] / Operations Director：** The connection failure hasn't recurred. Can we count that absence as proof that the new configuration validation eliminated this particular incident class from production?
> 连接故障没有再次发生。能否据此证明新配置验证已经从生产中消除了这类故障？

**06 [A] / Ops Engineer：** It is encouraging, but the observation period is short. We replayed five invalid configurations, and the new gate blocked all five before deployment.
> 这是积极信号，但观察期较短。我们重放了五种无效配置，新关卡在部署前阻止了全部五种。

**07 [B] / Operations Director：** That demonstrates specific test coverage. Do we also have evidence that responders find problems sooner and restore business service faster when prevention fails?
> 这证明了特定测试覆盖。如果预防失效，是否还有证据表明响应人员能更早发现问题、更快恢复业务？

**08 [A] / Ops Engineer：** In matched exercises, detection fell from seven minutes to two, and restoration from twenty-eight to eighteen. Real incidents remain too few for a strong trend claim.
> 在匹配的演练中，发现耗时从七分钟降至两分钟，恢复从二十八分钟降至十八分钟。真实故障仍太少，不足以形成强趋势结论。

**09 [B] / Operations Director：** Where is the largest remaining gap, and what should we prioritize next instead of spreading effort evenly across every open suggestion from the reviews?
> 最大的剩余缺口在哪里？下一步应优先做什么，而不是在复盘的每条开放建议上平均投入？

**10 [A] / Ops Engineer：** Exercises exposed stale recovery permissions that delayed access. I recommend validating those permissions and their approved renewal process before adding more low-value dashboard changes.
> 演练暴露了过期恢复权限，延误了访问。我建议先验证这些权限及其批准的续期流程，再增加价值较低的看板修改。

**11 [B] / Operations Director：** Prepare a focused proposal with the access owner, acceptance evidence, and expected effort by November 20 at 15:00 UTC. Keep the longer-term measures running.
> 请在11月20日15:00 UTC前与权限负责人准备聚焦方案，写明验收证据和预计投入，同时持续跟踪长期指标。

**12 [A] / Ops Engineer：** I'll bring that proposal and label each conclusion by its evidence strength. Next month, we'll review both control effectiveness and the limits of our confidence.
> 我会带来方案，并按证据强度标注每项结论。下个月同时评审控制有效性和我们判断把握的局限。

**可复用表达：**

- The observation period is short. — 观察期较短。
- We'll compare similar load bands and disclose remaining differences. — 我们会比较相近负载区间，并披露剩余差异。

**落实动作：** 运维与权限负责人11月20日15:00 UTC前提交恢复权限改进方案，持续跟踪可比运营指标和受控验证结果。
**练习重点：** 区分行动关闭与效果证明，说明样本和可比性局限，以证据决定下一轮优先级。

---

## 常用术语

|术语|英文|中文|
|---|---|---|
|Infra|Infrastructure|基础设施|
|Ops|Operations|运维|
|SRE|Site Reliability Engineering|站点可靠性工程|
|APAC|Asia-Pacific|亚太地区|
|EMEA|Europe, Middle East and Africa|欧洲、中东及非洲|
|UTC|Coordinated Universal Time|协调世界时|
|On-call|On-call engineer / rotation|值班工程师／值班轮换|
|Incident bridge|Incident coordination call|故障协调会议|
|Escalation|Escalation|升级协调／上报|
|Mitigation|Mitigation|缓解措施|
|Rollback|Rollback|回退|
|Failover|Failover|故障切换|
|RCA|Root Cause Analysis|根因分析|
|Post-incident review|Post-incident review|故障复盘|
|Runbook|Operational runbook|操作手册|
|CAB|Change Advisory Board|变更咨询委员会|
|Go / No-Go|Proceed / hold decision|继续／暂停决策|
|Canary|Canary rollout|灰度发布|
|OOM|Out Of Memory|内存耗尽|
|DNS|Domain Name System|域名系统|
|TLS|Transport Layer Security|传输层安全协议|
|SSO|Single Sign-On|单点登录|
|WAF|Web Application Firewall|Web应用防火墙|
|P95|95th percentile|95分位数：约95%的样本不超过此值|
|SLI|Service Level Indicator|服务水平指标|
|SLO|Service Level Objective|服务水平目标|
|Error budget|Allowed unreliability under an SLO|SLO允许的不可靠额度|
|RTO|Recovery Time Objective|恢复时间目标|
|RPO|Recovery Point Objective|恢复点目标|
|Idempotency|Idempotency|幂等性：重复执行不产生额外业务效果|

## 三类汇报模板

### Incident Update｜故障进展汇报

Current impact: [function] is affected in [region] since [date/time UTC].
> 当前影响：[功能]自[日期／UTC时间]起在[区域]受影响。

Confirmed facts: [evidence]. We are still checking [open question].
> 已确认：[证据]。仍在检查：[待确认问题]。

We are [current action]. Recovery time is [estimate / not yet confirmed].
> 我们正在[当前措施]。恢复时间为[预估／尚未确认]。

The next update will be at [date/time UTC].
> 下次更新将在[日期／UTC时间]。

### Follow-up｜行动项跟进

Could you share the status of [action], which was due on [date]?
> 能更新一下原定[日期]到期的[行动]吗？

What is blocking progress, and what support would help?
> 进展被什么阻塞，需要什么支持？

Please confirm the owner, revised checkpoint, and acceptance evidence.
> 请确认负责人、新检查节点和验收证据。

### Handover｜跨时区交接

Current state: [status]. Completed actions: [actions and results].
> 当前状态：[状态]。已完成：[动作及结果]。

Open risks: [risks]. Next actions: [owner, action, deadline UTC].
> 未解决风险：[风险]。下一步：[负责人、动作、UTC截止时间]。

Escalate if [trigger]. The next stakeholder update is at [date/time UTC].
> 若[触发条件]则升级。下次干系人更新在[日期／UTC时间]。

Please confirm that you have reviewed the handover and accepted ownership.
> 请确认你已查阅交接内容并接手责任。
